  function parseDMY(d) {
  if (d instanceof Date) return d.getTime();
  var s = d.toString().split('/');
  if (s.length === 3) return new Date(s[2], s[1]-1, s[0]).getTime();
  return new Date(d).getTime();
}
// ─── NARRATIVE QUALITY RULES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Single definition of epistemic labelling, grey zones, result interactions,
// analyte citation rules, and recommendation quality standards.
// Referenced in all narrative and specialist prompts — edit here to update all.
var NARRATIVE_RULES =
'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' +
  'The data block may include a PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES section containing qualitative context\n' +
  'not captured in structured test results or records.\n' +
  'These notes are supplementary — they enrich clinical interpretation but never override\n' +
  'test results, clinical records, or evidence-based findings.\n' +
  'Where patient notes are relevant to a clinical finding, weave them naturally into your analysis.\n' +
  'Where a patient asks a question, answer it within the relevant clinical section.\n' +
  'PATIENT NOTES CRITICAL EVALUATION — MANDATORY:\n' +
'Patient context notes may contain the patient\'s own hypotheses or suspected diagnoses.\n' +
'These must be evaluated against the objective evidence, not accepted uncritically.\n' +
'If a patient suggestion is consistent with the clinical data, note it as consistent.\n' +
'If it is inconsistent with or unsupported by the objective evidence, say so clearly and\n' +
'explain why — do not simply validate it because the patient believes it.\n' +
'Patient notes are anecdotal self-report. Clinical records, test results, and published\n' +
'evidence always take precedence over patient-reported interpretation.\n' +
'When drawing inferences from family history, always consider the base rate of the\n' +
'finding in the general population before concluding a familial pattern is clinically\n' +
'significant. Many findings are common enough that clustering in a family may simply\n' +
'reflect population prevalence rather than a shared inherited condition.\n' +
'Label any family pattern inference as HYPOTHESIS unless formally confirmed.\n\n' +
'EPISTEMIC LABELLING — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'State confirmed facts plainly — do not prefix them with "FACT:". Just write them directly, always citing the actual value and date.\n' +
  'Only label statements that are NOT directly evidenced, using these markers:\n' +
  '• ASSOCIATION: a known clinical link, but not confirmed in this patient\'s data — state explicitly that this has or has not been seen in their results\n' +
  '• HYPOTHESIS: a plausible explanation worth considering but not proven — use language like "one possible explanation", "worth considering", "may suggest"\n' +
  '• RECOMMENDATION: an action suggested based on evidence or clinical reasoning\n\n' +
  'Never present a hypothesis as a fact.\n' +
  'Never present an association as confirmed without checking the actual values provided.\n' +
  'Never use lab reference ranges as the definition of normal — use research thresholds.\n' +
  'Always cite the specific value and date when making any factual claim.\n\n' +
  'GREY ZONES — do not describe values in these ranges as simply "normal":\n' +
  '• B12: 350-500 ng/L is a functional deficiency grey zone — neurological symptoms can occur even within lab range\n' +
  '• Ferritin: lab minimum ~13 µg/L but research suggests symptoms below 30-50 µg/L\n' +
  '• Vitamin D: lab sufficient at 50 nmol/L but research optimal is 75-125 nmol/L\n' +
  '• TSH: lab range 0.35-4.94 but wellbeing research favours 0.5-2.0\n' +
  '• MCV: values above 95 fL warrant B12/folate investigation even if below 100 fL macrocytosis threshold\n\n' +
  'RESULT INTERACTIONS — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'Where two results appear contradictory or modify each other\'s interpretation, analyse explicitly.\n' +
  'Do NOT interpret results in isolation. Key interactions to always check:\n' +
  '• High MCV + low iron/ferritin: masking effect — true B12/folate deficiency may be more severe than MCV suggests\n' +
  '• Low B12 + high MCV or homocysteine: functional deficiency even if B12 appears borderline\n' +
  '• Low ferritin + normal Hb: non-anaemic iron deficiency — still clinically significant\n' +
  '• Rising haematocrit alongside rising ferritin: likely iron recovery, not pathological\n\n' +
  'ANALYTE CITATION RULE:\n' +
  'Never mention an analyte by name without either:\n' +
  '(a) citing its actual value and date from the data provided, OR\n' +
  '(b) explicitly stating "no [analyte] result is available in the data"\n' +
  'Never assume a result is normal or abnormal without citing the actual value.\n\n' +
  'RECOMMENDATION QUALITY:\n' +
  'Recommendations must be specific to this patient\'s documented history.\n' +
  'Never give generic dietary or lifestyle advice when specific clinical investigation is more appropriate.\n' +
  'Always specify the exact test, referral type, or action — not vague guidance like "improve diet" or "see your GP".\n\n' +
  'ACTION PATHWAY TRIAGE — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'For every recommended action, assess the most realistic pathway to actually achieve it,\n' +
' not just the theoretically correct pathway. Apply this framework:\n\n' +
'• HIGH CONFIDENCE + GP CAN ACTION IT: Route via GP. Be specific about what to request,\n' +
'  how to frame it, and what NICE guidance supports the request.\n\n' +
'• HIGH CONFIDENCE + GP UNLIKELY TO ACTION OR TIMELINE TOO SLOW: Route via private\n' +
'  testing or private consultation. Name specific services where possible:\n' +
'  - Medichecks, Thriva, Monitor My Health for blood tests\n' +
'  - Private GP services (Babylon, Push Doctor, local private GPs) for prescriptions\n' +
'  - Private consultants (gynaecology, haematology, endocrinology) where specialist\n' +
'    input is needed without waiting months on NHS\n\n' +
'• LOWER CONFIDENCE BUT WORTH PURSUING: Recommend private testing first to build\n' +
'  an evidence base, then return to GP with results. This is often faster and more\n' +
'  productive than requesting speculative tests via NHS.\n\n' +
'• EVIDENCE BUILDING BEFORE GP: Where a test is unlikely to be ordered by a GP\n' +
'  without prior evidence (e.g. holotranscobalamin, MMA, SIBO breath test, detailed\n' +
'  hormone panels), recommend getting it privately first, then presenting results.\n\n' +
  'Quality of life impact matters. Where a finding is significantly affecting daily\n' +
  'function, recommend the fastest realistic pathway to resolution — not the slowest\n' +
  'bureaucratically correct one. A 6-month NHS waiting list is not an acceptable\n' +
  'action plan for something causing daily symptoms.\n\n' +
  'Always note approximate private costs where known, so the patient can make\n' +
  'an informed decision about which route to take.\n\n' +
  'PRIOR INVESTIGATION CHECK — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'Before recommending ANY test or investigation, you must first check the GP records, test results, and consultation notes provided.\n' +
  'If a test has already been performed and a result is available, do NOT recommend it — instead cite the result, its date, and interpret what it means for the current clinical picture.\n' +
  'If a test was done but the result is borderline or inconclusive, say so explicitly and explain whether repeat testing is warranted.\n' +
  'A test that has returned a normal result should never appear as Priority 1 — it should appear only if there is a specific clinical reason to repeat it, with that reason explicitly stated.\n\n' +
  'CALIBRATED LANGUAGE — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'Use measured, non-alarmist language throughout. Do not dramatise findings or medication side effects.\n' +
  'GI side effects from oral iron including bleeding are clinically recognised — do not describe them as "highly unusual" or "highly concerning".\n' +
  'Oral iron intolerance requiring IV iron should be framed as: "oral iron was not tolerated, necessitating IV iron."\n' +
  'When citing historical results (older than 12 months), note the age of the data explicitly.\n' +
  'Never describe a result as simply "normal" or "abnormal" without citing the specific value, date, and relevant research context.\n\n' +
  'DIAGNOSIS STATUS — MANDATORY:\n' +
  'Never state a condition as confirmed unless it appears as a formal diagnosis in the clinical records.\n' +
  'If a condition is suspected, queried, or under investigation, always use language like:\n' +
  '"possible", "suspected", "under investigation", "not yet confirmed", "raised as a possibility".\n' +
  'This applies even if the clinical picture strongly suggests a particular diagnosis.\n' +
  'A hypothesis however compelling is not a diagnosis until formally confirmed by a clinician.';

// ─── STEP TIMER HELPERS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Lightweight run timing — uses ScriptProperties only, zero API calls.
// Tracks: current run elapsed, last run time, rolling 5-run average per step.

function _stepStart(stepKey) {
  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
      .setProperty('TIMER_START_' + stepKey, Date.now().toString());
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _stepEnd(stepKey) {
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var start = +(props.getProperty('TIMER_START_' + stepKey) || '0');
    if (!start) return 0;
    var elapsed = Math.round((Date.now() - start) / 1000);
    props.setProperty('TIMER_LAST_' + stepKey, elapsed.toString());
    var hist = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TIMER_HIST_' + stepKey) || '[]');
    hist.push(elapsed);
    if (hist.length > 5) hist.shift();
    props.setProperty('TIMER_HIST_' + stepKey, JSON.stringify(hist));
    props.deleteProperty('TIMER_START_' + stepKey);
    return elapsed;
  } catch(e) { return 0; }
}

function _stepCurrent(stepKey) {
  // Returns seconds elapsed since _stepStart without stopping the timer
  try {
    var start = +(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
      .getProperty('TIMER_START_' + stepKey) || '0');
    return start ? Math.round((Date.now() - start) / 1000) : 0;
  } catch(e) { return 0; }
}

function _fmtSecs(s) {
  if (!s || s < 0) return '—';
  if (s < 60) return s + 's';
  return Math.floor(s/60) + 'm ' + (s % 60) + 's';
}

function _avgHist(stepKey) {
  try {
    var hist = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
      .getProperty('TIMER_HIST_' + stepKey) || '[]');
    if (!hist.length) return null;
    return Math.round(hist.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0) / hist.length);
  } catch(e) { return null; }
}

function _runTotal(keys) {
  // Sum of TIMER_LAST for all keys — gives total for this run
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    return keys.reduce(function(sum, k) {
      return sum + (+(props.getProperty('TIMER_LAST_' + k) || '0'));
    }, 0);
  } catch(e) { return 0; }
}

function clearTimingHistory() {
  // Menu callable — wipes all timer data for a clean slate
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var all = props.getKeys();
  all.forEach(function(k) {
    if (k.indexOf('TIMER_') === 0) props.deleteProperty(k);
  });
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Timing history cleared.');
}

function showTimingReport() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var STEPS = [
    { key: 'rich_s0_prereason', label: 'Step 1  Pre-reasoning (silent pass)' },
    { key: 'rich_s1_story',     label: 'Step 2  Story + blood results' },
    { key: 'rich_s2_meds',      label: 'Step 3  Medications' },
    { key: 'rich_s3_annexes',   label: 'Step 4  Action plan + annexes' },
    { key: 'rich_s4_questions', label: 'Step 5  Patient questions' },
    { key: 'rich_s5_matrix',    label: 'Step 5b Diagnosis matrix' },
  ];
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss');

  var tabName = '⏱ Timing';
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
  if (!sh) sh = ss.insertSheet(tabName);
  sh.clearContents(); sh.clearFormats();

  sh.getRange(1,1).setValue('⏱ AI Step Timing Report').setFontSize(14).setFontWeight('bold');
  sh.getRange(2,1).setValue('Updated: ' + now).setFontSize(9).setFontStyle('italic');
  sh.getRange(3,1).setValue('Zero API calls — ScriptProperties only. Tracks last 5 runs per step.')
    .setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#64748b');

  var headers = ['Step', 'Last run', 'Run before', 'Avg (up to 5 runs)', 'Runs recorded'];
  sh.getRange(5, 1, 1, headers.length)
    .setValues([headers])
    .setFontWeight('bold')
    .setBackground('#1e293b')
    .setFontColor('#ffffff')
    .setFontSize(10);

  var rows = [];
  var lastRunTotal = 0;

  STEPS.forEach(function(step) {
    var hist = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TIMER_HIST_' + step.key) || '[]');
    var last = hist.length >= 1 ? hist[hist.length - 1] : null;
    var prev = hist.length >= 2 ? hist[hist.length - 2] : null;
    var avg  = hist.length > 0
      ? Math.round(hist.reduce(function(a,b){ return a+b; }, 0) / hist.length)
      : null;
    if (last !== null) lastRunTotal += last;
    rows.push([
      step.label,
      last !== null ? _fmtSecs(last) : '—',
      prev !== null ? _fmtSecs(prev) : '—',
      avg  !== null ? _fmtSecs(avg)  : '—',
      hist.length
    ]);
  });

  sh.getRange(6, 1, rows.length, headers.length).setValues(rows).setFontSize(10);
  for (var r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
    sh.getRange(6 + r, 1, 1, headers.length)
      .setBackground(r % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff');
  }

  var totalRow = rows.length + 6;
  sh.getRange(totalRow, 1, 1, headers.length)
    .setValues([['TOTAL', _fmtSecs(lastRunTotal), '—', '—', '—']])
    .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#dbeafe').setFontSize(10);

  sh.setColumnWidth(1, 260); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 90);
  sh.setColumnWidth(3, 90);  sh.setColumnWidth(4, 130); sh.setColumnWidth(5, 110);
  ss.setActiveSheet(sh);

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '⏱ Timing Report',
    'Written to ⏱ Timing tab.\n\nLast run total: ' + _fmtSecs(lastRunTotal) + '\n\nRun again after a few reports to build averages.',
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// ─── HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────────────
function setWidths(sheet, widthsArr) {
  for (var i = 0; i < widthsArr.length; i++) sheet.setColumnWidth(i + 1, widthsArr[i]);
}
function getPatientNotes(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('📝 Patient Notes');
  if (!sheet) return '';
  var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
  var lines = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (!data[i][0] && !data[i][1]) continue;
    var topic = data[i][0] ? data[i][0].toString().trim() : '';
    var notes = data[i][1] ? data[i][1].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!notes) continue;
    lines.push((topic ? topic + ': ' : '') + notes);
  }
  return lines.join('\n');
}
function addDropdown(sheet, row, col, options) {
  sheet.getRange(row, col).setDataValidation(
    SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(options, true).setAllowInvalid(true).build());
}
function freeze(sheet, rows) { sheet.setFrozenRows(rows || 0); }
function sectionBanner(sheet, row, text, numCols, bg, fg) {
  bg = bg || C.navy; fg = fg || '#ffffff';
  var r = sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, numCols);
  if (numCols > 1) r.merge();
  r.setValue(text).setBackground(bg).setFontColor(fg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(row, 32);
}
function colHeaders(sheet, row, headers, bg, fg) {
  bg = bg || C.teal; fg = fg || '#ffffff';
  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    sheet.getRange(row, i + 1).setValue(headers[i]).setBackground(bg).setFontColor(fg)
      .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  }
  sheet.setRowHeight(row, 24);
}
function borders(sheet, startRow, startCol, numRows, numCols) {
  sheet.getRange(startRow, startCol, numRows, numCols)
    .setBorder(true,true,true,true,true,true, C.midGrey, SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_THIN);
}
function extractNumber(str) {
  if (!str) return null;
  var s = str.toString();
  // Strip ≤ ≥ operators alongside standard > < = operators
  var m = s.match(/[><=≤≥\s]*([\d.]+)/);
  return m ? parseFloat(m[1]) : null;
}
function findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap) {
  var keywords = aKey.split(/[\s,.()\[\]/]+/);
  for (var thKey in thMap) {
    for (var k = 0; k < keywords.length; k++) {
      if (keywords[k].length > 3 && thKey.indexOf(keywords[k]) >= 0) return thMap[thKey];
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function formatDate(d) {
  return d instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy') : d.toString();
}
function buildThresholdMap(thData) {
  var thMap = {};
  for (var t = 0; t < thData.length; t++) {
    if (!thData[t][0]) continue;
    var rawName = thData[t][0].toString().trim();
    // Normalise threshold keys through the same synonym map as results,
    // so rows stored under "Systolic arterial pressure" are found when
    // looking up "Systolic blood pressure", etc.
    var normName = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(rawName));
    var key = normName.toLowerCase();
    if (!thMap[key]) thMap[key] = [];
    thMap[key].push(thData[t]);
  }
  return thMap;
}
// ── Clinical unit allowlist ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Only results whose unit matches one of these families are treated as blood /
// urine / clinical test results.  Anything with a blank unit OR a non-clinical
// unit (cm, kg, lbs, steps, bpm raw string, etc.) is silently skipped here.
// This is the primary guard against NHS SAR junk rows (height, weight, SMS
// messages, read codes, appointment notes) entering the flagging pipeline.
//
// Units are matched case-insensitively after stripping whitespace.
// A blank unit is NOT automatically excluded — it falls through to the
// analyte-name blocklist below (some valid results e.g. eGFR have no unit).
var CLINICAL_UNITS = (function() {
  var u = [
    // molar concentration
    'mmol/l','µmol/l','umol/l','nmol/l','pmol/l','mol/l','mmol/dl',
    // mass concentration
    'g/l','g/dl','mg/l','mg/dl','µg/l','ug/l','ng/l','ng/ml','pg/ml',
    'µg/dl','ug/dl','µg/ml','ug/ml',
    // enzyme / catalytic
    'u/l','iu/l','mu/l','iu/ml','u/ml','ku/l',
    // haematology counts
    '10^9/l','10^12/l','x10^9/l','x10^12/l','×10^9/l','×10^12/l',
    '10⁹/l','10¹²/l','/µl','/ul','cells/µl','cells/ul',
    // cell volume / size
    'fl','pg',
    // percentage / ratio
    '%','ratio','index',
    // pressure (blood pressure IS a valid clinical result)
    'mmhg','kpa',
    // thyroid / endocrine (sometimes unitless or 'miu/l')
    'miu/l','miu/ml','mu/l',
    // urine
    'mmol/mol','g/mol','µmol/mmol','umol/mmol',
    // eGFR and creatinine clearance (ml/min/1.73m² — accept any ml/min variant)
    'ml/min','ml/min/1.73m2','ml/min/1.73m²'
  ];
  var map = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < u.length; i++) map[u[i]] = true;
  return map;
})();

// Analyte-name blocklist — rows whose name contains any of these strings
// are always skipped regardless of unit.  Catches things like "Height (cm)",
// "Body weight", "SMS sent", "Read code", "Appointment date".
var SKIP_ANALYTE_TERMS = [
  // Anthropometric / physical measurements
  'height','weight','body fat','body mass','waist','hip circumference',
  // Vital signs that aren't blood tests (BP handled separately below)
  'pulse rate','heart rate','resting heart rate','respiratory rate',
  'temperature','oxygen saturation','spo2','peak flow','peak expiratory',
  // Fitness / lifestyle / wellness app metrics (Garmin, Apple Health, Vitality etc.)
  // Matched as substrings so "Wellbeing age", "Wellbeing score" etc. all caught
  'sleep score','wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score',
  'nutrition score','mind (stress)','mind score','stress score',
  'work/life','activity score','steps','hydration','body fat',
  // Communications / admin rows from SAR exports
  'sms','message sent','reminder','appointment','referral',
  'read code','smoking status','exercise',
  // Total body water (non-blood test)
  'total body water',
  // Blood pressure raw text rows (BP with mmHg unit is handled separately)
  'systolic','diastolic'
];

function getClinicalSummary(ss) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Clinical Summary');
  if (!sheet) return { summary: '', stale: true, missing: true };

  var summary = sheet.getRange(3, 1).getValue().toString();
  if (!summary) return { summary: '', stale: true, missing: true };

  // Check if stale — compare current record counts to when summary was built
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var currentRecCount = recSheet ? recSheet.getLastRow() - 3 : 0;
  var currentTrCount  = trSheet  ? trSheet.getLastRow()  - 3 : 0;

  var savedRecCount = parseInt(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('CS_RECORD_COUNT') || '0');
  var savedTrCount  = parseInt(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('CS_TR_COUNT')     || '0');
  var builtDate     = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('CS_BUILT_DATE') || 'unknown';

  var stale = (currentRecCount !== savedRecCount || currentTrCount !== savedTrCount);

  return {
    summary:   summary,
    stale:     stale,
    missing:   false,
    builtDate: builtDate,
    savedRecCount: savedRecCount,
    currentRecCount: currentRecCount
  };
}

function isClinicalResult(analyte, unit) {
  var uNorm = (unit || '').toString().toLowerCase().trim()
                .replace(/\s+/g, '').replace('μ','µ');

  // 1. Explicit non-clinical unit → reject
  var NON_CLINICAL = { 'cm':1,'mm':1,'m':1,'kg':1,'lbs':1,'lb':1,
                       'stone':1,'st':1,'steps':1,'bpm':1,'°c':1,
                       'c':1,'f':1,'°f':1,'' :0 }; // '' handled below
  if (uNorm && NON_CLINICAL[uNorm] === 1) return false;

  // 2. Known clinical unit → accept
  if (CLINICAL_UNITS[uNorm]) return true;

  // 3. Analyte-name blocklist (catches junk with blank or unknown units)
  var aLower = analyte.toLowerCase();
  for (var j = 0; j < SKIP_ANALYTE_TERMS.length; j++) {
    if (aLower.indexOf(SKIP_ANALYTE_TERMS[j]) >= 0) return false;
  }

  // 4. Blood pressure special case: accept if unit is mmhg
  if ((aLower.indexOf('systolic') >= 0 || aLower.indexOf('diastolic') >= 0) &&
       uNorm === 'mmhg') return true;

  // 5. Blank unit — only accept if the analyte name looks like a real test
  //    (contains a recognisable clinical keyword).  Catches eGFR, INR, etc.
  if (!uNorm) {
    var CLINICAL_KEYWORDS = ['egfr','glomerular','inr','ratio','creatinine',
      'bilirubin','albumin','protein','glucose','haemoglobin','hemoglobin',
      'haematocrit','hematocrit','platelet','neutrophil','lymphocyte',
      'eosinophil','basophil','monocyte','ferritin','folate','vitamin',
      'cholesterol','triglyceride','hdl','ldl','tsh','t3','t4','psa',
      'hba1c','cortisol','insulin','testosterone','oestradiol','estradiol',
      'progesterone','prolactin','lh','fsh','shbg','dhea','igf',
      'calcium','phosphate','magnesium','potassium','sodium','chloride',
      'bicarbonate','urate','urea','crp','esr'];
    for (var k = 0; k < CLINICAL_KEYWORDS.length; k++) {
      if (aLower.indexOf(CLINICAL_KEYWORDS[k]) >= 0) return true;
    }
    return false; // blank unit + no clinical keyword → reject
  }

  // 6. Unknown non-empty unit → accept cautiously (don't reject valid
  //    exotic units we haven't listed; the analyte blocklist above already
  //    caught known junk)
  return true;
}

function collectLatestResults(trData) {
  var results = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var row = trData[i];
    if (!row[1] || !row[3]) continue;
    var analyte = row[3].toString().trim();
    var unit    = row[5] ? row[5].toString().trim() : '';
    var numVal  = parseFloat(row[4]);
    if (isNaN(numVal)) continue;
    // ── Guard: skip non-clinical rows (height, weight, SMS, etc.) ──
    if (!isClinicalResult(analyte, unit)) continue;
    // ── Use cleaned name as key so embedded values/notes don't break matching ──
    var cleanedName = cleanAnalyteName(analyte);
    // ── Normalise BP synonyms to a single canonical name ─────────────────
    // NHS SARs use several names for the same measurement. Deduplicate so
    // only one row per measurement appears and the most recent value wins.
    cleanedName = normaliseSynonyms(cleanedName);
    var aKey = cleanedName.toLowerCase();
    if (!results[aKey] || parseDMY(row[1]) > parseDMY(results[aKey].date)) {
      // Store cleaned analyte name so display uses clean version too
      results[aKey] = { analyte: cleanedName, rawAnalyte: analyte, date: row[1],
                        value: numVal, unit: unit, refRange: row[6], status: row[7] };
    }
  }
  return results;
}
function collectAllResults(trData) {
  var all = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var row = trData[i];
    if (!row[1] || !row[3]) continue;
    var numVal = parseFloat(row[4]);
    if (isNaN(numVal)) continue;
    var analyte = row[3].toString().trim();
    var unit    = row[5] ? row[5].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!isClinicalResult(analyte, unit)) continue;
    // Use cleaned + normalised name as key — consistent with collectLatestResults
    var aKey = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(analyte)).toLowerCase();
    if (!all[aKey]) all[aKey] = [];
    all[aKey].push({ date: row[1], value: numVal, unit: row[5] });
  }
  return all;
}
function isTriggered(value, th) {
  var direction  = th[2].toString().toLowerCase();
  var threshStr  = th[1].toString();
  // Optimal zone rows are informational — never trigger a flag
  if (direction.indexOf('optimal') >= 0) return false;

  // ── Haematocrit L/L → % scale conversion ─────────────────────────────
  var compareValue = value;
  var analyteName  = (th[0] || '').toString().toLowerCase();
  if ((analyteName.indexOf('haematocrit') >= 0 || analyteName.indexOf('hematocrit') >= 0 ||
       analyteName.indexOf('packed cell') >= 0 || analyteName.indexOf('pcv') >= 0) &&
       value > 0 && value < 1.5) {
    var thValCheck = extractNumber(threshStr);
    if (thValCheck !== null && thValCheck > 5) compareValue = value * 100;
  }

  // ── Determine operator from BOTH direction column AND threshold string ─
  // AI-generated thresholds are inconsistent:
  //   "Above 4.0 mmol/L" / direction "Above"  → flag if > 4.0
  //   "< 3 mmol/L"       / direction "Above"  → flag if > 3  (direction wins, < is the boundary)
  //   "> 21 µmol/L"      / direction "Above"  → flag if > 21
  //   "Below 27 pg"      / direction "Below"  → flag if < 27
  //   "< 40%"            / direction "Below"  → flag if < 40
  // Strategy: direction column is authoritative for above/below intent.
  //           Extract the numeric value from threshold string regardless of operator.
  var thVal = extractNumber(threshStr);
  if (thVal === null) return false;

  // Check direction column first (most reliable)
  if (direction.indexOf('above') >= 0 || direction.indexOf('high') >= 0)  return compareValue > thVal;
  if (direction.indexOf('below') >= 0 || direction.indexOf('low') >= 0)   return compareValue < thVal;

  // Direction column doesn't say above/below — infer from threshold string operator
  var ts = threshStr.trim();
  if (ts.charAt(0) === '>' || ts.toLowerCase().indexOf('above') === 0) return compareValue > thVal;
  if (ts.charAt(0) === '<' || ts.toLowerCase().indexOf('below') === 0) return compareValue < thVal;
  if (ts.indexOf('\u2264') >= 0 || ts.indexOf('<=') >= 0) return compareValue <= thVal; // ≤
  if (ts.indexOf('\u2265') >= 0 || ts.indexOf('>=') >= 0) return compareValue >= thVal; // ≥

  // Range format in threshold string (e.g. "3.5 - 4.5") — only flag if direction says so
  var rangeMatch = ts.match(/(\d+\.?\d*)[\s]*[-–][\s]*(\d+\.?\d*)/);
  if (rangeMatch && (direction.indexOf('range') >= 0 || direction.indexOf('within') >= 0)) {
    var lo = parseFloat(rangeMatch[1]); var hi = parseFloat(rangeMatch[2]);
    return (compareValue >= lo && compareValue <= hi);
  }

  return false;
}
function assessResult(res, thRows) {
  var assessment = '', bandHit = '', commentary = '';
  if (thRows && thRows.length > 0) {
    var bands = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) { if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) bands.push(thRows[b]); }
    if (bands.length > 0) {
      assessment = '⚠️ ' + bands.length + ' threshold(s) flagged';
      bandHit    = bands.map(function(b){ return b[1]; }).join(' | ');
      commentary = bands.map(function(b){
        var t = '• ' + b[1] + ':\n  ' + b[3];
        if (b[4]) t += '\n  Symptoms: '  + b[4];
        if (b[5]) t += '\n  Evidence: '  + b[5];
        if (b[6]) t += '\n  Source: '    + b[6];
        if (b[9]) t += '\n\n  Common causes:\n  ' + b[9];
        return t;
      }).join('\n\n─────────────────\n\n');
    } else {
      assessment = '✅ Within research thresholds'; bandHit = 'No flags';
      commentary = 'Result of ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' does not trigger any threshold flags.';

      // Show all threshold rows so user can see what the thresholds are
      // and understand why this result was not flagged
      var nonOptRows = thRows.filter(function(th){ return th[2].toString().toLowerCase().indexOf('optimal') < 0; });
      var optRows    = thRows.filter(function(th){ return th[2].toString().toLowerCase().indexOf('optimal') >= 0; });
      if (nonOptRows.length > 0) {
        commentary += '\n\nResearch thresholds for this analyte:';
        for (var nt = 0; nt < nonOptRows.length; nt++) {
          commentary += '\n  • ' + nonOptRows[nt][1] + '  (' + nonOptRows[nt][2] + ')';
          if (nonOptRows[nt][3]) commentary += ' — ' + nonOptRows[nt][3].substring(0, 120);
        }
      }
      if (optRows.length > 0) commentary += '\n\nOptimal zone: ' + optRows[0][1] + ' — ' + optRows[0][3];
      if (thRows[0] && thRows[0][9]) commentary += '\n\nContext:\n' + thRows[0][9];
    }
  } else {
    assessment = '— Run AI refresh'; bandHit = '';
    commentary = 'No threshold data yet.\nRun: 🏥 Medical Tracker → Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI';
  }
  return { assessment: assessment, bandHit: bandHit, commentary: commentary };
}
// ─── AI PROVIDER CONFIGURATION ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Set AI_PROVIDER to 'openai' or 'anthropic' in Script Properties,
// or change the default below.  All AI calls route through callAI().
//
// Script Property keys:
//   AI_PROVIDER      — 'openai' (default) or 'anthropic'
//   OPENAI_API_KEY   — your OpenAI key (for OpenAI)
//   ANTHROPIC_API_KEY — your Anthropic key (for Anthropic)
//
// Models used:
//   OpenAI:    gpt-4o
//   Anthropic: claude-sonnet-4-5-20251001  (fast, cost-effective, excellent reasoning)

var AI_PROVIDER_DEFAULT = 'openai'; // change to 'anthropic' to switch default

function getAIProvider() {
  return (PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('AI_PROVIDER') || AI_PROVIDER_DEFAULT).toLowerCase().trim();
}
  function getProviderLabel() {
  var provider = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('AI_PROVIDER') || 'gpt';
  return provider.toLowerCase().indexOf('anthropic') >= 0 ? 'Claude' : 'GPT';
}

function getApiKey() {
  var provider = getAIProvider();
  if (provider === 'anthropic') {
    return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY');
  }
  return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('OPENAI_API_KEY');
}

function getApiKeyForProvider(provider) {
  if (provider === 'anthropic') {
    return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY');
  }
  return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('OPENAI_API_KEY');
}

// ── Core AI call — routes to OpenAI or Anthropic transparently ─────────────
// Drop-in replacement for callOpenAI(). All features use this.
function callAI(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel) {
  var provider = getAIProvider();
  if (provider === 'anthropic') {
    return _callAnthropic(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel);
  }
  return _callOpenAI(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel);
}

function _callOpenAI(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel) {
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error('No API key set in Script Properties. Add OPENAI_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY and set AI_PROVIDER accordingly.');
  var response = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', {
    method: 'post',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + apiKey },
    payload: JSON.stringify({ model: 'gpt-4o', max_tokens: maxTokens || 3000,
      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }] }),
    muteHttpExceptions: true,
    timeout: 120
  });
  if (response.getResponseCode() !== 200)
    throw new Error('OpenAI API error ' + response.getResponseCode() + ': ' + response.getContentText().substring(0, 200));
  var parsed = JSON.parse(response.getContentText());
  try {
    var usage = parsed.usage || {};
    logAIUsage(eventLabel || 'Unknown', 'gpt-4o', usage.prompt_tokens || 0, usage.completion_tokens || 0);
  } catch(e) {}
  return parsed.choices[0].message.content;
}

function _callAnthropic(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel) {
  var apiKey = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY');
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error('No ANTHROPIC_API_KEY set in Script Properties.');
  var response = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
    method: 'post',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type':      'application/json',
      'x-api-key':         apiKey,
      'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01'
    },
    payload: JSON.stringify({
      model:      'claude-sonnet-4-5',
      max_tokens: maxTokens || 3000,
      messages:   [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }]
    }),
    muteHttpExceptions: true,
    timeout: 120
  });
  if (response.getResponseCode() !== 200)
    throw new Error('Anthropic API error ' + response.getResponseCode() + ': ' + response.getContentText().substring(0, 200));
  var parsed = JSON.parse(response.getContentText());
  try {
    var usage = parsed.usage || {};
    logAIUsage(eventLabel || 'Unknown', 'claude-sonnet-4-5',
      usage.input_tokens || 0, usage.output_tokens || 0);
  } catch(e) {}
  return parsed.content[0].text;
}

// Keep callOpenAI as an alias so any direct calls still work
function callOpenAI(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel) {
  return callAI(prompt, maxTokens, eventLabel);
}




// ─── ENTRY POINT ────────────────────────────────────────────
// ─── SHEET: SPECIAL REQUESTS ────────────────────────────────
// Builds the Special Requests tab layout.
function generateCompleteMedicalRecord() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) { ui.alert('No API key found. Add OPENAI_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY to Script Properties and set AI_PROVIDER to \'openai\' or \'anthropic\'.'); return; }

  var confirm = ui.alert(
    '📄 Generate Complete Medical Record',
    'This creates a comprehensive portable document containing:\n\n' +
    '• AI-written patient summary (significant history, key events, current status)\n' +
    '• Full test results history (all analytes, all dates)\n' +
    '• All GP and consultation records\n' +
    '• Symptom investigation records\n' +
    '• Current medications and supplements\n' +
    '• Active treatment tracker summaries\n\n' +
    'One AI call (~30 seconds) to write the patient summary.\n' +
    'Then generates a PDF in your Google Drive.\n\n' +
    'Suitable for sharing with a new GP or consultant.\n\n' +
    'Continue?',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  ui.alert('⏳ Building complete medical record... (one AI call, ~30 seconds)');

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  // ── Collect all data ───────────────────────────────────────
  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var thSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');

  var allResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
      if (!trData[i][1] || !trData[i][3]) continue;
      allResults.push({
        date: trData[i][1], panel: trData[i][2] || '',
        analyte: trData[i][3], value: trData[i][4],
        unit: trData[i][5] || '', refRange: trData[i][6] || '',
        status: trData[i][7] || '', doctor: trData[i][8] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  allResults.sort(function(a, b) { return a.date > b.date ? 1 : a.date < b.date ? -1 : 0; });

  var allRecords = [];
  if (recSheet) {
    var recData = recSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 7).getValues();
    for (var r = 0; r < recData.length; r++) {
      if (!recData[r][1] || !recData[r][3]) continue;
      allRecords.push({
        date: recData[r][1], type: recData[r][2] || '',
        title: recData[r][3], details: recData[r][4] || '',
        doctor: recData[r][5] || ''
      });
    }
  }
  allRecords.sort(function(a, b) { return a.date > b.date ? 1 : a.date < b.date ? -1 : 0; });

  var allMeds = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var m = 0; m < mData.length; m++) {
      if (!mData[m][0]) continue;
      allMeds.push({
        name: mData[m][0], type: mData[m][1] || '',
        dose: mData[m][2] || '', freq: mData[m][3] || '',
        started: mData[m][5], stopped: mData[m][6],
        prescribedBy: mData[m][7] || '', notes: mData[m][8] || ''
      });
    }
  }

  var invIndex = [];
  try { invIndex = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}
  var investigations = [];
  for (var ii = 0; ii < invIndex.length; ii++) {
    try {
      var invRaw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIndex[ii]);
      if (invRaw) investigations.push(JSON.parse(invRaw));
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  investigations.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.date||'') > (b.date||'') ? 1 : -1; });

  var trackers = [];
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  for (var s = 0; s < sheets.length; s++) {
    if (sheets[s].getName().indexOf('📈') !== 0) continue;
    var ts = sheets[s];
    var tAnalyte = ts.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
    if (!tAnalyte || tAnalyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) continue;
    trackers.push({
      analyte:    tAnalyte,
      starting:   ts.getRange(5, 2).getValue().toString(),
      target:     ts.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString(),
      started:    ts.getRange(7, 2).getValue(),
      supplement: ts.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString(),
      dose:       ts.getRange(9, 2).getValue().toString(),
      trajectory: ts.getRange(17, 2).getValue().toString().substring(0, 300)
    });
  }

  var flaggedStr = '';
  if (trSheet && thSheet) {
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var latest = collectLatestResults(trData);
    var flagLines = [];
    for (var k in latest) {
      var res = latest[k];
      var thRows = thMap[k] || findThresholdFuzzy(k, thMap);
      var isFlagged = res.status && res.status.toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('NORMAL') < 0 && res.status.toString().trim() !== '';
      if (thRows) {
        for (var t = 0; t < thRows.length; t++) {
          if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[t])) { isFlagged = true; break; }
        }
      }
      if (isFlagged) flagLines.push(res.analyte + ': ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit);
    }
    flaggedStr = flagLines.join(', ');
  }

  // ── AI summary ────────────────────────────────────────────
  var summaryPrompt =
    'Write a concise, professional patient summary for a medical record document.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS (research-threshold flags): ' + (flaggedStr || 'None currently') + '\n\n' +
    'ACTIVE TREATMENTS:\n' + (trackers.map(function(t){ return t.analyte + ' — ' + t.supplement + ' ' + t.dose + ', started ' + (t.started instanceof Date ? formatDate(t.started) : t.started); }).join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'ACTIVE MEDICATIONS/SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (allMeds.filter(function(m){ return !m.stopped; }).map(function(m){
      var line = m.name + ' ' + m.dose + (m.freq ? ' x' + m.freq + '/day' : '');
      if (m.notes) line += ' — Indication: ' + m.notes;
      return line;
    }).join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'NUMBER OF TEST RESULTS IN RECORD: ' + allResults.length + '\n' +
    'NUMBER OF GP/CONSULTATION RECORDS: ' + allRecords.length + '\n' +
    'RECENT GP/CONSULTATION RECORDS (most recent 10):\n' +
    (allRecords.slice(-10).map(function(r){
      return (r.date instanceof Date ? formatDate(r.date) : r.date) + ' [' + r.type + '] ' + r.title +
             (r.details ? ': ' + r.details.toString().substring(0, 200) : '');
    }).join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'Write 3–5 paragraphs covering:\n' +
    '1. Patient overview (age derived from year of birth, sex, key relevant factors)\n' +
    '2. Current health concerns and significant test result history\n' +
    '3. Ongoing treatment and monitoring\n' +
    '4. Key points for any new clinician reviewing this record\n\n' +
    'Write in professional third-person clinical language. Be factual and concise. Today is ' + today + '.';

  var aiSummary = '';
  try {
    aiSummary = callOpenAI(summaryPrompt, 800, 'Medical Record PDF');
  } catch(e) {
    aiSummary = '[AI summary could not be generated: ' + e.toString() + ']\n\nProfile: ' + profile;
  }

  // ── Helper: save and reopen doc to avoid "too many changes" error ─────────
  var docName = 'Complete Medical Record — ' + todayFs;
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var docId = doc.getId();
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(40); body.setMarginBottom(40);
  body.setMarginLeft(56); body.setMarginRight(56);

  function checkpoint() {
    doc.saveAndClose();
    doc  = DocumentApp.openById(docId);
    body = doc.getBody();
  }

  function h1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(14); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function h2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function para(txt, sz, bold, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (bold)   p.setBold(true);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
    return p;
  }
  function rule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }
  function gap()  { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }

  function cleanPanelName(raw) {
    if (!raw) return '';
    var s = raw.toString().trim();
    s = s.replace(/\s*\([A-Z]{2,6}\d{3,8}\)\s*[-–]?\s*(Normal|Abnormal|Borderline)?.*$/i, '');
    s = s.replace(/\s*[-–]\s*(Normal|Abnormal|Borderline|Urgent|Non Urgent).*$/i, '');
    var noisePatterns = [
      /DIAGNOSIS OF DIABETES/i, /Normal kidney if/i, /EGFR calculated/i,
      /tTG abs is used/i, /HDL values </i, /Fasting or Random/i,
      /lipid monitoring/i, /Please note that/i, /Borderline result of/i,
      /abdo cramps/i, /Awaiting clinical code/i
    ];
    for (var np = 0; np < noisePatterns.length; np++) {
      var m = s.search(noisePatterns[np]);
      if (m > 3) { s = s.substring(0, m).trim(); break; }
    }
    s = s.replace(/[*;,.\s]+$/, '').trim();
    return s || raw.toString().trim().substring(0, 60);
  }

  function cleanAnalyteDisplay(raw) {
    if (!raw) return '';
    var s = raw.toString().trim();
    s = s.replace(/,\s*[\d.]+\s*[a-zA-Z\/µ%^0-9]+\s*$/, '');
    s = s.split('\\').join('');
    s = s.replace(/\s*\([A-Z]{2,6}\d{3,8}\)\s*$/, '');
    return s.trim();
  }

  var REPORT_SKIP = [
    'wellbeing age','wellbeing score','alcohol score','smoking score',
    'fitness score','nutrition score','mind (stress)','work/life',
    'sleep score','body fat','total body water','resting heart rate',
    'height','weight','steps','hydration','bmi'
  ];
  function isSkippedAnalyte(name) {
    var n = name.toLowerCase();
    for (var rs = 0; rs < REPORT_SKIP.length; rs++) {
      if (n.indexOf(REPORT_SKIP[rs]) >= 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  var SKIP_PANEL_TYPES = [
    'read code','test request','sms','appointment','referral','lifestyle',
    'wellbeing','anthropometric','body composition','vitals'
  ];
  function isSkippedPanel(panelName) {
    var p = panelName.toLowerCase();
    for (var sp = 0; sp < SKIP_PANEL_TYPES.length; sp++) {
      if (p.indexOf(SKIP_PANEL_TYPES[sp]) >= 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  var SF_SKIP_REPORT = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score',
    'nutrition score','mind (stress)','work/life','sleep score','body fat',
    'total body water','resting heart rate','height','weight'];
  function isSFSkipped(name) {
    var n = (name||'').toLowerCase();
    for (var ss2 = 0; ss2 < SF_SKIP_REPORT.length; ss2++) {
      if (n.indexOf(SF_SKIP_REPORT[ss2]) >= 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  // ── COVER ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var titleP = body.appendParagraph('Complete Medical Record');
  titleP.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
  try { titleP.setFontSize(22); } catch(e) {}
  gap();
  para('Generated: ' + today, 9, false, true);
  para('⚠️  AI-assisted personal health record — for personal use only. Not a clinical document. Discuss all findings with your GP or specialist.', 9, false, true);
  rule();

if (cs.stale || cs.missing) {
    para('⚠️ Clinical Summary ' + (cs.missing ? 'has not been built yet' : 'may be outdated — records changed since ' + cs.builtDate) +
         '. Run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.', 8, true);
  }

  // ── 1. PATIENT PROFILE ────────────────────────────────────
  h1('1. Patient Profile');
  var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc64 Profile');
  if (profSheet) {
    var pRows = profSheet.getRange(3, 1, 13, 2).getValues();
    var profTbl = body.appendTable();
    for (var pi = 0; pi < pRows.length; pi++) {
      var lbl = pRows[pi][0] ? pRows[pi][0].toString().trim() : '';
      var val = pRows[pi][1] ? pRows[pi][1].toString().trim() : '';
      var skipProf = ['bmi  (auto-calculated)','healthy bmi range',''].indexOf(lbl.toLowerCase()) >= 0;
      if (!lbl || !val || skipProf) continue;
      var pRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
      var c1 = pRow.appendTableCell(lbl);
      c1.editAsText().setFontSize(9).setBold(true);
      c1.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
      var c2 = pRow.appendTableCell(val);
      c2.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 1 ──

  // ── 2. CLINICAL SUMMARY ───────────────────────────────────
  h1('2. Clinical Summary');
  para(aiSummary, 10);
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 2 ──

  // ── 3. SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS ───────────────────────────────
  h1('3. Current Significant Findings');
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 13).getValues();
    var sfRows = [];
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfRow = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfRow[0] || !sfRow[1]) continue;
      var sfLbl = sfRow[0].toString();
      if (sfLbl.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || sfLbl.indexOf('GUIDANCE CHANGE') >= 0) break;
      if (isSFSkipped(sfLbl)) continue;
      sfRows.push(sfRow);
    }
    if (sfRows.length === 0) {
      para('No significant findings currently flagged.', 10, false, true);
    } else {
      para(sfRows.length + ' result(s) flagged against research thresholds:', 10);
      gap();
      var sfTbl = body.appendTable();
      var sfHdr = sfTbl.appendTableRow();
      [['#','4%'],['Analyte','22%'],['Value','10%'],['Lab Status','10%'],['Research Threshold(s)','18%'],['Clinical Significance','36%']].forEach(function(col) {
        var c = sfHdr.appendTableCell(col[0]);
        c.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
        c.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(8);
      });
      for (var sfi = 0; sfi < sfRows.length; sfi++) {
        var sf2 = sfRows[sfi];
        var meaning = (sf2[5] || '').toString().replace(/^[▸•]\s*/,'').replace(/^>[^:]+:\s*/,'').substring(0, 180);
        var flags   = (sf2[4] || '').toString().replace(/^[•]\s*/,'').substring(0, 60);
        var sfTRow = sfTbl.appendTableRow();
        var sfBg = sfi % 2 === 0 ? '#fef9f0' : '#ffffff';
        [(sfi+1).toString(), sf2[0]||'', (sf2[1]||'') + ' ' + (sf2[3]||''), sf2[2]||'', flags, meaning].forEach(function(val, ci) {
          var c = sfTRow.appendTableCell(val.toString().trim());
          c.setBackgroundColor(sfBg);
          c.editAsText().setFontSize(ci === 0 ? 8 : 9);
        });
      }
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 3 ──

  // ── 4. LATEST BLOOD TEST RESULTS SUMMARY ──────────────────
  h1('4. Latest Blood Test Results');
  para('Most recent value per analyte from all test dates. Clinical tests only.', 9, false, true);
  gap();

  if (trSheet) {
    var trDataRpt = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var latestMap = {};
    for (var lri = 0; lri < trDataRpt.length; lri++) {
      var lr = trDataRpt[lri];
      if (!lr[1] || !lr[3]) continue;
      var lAnalyte = lr[3].toString().trim();
      var lUnit    = (lr[5] || '').toString().trim();
      if (isSkippedAnalyte(lAnalyte)) continue;
      var lKey = cleanAnalyteName(lAnalyte).toLowerCase();
      var lDate = lr[1];
      if (!latestMap[lKey] || parseDMY(lDate) > parseDMY(latestMap[lKey].date)) {
        latestMap[lKey] = {
          analyte: cleanAnalyteDisplay(lAnalyte),
          value:   lr[4], unit: lUnit,
          refRange: (lr[6]||'').toString().trim(),
          status:  (lr[7]||'').toString().trim(),
          date:    lDate,
          dateStr: lDate instanceof Date ? formatDate(lDate) : lDate.toString()
        };
      }
    }
    var latestRows = Object.values(latestMap).sort(function(a,b){ return a.analyte.toLowerCase() < b.analyte.toLowerCase() ? -1 : 1; });

    if (latestRows.length === 0) {
      para('No clinical test results found.', 10, false, true);
    } else {
      var ltTbl = body.appendTable();
      var ltHdr = ltTbl.appendTableRow();
      ['Analyte','Latest Value','Unit','Ref Range','Status','Date'].forEach(function(hdr) {
        var c = ltHdr.appendTableCell(hdr);
        c.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
        c.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(8);
      });
      for (var lri2 = 0; lri2 < latestRows.length; lri2++) {
        var lr2 = latestRows[lri2];
        var lStatus = lr2.status;
        var isAbnormal = lStatus && lStatus.toLowerCase() !== 'normal' && lStatus.trim() !== '';
        var lBg = isAbnormal ? '#fef2f2' : (lri2 % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff');
        var ltRow = ltTbl.appendTableRow();
        [lr2.analyte, lr2.value.toString(), lr2.unit, lr2.refRange, lr2.status, lr2.dateStr].forEach(function(val) {
          var c = ltRow.appendTableCell(val || '');
          c.setBackgroundColor(lBg);
          c.editAsText().setFontSize(8);
        });
      }
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 4 ──

  // ── 5. BLOOD TEST HISTORY ─────────────────────────────────
  h1('5. Full Blood Test History');
  para('All clinical blood test results by date, newest first. Lifestyle, wellness, anthropometric and administrative records excluded.', 9, false, true);
  gap();

  if (allResults.length === 0) {
    para('No test results in record.', 10, false, true);
  } else {
    var panels2 = {};
    var panelOrder = [];
    for (var ri = 0; ri < allResults.length; ri++) {
      var res2 = allResults[ri];
      if (isSkippedAnalyte(res2.analyte)) continue;
      var pName = cleanPanelName(res2.panel || 'Blood Tests');
      if (isSkippedPanel(pName)) continue;
      var dateStr2 = res2.date instanceof Date ? formatDate(res2.date) : res2.date.toString();
      var pKey = dateStr2 + ' — ' + pName;
      if (!panels2[pKey]) { panels2[pKey] = []; panelOrder.push(pKey); }
      panels2[pKey].push(res2);
    }
    var seenPanels = {};
    var uniquePanelOrder = [];
    panelOrder.forEach(function(pk) {
      if (!seenPanels[pk]) { seenPanels[pk] = true; uniquePanelOrder.push(pk); }
    });
    uniquePanelOrder.sort().reverse();

    for (var dpk = 0; dpk < uniquePanelOrder.length; dpk++) {
      var pKey2 = uniquePanelOrder[dpk];
      var rows = panels2[pKey2];
      var seenAnalytes = {};
      var deduped = [];
      for (var dri = 0; dri < rows.length; dri++) {
        var dKey = cleanAnalyteName(rows[dri].analyte).toLowerCase();
        if (!seenAnalytes[dKey]) { seenAnalytes[dKey] = true; deduped.push(rows[dri]); }
      }
      panels2[pKey2] = deduped;
    }

    if (uniquePanelOrder.length === 0) {
      para('No clinical blood test panels found.', 10, false, true);
    } else {
      for (var pk2 = 0; pk2 < uniquePanelOrder.length; pk2++) {
        var panelKey2 = uniquePanelOrder[pk2];
        var panelRows2 = panels2[panelKey2];
        if (!panelRows2 || panelRows2.length === 0) continue;
        h2(panelKey2);
        var tbl2 = body.appendTable();
        var hRow2 = tbl2.appendTableRow();
        ['Analyte','Value','Unit','Ref Range','Status','Doctor'].forEach(function(hdr) {
          var c = hRow2.appendTableCell(hdr);
          c.setBackgroundColor('#2d5a8e');
          c.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(8);
        });
        for (var pr2 = 0; pr2 < panelRows2.length; pr2++) {
          var row2 = panelRows2[pr2];
          var rStatus2 = (row2.status||'').toString().toLowerCase().trim();
          var isAbn2 = rStatus2 && rStatus2 !== 'normal';
          var rowBg2 = isAbn2 ? '#fef2f2' : (pr2 % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff');
          var tRow2 = tbl2.appendTableRow();
          var displayAnalyte = cleanAnalyteDisplay(row2.analyte);
          var displayDoctor  = (row2.doctor||'').toString().replace(/\([A-Z]{2,6}\d{3,8}\)/g,'').trim().substring(0,40);
          [displayAnalyte, row2.value.toString(), row2.unit, row2.refRange, row2.status, displayDoctor].forEach(function(val) {
            var c = tRow2.appendTableCell((val||'').toString().substring(0, 60));
            c.setBackgroundColor(rowBg2);
            c.editAsText().setFontSize(8);
          });
        }
        gap();
        // Save every 5 panels to avoid overflow
        if (pk2 % 5 === 4) checkpoint();
      }
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 5 ──

  // ── 6. GP & CONSULTATION RECORDS ─────────────────────────
  h1('6. GP & Consultation Records');
  var usefulRecords = allRecords.filter(function(rec) {
    var t = (rec.type||'').toLowerCase();
    var d = (rec.details||'').toString().trim();
    if (t.indexOf('test request') >= 0 && (d === 'code' || d === '' || d.length < 10)) return false;
    if (t.indexOf('read code') >= 0 && d.length < 15) return false;
    if (d.toLowerCase().indexOf('sms message sent') >= 0) return false;
    if (d.toLowerCase().indexOf('appointment notification') >= 0) return false;
    return true;
  });

  if (usefulRecords.length === 0) {
    para('No GP records with substantive content.', 10, false, true);
  } else {
    para(usefulRecords.length + ' records with substantive content (test requests and admin messages excluded).', 9, false, true);
    gap();
    for (var recIdx = usefulRecords.length - 1; recIdx >= 0; recIdx--) {
      var rec2 = usefulRecords[recIdx];
      var recDate2 = rec2.date instanceof Date ? formatDate(rec2.date) : rec2.date.toString();
      var cleanType = (rec2.type||'').replace(/Test Panel:/i,'').replace(/Consultation:/i,'').trim();
      var cleanTitle = cleanPanelName(rec2.title);
      h2(recDate2 + '  —  ' + (cleanType ? cleanType + ': ' : '') + cleanTitle);
      if (rec2.doctor) {
        var cleanDoc = rec2.doctor.toString().replace(/\([A-Z]{2,6}\d{3,8}\)/g,'').trim();
        para('Clinician: ' + cleanDoc, 9, false, true);
      }
      if (rec2.details) {
        var d2 = rec2.details.toString();
        if (d2.length > 500) {
          var cutAt = d2.lastIndexOf('.', 500);
          d2 = (cutAt > 200 ? d2.substring(0, cutAt+1) : d2.substring(0, 500)) + '…';
        }
        para(d2, 9);
      }
      gap();
      // Save every 10 records
      if (recIdx % 10 === 0) checkpoint();
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 6 ──

  // ── 7. SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS ─────────────────────────────
  h1('7. Symptom Investigations');
  if (investigations.length === 0) {
    para('No saved investigations.', 10, false, true);
  } else {
    for (var invIdx = investigations.length - 1; invIdx >= 0; invIdx--) {
      var inv = investigations[invIdx];
      if (!inv.symptoms || inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') >= 0) continue;
      h2((inv.date||'Unknown date') + '  —  ' + (inv.id||''));
      if (inv.symptoms) { para('Symptoms:', 9, true); para(inv.symptoms.substring(0,300), 9); }
      if (inv.aiAssess) { para('AI Assessment:', 9, true); para(inv.aiAssess.replace(/\*\*/g,'').substring(0,400), 9); }
      if (inv.gpAdvice) { para('GP Advice:', 9, true); para(inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,200), 9); }
      gap();
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 7 ──

  // ── 8. MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS ─────────────────────────
  h1('8. Medications & Supplements');
  if (allMeds.length === 0) {
    para('None logged.', 10, false, true);
  } else {
    var currentMeds = allMeds.filter(function(m){ return !m.stopped; });
    var stoppedMeds = allMeds.filter(function(m){ return !!m.stopped; });
    if (currentMeds.length > 0) {
      h2('Current');
      var medTbl2 = body.appendTable();
      var medHdr2 = medTbl2.appendTableRow();
      ['Name','Type','Dose','Frequency','Notes'].forEach(function(hdr) {
        var c = medHdr2.appendTableCell(hdr);
        c.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
        c.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(8);
      });
      for (var mi2 = 0; mi2 < currentMeds.length; mi2++) {
        var med = currentMeds[mi2];
        var mRow2 = medTbl2.appendTableRow();
        var mBg2 = mi2 % 2 === 0 ? '#f0fdf4' : '#ffffff';
        [med.name, med.type, med.dose.toString(), med.freq.toString(), (med.notes||'').toString().substring(0,80)].forEach(function(val) {
          var c = mRow2.appendTableCell(val||'');
          c.setBackgroundColor(mBg2);
          c.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
        });
      }
      gap();
    }
    if (stoppedMeds.length > 0) {
      h2('Previously taken (stopped)');
      for (var si2 = 0; si2 < stoppedMeds.length; si2++) {
        var sm2 = stoppedMeds[si2];
        var stoppedStr2 = sm2.stopped instanceof Date ? formatDate(sm2.stopped) : sm2.stopped.toString();
        para(sm2.name + '  ' + sm2.dose + '  (stopped ' + stoppedStr2 + ')', 9);
      }
    }
  }
  rule();
  checkpoint(); // ── SAVE 8 ──

  // ── ANNEX A ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  rule();
  h1('Annex A — Threshold Evidence Audit');
  para(
    'This annex lists every analyte that generated a finding in this report, showing ' +
    'both the NHS lab reference range and all AI-generated research thresholds used to ' +
    'assess your results. Review confidence levels and sources before acting on any ' +
    'recommendation. Low-confidence or suspicious thresholds should be verified with a ' +
    'clinician, or deleted from the Thresholds sheet (they regenerate on next Full Refresh).',
    9, false, true);
  gap();

  var annexAnalytes = {};
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfAnnexData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 13).getValues();
    for (var sfa = 4; sfa < sfAnnexData.length; sfa++) {
      var sfaRow = sfAnnexData[sfa];
      if (!sfaRow[0] || !sfaRow[1]) continue;
      var sfaLbl = sfaRow[0].toString();
      if (sfaLbl === 'Analyte') continue;
      if (sfaLbl.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || sfaLbl.indexOf('GUIDANCE CHANGE') >= 0) break;
      if (isSFSkipped(sfaLbl)) continue;
      annexAnalytes[sfaLbl.toLowerCase()] = sfaLbl;
    }
  }

  var latestForAnnex = {};
  if (trSheet) {
    var trAnnex = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    for (var tri2 = 0; tri2 < trAnnex.length; tri2++) {
      var tr2 = trAnnex[tri2];
      if (!tr2[3] || isNaN(parseFloat(tr2[4]))) continue;
      var aKey2 = cleanAnalyteName(tr2[3].toString()).toLowerCase();
      if (!latestForAnnex[aKey2] || parseDMY(tr2[1]) > parseDMY(latestForAnnex[aKey2].date)) {
        latestForAnnex[aKey2] = {
          analyte: tr2[3].toString(), value: tr2[4],
          unit: tr2[5] || '', refRange: tr2[6] || '',
          labStatus: tr2[7] || '', date: tr2[1]
        };
      }
      if (tr2[7] && tr2[7].toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('ABNORMAL') >= 0) {
        annexAnalytes[aKey2] = tr2[3].toString();
      }
    }
  }

  var thByAnalyte = {};
  if (thSheet) {
    var thAnnexData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    for (var tha = 0; tha < thAnnexData.length; tha++) {
      if (!thAnnexData[tha][0]) continue;
      var thRaw2   = thAnnexData[tha][0].toString().toLowerCase().trim();
      var thNorm2  = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(thAnnexData[tha][0].toString())).toLowerCase();
      if (!thByAnalyte[thRaw2])  thByAnalyte[thRaw2]  = [];
      thByAnalyte[thRaw2].push(thAnnexData[tha]);
      if (thNorm2 !== thRaw2) {
        if (!thByAnalyte[thNorm2]) thByAnalyte[thNorm2] = [];
        thByAnalyte[thNorm2].push(thAnnexData[tha]);
      }
    }
  }

  var annexKeys = Object.keys(annexAnalytes).sort();

  if (annexKeys.length === 0) {
    para('No flagged analytes to audit — no significant findings or lab-abnormal results found.', 10, false, true);
  } else {
    para(annexKeys.length + ' analyte(s) audited below.', 9);
    gap();

    for (var ak = 0; ak < annexKeys.length; ak++) {
      var aKey3   = annexKeys[ak];
      var aName3  = annexAnalytes[aKey3];
      var cleaned3 = cleanAnalyteName(aName3).toLowerCase();
      var latest3 = latestForAnnex[aKey3] || latestForAnnex[cleaned3] || null;
      var normKey3 = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(aName3)).toLowerCase();
      var thRows3 = thByAnalyte[aKey3] || thByAnalyte[cleaned3] || thByAnalyte[normKey3] || [];

      var aHead = body.appendParagraph(aName3);
      aHead.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);

      if (latest3) {
        var dateStr3 = latest3.date instanceof Date
          ? Utilities.formatDate(latest3.date, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy')
          : (latest3.date||'').toString();
        var labLine = 'Latest result (' + dateStr3 + '):  ' +
          latest3.value + ' ' + latest3.unit +
          '     |     NHS lab range: ' + (latest3.refRange || 'not provided') +
          '     |     Lab status: ' + (latest3.labStatus || 'not provided');
        var labPara = body.appendParagraph(labLine);
        var labText = labPara.editAsText();
        labText.setFontSize(9);
        if (latest3.labStatus && latest3.labStatus.toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('ABNORMAL') >= 0) {
          labText.setForegroundColor('#991b1b').setBold(true);
        } else {
          labText.setForegroundColor('#1e3a5f');
        }
      } else {
        var noRes = body.appendParagraph('No test result found in TestResults for this analyte.');
        noRes.editAsText().setFontSize(9).setForegroundColor('#6b7280');
      }

      if (thRows3.length === 0) {
        var noTh = body.appendParagraph('No AI thresholds generated yet. Run Full Refresh to generate.');
        noTh.editAsText().setFontSize(9).setForegroundColor('#92400e').setItalic(true);
      } else {
        var thTbl3 = body.appendTable();
        var thHdr3 = thTbl3.appendTableRow();
        ['Threshold', 'Direction', 'Clinical meaning', 'Confidence / Evidence', 'Source / Guideline'].forEach(function(col3) {
          var c3 = thHdr3.appendTableCell(col3);
          c3.setBackgroundColor('#3b0764');
          c3.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(7);
        });

        for (var thr = 0; thr < thRows3.length; thr++) {
          var tRow3    = thRows3[thr];
          var conf3    = (tRow3[5]||'').toString();
          var confLower3 = conf3.toLowerCase();
          var confBg3 = (confLower3.indexOf('low') >= 0 || confLower3.indexOf('weak') >= 0 ||
                         confLower3.indexOf('limited') >= 0 || confLower3.indexOf('insufficient') >= 0)
                        ? '#fef2f2'
                        : (confLower3.indexOf('moderate') >= 0 || confLower3.indexOf('mixed') >= 0)
                        ? '#fffbeb'
                        : '#f0fdf4';
          var rowBg3   = thr % 2 === 0 ? '#faf5ff' : '#ffffff';
          var thTRow3  = thTbl3.appendTableRow();
          [
            (tRow3[1]||'').toString(),
            (tRow3[2]||'').toString(),
            (tRow3[3]||'').toString().substring(0, 150),
            conf3.substring(0, 100),
            (tRow3[6]||'').toString().substring(0, 100)
          ].forEach(function(val3, ci3) {
            var c3 = thTRow3.appendTableCell(val3);
            c3.setBackgroundColor(ci3 === 3 ? confBg3 : rowBg3);
            c3.editAsText().setFontSize(8).setForegroundColor('#111111');
          });
        }

        var legPara = body.appendParagraph('Confidence: green = high evidence  |  yellow = moderate  |  red = low/weak — verify before acting');
        legPara.editAsText().setFontSize(7).setForegroundColor('#6b7280').setItalic(true);
      }
      gap();
      // Save every 5 analytes in annex
      if (ak % 5 === 4) checkpoint();
    }
  }

  var annexNote = body.appendParagraph(
    'AI thresholds are generated from published research and guidelines (primarily NICE, BSH, ' +
    'BCSH, ESC) but are not clinically validated. Suspicious ranges that do not match standard ' +
    'NHS reference ranges should be queried with your clinician. To remove a bad threshold row, ' +
    'delete it from the Thresholds sheet — it will regenerate on the next Full Refresh.');
  annexNote.editAsText().setFontSize(8).setForegroundColor('#374151').setItalic(true);
  rule();

  para('Generated ' + today + ' using AI-assisted personal health tracking. For personal use only — not a clinical record or diagnosis. Verify all AI-generated content with a qualified clinician.', 8, false, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  // Convert to PDF
  var docFile  = DriveApp.getFileById(docId);
  var pdfBlob  = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile  = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Complete Medical Record saved to Google Drive.\n\n' +
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Contains:\n' +
    '• AI clinical summary\n' +
    '• ' + allResults.length + ' test results\n' +
    '• ' + allRecords.length + ' GP/consultation records\n' +
    '• ' + investigations.length + ' symptom investigation records\n' +
    '• ' + allMeds.length + ' medications/supplements\n' +
    '• ' + trackers.length + ' treatment tracker(s)\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download and share it.'
  );
}



// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER PDF EXPORT ────────────────────────────
// Exports the active tracker sheet as a formatted PDF:
// setup details, trajectory summary, milestone table, assessment notes.
function exportTrackerPDF() {
  var ss          = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui          = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet       = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('No Treatment Tracker found. Navigate to a 📈 tracker tab first.'); return; }

  var analyte    = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    ui.alert('Please run Setup & Generate Trajectory first before exporting.');
    return;
  }

  var today       = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs     = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);

  // Read setup fields
  var starting   = sheet.getRange(5,  2).getValue().toString();
  var target     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var started    = sheet.getRange(7,  2).getValue();
  var supplement = sheet.getRange(8,  2).getValue().toString();
  var dose       = sheet.getRange(9,  2).getValue().toString();
  var timesDay   = sheet.getRange(10, 2).getValue().toString();
  var menstrual  = sheet.getRange(13, 2).getValue().toString();
  var lifestyle  = sheet.getRange(14, 2).getValue().toString();
  var trajectory = sheet.getRange(17, 2).getValue().toString();

  // Read milestone table
  var milestones = [];
  for (var r = 21; r <= 40; r++) {
    var rowVals = sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).getValues()[0];
    var lbl = rowVals[0] ? rowVals[0].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!lbl) continue;
    milestones.push({
      label:    lbl,
      expected: rowVals[3] ? rowVals[3].toString() : '',
      actual:   rowVals[4] !== '' && rowVals[4] !== null ? rowVals[4].toString() : '',
      unit:     rowVals[5] ? rowVals[5].toString() : '',
      onTrack:  rowVals[6] ? rowVals[6].toString() : '',
      comment:  rowVals[7] ? rowVals[7].toString().substring(0, 300) : ''
    });
  }

  // Build doc
  var docName = 'Treatment Tracker — ' + analyte + ' — ' + todayFs;
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(36); body.setMarginBottom(36);
  body.setMarginLeft(54); body.setMarginRight(54);

  function h1(txt) { var p = body.appendParagraph(txt); p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1); return p; }
  function h2(txt) { var p = body.appendParagraph(txt); p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2); return p; }
  function para(txt, sz, bold, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (bold) p.setBold(true);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
    return p;
  }

  // Cover
  var titleP = body.appendParagraph('Treatment Progress Report: ' + analyte);
  titleP.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
  para('Generated: ' + today, 9, false, true);
  para('Patient: ' + profile.split(',').slice(0, 3).join(','), 9, false, true);
  para('⚠️ AI-assisted personal health tracking. Not a clinical document. Discuss with your GP or specialist.', 9, false, true);
  body.appendHorizontalRule();

  // Setup
  h1('Treatment Setup');
  para('Analyte monitored: ' + analyte, 10, true);
  para('Starting value: ' + starting, 10);
  para('Research target: ' + target, 10);
  para('Treatment started: ' + (started instanceof Date ? formatDate(started) : started.toString()), 10);
  para('Supplement / medication: ' + supplement, 10);
  para('Dose: ' + dose + ' × ' + timesDay + '/day', 10);
  if (menstrual && menstrual.indexOf('[') < 0) { body.appendParagraph(''); para('Menstrual / hormonal factors: ' + menstrual, 9, false, true); }
  if (lifestyle && lifestyle.indexOf('[') < 0)  { para('Lifestyle / dietary guidance: ' + lifestyle.substring(0, 300), 9); }
  body.appendHorizontalRule();

  // AI trajectory
  h1('AI Trajectory Assessment');
  if (trajectory && trajectory.indexOf('[') < 0) {
    para(trajectory, 10);
  } else {
    para('Not yet generated. Run Setup & Generate Trajectory from the menu.', 10, false, true);
  }
  body.appendHorizontalRule();

  // Milestone table
  h1('Progress Milestones');
  var actualCount = milestones.filter(function(m){ return !!m.actual; }).length;
  para(milestones.length + ' milestones  |  ' + actualCount + ' actual results recorded', 9, false, true);
  body.appendParagraph('');

  if (milestones.length > 0) {
    var tbl = body.appendTable();
    var hRow = tbl.appendTableRow();
    ['Milestone', 'Expected Range', 'Actual Value', 'On Track?', 'AI Comment'].forEach(function(hdr) {
      var cell = hRow.appendTableCell(hdr);
      cell.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
      cell.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(8);
    });
    for (var mi = 0; mi < milestones.length; mi++) {
      var ms = milestones[mi];
      var isSection = ms.label.toUpperCase().indexOf('HISTORIC') >= 0 || ms.label.toUpperCase().indexOf('FUTURE') >= 0;
      if (isSection) continue;
      var rowBg = ms.onTrack.indexOf('✅') >= 0 ? '#f0fdf4' : ms.onTrack.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0 ? '#fefce8' : ms.onTrack.indexOf('🔴') >= 0 ? '#fef2f2' : (mi % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff');
      var mRow = tbl.appendTableRow();
      [ms.label, ms.expected, ms.actual + (ms.actual && ms.unit ? ' ' + ms.unit : ''), ms.onTrack, ms.comment].forEach(function(val) {
        var cell = mRow.appendTableCell(val || '');
        cell.setBackgroundColor(rowBg);
        cell.editAsText().setFontSize(8);
      });
    }
  }
  body.appendHorizontalRule();

  para('✅ = on track  |  ⚠️ = slightly behind  |  🔴 = significantly behind target', 8, false, true);
  body.appendParagraph('');
  para('Generated ' + today + ' by AI-assisted Medical Tracker. For personal use only.', 8, false, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert('✅ Tracker PDF saved to Google Drive.\n\nFilename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\nOpen Google Drive to download.');
}

// ─── BUILD CLINICAL SUMMARY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// One-time function to screen all Records and TestResults using AI.
// Writes output to 📋 Clinical Summary tab.
// Re-run manually after adding new records or test results.
function buildClinicalSummary() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var confirm = ui.alert(
    '🔬 Build Clinical Summary',
    'This screens your complete medical history using AI and builds a\n' +
    'structured Clinical Summary used by all narrative and specialist reports.\n\n' +
    'Runs in stages — if it times out it will automatically continue.\n\n' +
    'Continue?',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!recSheet || !trSheet) {
    ui.alert('❌ Error: Could not find Records or TestResults sheet.');
    return;
  }

  // ── Read all Records ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var recData = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, recSheet.getLastRow() - 3, 7).getValues();
  var recRows = [];
  for (var ri = 0; ri < recData.length; ri++) {
    var r = recData[ri];
    if (!r[1] || !r[3]) continue;
    var rDate   = r[1] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[1], Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy') : r[1].toString();
    var rType   = (r[2] || '').toString().trim();
    var rTitle  = (r[3] || '').toString().trim();
    var rDetail = (r[4] || '').toString().trim().substring(0, 200);
    recRows.push(rDate + ' | ' + rType + ' | ' + rTitle + (rDetail ? ' | ' + rDetail : ''));
  }

  // ── Read all TestResults ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  var trData = trSheet.getRange(4, 1, trSheet.getLastRow() - 3, 10).getValues();
  var trRows = [];
  for (var ti = 0; ti < trData.length; ti++) {
    var t = trData[ti];
    if (!t[1] || !t[3]) continue;
    var tDate    = t[1] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(t[1], Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy') : t[1].toString();
    var tAnalyte = (t[3] || '').toString().trim();
    var tValue   = (t[4] || '').toString().trim();
    var tUnit    = (t[5] || '').toString().trim();
    var tRange   = (t[6] || '').toString().trim();
    var tStatus  = (t[7] || '').toString().trim();
    trRows.push(tDate + ' | ' + tAnalyte + ': ' + tValue + ' ' + tUnit +
      (tRange ? ' (range: ' + tRange + ')' : '') +
      (tStatus ? ' [' + tStatus + ']' : ''));
  }

  var recCount = recRows.length;
  var trCount  = trRows.length;
  var batchSize = 80;
  var totalBatches = Math.ceil(recRows.length / batchSize);

  // ── Write staging data to a hidden sheet ─────────────────────────────
  var stageSheet = ss.getSheetByName('_CS_STAGING');
  if (!stageSheet) {
    stageSheet = ss.insertSheet('_CS_STAGING');
    ss.setActiveSheet(ss.getSheets()[0]);
  }
  stageSheet.clearContents();

  // Write rec rows to col A, tr rows to col B
  var maxRows = Math.max(recRows.length, trRows.length);
  var stageData = [];
  for (var si = 0; si < maxRows; si++) {
    stageData.push([
      si < recRows.length ? recRows[si] : '',
      si < trRows.length  ? trRows[si]  : ''
    ]);
  }
  if (stageData.length > 0) {
    stageSheet.getRange(1, 1, stageData.length, 2).setValues(stageData);
  }

  // ── Save minimal job state to properties ──────────────────────────────
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_RECCOUNT',    recCount.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_TRCOUNT',     trCount.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_BATCH',       '0');
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_TOTALBATCH',  totalBatches.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_BATCHSIZE',   batchSize.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_JOB_SSID',        ss.getId());
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS');

  // Clear previous batch results
  for (var cb = 0; cb < 20; cb++) {
    props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_RESULT_' + cb);
  }

  ui.alert('⏳ Building Clinical Summary — ' + totalBatches + ' batches + trends + merge.\n\n' +
           'Stage 1 starting now. Will auto-continue between stages.\n' +
           'Use "▶️ Continue Clinical Summary" if it stops unexpectedly.');

  _continueClinicalSummary();
}

function _continueClinicalSummary() {
  var props        = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var ssId         = props.getProperty('CS_JOB_SSID');
  if (!ssId) { 
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Clinical Summary job in progress. Run "Build Clinical Summary" first.');
    return; 
  }

  var ss           = SpreadsheetApp.openById(ssId);
  var recCount     = parseInt(props.getProperty('CS_JOB_RECCOUNT')   || '0');
  var trCount      = parseInt(props.getProperty('CS_JOB_TRCOUNT')    || '0');
  var batchIdx     = parseInt(props.getProperty('CS_JOB_BATCH')      || '0');
  var totalBatches = parseInt(props.getProperty('CS_JOB_TOTALBATCH') || '1');
  var batchSize    = parseInt(props.getProperty('CS_JOB_BATCHSIZE')  || '80');

  // ── Read staging sheet ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  var stageSheet = ss.getSheetByName('_CS_STAGING');
  if (!stageSheet) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('❌ Staging sheet missing. Please re-run Build Clinical Summary.');
    return;
  }
  var stageData = stageSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var recRows = [];
  var trRows  = [];
  for (var si = 0; si < stageData.length; si++) {
    if (stageData[si][0]) recRows.push(stageData[si][0].toString());
    if (stageData[si][1]) trRows.push(stageData[si][1].toString());
  }

  var batchPromptBase =
    'You are a clinical data analyst screening a patient\'s medical records.\n' +
    'Extract only clinically relevant entries from the batch below.\n' +
    'Return a structured summary with EXACTLY these headings:\n\n' +
    'DIAGNOSES: confirmed conditions, read codes with clinical meaning\n' +
    'INVESTIGATIONS: test panels, imaging, procedures — date, what was done, result/outcome if stated\n' +
    'REFERRALS: date, specialty, reason, outcome if recorded\n' +
