    'SIGNIFICANT CONSULTATIONS: date, clinician, key clinical decision or finding\n' +
    'MEDICATION CHANGES: date, drug, change made\n' +
    'UNRESOLVED/PENDING: anything awaiting follow up, not yet actioned, or outcome not recorded\n\n' +
    'Rules:\n' +
    '• One line per entry maximum\n' +
    '• Always include the date\n' +
    '• Exclude pure admin entries, blank entries, and duplicates\n' +
    '• If a section has no relevant entries write "None in this batch"\n\n' +
    'RECORDS BATCH:\n';

  // ── Process one batch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (batchIdx < totalBatches) {
    var batch = recRows.slice(batchIdx * batchSize, (batchIdx + 1) * batchSize);
    try {
      var result = callAI(
        batchPromptBase + batch.join('\n'), 1500,
        'Clinical Summary Batch ' + (batchIdx + 1) + ' of ' + totalBatches
      );
      props.setProperty('CS_JOB_RESULT_' + batchIdx, result);
    } catch(e) {
      props.setProperty('CS_JOB_RESULT_' + batchIdx, 'Batch failed: ' + e.message);
    }
    props.setProperty('CS_JOB_BATCH', (batchIdx + 1).toString());
    _scheduleCSContinuation();
    return;
  }

  // ── All batches done — run trends ─────────────────────────────────────
  if (!props.getProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS')) {
    var trendsPrompt =
      'You are a clinical data analyst. Below are all test results for a patient.\n' +
      'Produce a TRENDS summary showing:\n' +
      '• For each analyte: all values over time with dates, oldest to newest\n' +
      '• Flag any analyte showing a concerning trend\n' +
      '• Flag any analyte tested once and never repeated where repeat would be clinically relevant\n' +
      '• Note the most recent value for each analyte\n\n' +
      'Format: Analyte: [value date] → [value date] (most recent: X) [TREND NOTE if relevant]\n\n' +
      'TEST RESULTS:\n' + trRows.join('\n');
    try {
      var trendsResult = callAI(trendsPrompt, 2000, 'Clinical Summary Trends');
      props.setProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS', trendsResult);
    } catch(e) {
      props.setProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS', 'Trends failed: ' + e.message);
    }
    _scheduleCSContinuation();
    return;
  }

  // ── All done — run merge ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  var batchSummaries = [];
  for (var bi = 0; bi < totalBatches; bi++) {
    var br = props.getProperty('CS_JOB_RESULT_' + bi) || 'Batch ' + (bi+1) + ' missing';
    batchSummaries.push('--- BATCH ' + (bi + 1) + ' ---\n' + br);
  }
  var trendsResult = props.getProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS') || '';

  var mergePrompt =
    'You have received batch summaries of a patient\'s complete medical history.\n' +
    'Consolidate into a single clean clinical summary.\n' +
    'Use EXACTLY these section headings:\n\n' +
    '## DIAGNOSES\n' +
    '## INVESTIGATIONS\n' +
    '## REFERRALS\n' +
    '## SIGNIFICANT CONSULTATIONS\n' +
    '## MEDICATION HISTORY\n' +
    '## UNRESOLVED / PENDING\n' +
    '## ANALYTE TRENDS\n\n' +
    'Rules:\n' +
    '• Remove duplicates — keep the most detailed version\n' +
    '• Preserve all dates\n' +
    '• One line per entry\n' +
    '• Sort each section chronologically\n' +
    '• ANALYTE TRENDS section from the trends summary below\n\n' +
    'BATCH SUMMARIES:\n' + batchSummaries.join('\n\n') + '\n\n' +
    'ANALYTE TRENDS SUMMARY:\n' + trendsResult;

  var finalSummary = '';
  try {
    finalSummary = callAI(mergePrompt, 2000, 'Clinical Summary Merge');
  } catch(e) {
    finalSummary = 'Merge failed: ' + e.message + '\n\nRaw summaries:\n' + batchSummaries.join('\n\n');
  }

  // ── Write to Clinical Summary tab ─────────────────────────────────────
  var csSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Clinical Summary');
  if (!csSheet) {
    csSheet = ss.insertSheet('📋 Clinical Summary');
  } else {
    csSheet.clearContents();
  }

  var today     = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm');
  var stampLine = 'Built: ' + today + '  |  Records: ' + recCount + '  |  Test results: ' + trCount;

  csSheet.getRange(1, 1).setValue('CLINICAL SUMMARY');
  csSheet.getRange(2, 1).setValue(stampLine);
  csSheet.getRange(3, 1).setValue(finalSummary);
  csSheet.getRange(1, 1).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(12);
  csSheet.getRange(2, 1).setFontStyle('italic').setFontSize(9);
  csSheet.getRange(3, 1).setFontSize(10).setWrap(true);
  csSheet.setColumnWidth(1, 900);

  // Save counts for stale detection
  props.setProperty('CS_RECORD_COUNT', recCount.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_TR_COUNT',     trCount.toString());
  props.setProperty('CS_BUILT_DATE',   today);

  // Clean up
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_RECCOUNT');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_TRCOUNT');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_BATCH');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_TOTALBATCH');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_BATCHSIZE');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_SSID');
  props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_TRENDS');
  for (var cb2 = 0; cb2 < totalBatches; cb2++) {
    props.deleteProperty('CS_JOB_RESULT_' + cb2);
  }

  // Delete staging sheet
  try { ss.deleteSheet(stageSheet); } catch(e) {}

  _clearCSContinuationTrigger();

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '✅ Clinical Summary built successfully.',
    'Records screened: ' + recCount + '\n' +
    'Test results screened: ' + trCount + '\n' +
    'Built: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    'All narrative and specialist reports will now use this summary.\n' +
    'Re-run after adding new records or test results.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

function _scheduleCSContinuation() {
  _clearCSContinuationTrigger();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('_continueClinicalSummary')
    .timeBased()
    .after(10000)
    .create();
}

function _clearCSContinuationTrigger() {
  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var t = 0; t < triggers.length; t++) {
    if (triggers[t].getHandlerFunction() === '_continueClinicalSummary') {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(triggers[t]);
    }
  }
}
// ─── PROTECT COL A: TestResults & Records ────────────────────
// Adds a soft protection note to col A header and locks the
// ARRAYFORMULA row so users know not to edit it.
// (Full sheet protection requires manual setup in Sheets UI —
//  this adds a visual and note-based guard.)
function protectAutoColumns() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var protections = [];

  try {
    var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
    if (trSheet) {
      // Protect the ARRAYFORMULA cell (row 4, col A) and the header row
      var trProt = trSheet.getRange(4, 1).protect();
      trProt.setDescription('UniqueKey — auto-generated ARRAYFORMULA. Do not edit.');
      trProt.setWarningOnly(true);  // Warning mode — doesn't block but alerts
      protections.push('TestResults col A (row 4 UniqueKey formula)');

      // Also lock row 3 headers
      var trHdrProt = trSheet.getRange(3, 1, 1, 10).protect();
      trHdrProt.setDescription('Column headers — do not edit.');
      trHdrProt.setWarningOnly(true);
      protections.push('TestResults row 3 headers');
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  try {
    var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
    if (recSheet) {
      var recProt = recSheet.getRange(4, 1).protect();
      recProt.setDescription('UniqueKey — auto-generated ARRAYFORMULA. Do not edit.');
      recProt.setWarningOnly(true);
      protections.push('Records col A (row 4 UniqueKey formula)');

      var recHdrProt = recSheet.getRange(3, 1, 1, 7).protect();
      recHdrProt.setDescription('Column headers — do not edit.');
      recHdrProt.setWarningOnly(true);
      protections.push('Records row 3 headers');
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  if (protections.length > 0) {
    ui.alert('✅ Protected ranges set (warning-only mode):\n\n• ' + protections.join('\n• ') + '\n\n' +
             'These cells will show a warning if someone tries to edit them.\n' +
             'This protects the auto-generated UniqueKey formulas from accidental deletion.\n\n' +
             'Note: To add full editors-only protection, go to Data → Protect Sheets & Ranges in the Sheets menu.');
  } else {
    ui.alert('Could not set protections — TestResults and Records sheets not found.');
  }
}


// ─── STRUCTURED MENSTRUAL INPUT FOR IRON TRACKER ─────────────
// Prompts the user with structured questions about their cycle
// and builds a detailed, standardised menstrual factor string.
// Called from menu (Treatment Tracker → Update Menstrual Factors).
// Works on the active 📈 tracker sheet — only relevant if the
// analyte is iron-related (ferritin, iron, haemoglobin, etc).
function updateMenstrualFactors() {
  var ss          = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui          = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet       = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('No Treatment Tracker found. Navigate to a 📈 tracker tab first.'); return; }

  var analyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString().toLowerCase();
  var isIronRelated = analyte.indexOf('ferritin') >= 0 || analyte.indexOf('iron') >= 0 ||
                      analyte.indexOf('haemoglobin') >= 0 || analyte.indexOf('hemoglobin') >= 0 ||
                      analyte.indexOf('hgb') >= 0 || analyte.indexOf('hb ') >= 0 || analyte === 'hb' ||
                      analyte.indexOf('transferrin') >= 0 || analyte.indexOf('mch') >= 0;

  if (!isIronRelated) {
    var proceed = ui.alert(
      'Non-Iron Analyte',
      'This tracker is for ' + sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue() + ', which is not a typical iron-related analyte.\n\n' +
      'Menstrual factors primarily affect iron, ferritin, haemoglobin, and related markers.\n\n' +
      'Do you still want to update the Menstrual / Hormonal Factors field?',
      ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
    );
    if (proceed !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  }

  // Step 1: Current menstrual status
  var statusResp = ui.prompt(
    'Menstrual Factors — Step 1 of 4',
    'What is your current menstrual status? Enter the number:\n\n' +
    '1 = Regular periods (not on hormonal contraception)\n' +
    '2 = On oral contraceptive pill (combined)\n' +
    '3 = On progestogen-only pill / mini-pill\n' +
    '4 = Hormonal IUD (Mirena / Kyleena) — periods usually lighter or stopped\n' +
    '5 = Copper IUD (non-hormonal) — periods may be heavier\n' +
    '6 = Implant (Nexplanon) — periods often irregular or stopped\n' +
    '7 = Depo-Provera injection — periods often stopped\n' +
    '8 = Perimenopausal (irregular periods)\n' +
    '9 = Postmenopausal (periods stopped)\n' +
    '10 = Pregnant\n' +
    '11 = Breastfeeding (periods may be absent)\n' +
    '12 = No periods for other reason (illness, low weight, etc)',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
  );
  if (statusResp.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var statusNum = parseInt(statusResp.getResponseText().trim());

  var statusLabels = {
    1:  'Regular periods (no hormonal contraception)',
    2:  'Combined oral contraceptive pill',
    3:  'Progestogen-only pill (mini-pill)',
    4:  'Hormonal IUD (Mirena/Kyleena) — periods typically lighter or absent',
    5:  'Copper IUD — periods may be heavier than average',
    6:  'Nexplanon implant — periods often irregular or absent',
    7:  'Depo-Provera injection — periods often absent',
    8:  'Perimenopausal — irregular periods',
    9:  'Postmenopausal — no periods',
    10: 'Currently pregnant',
    11: 'Breastfeeding — periods may be absent',
    12: 'Absent periods (other cause: illness, low weight, etc)'
  };
  var statusLabel = statusLabels[statusNum] || 'Status not specified';

  // Periods stopped or absent — skip flow/cycle questions
  var periodsAbsent = [4,6,7,9,10,11,12].indexOf(statusNum) >= 0;
  // Possible heavier periods
  var possiblyHeavy = [1,5,8].indexOf(statusNum) >= 0;

  var flowDesc = '';
  var cycleDays = '';
  var daysBleeding = '';
  var clots = '';

  if (!periodsAbsent) {
    // Step 2: Flow heaviness
    var flowResp = ui.prompt(
      'Menstrual Factors — Step 2 of 4',
      'How would you describe your flow on your heaviest day(s)?\n\n' +
      '1 = Light — pantyliner sufficient, rarely soak a pad\n' +
      '2 = Moderate — regular pad/tampon needed, changing every 4-6 hrs\n' +
      '3 = Heavy — soaking a regular pad/tampon in 2-3 hours\n' +
      '4 = Very heavy — soaking through a pad/tampon in under 2 hours\n' +
      '5 = Flooding/overflow — frequently leaking through protection\n\n' +
      '(Enter the number)',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
    );
    if (flowResp.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
    var flowNum = parseInt(flowResp.getResponseText().trim());
    var flowLabels = {1:'Light flow', 2:'Moderate flow', 3:'Heavy flow', 4:'Very heavy flow', 5:'Flooding/overflow'};
    flowDesc = flowLabels[flowNum] || 'Flow not specified';

    // Step 3: Cycle length and bleeding days
    var cycleResp = ui.prompt(
      'Menstrual Factors — Step 3 of 4',
      'Enter two numbers separated by a comma:\n\n' +
      'First:  How many days is your full cycle? (e.g. 28)\n' +
      'Second: How many days do you bleed? (e.g. 5)\n\n' +
      'Example: 28, 5\n\n' +
      'If irregular, give your best average.',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
    );
    if (cycleResp.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
    var cycleParts = cycleResp.getResponseText().split(',');
    cycleDays    = cycleParts[0] ? cycleParts[0].trim() : '?';
    daysBleeding = cycleParts[1] ? cycleParts[1].trim() : '?';

    // Step 4: Clots
    var clotsResp = ui.prompt(
      'Menstrual Factors — Step 4 of 4',
      'Do you typically pass blood clots during your period?\n\n' +
      '1 = No clots\n' +
      '2 = Small clots (smaller than a 50p coin)\n' +
      '3 = Large clots (50p coin or larger)\n' +
      '4 = Frequent large clots\n\n' +
      '(Enter the number)',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
    );
    if (clotsResp.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
    var clotsNum = parseInt(clotsResp.getResponseText().trim());
    var clotsLabels = {1:'No clots', 2:'Small clots', 3:'Large clots', 4:'Frequent large clots'};
    clots = clotsLabels[clotsNum] || '';
  }

  // Build the structured summary string
  var parts = [statusLabel];
  if (!periodsAbsent && flowDesc) {
    parts.push(flowDesc);
    if (cycleDays && daysBleeding) parts.push('Cycle: ' + cycleDays + '-day cycle, ' + daysBleeding + ' days bleeding');
    if (clots) parts.push(clots);

    // Add clinical interpretation for iron impact
    var flowNum2 = parseInt(statusResp.getResponseText()); // re-use doesn't work — estimate from flowDesc
    if (flowDesc.indexOf('Very heavy') >= 0 || flowDesc.indexOf('Flooding') >= 0) {
      parts.push('⚠️ Significant iron loss expected — trajectory adjusted for heavy blood loss');
    } else if (flowDesc.indexOf('Heavy') >= 0 || statusNum === 5) {
      parts.push('⚠️ Moderate-to-significant iron loss — consider adjusting dose target');
    } else if (flowDesc.indexOf('Light') >= 0 || periodsAbsent) {
      parts.push('✅ Minimal iron loss from menstruation — standard trajectory applies');
    }
  }
  if (periodsAbsent) {
    parts.push('✅ No active period blood loss — standard iron absorption trajectory applies');
    if (statusNum === 10) parts.push('⚠️ Pregnancy significantly increases iron requirements');
    if (statusNum === 5)  parts.push('⚠️ Copper IUD can increase flow — monitor');
  }

  var menstrualStr = parts.join('.\n');

  // Write to the sheet
  sheet.getRange(13, 2).setValue(menstrualStr).setBackground('#fef9c3').setFontColor('#713f12').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(13, Math.max(60, parts.length * 18));

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Menstrual / Hormonal Factors updated.\n\n' +
    menstrualStr + '\n\n' +
    'This will be factored into the AI trajectory — run Setup & Generate Trajectory to regenerate with these updated factors.'
  );
}



function buildSpecialRequestsSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#b45309');
  setWidths(sheet, [200, 580]);

  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '\U0001f4cb  SPECIAL REQUESTS  \u2014  Consultant letters, NHS referral requests, and private specialist finder', 2, '#b45309');

  sheet.getRange(2,1,1,2).merge()
    .setValue('Use this tab to generate referral letters and find private consultants. Select a saved investigation, choose your request type, then use the \U0001f3e5 menu \u2192 Special Requests.')
    .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#92400e');
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 35);

  var r = 3;

  // ── Section 1: Basis ─────────────────────────────────────────
  sectionBanner(sheet, r, 'STEP 1 \u2014 SELECT BASIS', 2, '#1e40af'); r++;
  var labelStyle = function(row) {
    sheet.getRange(row,1).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setBackground('#f0f9ff').setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(row, 22);
  };
  var inputStyle = function(row, note) {
    var cell = sheet.getRange(row,2);
    cell.setBackground('#fffde7').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    if (note) cell.setNote(note);
    sheet.setRowHeight(row, 22);
  };

  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('Investigation ID'); labelStyle(r);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('').setBackground('#fffde7').setNote('Enter a saved Investigation ID (e.g. INV-123456) from 📋 Investigation Records. Leave blank to use all context.'); inputStyle(r); r++;

  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('Primary concern / symptom'); labelStyle(r);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('').setBackground('#fffde7').setNote('Briefly describe the main issue e.g. "persistent fatigue, low ferritin, not responding to supplements". This focuses the letter.'); inputStyle(r);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 32); r++;

  // ── Section 2: Request Type ──────────────────────────────────
  sectionBanner(sheet, r, 'STEP 2 \u2014 REQUEST TYPE', 2, '#1e40af'); r++;
  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('Request type'); labelStyle(r);
  addDropdown(sheet, r, 2, [
    'Private consultant referral letter',
    'NHS GP referral request letter',
    'Both letters'
  ]);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setBackground('#fffde7').setNote('Select what you need. Both letters can be generated together.').setFontSize(9);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22); r++;

  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('Specialist type (optional)'); labelStyle(r);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('').setBackground('#fffde7').setNote('e.g. Haematologist, Endocrinologist, Gastroenterologist. Leave blank and AI will suggest based on your results.'); inputStyle(r); r++;

  // ── Section 3: Output area ───────────────────────────────────
  sectionBanner(sheet, r, 'STEP 3 \u2014 GENERATED LETTERS  (run \U0001f3e5 menu \u2192 Special Requests \u2192 Generate Letters)', 2, '#1e40af'); r++;

  // Private consultant letter
  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('\U0001f4e7 Private Consultant Letter').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setBackground('#eff6ff').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('[ Run Generate Letters from \U0001f3e5 menu ]').setBackground('#f8fafc').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#94a3b8');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 100); r++;

  // NHS letter
  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('\U0001f4e7 NHS GP Referral Request Letter').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setBackground('#eff6ff').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('[ Run Generate Letters from \U0001f3e5 menu ]').setBackground('#f8fafc').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#94a3b8');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 100); r++;

  // NHS likelihood
  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('\U0001f4ca NHS Referral Likelihood').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setBackground('#eff6ff').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('[ Run Generate Letters from \U0001f3e5 menu ]').setBackground('#f8fafc').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#94a3b8');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 80); r++;

  // ── Section 4: Consultant finder ────────────────────────────
  sectionBanner(sheet, r, 'STEP 4 \u2014 PRIVATE CONSULTANT FINDER  (run \U0001f3e5 menu \u2192 Special Requests \u2192 Find Consultants)', 2, '#b45309'); r++;

  sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue('\U0001f50d Consultant Finder Results').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setBackground('#fef3c7').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
  sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue('[ Run Find Consultants from \U0001f3e5 menu ]').setBackground('#f8fafc').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#94a3b8');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 200); r++;

  freeze(sheet, 2);
}

// ─── AI: GENERATE SPECIAL REQUEST LETTERS ───────────────────
// Called from menu — Special Requests → Generate Letters.
// Reads Step 1 & 2, calls OpenAI, writes to Step 3.
function generateSpecialRequestLetters() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f4cb Special Requests');
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('Special Requests sheet not found. Run Rebuild Tracker first.'); return; }

  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) { ui.alert('No API key found. Check Script Properties — set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY and AI_PROVIDER=anthropic, or OPENAI_API_KEY and AI_PROVIDER=openai.'); return; }

  // Read inputs
  var invId       = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var concern     = sheet.getRange(5, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var requestType = sheet.getRange(7, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var specType    = sheet.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString().trim();

  if (!concern) {
    ui.alert('Please enter a primary concern / symptom in Step 1 before generating letters.');
    return;
  }
  if (!requestType || requestType.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    ui.alert('Please select a request type in Step 2 (Private / NHS / Both).');
    return;
  }

  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today       = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var context     = _buildSpecialRequestContext(ss, invId, concern);

  var doPrivate   = requestType.indexOf('Private') >= 0 || requestType.indexOf('Both') >= 0;
  var doNHS       = requestType.indexOf('NHS') >= 0    || requestType.indexOf('Both') >= 0;

  try {
    if (doPrivate) {
      ui.alert('\u23f3 Generating private consultant letter... (may take ~20 seconds)');
      var privateResult = _generatePrivateConsultantLetter(context, concern, specType, profile, today);
      sheet.getRange(10, 2).setValue(privateResult).setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.setRowHeight(10, Math.max(120, privateResult.split('\n').length * 13));
    }

    if (doNHS) {
      ui.alert('\u23f3 Generating NHS GP referral letter... (may take ~20 seconds)');
      var nhsResult = _generateNHSReferralLetter(context, concern, specType, profile, today);
      // Parse out the letter and likelihood assessment
      var letterPart     = '';
      var likelihoodPart = '';
      var splitMarker    = nhsResult.indexOf('LIKELIHOOD ASSESSMENT:');
      if (splitMarker >= 0) {
        letterPart     = nhsResult.substring(0, splitMarker).trim();
        likelihoodPart = nhsResult.substring(splitMarker).trim();
      } else {
        letterPart     = nhsResult;
        likelihoodPart = '(See letter above for likelihood discussion)';
      }
      sheet.getRange(11, 2).setValue(letterPart).setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.setRowHeight(11, Math.max(120, letterPart.split('\n').length * 13));
      sheet.getRange(12, 2).setValue(likelihoodPart).setBackground('#f0fdf4').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.setRowHeight(12, Math.max(80, likelihoodPart.split('\n').length * 13));
    }

    ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);
    ui.alert('\u2705 Letters generated.\n\nScroll down to Step 3 to read and copy the text.\nUse \U0001f3e5 menu \u2192 Special Requests \u2192 Download Letters as PDF to save.');

  } catch(e) {
    ui.alert('Error generating letters:\n' + e.toString());
  }
}

function _buildSpecialRequestContext(ss, invId, concern) {
  var profile  = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today    = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var context  = 'Today: ' + today + '\nPATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n';
  context     += 'PRIMARY CONCERN: ' + concern + '\n\n';

  // Add investigation context if ID provided
  if (invId) {
    try {
      var invRaw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invId);
      if (invRaw) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(invRaw);
        context += 'INVESTIGATION ' + invId + ' (' + (inv.date || '') + '):\n';
        context += 'Symptoms: ' + (inv.symptoms || '') + '\n';
        context += 'AI Assessment: ' + (inv.aiAssess || '').substring(0, 600) + '\n';
        context += 'GP Advice: ' + (inv.gpAdvice || '') + '\n';
        context += 'Status: ' + (inv.status || '') + '\n\n';
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  // Add current significant findings
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f52c Thresholds');
  if (trSheet && thSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var latest = collectLatestResults(trData);
    var flags  = [];
    for (var k in latest) {
      var res = latest[k];
      var thRows = thMap[k] || findThresholdFuzzy(k, thMap);
      if (thRows) {
        for (var t = 0; t < thRows.length; t++) {
          if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[t])) {
            flags.push(res.analyte + ': ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' \u2014 ' + thRows[t][1]);
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      var labStatus = res.status ? res.status.toString() : '';
      if (labStatus && labStatus.toUpperCase() !== 'NORMAL' && labStatus.trim() !== '' && flags.indexOf(res.analyte) < 0) {
        flags.push(res.analyte + ': ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' (lab: ' + labStatus + ')');
      }
    }
    if (flags.length > 0) context += 'FLAGGED / ABNORMAL RESULTS:\n' + flags.join('\n') + '\n\n';
  }

  // Add treatment history
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  var trackers = [];
  for (var s = 0; s < sheets.length; s++) {
    if (sheets[s].getName().indexOf('\U0001f4c8') === 0) {
      var ts = sheets[s];
      var tName = ts.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
      var tStart = ts.getRange(5, 2).getValue().toString();
      var tTarget = ts.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString();
      var tSupp = ts.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString();
      if (tName) trackers.push(tName + ': started ' + tStart + ', target ' + tTarget + ', treatment: ' + tSupp);
    }
  }
  if (trackers.length > 0) context += 'TREATMENT TRACKERS:\n' + trackers.join('\n') + '\n\n';

  // Add GP records summary
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f4cb Investigation Records');
  if (!recSheet) recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  if (recSheet) {
    var recLastRow = recSheet.getLastRow();
    if (recLastRow >= 4) {
      var recData = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, Math.min(recLastRow - 3, 10), 10).getValues();
      var recLines = [];
      for (var ri = 0; ri < recData.length; ri++) {
        if (recData[ri][1]) recLines.push(recData[ri][1] + ': ' + (recData[ri][2] || '').toString().substring(0, 80));
      }
      if (recLines.length > 0) context += 'RECENT GP/CONSULTATION RECORDS:\n' + recLines.join('\n') + '\n\n';
    }
  }

  return context;
}

function _generatePrivateConsultantLetter(context, concern, specType, profile, today) {
  var specLabel = specType || 'appropriate specialist';
  var prompt =
    'You are helping a patient write a professional letter to a private ' + specLabel + '.\n\n' +
    'CONTEXT:\n' + context + '\n\n' +
    'Write a professional, courteous letter FROM the patient TO the consultant.\n\n' +
    'The letter must:\n' +
    '1. Open professionally (Dear Dr / Dear [Specialty] Consultant)\n' +
    '2. Clearly state the main concern and how long it has been ongoing\n' +
    '3. Summarise the key test results and what has been investigated so far\n' +
    '4. Mention any treatments tried and their outcomes\n' +
    '5. State clearly what the patient is hoping the consultation will address\n' +
    '6. Be respectful, factual, and appropriately concise (300-450 words)\n' +
    '7. End with a request to arrange an appointment\n\n' +
    'Use [Your Name] and [Your Address] as placeholders.\n' +
    'Today is ' + today + '.';
  return callOpenAI(prompt, 1200, 'GP Summary');
}

function _generateNHSReferralLetter(context, concern, specType, profile, today) {
  var specLabel = specType || 'an appropriate specialist';
  var prompt =
    'You are helping a patient write a formal letter to their GP requesting an NHS referral to ' + specLabel + '.\n\n' +
    'CONTEXT:\n' + context + '\n\n' +
    'PART 1 — Write the referral request letter. It must:\n' +
    '1. Open formally (Dear Dr [GP surname])\n' +
    '2. State clearly the request: referral to ' + specLabel + '\n' +
    '3. Build a clinical case using the specific results, symptoms, duration, and treatments tried\n' +
    '4. Reference relevant NICE guidelines or NHS pathways by name where applicable\n' +
    '5. Note any red flag symptoms if relevant\n' +
    '6. Be respectful and collaborative in tone — the GP is an ally, not an adversary\n' +
    '7. Be 300-450 words\n\n' +
    'PART 2 — Immediately after the letter, on a new line write:\n' +
    'LIKELIHOOD ASSESSMENT:\n' +
    'Rate NHS referral likelihood using EXACTLY ONE of these four tiers:\n' +
    '  LIKELY — Clear clinical need, NICE pathway exists, strong case\n' +
    '  JUSTIFIED BUT DIFFICULT — Clinically reasonable but resource-pressured; may need persistence\n' +
    '  UNLIKELY — Below NHS threshold for this condition/severity\n' +
    '  NOT APPROPRIATE — Self-manageable or private-only realistic path\n\n' +
    'Then provide:\n' +
    '- Tier: [one of the four above]\n' +
    '- Likelihood: [X]% (honest estimate)\n' +
    '- Reasoning: [2-3 sentences citing NICE guidance, condition severity, history]\n' +
    '- What would strengthen the case: [1-2 specific suggestions if applicable]\n' +
    '- What to do if declined: [practical next step]\n\n' +
    'Today is ' + today + '. Use [Your Name] and [GP Surgery] as placeholders.';
  return callOpenAI(prompt, 1500, 'Clinical Summary');
}

// ─── AI: FIND PRIVATE CONSULTANTS ───────────────────────────
// Generates specialty-matched, profile-aware consultant guidance
// with links to major directories and distance-banding from home town.
function findPrivateConsultants() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f4cb Special Requests');
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('Special Requests sheet not found. Run Rebuild Tracker first.'); return; }

  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) { ui.alert('No API key found. Check Script Properties — OPENAI_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY required.'); return; }

  var concern     = sheet.getRange(5, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var specType    = sheet.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today       = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  // Get home town from profile
  var homeTown = '';
  try {
    var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f464 Profile');
    if (profSheet) {
      var pRows = profSheet.getRange(3, 1, 15, 2).getValues();
      for (var p = 0; p < pRows.length; p++) {
        if (pRows[p][0] && pRows[p][0].toString().toLowerCase().indexOf('home town') >= 0) {
          homeTown = pRows[p][1].toString().trim();
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  if (!concern) {
    ui.alert('Please enter a primary concern in Step 1 first.');
    return;
  }

  var context = _buildSpecialRequestContext(ss, sheet.getRange(4,2).getValue().toString().trim(), concern);

  var locationText = homeTown ? 'Patient home town: ' + homeTown + ' (UK). Please include distance banding (approximate \u2014 e.g. "within ~30 miles of " + homeTown + " based on general geography").' : 'Patient location not provided \u2014 suggest nationally and note location is unknown.';

  var prompt =
    'You are helping a patient find the right private medical consultant in the UK.\n\n' +
    'CONTEXT:\n' + context + '\n' +
    locationText + '\n\n' +
    'SPECIALIST TYPE REQUESTED: ' + (specType || 'suggest based on context') + '\n\n' +
    'Please provide:\n\n' +
    '1. RECOMMENDED SPECIALTY: Which type of specialist is most appropriate for this case and why?\n\n' +
    '2. WHAT TO LOOK FOR IN A CONSULTANT:\n' +
    '   List 4-6 specific sub-specialties, research areas, or experience criteria that would make a consultant particularly good for this patient\'s specific situation. Be specific — not just "experience in haematology" but "experience in iron deficiency anaemia with malabsorption" etc.\n\n' +
    '3. HOW TO FIND ONE:\n' +
    '   Provide direct search guidance for each of these UK directories (include specific search terms the patient should use for their condition):\n' +
    '   \u2022 Doctify: https://www.doctify.com/uk/search\n' +
    '   \u2022 Top Doctors: https://www.topdoctors.co.uk\n' +
    '   \u2022 BMI Healthcare: https://www.bmihealthcare.co.uk\n' +
    '   \u2022 Spire Healthcare: https://www.spirehealthcare.com\n' +
    '   \u2022 HCA Healthcare UK: https://www.hcahealthcare.co.uk\n\n' +
    '4. QUESTIONS TO ASK WHEN BOOKING:\n' +
    '   List 4-5 specific questions the patient should ask a clinic when enquiring, to check a consultant is the right fit.\n\n' +
    '5. WHAT TO BRING TO THE APPOINTMENT:\n' +
    '   Specific list based on this patient\'s test history and records.\n\n' +
    '6. RED FLAGS TO AVOID:\n' +
    '   What would suggest a consultant is NOT the right fit?\n\n' +
    '7. TYPICAL COST GUIDANCE:\n' +
    '   Approximate first consultation cost range and what follow-up typically involves (NHS England private rates).\n\n' +
    'Be specific and practical. Today is ' + today + '.';

  try {
    ui.alert('\u23f3 Finding consultant guidance... (may take ~20 seconds)');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 1800, 'Symptom Analysis');

    var outputRow = 14; // Step 4 row
    sheet.getRange(outputRow, 2).setValue(response).setBackground('#fffbeb').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(outputRow, Math.max(200, response.split('\n').length * 13));

    ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);
    ui.alert('\u2705 Consultant guidance generated.\n\nScroll to Step 4 to read.\n\nNote: directory links are provided for you to search \u2014 the AI cannot access live listings. Use the search terms suggested for best results.');
  } catch(e) {
    ui.alert('Error:\n' + e.toString());
  }
}

// ─── DOWNLOAD SPECIAL REQUEST LETTERS AS PDF ────────────────
function downloadSpecialRequestPDF() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f4cb Special Requests');
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('Special Requests sheet not found.'); return; }

  var privateLetter = sheet.getRange(10, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var nhsLetter     = sheet.getRange(11, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var likelihood    = sheet.getRange(12, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var concern       = sheet.getRange(5, 2).getValue().toString().trim();
  var today         = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  if (!privateLetter && !nhsLetter) {
    ui.alert('No letters to download yet. Run Generate Letters first.');
    return;
  }

  try {
    var doc  = DocumentApp.create('Referral Letters \u2014 ' + today);
    var body = doc.getBody();
    body.setMarginTop(36); body.setMarginBottom(36);
    body.setMarginLeft(54); body.setMarginRight(54);

    // Cover
    var titlePara = body.appendParagraph('Referral Letters');
    titlePara.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { titlePara.setFontSize(18); } catch(e) { try { titlePara.getEditAsText().setFontSize(0, titlePara.getText().length - 1, 18); } catch(e2) {} }

    body.appendParagraph('Generated: ' + today).setItalic(true);
    body.appendParagraph('Concern: ' + concern).setBold(true);
    body.appendParagraph('\u26a0\ufe0f These letters are AI-generated drafts. Please review and personalise before sending. Nothing here constitutes medical advice.')
      .setItalic(true);
    body.appendParagraph('');

    if (privateLetter) {
      var ph = body.appendParagraph('PRIVATE CONSULTANT LETTER');
      ph.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
      body.appendParagraph(privateLetter);
      body.appendParagraph('');
    }

    if (nhsLetter) {
      var nh = body.appendParagraph('NHS GP REFERRAL REQUEST LETTER');
      nh.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
      body.appendParagraph(nhsLetter);
      body.appendParagraph('');
    }

    if (likelihood) {
      var lh = body.appendParagraph('NHS REFERRAL LIKELIHOOD ASSESSMENT');
      lh.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
      body.appendParagraph(likelihood);
    }

    doc.saveAndClose();

    // Convert to PDF and save to Drive
    var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
    pdfBlob.setName('ReferralLetters_' + today.replace(/ /g,'_') + '.pdf');
    var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
    docFile.setTrashed(true); // Remove the Google Doc

    ui.alert('\u2705 PDF created in Google Drive.\n\nFilename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\nOpen Google Drive to find and download it.');
  } catch(e) {
    ui.alert('Error creating PDF:\n' + e.toString());
  }
}


function buildMedicalTracker() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // ── Step 1: Backup core data before touching anything ─────
  var backupTimestamp = backupCoreData(ss);
  var backupMsg       = backupTimestamp
    ? '✅ Data backed up to tabs prefixed "BAK ' + backupTimestamp + '"'
    : 'ℹ️  No existing data to back up (first build).';

  // ── Step 2: Rebuild all sheets ────────────────────────────
  var sheetDefs = [
    { name: '📖 How To Use',           fn: buildHowToUseSheet },
    { name: '🧠 Operations Reference', fn: buildOperationsReferenceSheet },
    { name: '👤 Profile',              fn: buildProfileSheet },
    { name: 'TestResults',             fn: buildTestResultsSheet },
    { name: 'Records',                 fn: buildRecordsSheet },
    { name: '💊 Meds & Supplements Log',       fn: buildMedicationLogSheet },
    { name: '🔍 Symptom Investigation',fn: buildSymptomInvestigationSheet },
    { name: '📋 Investigation Records',fn: buildInvestigationRecordsSheet },
    { name: '📈 Treatment Tracker',    fn: buildTreatmentTrackerSheet },
    { name: '📊 Analysis',             fn: buildAnalysisSheet },
    { name: '🚨 Significant Findings', fn: buildSignificantFindingsSheet },
    { name: '🔬 Thresholds',           fn: buildThresholdsSheet },
    { name: '📚 Reference Guide',      fn: buildReferenceGuide },
    { name: '📋 Special Requests',     fn: buildSpecialRequestsSheet },
  ];

  for (var i = 0; i < sheetDefs.length; i++) {
    var existing = ss.getSheetByName(sheetDefs[i].name);
    var sheet    = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(sheetDefs[i].name);
    sheet.clear(); sheet.clearFormats();
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), sheet.getMaxColumns()).breakApart();
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), sheet.getMaxColumns()).clearDataValidations();
    sheetDefs[i].fn(sheet, ss);
  }

  var def = ss.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
  if (def) { try { ss.deleteSheet(def); } catch(e) {} }

  // ── Step 3: Attempt auto-restore from backup ──────────────
  var restoreResults = [];
  if (backupTimestamp) {
    restoreResults = restoreCoreData(ss, backupTimestamp);
  }

  // ── Step 4: Order tabs ────────────────────────────────────
  for (var j = 0; j < sheetDefs.length; j++) {
    var s = ss.getSheetByName(sheetDefs[j].name);
    if (s) { ss.setActiveSheet(s); ss.moveActiveSheet(j + 1); }
  }

  // Push backup tabs to end
  var allSheets = ss.getSheets();
  for (var k = 0; k < allSheets.length; k++) {
    if (allSheets[k].getName().indexOf('BAK ') === 0) {
      ss.setActiveSheet(allSheets[k]);
      ss.moveActiveSheet(ss.getSheets().length);
    }
  }

  ss.setActiveSheet(ss.getSheetByName('📖 How To Use'));

  // ── Step 5: Report ────────────────────────────────────────
  var restored  = restoreResults.filter(function(r){ return r.ok; }).map(function(r){ return '  ✅ ' + r.sheet; });
  var failed    = restoreResults.filter(function(r){ return !r.ok; }).map(function(r){ return '  ⚠️ ' + r.sheet + ' — ' + r.reason; });

  var msg = '✅  Medical Tracker v5 built!\n\n' + backupMsg + '\n\n';
  if (restored.length > 0) msg += 'AUTO-RESTORED:\n' + restored.join('\n') + '\n\n';
  if (failed.length > 0)   msg += 'COULD NOT AUTO-RESTORE (data preserved in BAK tabs):\n' + failed.join('\n') + '\n\n';
  if (failed.length > 0)   msg += 'The BAK tabs at the end of your sheet contain your original data.\nYou can copy-paste from them manually.\n\n';
  msg += 'Next steps:\n1. Fill in 👤 Profile\n2. Paste SAR data\n3. 🏥 menu → Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI';

  ui.alert(msg);
}


// ─── BACKUP & RESTORE SYSTEM ─────────────────────────────────
// Core data sheets — these contain user-entered data we must preserve.
// Derived/AI sheets (Analysis, Significant Findings, Thresholds) are
// excluded — they can always be regenerated from the source data.
var DATA_SHEETS = [
  { name: 'TestResults',              dataStartRow: 5, numCols: 10 },
  { name: 'Records',                  dataStartRow: 5, numCols: 7  },
  { name: '💊 Meds & Supplements Log',        dataStartRow: 5, numCols: 10 },
  { name: '📋 Investigation Records', dataStartRow: 4, numCols: 10 },
  { name: '👤 Profile',               dataStartRow: 3, numCols: 2  },
];

// Returns timestamp string if any data was backed up, null otherwise.
function backupCoreData(ss) {
  var anyData   = false;
  var timestamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyy HHmm');

  for (var i = 0; i < DATA_SHEETS.length; i++) {
    var def    = DATA_SHEETS[i];
    var source = ss.getSheetByName(def.name);
    if (!source) continue;

    var lastRow = source.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < def.dataStartRow) continue;

    var numRows = lastRow - def.dataStartRow + 1;
    var values  = source.getRange(def.dataStartRow, 1, numRows, def.numCols).getValues();

    var hasData = false;
    for (var r = 0; r < values.length; r++) {
      for (var c = 0; c < values[r].length; c++) {
        if (values[r][c] !== '' && values[r][c] !== null) { hasData = true; break; }
      }
      if (hasData) break;
    }
    if (!hasData) continue;

    anyData = true;

    var bakName  = 'BAK ' + timestamp + ' ' + def.name.replace(/[📖👤💊🔍📋📈📊🚨🔬📚]/g, '').trim();
    bakName      = bakName.substring(0, 100);

    // ── Delete all previous backups for this sheet (keep only the new one) ──
    var sheetBaseName = def.name.replace(/[📖👤💊🔍📋📈📊🚨🔬📚]/g, '').trim();
    var allSheets = ss.getSheets();
    for (var d = 0; d < allSheets.length; d++) {
      var sName = allSheets[d].getName();
      if (sName.indexOf('BAK ') === 0 && sName !== bakName &&
          sName.indexOf(sheetBaseName) >= 0) {
        try { ss.deleteSheet(allSheets[d]); } catch(e) {}
      }
    }

    var existing = ss.getSheetByName(bakName);
    var bakSheet = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(bakName);
    bakSheet.clear();
    if (bakSheet.isSheetHidden()) bakSheet.showSheet(); // unhide briefly to write

    // Header row
    bakSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.numCols).merge()
      .setValue('BACKUP of "' + def.name + '" — ' + timestamp + '. This tab is hidden. Access via 🏥 menu → Data & Backups.')
      .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#92400e').setFontWeight('bold');
    bakSheet.setRowHeight(1, 35);

    // Copy column headers from row above data
    if (def.dataStartRow > 1) {
      try {
        var hdrs = source.getRange(def.dataStartRow - 1, 1, 1, def.numCols).getValues();
        bakSheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, def.numCols).setValues(hdrs)
          .setBackground('#d1fae5').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(8);
        bakSheet.setRowHeight(2, 20);
      } catch(e) {}
    }

    var dataStartInBak = def.dataStartRow > 1 ? 3 : 2;
    bakSheet.getRange(dataStartInBak, 1, numRows, def.numCols).setValues(values).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true);

    bakSheet.setTabColor('#d97706');
    bakSheet.hideSheet(); // ← hidden — clean workbook, data safe
  }

  return anyData ? timestamp : null;
}


// Attempts to restore data from backup tabs into freshly rebuilt sheets.
// Returns array of {sheet, ok, reason} result objects.
function restoreCoreData(ss, timestamp) {
  var results = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < DATA_SHEETS.length; i++) {
    var def     = DATA_SHEETS[i];
    var bakName = 'BAK ' + timestamp + ' ' + def.name.replace(/[📖👤💊🔍📋📈📊🚨🔬📚]/g, '').trim();
    bakName     = bakName.substring(0, 100);
    var bakSheet = ss.getSheetByName(bakName);

    if (!bakSheet) continue; // no backup = sheet was empty, nothing to restore

    var target = ss.getSheetByName(def.name);
    if (!target) {
      results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: false, reason: 'Target sheet not found after rebuild' });
      continue;
    }

    try {
      var bakLastRow = bakSheet.getLastRow();
      // Backup layout:
      //   Row 1 = banner
      //   Row 2 = column headers (copied from row above dataStartRow, e.g. the ARRAYFORMULA row or col headers)
      //   Row 3 = first actual data row  <-- read from here
      var dataReadRow = 3;
      if (bakLastRow < dataReadRow) {
        results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: true, reason: 'Empty backup — nothing to restore' });
        continue;
      }

      var numRows     = bakLastRow - (dataReadRow - 1);
      var values      = bakSheet.getRange(dataReadRow, 1, numRows, def.numCols).getValues();
      var cleanValues = values.filter(function(row) {
        // For TestResults/Records, col A is UniqueKey (computed string) — check cols B+ for real data
        var checkFrom = (def.name === 'TestResults' || def.name === 'Records') ? 1 : 0;
        return row.slice(checkFrom).some(function(cell) { return cell !== '' && cell !== null; });
      });

      if (cleanValues.length === 0) {
        results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: true, reason: 'No data rows to restore' });
        continue;
      }

      var targetCols = target.getLastColumn();
      if (cleanValues[0].length > targetCols && targetCols > 0) {
        bakSheet.showSheet();
        bakSheet.setTabColor('#dc2626');
        results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: false, reason: 'Column mismatch — backup tab unhidden for manual paste' });
        continue;
      }

      // TestResults and Records: col A = ARRAYFORMULA — restore cols B onwards only (slice off col A)
      if (def.name === 'TestResults' || def.name === 'Records') {
        var subValues = cleanValues.map(function(row){ return row.slice(1); });
        // Ensure we don't accidentally overwrite the ARRAYFORMULA in row 4
        var writeRow = def.dataStartRow; // row 5 for these sheets
        target.getRange(writeRow, 2, subValues.length, subValues[0].length).setValues(subValues);

      } else if (def.name === '\u{1F464} Profile') {
        // Profile restore: match by label (col A) not by row position.
        // This handles profile field additions/removals between versions safely.
        var targetLastRow = target.getLastRow();
        if (targetLastRow >= 3) {
          var targetLabels = target.getRange(3, 1, targetLastRow - 2, 1).getValues();
          // Build a map from label → target row
          var labelToTargetRow = {};
          for (var tl = 0; tl < targetLabels.length; tl++) {
            var lbl = targetLabels[tl][0] ? targetLabels[tl][0].toString().trim() : '';
            if (lbl) labelToTargetRow[lbl.toLowerCase()] = tl + 3;
          }
          var profileRestored = 0;
          var skipProfileLabels = { 'calculated bmi': true, 'bmi category': true, 'healthy bmi range': true, 'bmi  (auto-calculated)': true };
          for (var bv = 0; bv < cleanValues.length; bv++) {
            var backupLabel = cleanValues[bv][0] ? cleanValues[bv][0].toString().trim() : '';
            var backupVal   = cleanValues[bv].length > 1 ? cleanValues[bv][1] : '';
            if (!backupLabel || backupVal === '') continue;
            if (skipProfileLabels[backupLabel.toLowerCase()]) continue; // never overwrite formula cells
            var matchRow = labelToTargetRow[backupLabel.toLowerCase()];
            if (matchRow) {
              target.getRange(matchRow, 2).setValue(backupVal);
              profileRestored++;
            }
          }
          results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: true, reason: profileRestored + ' profile fields restored (matched by label)' });
          continue; // skip the generic results.push below
        }

      } else {
        target.getRange(def.dataStartRow, 1, cleanValues.length, cleanValues[0].length).setValues(cleanValues);
      }

      // Re-assert the ARRAYFORMULA after restore in case setValues touched row 4
      if (def.name === 'TestResults') {
        var tf = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(IF(LEN(B5:B2000)=0,"",LOWER(IFERROR(TEXT(B5:B2000,"yyyymmdd"),TEXT(DATEVALUE(B5:B2000),"yyyymmdd"))&"|"&TRIM(D5:D2000)&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(E5:E2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(F5:F2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(G5:G2000),"")))),")';
        target.getRange(4, 1).setFormula(tf).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setNote('Auto-generated — do not edit.');
      }
      if (def.name === 'Records') {
        var rf = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(IF(LEN(B5:B2000)=0,"",LOWER(IFERROR(TEXT(B5:B2000,"yyyymmdd"),TEXT(DATEVALUE(B5:B2000),"yyyymmdd"))&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(C5:C2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(D5:D2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(LEFT(TRIM(E5:E2000),80),"")))),")';
        target.getRange(4, 1).setFormula(rf).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setNote('Auto-generated — do not edit.');
      }

      results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: true, reason: cleanValues.length + ' rows restored' });

    } catch(e) {
      try { bakSheet.showSheet(); bakSheet.setTabColor('#dc2626'); } catch(e2) {}
      results.push({ sheet: def.name, ok: false, reason: e.toString().substring(0, 120) + ' — backup tab unhidden' });
    }
  }

  return results;
}


// ── Manual backup — hidden, clean ────────────────────────────
function manualBackup() {
  var ss        = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var timestamp = backupCoreData(ss);
  if (timestamp) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      '✅ Backup complete — ' + timestamp + '\n\n' +
      'All core data (TestResults, Records, Meds & Supplements Log, Investigation Records, Profile) ' +
      'has been saved to hidden backup tabs.\n\n' +
      'The tabs are invisible during normal use. To access them:\n' +
      '🏥 menu → Data & Backups → View / Unhide Backups'
    );
  } else {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('ℹ️  No data to back up — data sheets appear empty.');
  }
}


// ── Unhide backups so user can inspect / paste from them ─────
function unhideBackups() {
  var ss     = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  var baks   = sheets.filter(function(s){ return s.getName().indexOf('BAK ') === 0; });

  if (baks.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No backup tabs found.\n\nCreate one via: 🏥 menu → Data & Backups → Manual Backup Now.');
    return;
  }

  for (var i = 0; i < baks.length; i++) { baks[i].showSheet(); }
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '✅ ' + baks.length + ' backup tab(s) are now visible at the end of your tab list.\n\n' +
    'Once you have finished inspecting them, re-hide with:\n' +
    '🏥 menu → Data & Backups → Hide Backups'
  );
}


// ── Re-hide backup tabs ───────────────────────────────────────
function hideBackups() {
  var ss     = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  var baks   = sheets.filter(function(s){ return s.getName().indexOf('BAK ') === 0; });

  if (baks.length === 0) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No backup tabs found.'); return; }

  var hidden = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < baks.length; i++) {
    try { baks[i].hideSheet(); hidden++; } catch(e) {}
  }
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ ' + hidden + ' backup tab(s) hidden. Workbook is tidy again.');
}


// ── View backup status ────────────────────────────────────────
function viewBackupStatus() {
  var ss     = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  var baks   = sheets.filter(function(s){ return s.getName().indexOf('BAK ') === 0; });

  if (baks.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      'No backup tabs found.\n\n' +
      'Backups are created automatically on every rebuild.\n' +
      'Create one now: 🏥 menu → Data & Backups → Manual Backup Now.'
    );
    return;
  }

  // Group by timestamp prefix
  var groups = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < baks.length; i++) {
    var name  = baks[i].getName(); // "BAK 15Feb25 1430 TestResults"
    var parts = name.split(' ');
    var ts    = parts.length >= 3 ? parts[1] + ' ' + parts[2] : parts[1];
    if (!groups[ts]) groups[ts] = [];
    groups[ts].push({ name: name, hidden: baks[i].isSheetHidden(), rows: baks[i].getLastRow() - 2 });
  }

  var msg = baks.length + ' backup tab(s) — all hidden from normal view:\n\n';
  for (var ts in groups) {
    msg += '📦 Snapshot: ' + ts + '\n';
    for (var j = 0; j < groups[ts].length; j++) {
      var b = groups[ts][j];
      msg += '   ' + (b.hidden ? '👁 hidden' : '👁 visible') + '  ' + b.name + '  (' + Math.max(0, b.rows) + ' data rows)\n';
    }
    msg += '\n';
  }
  msg += 'To inspect: 🏥 → Data & Backups → Unhide Backups\n';
  msg += 'To clean up: 🏥 → Data & Backups → Delete Old Backups';
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(msg);
}


// ── Delete old backups ────────────────────────────────────────
function deleteOldBackups() {
  var ui      = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var ss      = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheets  = ss.getSheets();
  var baks    = sheets.filter(function(s){ return s.getName().indexOf('BAK ') === 0; });

  if (baks.length === 0) { ui.alert('No backup tabs found.'); return; }

  // Group by timestamp so user can keep recent and delete old
  var groups = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < baks.length; i++) {
    var parts = baks[i].getName().split(' ');
    var ts    = parts.length >= 3 ? parts[1] + ' ' + parts[2] : parts[1];
    if (!groups[ts]) groups[ts] = [];
    groups[ts].push(baks[i]);
  }

  var timestamps = Object.keys(groups).sort();
  var msg = 'Found ' + timestamps.length + ' backup snapshot(s):\n\n';
  for (var t = 0; t < timestamps.length; t++) {
    msg += (t === timestamps.length - 1 ? '🟢 LATEST: ' : '🔴 OLD:    ') + timestamps[t] + ' (' + groups[timestamps[t]].length + ' tabs)\n';
  }
  msg += '\nDelete ALL old snapshots (keep only the latest)?';

  var confirm = ui.alert('Delete Old Backups', msg, ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  var deleted = 0;
  for (var t2 = 0; t2 < timestamps.length - 1; t2++) { // keep latest (last in sorted array)
    var tabsToDelete = groups[timestamps[t2]];
    for (var j = 0; j < tabsToDelete.length; j++) {
      try { tabsToDelete[j].showSheet(); ss.deleteSheet(tabsToDelete[j]); deleted++; } catch(e) {}
    }
  }
  ui.alert('✅ Deleted ' + deleted + ' old backup tab(s).\nLatest snapshot (' + timestamps[timestamps.length - 1] + ') kept and re-hidden.');
}


// ─── SHEET: HOW TO USE ──────────────────────────────────────
// ─── OPERATIONS REFERENCE SHEET ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Builds a detailed reference tab describing every menu operation, AI call
// structure, data flow, and technical implementation detail.
// Audience: end users wanting to understand what each function does,
// and AI/technical users who want to understand prompt architecture.
function buildOperationsReferenceSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#1e3a5f');

  // ── Layout helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var COL = { A: 1, B: 2, C: 3, D: 4, E: 5 };
  var NAVY    = '#1e3a5f';
  var TEAL    = '#0d4f4f';
  var PURPLE  = '#3b1f6e';
  var AMBER   = '#7c2d12';
  var GREEN   = '#14532d';
  var LTBLUE  = '#e0f0ff';
  var LTGREEN = '#f0fdf4';
  var LTYELLOW= '#fefce8';
  var LTPURPLE= '#f5f3ff';
  var LTORANGE= '#fff7ed';
  var GREY    = '#f8fafc';
  var WHITE   = '#ffffff';

  sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 26);   // indent gutter
  sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 220);  // operation name
  sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 340);  // what it does
  sheet.setColumnWidth(4, 340);  // AI / technical detail
  sheet.setColumnWidth(5, 180);  // notes / caveats

  var r = 1;

  function title(text) {
    sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue(text)
      .setBackground(NAVY).setFontColor('#ffffff')
      .setFontSize(16).setFontWeight('bold')
      .setVerticalAlignment('middle')
      .setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 44);
    r++;
  }

  function subtitle(text) {
    sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue(text)
      .setBackground('#2d4a7a').setFontColor('#e2e8f0')
      .setFontSize(10).setFontStyle('italic')
      .setVerticalAlignment('middle')
      .setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 28);
    r++;
  }

  function gap(h) {
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, h || 10);
    r++;
  }

  function sectionHeader(text, bg) {
    sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue(text)
      .setBackground(bg || TEAL).setFontColor('#ffffff')
      .setFontSize(12).setFontWeight('bold')
      .setVerticalAlignment('middle')
      .setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 32);
    r++;
  }

  function colHeaders() {
    var hdr = sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5);
    var vals = [['', 'OPERATION / MENU ITEM', 'WHAT IT DOES', 'AI & TECHNICAL DETAIL', 'NOTES & CAVEATS']];
    hdr.setValues(vals)
      .setBackground('#334155').setFontColor('#f1f5f9')
      .setFontSize(8).setFontWeight('bold')
      .setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22);
    r++;
  }

  function row(name, what, aiDetail, notes, bg, indent) {
    var bg2 = bg || WHITE;
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setBackground('#e2e8f0');  // gutter
    sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue((indent ? '    ' : '') + name)
      .setBackground(bg2).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 3).setValue(what || '')
      .setBackground(bg2).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 4).setValue(aiDetail || '')
      .setBackground(bg2).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 5).setValue(notes || '')
      .setBackground(bg2).setFontSize(8).setFontStyle('italic').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 16);  // auto-expand via wrap
    r++;
  }

  function subrow(name, what, aiDetail, notes) {
    row(name, what, aiDetail, notes, GREY, true);
  }

  function aiCallBadge(text) {
    return '🤖 AI CALL: ' + text;
  }

  function noAI() { return '⬛ No AI — pure data operations only'; }

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // TITLE
  title('🧠  Medical Tracker v5 — Operations Reference');
  subtitle('Complete technical reference for all menu operations, AI call architectures, data flows, and implementation details. Audience: users and AI/technical reviewers.');
  gap(8);

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 1: DATA REFRESH
  sectionHeader('① 🔄  DATA REFRESH', NAVY);
  subtitle('Controls when the tracker re-reads your data and re-runs AI analysis. Two modes: quick (no AI) and full (AI regenerates everything).');
  colHeaders();

  row('⚡ Update Views\n(Quick Refresh)',
    'Rebuilds the Analysis and Significant Findings sheets from data already in the Thresholds sheet. Does not call the AI. Takes ~10 seconds.\n\nUse this after pasting new test results — it recalculates which analytes are flagged without spending AI tokens.',
    noAI() + '\n\nData flow:\n1. Reads TestResults (all rows)\n2. For each analyte, looks up existing threshold rows in Thresholds sheet\n3. Runs isTriggered() — compares latest value against each threshold\n4. Writes flagged rows to 📊 Analysis and 🚨 Significant Findings\n5. Writes lab-abnormal rows (from lab reference range col) separately\n\nKey function: quickRefresh() → _quickRefreshSilent()',
    'Run this every time you add new results. No AI cost.', LTBLUE);

  row('🔄 Full AI Refresh\n(3–6 minutes)',
    'Complete regeneration pipeline. Runs AI threshold generation, supplement analysis, significance rating, and rebuilds all views. This is the main "make the tracker current" operation.\n\nRun when: first setup, after adding many new analytes, or when you want fresh AI thresholds.',
    aiCallBadge('Multiple — runs as a background chain to avoid 6-minute Apps Script timeout') + '\n\nPipeline stages (in order):\n1. THRESHOLDS — AI generates research thresholds for each analyte in batches of 15. Each batch = 1 AI call. Typically 3-8 calls total.\n2. SUPPLEMENTS — 1 AI call analyses all medications + supplements together for interactions\n3. SIGNIFICANCE RATINGS — 1 AI call rates clinical significance of flagged findings\n4. VIEWS — non-AI rebuild of Analysis + Significant Findings sheets\n\nTimeout handling: each stage sets a trigger for the next stage. If a stage exceeds ~4 mins, the trigger fires anyway and picks up where it left off.\n\nKey functions: refreshEverything() → _runRefreshChain() → [threshold chain] → completeRefreshPostThreshold()',
    'Costs ~15-30 AI calls. Expect 3-6 minutes. The 📊 Refresh Status tab shows live progress. You can keep using the spreadsheet while it runs.', LTBLUE);

  row('📊 Check Refresh Status',
    'Opens or refreshes the Refresh Status tab, showing which pipeline stages have completed, are running, or are pending.',
    noAI() + '\n\nReads ScriptProperties for stage flags set during the refresh chain. Writes a formatted status table to the 📊 Refresh Status sheet.',
    'If refresh appears stuck, check this tab first before using troubleshooting tools.', GREY);

  subrow('▶️ Resume Refresh',
    'Continues a Full Refresh from the post-threshold stage if it got stuck or timed out mid-pipeline.',
    noAI() + ' for the resume itself.\nThen triggers: supplement analysis → significance ratings → view rebuild.\n\nFunction: completeRefreshPostThreshold()',
    'Use if Full Refresh completed thresholds but then stopped. Check Refresh Status to confirm thresholds are done before using.');

  subrow('🔬 Regenerate Thresholds Only',
    'Re-runs only the AI threshold generation step without the full pipeline. Useful if thresholds look wrong or incomplete.',
    aiCallBadge('Batch AI calls — same as the threshold stage of Full Refresh') + '\n\nFunction: refreshThresholdsWithAI()',
    'Does not update Significant Findings. Run Quick Refresh after to see the effect.');

  subrow('🔍 Show Analyte Diagnostic List',
    'Writes a diagnostic tab listing every analyte in TestResults alongside its mapping status — whether a threshold was found and under what key.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: showAnalyteList()',
    'Use to find analytes that are not being picked up in Significant Findings. Shows the lookup key used vs the key in Thresholds.');

  subrow('🧹 Clean Junk from Thresholds Sheet',
    'Removes rows in the Thresholds sheet that are clearly AI-generated artefacts — blank rows, header rows duplicated mid-sheet, rows with no threshold value.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: cleanThresholdSheetJunkManual()',
    'Safe to run anytime. Does not regenerate thresholds — run Full Refresh if many rows are removed.');

  subrow('🚨 Clear Stuck Threshold Job',
    'Deletes all ScriptProperties related to the current threshold batch job and removes any lingering continuation triggers. Use if the threshold stage appears permanently stuck.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: clearStuckThresholdJob()',
    'Nuclear-ish for the threshold stage. After running, start a Fresh Full AI Refresh or Regenerate Thresholds Only.');

  subrow('☢️ Clear ALL Triggers',
    'Deletes every Apps Script trigger associated with this spreadsheet. Complete reset of the background processing system.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: clearAllTriggers()',
    '⚠️ Only use if multiple troubleshooting steps have failed. After running, the background chain will not restart automatically — you must manually run a refresh.');

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 2: SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION
  sectionHeader('② 🔍  SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION', TEAL);
  subtitle('The investigation workflow: describe symptoms, get AI analysis, generate emails for GP appointments, record the outcome, and iterate.');
  colHeaders();

  row('▶ Run AI Analysis',
    'Takes the symptoms, timeline, and context you have typed into the Symptom Investigation tab and produces a structured AI analysis: differential patterns, questions to ask the GP, possible connections to existing findings.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — model: active provider (Claude Sonnet or GPT-4o), max_tokens: ~2000') + '\n\nContext fed to AI:\n• Patient profile (age, sex, diagnoses, key metrics)\n• Current symptoms + timeline from the investigation form\n• All current medications with indication\n• Most recent significant findings (flagged analytes with threshold context)\n• Saved GP records (most recent 20)\n• Previous saved investigations (as context)\n\nOutput written back to the Analysis section of the investigation form.\n\nFunction: runSymptomAnalysis()',
    'Does not save automatically — use Save Investigation to persist. The AI has no memory between sessions; all context is rebuilt fresh each call.', LTGREEN);

  row('📧 Generate Pre-GP Email',
    'Writes a structured email to send to your GP before an appointment, summarising your current symptoms and the key questions the AI analysis identified.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — uses the current investigation form content') + '\n\nPrompt instructs the AI to write in a patient voice: concise, non-alarmist, structured with clear questions. Includes: symptom summary, duration, relevant test results, specific asks.\n\nFunction: generatePreGPEmail()',
    'Edit the output before sending — AI may be overly formal or include clinical language your GP might find unusual.', LTGREEN);

  row('🩺 Analyse GP / Consultant Advice',
    'After a GP appointment, paste the advice you received into the form and run this. The AI analyses whether the advice addresses the questions raised, flags anything that may have been missed, and suggests follow-up.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nContext: the original symptom analysis + the GP advice text entered. AI is prompted to be honest about gaps without being dismissive of GP judgment.\n\nFunction: analyseGPAdvice()',
    'The AI is explicitly prompted NOT to dismiss GP advice — it flags gaps, not errors.', LTGREEN);

  row('📧 Generate Post-GP Email',
    'Writes a follow-up email to your GP summarising what was discussed, any agreed actions, and any outstanding questions.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nFunction: generatePostGPEmail()',
    'Useful for creating a paper trail of what was agreed.', LTGREEN);

  row('💾 Save / 📂 Load Investigation',
    'Saves the current investigation form (symptoms, AI analysis, GP advice, outcome) as a named record in ScriptProperties. Load retrieves a saved investigation back into the form.',
    noAI() + '\n\nStorage: JSON-serialised in ScriptProperties under keys INV_FULL_{id} and INV_INDEX (array of ids). Max ~500KB total across all investigations (Apps Script property limit).\n\nFunctions: saveInvestigation(), loadInvestigation()',
    'ScriptProperties have a ~500KB total limit. If you hit errors saving, delete old investigations via the form.', GREY);

  row('🧠 View Full AI Context',
    'Opens a tab showing exactly what data the AI receives when you run an analysis — the full prompt context including profile, results, meds, records, and investigations.',
    noAI() + '\n\nWrites a formatted view of buildFullContext() output. Useful for understanding what the AI "knows" and diagnosing why an analysis might miss something.\n\nFunction: viewAIContext()',
    'Read-only diagnostic. The AI context is rebuilt fresh for every call — this shows what it would receive right now.', GREY);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 3: SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS
  sectionHeader('③ 🚨  SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS', '#7c2d12');
  subtitle('Operations on the Significant Findings sheet — the list of analytes that have exceeded research thresholds.');
  colHeaders();

  row('⭐ Generate AI Significance Ratings',
    'Adds a significance score (1–10) and clinical interpretation to each row in Significant Findings. Helps prioritise which flagged results actually matter most given this patient\'s full profile.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nPrompt: AI receives all flagged findings together and rates each for clinical significance given the patient profile, other medications, and comorbidities. It is specifically instructed to consider interactions between findings rather than rating each in isolation.\n\nOutput: writes significance score and interpretation to col J of Significant Findings.\n\nFunction: addSignificancePanelToFindings()',
    'Run after a Full Refresh. Ratings are overwritten on each run.', LTORANGE);

  row('🔬 Deep-dive on Specific Finding',
    'Select any row in Significant Findings and run this. The AI provides a detailed clinical deep-dive on that single analyte: what it means, what causes it, what research says about this specific value, and what should be done.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nContext: the selected analyte value + all threshold rows for that analyte + patient profile + relevant medications. AI is prompted to go beyond the threshold framework and engage with the specific clinical literature.\n\nFunction: deepDiveOnFinding()',
    'Click the cell in col A (analyte name) before running. Output shown in a dialog — not saved automatically.', LTORANGE);

  row('🤖 Generate AI Advice & Action Plan',
    'Generates a prioritised advice document for all current significant findings — what to do, in what order, why, and with what expected impact.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — max_tokens: 3000') + '\n\nContext: all flagged findings with thresholds, patient profile, medications, key records. Prompt instructs the AI to produce prioritised actions (Priority 1/2/3), be specific about numbers, and be honest about evidence quality.\n\nFunction: generateSignificantFindingsAdvice()',
    'Output displayed in a dialog. Not saved to Drive — copy/paste if you want to keep it.', LTORANGE);

  row('📬 Generate GP Letter',
    'Writes a professional GP letter summarising the significant findings and requesting specific investigation or action. Framed as a patient-written letter informed by their own test results.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nPrompt instructs a measured, non-alarmist tone. Letter includes: the flagged analytes with context, specific asks (test requests, referrals, medication reviews), and the research basis for concern.\n\nFunction: generateGPLetter()',
    'Review before sending — the AI may include clinical language that your GP could find unusual coming from a patient.', LTORANGE);

  row('📄 Generate PDF Report\n(Significant Findings)',
    'Creates a structured PDF report of all significant findings with full threshold evidence tables. This is a data-dense reference document rather than a narrative.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — AI writes an interpretive summary') + '\n\nStructure:\n• AI-written summary of the overall picture\n• Formatted table of all flagged analytes with values, thresholds, and meanings\n• Annex: full threshold evidence audit (threshold rows, confidence, sources)\n\nPDF is saved to Google Drive. The Google Doc used to create it is deleted after PDF conversion.\n\nFunction: generatePDFReport()',
    'This is distinct from the Personal Health Narrative — it is more technical and data-focused. Annex contains all AI threshold rows for inspection.', LTORANGE);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 4: TREATMENT TRACKER
  sectionHeader('④ 📈  TREATMENT TRACKER', GREEN);
  subtitle('Tracks a specific analyte or metric over time, generates a trajectory assessment, and monitors progress against it. One tracker tab per analyte/treatment.');
  colHeaders();

  row('➕ Add New Treatment Tracker',
    'Creates a new tab (📈 [analyte name]) pre-configured for tracking a specific analyte over time. Sets up the input form, chart area, and links to the main data.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: addNewTreatmentTracker()',
    'Run once per analyte you want to track. The tab name must match the analyte name in TestResults exactly (or close enough for fuzzy matching).', LTGREEN);

  row('① Setup & Generate Trajectory',
    'One-time setup for a tracker tab: loads all historic results for the analyte, calculates baseline, and generates an AI trajectory assessment — what to expect, on what timescale, with what intervention.',
    aiCallBadge('2 calls') + '\n\nCall 1 (trajectory): receives all historic values for this analyte, current medications, patient profile. AI produces a trajectory narrative: current state, expected direction, timescale, what would accelerate improvement, realistic targets.\n\nCall 2 (chart labels): generates contextual annotations for the chart based on the trajectory.\n\nFunction: setupAndGenerateTrajectory()',
    'Run once per tracker. Re-running overwrites the trajectory. Historic data is loaded from TestResults — ensure all past results are entered.', LTGREEN);

  row('② Assess Progress & Update Chart',
    'After adding new results, run this to get an AI assessment of current progress vs the trajectory, and update the chart with the latest data point.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nContext: the original trajectory + all historic values + latest result. AI assesses: are you on track, ahead, or behind trajectory? What does the latest result suggest about the underlying condition?\n\nUpdates the chart with the new data point and writes the assessment below the chart.\n\nFunction: assessAndUpdateChart()',
    'Run after each new result for the tracked analyte. Chart updates regardless of whether the AI call succeeds.', LTGREEN);

  row('📄 Export Tracker as PDF',
    'Exports the current treatment tracker tab as a PDF to Google Drive.',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: exportTrackerPDF()',
    'Captures the current state of the chart and assessment. Useful for sharing with a clinician.', GREY);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 5: SUPPLEMENTS & MEDS
  sectionHeader('⑤ 💊  SUPPLEMENTS & MEDICATIONS', PURPLE);
  subtitle('AI analysis of the current medication and supplement list for interactions, evidence quality, and relevance to the patient\'s conditions.');
  colHeaders();

  row('🔬 Analyse Supplements & Medications',
    'Reviews all current medications and supplements together. Identifies potential interactions, assesses evidence quality for each supplement, and flags anything relevant given the patient\'s blood results.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — max_tokens: 2500') + '\n\nContext: all medications with indication and dosage, all supplements, flagged blood results, patient profile, AND auto-detected conditions (swept from Records, Meds indications, Profile, Investigations via _sweepWorkbookForConditions()). AI is prompted to:\n• Assess evidence quality for each supplement (strong/moderate/weak/no evidence)\n• Flag drug-supplement interactions\n• Flag medication effects relevant to the patient\'s conditions (e.g. beta-blocker CNS effects in POTS)\n• Apply condition-specific rules — e.g. salt/sodium is NOT flagged negatively for POTS patients\n• Note where supplements address a genuine deficiency vs where evidence is weak\n\nCondition detection is fully automatic — a POTS diagnosis in any GP record, meds indication, or saved investigation will be picked up without any manual entry.\n\nOutput written to a new tab: 💊 Supplement Analysis\n\nFunction: analyseSupplements()\nBackground function: _runAnalyseSupplementsBackground()',
    'Runs as a background trigger to avoid timeout. Check the 📊 Refresh Status tab. This also runs automatically as part of Full AI Refresh.', LTPURPLE);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 6: SPECIAL REQUESTS
  sectionHeader('⑥ 📋  SPECIAL REQUESTS — LETTERS & REPORTS', AMBER);
  subtitle('Generates formal letters and multi-section PDF reports. These are the most AI-intensive operations and produce the most detailed outputs.');
  colHeaders();

  row('📧 Generate Letters\n(Private + NHS)',
    'Generates two letters: one addressed to a private consultant (formal referral tone), one to an NHS GP (requesting referral). Based on a specific concern you enter.',
    aiCallBadge('2 calls — one per letter') + '\n\nContext: patient profile, specific concern entered, significant findings, medications, key records. Each letter is framed appropriately for its recipient:\n• Private letter: detailed clinical summary, specific investigation requests, research basis\n• NHS letter: concise, within GP norms, focused on referral pathway\n\nFunctions: generateSpecialRequestLetters(), _generatePrivateConsultantLetter(), _generateNHSReferralLetter()',
    'Letters are saved to the Special Requests sheet. Use Download Letters as PDF to export.', LTORANGE);

  row('🔍 Find Private Consultants',
    'Provides guidance on finding private consultants for a given specialty and location — typical costs, how to search, what to say when booking.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call') + '\n\nNot a live search (no internet access from Apps Script). AI draws on training knowledge of typical pathways, costs, and directories. Output is guidance, not a live list.\n\nFunction: findPrivateConsultants()',
    'Treat output as a starting point. Verify consultant availability and costs directly.', LTORANGE);

  row('📋 Complete Medical Record PDF',
    'The most comprehensive output. Generates a full medical record PDF with: AI narrative summary, all test results (formatted tables), all medications, all GP records, all significant findings, and the full threshold evidence annex.',
    aiCallBadge('2 calls — narrative summary split into two parts due to token limits') + '\n\nCall 1: AI writes a clinical narrative summary of the complete history\nCall 2: AI writes the current-status section and key recommendations\n\nThe PDF then appends: formatted tables of ALL test results, Records, Medications, and the threshold annex.\n\nThis is designed to be a complete medical record for a specialist consultation — all data, all context, in one document.\n\nFunction: generateCompleteMedicalRecord()',
    'Takes 2-4 minutes. Output can be 20-40 pages. Save to Drive and share with consultants as a complete reference document.', LTORANGE);

  // ── Personal Health Narrative ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
