    .setValue('📖  PERSONAL HEALTH NARRATIVE — DETAILED')
    .setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#93c5fd')
    .setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold')
    .setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 24);
  r++;

  row('📖 Personal Health Narrative\n— STANDARD',
    'A 7-section plain-English personal health document. Designed to be the "best GP with unlimited time" explanation of your full health picture.\n\nSections:\n1. The Story So Far — narrative history\n2. Blood Results — what actually matters\n3. Medications & Supplements — honest assessment\n4. Key Underlying Drivers\n5. If You Do These Things — realistic expected impact\n6. Prioritised Next Steps (Priority 1/2/3)\n7. The Bigger Picture — prognosis\n\nPlus Annex A: Threshold Evidence Audit\n\nOutput: PDF to Google Drive',
    aiCallBadge('2 calls — one-shot per section group') + '\n\nCall 1 (sections 1–5): max_tokens 3000\nContext: profile, flagged findings with threshold detail, all latest results (up to 40), medications with indication, GP records (last 20), saved investigations\n\nCall 2 (sections 6–7): max_tokens 2500\nContext: same full data — sections 6-7 have the same context as 1-5 since v5.18a\n\nAI persona: "knowledgeable personal health advisor — like the best kind of GP who actually has time to explain things properly"\n\nLimitation: one-shot prompts apply a threshold framework. The AI produces competent but sometimes conservative output without iterative reasoning.\n\nFunction: generatePersonalHealthNarrative()',
    'Takes ~60 seconds. Use the Rich version for deeper, less generic analysis.', LTYELLOW);

  row('📖 Personal Health Narrative\n— RICH ⚡',
    'Identical 7-section structure to Standard, but adds a silent pre-reasoning AI pass before the two writing calls.\n\nThe pre-reasoning call thinks through clinical questions the one-shot prompt tends to miss:\n• Functional deficiency screen (lab-normal but functionally deficient?)\n• Medication CNS/autonomic/metabolic effects\n• Cross-system pattern analysis\n• Recovery trajectory evidence\n• Niche investigations not yet done\n\nThis reasoning is fed to the writing calls as enriched context — the AI writes knowing these insights have already been worked through.\n\nOutput: PDF to Google Drive (labelled "Rich")',
    aiCallBadge('3 calls total') + '\n\n── CALL 0 (SILENT PRE-REASONING) ──\nmax_tokens: 2000\nPrompt structure: 5 explicit clinical reasoning questions:\n  1. Functional deficiency screen (B12 functional vs serum, ferritin, Vit D, Mg, folate)\n  2. Medication effects screen (propranolol CNS penetration, COCP autonomic/B12 effects, interactions)\n  3. Cross-system pattern analysis (mTBI+POTS mechanisms, unifying hypotheses)\n  4. Recovery & trajectory (beyond conservative GP guidance, specific prognostic factors)\n  5. Niche investigations (specialist literature, not yet done based on records)\nOutput: clinical notes — never shown to user\n\n── CALL 1 (SECTIONS 1–5) ──\nmax_tokens: 3000\nSame context as Standard PLUS the full pre-reasoning output injected as:\n"PRIOR CLINICAL REASONING — use this to enrich your writing, weave insights into prose"\nAI is explicitly instructed NOT to default to conservative threshold-framework language\n\n── CALL 2 (SECTIONS 6–7) ──\nmax_tokens: 2500\nSame context + prior reasoning. Prognosis section instructed to engage with specific recovery trajectory evidence identified in call 0.\n\nFallback: if call 0 fails, calls 1+2 proceed without enriched context (graceful degradation)\n\nFunction: generatePersonalHealthNarrativeRich()',
    'Takes ~90-120 seconds (3 AI calls). Designed to surface the kind of insights that emerge from iterative clinical conversation — B12 functional deficiency, propranolol CNS effects, POTS neuroplasticity evidence — without requiring manual prompting.', LTYELLOW);

  // ── Specialist Review ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
    .setValue('🔬  SPECIALIST / CONSULTANT REVIEW — DETAILED')
    .setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#86efac')
    .setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold')
    .setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 24);
  r++;

  row('🔬 Specialist / Consultant Review\n— STANDARD',
    'Consultant-layer assessment of ALL patient data viewed through a specific specialist or condition lens. Unlike the GP-layer narrative, this deliberately weights specialist and rehabilitation literature over mainstream GP guidelines.\n\n5-section structure:\n1. Specialist Overview\n2. Result-by-Result Specialist Review\n3. What a Specialist Would Investigate\n4. Medication & Supplement Review (specialist lens)\n5. Specialist Summary & Recommended Next Steps\n\nCondition detection: auto-sweeps the workbook for condition mentions before prompting — you can select from found conditions or type your own.\n\nOutput: PDF to Google Drive',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — max_tokens: 3500') + '\n\nContext: patient profile, all latest results (with lab status), all flagged findings with full threshold context, medications with indication (inc stopped), GP records (last 25), saved investigations\n\nAI persona: "consultant specialist with deep expertise in [chosen specialism]"\n\nCritical prompt instructions (key differentiators from the narrative):\n• Weight specialist and rehabilitation literature over mainstream GP guidelines\n• Explicitly flag where assessment draws on niche, emerging, or cross-specialty research\n• Distinguish well-established / emerging association / theoretical but plausible\n• Flag tests a specialist would order that a GP assessment might not prioritise\n• Where mainstream says normal but specialist evidence says otherwise — say so explicitly\n• Include lab-NORMAL results that are significant through the specialist lens\n\nCondition sweep: _sweepWorkbookForConditions() — searches Profile, Records, Investigations, Meds indication fields against 25 condition patterns (POTS, mTBI, fibromyalgia, etc.)\n\nFunction: runSpecialistDeepdive()',
    'One-shot — the AI has no prior reasoning to draw on. Better than the narrative for niche findings due to the specialist-weighted prompt, but can still miss multi-step clinical reasoning.', LTGREEN);

  row('🔬 Specialist / Consultant Review\n— RICH ⚡',
    'Same 5-section structure as Standard, but adds a silent specialist-scoped pre-reasoning call before the report writing call.\n\nThe pre-reasoning call generates a clinical hypothesis list specifically under the chosen specialist lens:\n• Pattern recognition through the specialist lens\n• Niche result interpretation (lab-normal but significant)\n• Functional vs absolute deficiency analysis\n• Specialist investigation priorities (ranked)\n• Medication interactions (specialist-level)\n• Evidence quality notes for each hypothesis\n\nThe report writing call then has these hypotheses as context — it is explicitly instructed to engage with them substantively.\n\nOutput: PDF to Google Drive (labelled "Rich")',
    aiCallBadge('2 calls total') + '\n\n── CALL 0 (SILENT SPECIALIST PRE-REASONING) ──\nmax_tokens: 1800\nSix specialist-scoped reasoning questions:\n  1. Pattern recognition through specialist lens — what would a specialist see that a GP misses?\n  2. Niche result interpretation — lab-normal but specialist-significant?\n  3. Functional vs absolute deficiencies — context-specific functional impairment?\n  4. Investigation priorities — ranked high/worth considering/lower, with rationale\n  5. Medication interactions under specialist lens — what a GP might not flag\n  6. Evidence quality — well-established / emerging / theoretical\nOutput: structured clinical notes (never shown to user)\n\n── CALL 1 (SPECIALIST REPORT) ──\nmax_tokens: 3500\nSame as Standard PLUS:\n"SPECIALIST PRE-REASONING — engage with these hypotheses substantively; do not reproduce verbatim"\nAI is explicitly instructed to engage with the pre-reasoning hypotheses, not just repeat standard specialist review text\n\nFallback: if call 0 fails, report generates without enriched context\n\nFunction: runSpecialistDeepdiveRich()',
    'Takes ~60-90 seconds (2 AI calls). The pre-reasoning is specialist-scoped — for mTBI+POTS it asks specifically about autonomic-neurological interactions, functional B12 in neurological conditions, etc.', LTGREEN);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 7: AI INFRASTRUCTURE
  sectionHeader('⑦ 🤖  AI INFRASTRUCTURE & PROVIDER SETTINGS', '#374151');
  subtitle('How AI calls are made, which models are used, how usage is tracked, and how to switch between OpenAI and Anthropic.');
  colHeaders();

  row('✅ Use Anthropic / OpenAI\n(set active provider)',
    'Switches the active AI provider between Anthropic (Claude Sonnet) and OpenAI (GPT-4o). All subsequent AI calls use the selected provider.',
    noAI() + '\n\nStores provider choice in ScriptProperties under key AI_PROVIDER.\n\nAll AI calls route through callAI() which reads this property and dispatches to _callAnthropic() or _callOpenAI().\n\nActive models:\n• Anthropic: claude-sonnet-4-5\n• OpenAI: gpt-4o\n\nFunctions: setProviderAnthropic(), setProviderOpenAI()',
    'API keys must be set in Extensions → Apps Script → Project Settings → Script Properties.\nANTHROPIC_API_KEY or OPENAI_API_KEY', GREY);

  row('🔌 Test API Connection',
    'Runs a minimal live AI call (just "Reply with just: OK") and shows the full response or error. Use to confirm API key is valid and the connection is working.',
    aiCallBadge('1 call — uses claude-haiku-4-5 (Anthropic) or gpt-4o (OpenAI) — minimal tokens') + '\n\nFunction: testAIConnection()',
    'Haiku is used for Anthropic tests (cheapest, fastest). If test fails, shows the raw HTTP response code and error body for diagnosis.', GREY);

  row('💰 View AI Usage Dashboard',
    'Opens a tab showing cumulative AI token usage, cost estimates, and call counts broken down by operation. Helps track spend.',
    noAI() + '\n\nUsage is logged after every AI call via logAIUsage(eventLabel, model, inputTokens, outputTokens). Stored in ScriptProperties under AI_USAGE_LOG (JSON array).\n\nDashboard reads the log and renders a formatted summary with per-operation breakdown and estimated costs.\n\nFunction: buildAIUsageDashboard()',
    'Cost estimates are approximations based on published token pricing. Check provider dashboards for accurate billing.', GREY);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 8: THRESHOLD SYSTEM
  sectionHeader('⑧ 🔬  HOW THE THRESHOLD SYSTEM WORKS', '#1e3a5f');
  subtitle('The threshold system is the engine of the tracker. Understanding it explains how analytes get flagged, why some are missed, and how to fix problems.');
  colHeaders();

  row('What is a threshold?',
    'For each analyte (blood test marker), the AI generates a set of research thresholds — values above or below which the clinical literature suggests concern. Each analyte can have multiple threshold rows: e.g. "Optimal: 50–100", "Flag Above: 78", "Flag Below: 30".\n\nThresholds are stored in the 🔬 Thresholds sheet (one row per threshold per analyte). They are generated once and reused — they do not regenerate on every refresh.',
    'Threshold generation prompt (callAIForThresholds):\n• Receives a batch of 15 analytes + patient profile\n• Asks AI to produce: threshold value, direction (Above/Below), clinical meaning, confidence level, source/guideline\n• AI is instructed to echo back the exact analyte name provided (to prevent renaming)\n• After parsing, analyte names are enforced back to the original sent (v5.17o fix)\n\nThreshold matching (isTriggered):\n• Direction col is authoritative (Above/Below)\n• Falls back to operator in threshold string (>, <, ≤, ≥)\n• Handles: "Above 4.0 mmol/L", "< 3 mmol/L", "> 21 µmol/L"\n\nKey functions: callAIForThresholds(), isTriggered(), buildThresholdMap()',
    'Thresholds are AI-generated and not clinically validated. Low-confidence rows (red in annexes) should be verified or deleted. They regenerate on the next Full Refresh.', LTBLUE);

  row('Why is an analyte missing from Significant Findings?',
    'Most commonly: a name mismatch between the analyte name in TestResults and the name used in the Thresholds sheet. The lookup chain tries three key variants before giving up.',
    'Lookup chain (buildAnalysisRows):\n1. Raw name from TestResults (lowercase)\n2. cleanAnalyteName() — removes units, brackets, common suffixes\n3. normaliseSynonyms() — maps known synonyms (e.g. "Systolic arterial pressure" → "Systolic blood pressure")\n\nDiagnostic tools:\n• 🔍 Show Analyte Diagnostic List — shows lookup status for every analyte\n• 🔧 Debug Annex Tables — shows why annex tables are empty\n• Name Mappings → View Mappings Report — shows all synonym mappings in use\n\nFix options:\n1. Add a Name Mapping (Name Mappings → manually add synonym)\n2. Rename the analyte in TestResults to match the Threshold sheet\n3. Run Full Refresh (regenerates thresholds under the current name)',
    'The normaliseSynonyms() function has a fixed synonym table. New synonyms require a code update.', LTBLUE);

  gap();

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // SECTION 9: DATA MANAGEMENT
  sectionHeader('⑨ 💾  DATA MANAGEMENT & BACKUPS', '#374151');
  colHeaders();

  row('💾 Manual Backup Now',
    'Creates a snapshot of all core data sheets (TestResults, Records, Meds, Profile) as hidden backup tabs prefixed "BAK [timestamp]".',
    noAI() + '\n\nFunction: manualBackup()\nOnly the most recent backup per sheet is retained (previous deleted automatically).',
    'Backups are in the same spreadsheet — they are not exported to Drive. For a permanent copy, download the sheet as .xlsx.', GREY);

  row('🔄 Reset for New User',
    'Wipes all patient data (TestResults, Records, Meds, Profile, Findings, Analysis, Investigations) while preserving thresholds and name mappings. Allows the tracker to be reused for a new patient without regenerating thresholds.',
    noAI() + '\n\nClears: TestResults, Records, Meds, Profile values, Significant Findings, Analysis, Investigation Records, Treatment Tracker tabs, Backup tabs\nPreserves: Thresholds sheet, Name Mappings, API keys\n\nFunction: resetForNewUser()',
    '⚠️ Irreversible. Double-confirmation required. Back up the spreadsheet as .xlsx before running.', GREY);

  row('🏗️ Rebuild Entire Tracker',
    'Rebuilds all sheet structures from scratch (headers, formatting, dropdowns, formulas). Backs up data first, then restores it. Use after a code update to apply new sheet layouts.',
    noAI() + '\n\nSequence: backup → clear + rebuild all sheets → restore data from backup\nFunction: buildMedicalTracker()',
    'Safe to run on an existing spreadsheet with data — backup/restore is automatic. Do NOT run mid-refresh.', GREY);

  gap(12);

  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // FOOTER
  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 5).merge()
    .setValue('Medical Tracker v5 — Operations Reference  |  Auto-generated by buildOperationsReferenceSheet()  |  Regenerated on each Rebuild.')
    .setBackground('#1e293b').setFontColor('#64748b')
    .setFontSize(8).setFontStyle('italic')
    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
    .setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 20);

  sheet.setFrozenRows(3);
}

function buildOperationsReferenceSheet_addToMenu() {
  // Helper — not called directly. The sheet is built as part of buildMedicalTracker.
  // To rebuild just this sheet without a full rebuild:
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83e\udde0 Operations Reference');
  var sheet = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet('\ud83e\udde0 Operations Reference');
  sheet.clear(); sheet.clearFormats();
  buildOperationsReferenceSheet(sheet, ss);
}


function buildHowToUseSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#0f766e');
  sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 45);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 200);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 580);

  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).merge()
    .setValue('📖  HOW TO USE YOUR MEDICAL TEST RESULTS TRACKER v5')
    .setBackground('#0f766e').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(14)
    .setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(1, 45);

  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 3).merge()
    .setValue('AI-powered tracker for NHS test results. All clinical guidance is AI-generated — nothing hardcoded. ' +
              'Read this guide before starting.')
    .setBackground('#ccfbf1').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#134e4a').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 35);

  var rows = [
    ['section','GETTING STARTED','',''],
    ['row','①','Fill 👤 Profile first','Year of Birth, Biological Sex, Menopausal Status, Conditions, Medications, Supplements.\nThe AI uses this to personalise all guidance.'],
    ['row','②','Paste SAR data → TestResults & Records','Columns match your NHS SAR export exactly. Paste rows directly. UniqueKey auto-generates.'],
    ['row','③','Log Medications → 💊 Meds & Supplements Log','Enter any medications or supplements with dose, frequency, and start/stop dates.\nThe AI reads this when assessing your symptoms and results.'],
    ['row','④','Run AI Refresh → 🏥 menu','Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI — reads your analytes, generates current research thresholds + common causes for all of them.'],
    ['spacer','','',''],
    ['section','YOUR TABS','',''],
    ['tableheader','Tab','Purpose','Action'],
    ['tablerow','📖 How To Use','This guide','Read first'],
    ['tablerow','👤 Profile','Personal details','Fill once, update when things change'],
    ['tablerow','TestResults','Raw test data','Paste SAR data, add new results'],
    ['tablerow','Records','Consultations, referrals, notes','Paste SAR Records, add notes'],
    ['tablerow','💊 Meds & Supplements Log','Medications and supplements log','Add entries as you start/stop things'],
    ['tablerow','🔍 Symptom Investigation','AI symptom analysis','Enter symptoms → get AI analysis → enter GP advice → get AI response → generate email'],
    ['tablerow','📋 Investigation Records','Saved investigation history','Select a past investigation to view/edit'],
    ['tablerow','📈 Treatment Tracker','Track treatment progress','Set up tracking for a flagged analyte (e.g. ferritin) — shows expected vs actual with chart'],
    ['tablerow','📊 Analysis','All results vs thresholds','Full picture, all analytes'],
    ['tablerow','🚨 Significant Findings','Flagged results only','Start here for your priority view'],
    ['tablerow','🔬 Thresholds','AI threshold database','Populated by AI refresh, fully editable'],
    ['tablerow','📚 Reference Guide','Technical detail','Design philosophy and concepts'],
    ['spacer','','',''],
    ['section','SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION WORKFLOW','',''],
    ['row','1','Enter your symptoms','Go to 🔍 Symptom Investigation. Fill in your symptoms and any relevant context. Click Run AI Analysis.'],
    ['row','2','Review AI assessment','The AI analyses your symptoms against ALL your test results, thresholds, medications and supplements. It identifies likely contributing factors and suggests tests.'],
    ['row','3','Generate pre-GP email','Click Generate Email (Pre-GP) to get a professional email requesting initial tests or an appointment.'],
    ['row','4','Enter GP/Consultant advice','After your appointment, enter what your GP or consultant advised in the provided field. Click Analyse GP Advice.'],
    ['row','5','Review AI response to GP advice','The AI assesses the advice — confidence level, whether it aligns with research, suggested questions or alternative considerations.'],
    ['row','6','Generate post-GP email','Click Generate Email (Post-GP) to get a polite, evidence-based email if you want to follow up or request further consideration.'],
    ['row','7','Save the record','Click Save Investigation — the record is stored in 📋 Investigation Records and can be recalled and updated any time.'],
    ['spacer','','',''],
    ['section','TREATMENT TRACKER','',''],
    ['row','📈','How it works',
      'When a flagged analyte has a treatment plan (e.g. ferritin + iron tablets):\n' +
      '1. Go to 📈 Treatment Tracker\n' +
      '2. Select the analyte and enter your starting value, target, supplement dose and frequency\n' +
      '3. The AI generates an expected improvement trajectory based on your dose, absorption factors,\n' +
      '   any factors affecting response (e.g. heavy periods, low B12 affecting absorption)\n' +
      '4. A chart shows expected vs actual values over time\n' +
      '5. At each test milestone (1 month, 2 months etc) enter your actual result\n' +
      '6. The AI assesses whether you are on track and suggests dose adjustments if not'],
    ['spacer','','',''],
    ['section','LIMITATIONS',''],
    ['warn','Educational tool only','Nothing here is medical advice. Always discuss with your GP or specialist.',''],
    ['warn','AI can make mistakes','Treat source citations as starting points. Re-run refresh when guidelines change.',''],
  ];

  var r = 3; var altTable = false;
  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    var type = rows[i][0]; var c1 = rows[i][1]; var c2 = rows[i][2]; var c3 = rows[i][3];
    if (type === 'section') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).merge().setValue(c1).setBackground('#0f766e').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, 30); altTable = false;
    } else if (type === 'row') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(c1).setBackground('#f0fdfa').setFontSize(13).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor('#0f766e');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(c2).setBackground('#f0fdfa').setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
      sheet.getRange(r, 3).setValue(c3).setBackground('#ffffff').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(40, c3.split('\n').length * 15));
    } else if (type === 'tip') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue('💡').setBackground('#fefce8').setFontSize(13).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(c1).setBackground('#fefce8').setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true).setFontColor('#854d0e');
      sheet.getRange(r, 3).setValue(c2).setBackground('#fefce8').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#713f12');
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(40, c2.split('\n').length * 15));
    } else if (type === 'warn') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue('⚠️').setBackground('#fff1f2').setFontSize(12).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(c1).setBackground('#fff1f2').setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true).setFontColor('#9f1239');
      sheet.getRange(r, 3).setValue(c2).setBackground('#fff1f2').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#881337');
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(35, c2.split('\n').length * 15));
    } else if (type === 'spacer') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).setBackground('#f8fafc'); sheet.setRowHeight(r, 8);
    } else if (type === 'tableheader') {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[c1, c2, c3]]).setBackground('#0f766e').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22); altTable = false;
    } else if (type === 'tablerow') {
      var tbg = altTable ? '#f0fdfa' : '#ffffff';
      sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(c1).setBackground(tbg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(c2).setBackground(tbg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 3).setValue(c3).setBackground(tbg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(20, c3.split('\n').length * 15));
      altTable = !altTable;
    }
    r++;
  }

  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).merge().setValue('⚕️  Always discuss results with your GP or consultant. This tracker helps you have more informed conversations.')
    .setBackground('#0f766e').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9)
    .setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 35);
  freeze(sheet, 2);
}


// ─── SHEET: PROFILE ─────────────────────────────────────────
function buildProfileSheet(sheet, ss) {
  setWidths(sheet, [220, 280, 200, 180, 180, 180]);
  sheet.setTabColor(C.navy);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '👤  PATIENT PROFILE — Complete this first', 6, C.navy);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 6).merge()
    .setValue('Your profile personalises all AI guidance. Year of Birth and Biological Sex are most important. Medications and supplements are logged in detail in 💊 Meds & Supplements Log.')
    .setBackground(C.tealLight).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 35);

  // Fields: [label, default, note]
  var fields = [
    ['Year of Birth',           '', 'e.g. 1975 — used to personalise thresholds for age'],
    ['Biological Sex',          '', 'Male or Female — affects many reference ranges'],
    ['Menopausal Status',       '', 'Pre-menopausal / Peri-menopausal / Post-menopausal / N/A'],
    ['Smoker?',                 '', 'Yes / No / Ex-smoker'],
    ['Alcohol',                 '', 'None / Low (1–7 units/wk) / Medium (8–14 units/wk) / High (15+ units/wk)  —  1 pint beer/cider ≈ 2 units'],
    ['Ethnicity (optional)',    '', 'Some thresholds vary by ethnicity (e.g. Vit D, HbA1c)'],
    ['Height (cm)',             '', 'Used to calculate BMI'],
    ['Weight (kg)',             '', 'Used to calculate BMI'],
    ['GP Surgery',              '', 'Name of your GP surgery'],
    ['Home Town (optional)',    '', 'Town name only — used for distance banding when searching private consultants. No postcode needed.'],
  ];

  // Clear any stale validation from a previous build
  sheet.getRange(3, 2, 20, 1).clearDataValidations();

  var r = 3;
  // Track row positions dynamically so dropdowns and formulas always target the right field
  var rowSex = -1, rowMenopause = -1, rowSmoker = -1, rowAlcohol = -1;
  var rowHeight = -1, rowWeight = -1;

  for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(fields[i][0]).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9)
      .setBackground(C.offWhite).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    var inputCell = sheet.getRange(r, 2);
    inputCell.setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontSize(9)
      .setNote(fields[i][2]).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22);
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Biological Sex')    rowSex       = r;
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Menopausal Status') rowMenopause = r;
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Smoker?')           rowSmoker    = r;
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Alcohol')           rowAlcohol   = r;
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Height (cm)')     { rowHeight    = r; inputCell.setNumberFormat('0.##'); }
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Weight (kg)')     { rowWeight    = r; inputCell.setNumberFormat('0.##'); }
    if (fields[i][0] === 'Year of Birth')     inputCell.setNumberFormat('0');
    r++;
  }

  // Dropdowns — use dynamically tracked rows, never hardcoded numbers
  if (rowSex       > 0) addDropdown(sheet, rowSex,       2, ['Male', 'Female']);
  if (rowMenopause > 0) addDropdown(sheet, rowMenopause, 2, ['Pre-menopausal', 'Peri-menopausal', 'Post-menopausal', 'N/A']);
if (rowSmoker    > 0) addDropdown(sheet, rowSmoker,    2, ['Yes', 'No', 'Ex-smoker', 'Vaper', 'Ex-vaper']);
  if (rowAlcohol   > 0) addDropdown(sheet, rowAlcohol,   2, ['None', 'Low (1–7 units/wk)', 'Medium (8–14 units/wk)', 'High (15+ units/wk)']);

  // ── BMI section ────────────────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 6).merge()
    .setValue('BMI  (auto-calculated)')
    .setBackground(C.teal).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 24); r++;

  // Calculated BMI value — uses dynamically tracked height/weight rows
  sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue('Calculated BMI').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setBackground(C.offWhite).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  var bmiFormula = (rowHeight > 0 && rowWeight > 0)
    ? '=IF(AND(ISNUMBER(VALUE(B'+rowHeight+')),ISNUMBER(VALUE(B'+rowWeight+'))),ROUND(VALUE(B'+rowWeight+')/((VALUE(B'+rowHeight+')/100)^2),1),"Enter height & weight above")'
    : '"Enter height & weight above"';
  sheet.getRange(r, 2).setFormula(bmiFormula).setBackground(C.greenLight).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor(C.emerald).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 24);
  var bmiRow = r;
  r++;

  // BMI category — B13 is already a number (output of ROUND), so no VALUE() needed
  // Avoid emoji and en-dashes in formula strings — they cause #ERROR in setFormula()
  sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue('BMI Category').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setBackground(C.offWhite).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.getRange(r, 2).setFormula(
    '=IFERROR(IF(B' + bmiRow + '="Enter height & weight above","Enter height & weight",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<16,"Severely underweight",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<18.5,"Underweight",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<25,"Healthy weight",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<30,"Overweight",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<35,"Obese Class I",' +
    'IF(B' + bmiRow + '<40,"Obese Class II","Severely obese"))))))),"Enter height & weight")'
  ).setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22); r++;

  // Healthy BMI range note
  sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue('Healthy BMI Range').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setBackground(C.offWhite).setVerticalAlignment('top');
  sheet.getRange(r, 2, 1, 5).merge()
    .setValue(
      'NHS / WHO healthy range: 18.5 – 24.9\n' +
      'Overweight: 25.0 – 29.9  |  Obese: 30.0+\n' +
      'Note: BMI has limitations — it does not distinguish muscle from fat, and thresholds differ for some ethnicities (e.g. South Asian populations: healthy BMI 18.5–22.9, overweight 23–27.4).\n' +
      'For a more accurate picture, waist circumference is also useful: healthy <80 cm (women) or <94 cm (men).'
    )
    .setBackground('#f0f9ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey).setVerticalAlignment('top');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 65); r++;

  freeze(sheet, 2);
}


// ─── SHEET: TESTRESULTS ─────────────────────────────────────
function buildTestResultsSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#dc2626');
  sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 88); sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 90); sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 220);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(4, 180); sheet.setColumnWidth(5, 75); sheet.setColumnWidth(6, 75);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(7, 110); sheet.setColumnWidth(8, 90); sheet.setColumnWidth(9, 220); sheet.setColumnWidth(10, 55);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '🩸  TEST RESULTS — Paste from NHS SAR export. Matches format exactly.', 10, C.navy);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 10).merge().setValue('Matches NHS SAR TestResults columns. Paste rows directly. UniqueKey auto-generates.')
    .setBackground(C.sarHeader).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 30);
  colHeaders(sheet, 3, ['UniqueKey','date','panel','analyte','value','unit','ref_range','status','doctor','page'], C.sarHeader, C.darkGrey);
  var keyFormula = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(IF(LEN(B5:B2000)=0,"",LOWER(IFERROR(TEXT(B5:B2000,"yyyymmdd"),TEXT(DATEVALUE(B5:B2000),"yyyymmdd"))&"|"&TRIM(D5:D2000)&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(E5:E2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(F5:F2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(G5:G2000),"")))),")';
  sheet.getRange(4, 1).setFormula(keyFormula).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setNote('Auto-generated — do not edit.');
  sheet.setRowHeight(4, 16);
  sheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).setFontSize(9).setWrap(false).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.getRange(5, 2, 1996, 1).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
  sheet.getRange(5, 5, 1996, 1).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
  for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) { sheet.getRange(5+i, 2, 1, 9).setBackground(i%2===0 ? C.white : C.sarAlt); sheet.setRowHeight(5+i, 20); }
  freeze(sheet, 3);
  try { ss.setNamedRange('TestResultsData', sheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10)); } catch(e) {}
}


// ─── SHEET: RECORDS ─────────────────────────────────────────
function buildRecordsSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor(C.teal);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 88); sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 90); sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 125);
  sheet.setColumnWidth(4, 280); sheet.setColumnWidth(5, 420); sheet.setColumnWidth(6, 200); sheet.setColumnWidth(7, 55);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '📋  RECORDS — Consultations, Referrals, Imaging & Notes. Matches SAR format.', 7, C.navy);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 7).merge().setValue('Matches NHS SAR Records sheet — paste rows directly.')
    .setBackground(C.sarHeader).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 25);
  colHeaders(sheet, 3, ['UniqueKey','date','record_type','title','details','doctor','page'], C.sarHeader, C.darkGrey);
  var recKeyFormula = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(IF(LEN(B5:B2000)=0,"",LOWER(IFERROR(TEXT(B5:B2000,"yyyymmdd"),TEXT(DATEVALUE(B5:B2000),"yyyymmdd"))&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(C5:C2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(TRIM(D5:D2000),"")&"|"&IFERROR(LEFT(TRIM(E5:E2000),80),"")))),")';
  sheet.getRange(4, 1).setFormula(recKeyFormula).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setNote('Auto-generated — do not edit.');
  sheet.setRowHeight(4, 16);
  sheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 7).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
  sheet.getRange(5, 2, 1996, 1).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
  for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) { sheet.getRange(5+i, 2, 1, 6).setBackground(i%2===0 ? C.white : C.sarAlt); sheet.setRowHeight(5+i, 20); }
  sheet.getRange(5, 3, 1996, 1).setDataValidation(
    SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(['Consultation','Referral form','Imaging report','Test Panel','Test Request','Read code','Letter','Other'],true).setAllowInvalid(true).build());
  freeze(sheet, 3);
}


// ─── SHEET: MEDICATION LOG ───────────────────────────────────
function buildMedicationLogSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#7c3aed');
  setWidths(sheet, [200, 110, 90, 80, 90, 90, 90, 180, 180, 380]);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '💊  MEDICATION & SUPPLEMENT LOG — Log everything here so the AI can factor it in', 10, C.purple);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 10).merge()
    .setValue('Add ALL medications AND supplements. Column J: AI Analysis — run 🏥 → Analyse Supplements & Medications to get personalised AI assessment of each against your test results and profile. ' +
              'Total Daily Dose auto-calculates. Leave "Date Stopped" blank if still taking.')
    .setBackground(C.purpleLight).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 40);
  colHeaders(sheet, 3, ['Medication / Supplement','Type','Dose (mg/mcg/IU etc)','Times Per Day','Total Daily Dose','Date Started','Date Stopped','Prescribed By','Notes / Reason','🤖 AI Analysis (personalised)'], C.purple, '#ffffff');
  // Style col J header differently — it's AI-generated
  sheet.getRange(3, 10).setBackground('#4c1d95').setFontColor('#ddd6fe');
  // Row 4: invisible helper — holds ARRAYFORMULA for Total Daily Dose in col E only.
  var doseFml = '=IFERROR(ARRAYFORMULA(IF(ISNUMBER(C5:C500),IF(ISNUMBER(D5:D500),C5:C500*D5:D500,"add times/day"),"")),"")';
  sheet.getRange(4, 5).setFormula(doseFml).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey)
    .setNote('Auto-calculates Total Daily Dose. Do not edit.');
  sheet.getRange(4, 1, 1, 10).setBackground('#f3e8ff');
  sheet.setRowHeight(4, 12);

  // Type dropdown
  sheet.getRange(5, 2, 496, 1).setDataValidation(
    SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(
      ['Prescription medication','Over-the-counter medication','Vitamin supplement','Mineral supplement','Herbal supplement','Probiotic','Other supplement'],true)
    .setAllowInvalid(true).build());

  for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
    var bg = i%2===0 ? '#faf5ff' : '#ffffff';
    sheet.getRange(5+i, 1, 1, 9).setBackground(bg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(5+i, 10).setBackground('#f5f3ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top')
      .setValue(i === 0 ? '[ Run 🏥 menu → Analyse Supplements & Medications ]' : '');
    sheet.setRowHeight(5+i, 22);
  }
  sheet.getRange(5, 6, 496, 2).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
  sheet.getRange(5, 3, 496, 2).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
  sheet.getRange(5, 5, 496, 1).setBackground('#e9d5ff').setHorizontalAlignment('center').setFontWeight('bold');

  borders(sheet, 3, 1, 1, 10);
  freeze(sheet, 3);
}


// ─── SHEET: SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION ───────────────────────────
function buildSymptomInvestigationSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#b45309');
  setWidths(sheet, [220, 620]);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '🔍  SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION — AI-powered symptom analysis', 2, C.orange);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 2).merge()
    .setValue('Enter your symptoms below and run the AI analysis. The AI will consider ALL your test results, research thresholds, and medication/supplement log. ' +
              'After your GP/consultant appointment, enter their advice to get an AI assessment of it. At any point, generate a professional email.')
    .setBackground(C.orangeLight).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.orange);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 40);

  var fields = [
    ['STEP 1 — ENTER YOUR SYMPTOMS', null, '#b45309', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['Record ID (auto-assigned on save)', 'Leave blank — auto-fills on save', C.offWhite, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['Date of Investigation', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['Your Symptoms (describe fully)', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 80],
    ['How long have you had these symptoms?', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['Severity (1–10)', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['Any recent changes (diet, stress, illness, new meds)?', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 35],
    ['STEP 2 — AI ANALYSIS', null, '#b45309', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['AI Assessment of Symptoms', '[ Run AI Analysis from 🏥 menu ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 120],
    ['Likely Contributing Factors', '[ Auto-populated by AI ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 80],
    ['Suggested Tests / Investigations', '[ Auto-populated by AI ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 60],
    ['AI Confidence Level (pre-GP)', '[ Auto-populated by AI ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 20],
    ['STEP 3 — EMAIL (PRE-GP)', null, '#065f46', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['Email to GP (pre-appointment)', '[ Click Generate Pre-GP Email from 🏥 menu ]', '#ecfdf5', C.emerald, 120],
    ['STEP 4 — ENTER GP / CONSULTANT ADVICE', null, '#1e40af', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['Date of Appointment', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['GP / Consultant Name', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['GP / Consultant Advice (enter exactly)', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 100],
    ['Tests Ordered (if any)', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 35],
    ['Prescription / Treatment Given', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 35],
    ['STEP 5 — AI RESPONSE TO GP ADVICE', null, '#b45309', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['AI Assessment of GP Advice', '[ Run Analyse GP Advice from 🏥 menu ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 120],
    ['Research Alignment', '[ Auto-populated ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 40],
    ['AI Confidence in GP Advice', '[ Auto-populated ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 20],
    ['Suggested Follow-up / Questions for GP', '[ Auto-populated ]', '#fff7ed', '#92400e', 60],
    ['STEP 6 — EMAIL (POST-GP)', null, '#065f46', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['Email to GP (post-appointment)', '[ Click Generate Post-GP Email from 🏥 menu ]', '#ecfdf5', C.emerald, 120],
    ['STEP 7 — SAVE', null, '#1e293b', '#ffffff', 28],
    ['Status', 'Draft', C.offWhite, C.darkGrey, 20],
    ['Notes / Actions', '', C.inputYellow, C.darkGrey, 50],
  ];

  var r = 3;
  for (var i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
    var f = fields[i];
    if (f[1] === null) {
      // Section header
      sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).merge().setValue(f[0]).setBackground(f[2]).setFontColor(f[3])
        .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    } else {
      sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(f[0]).setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(f[1]).setBackground(f[2]).setFontColor(f[3]).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    }
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, f[4]);
    r++;
  }

  // Status dropdown
  var statusRow = r - 2;
  addDropdown(sheet, statusRow, 2, ['Draft','Active','GP Consulted','Resolved','Monitoring']);

  // Clear button note
  sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).merge()
    .setValue('Use 🏥 Medical Tracker menu: Run AI Analysis | Analyse GP Advice | Generate Pre-GP Email | Generate Post-GP Email | Save Investigation | Load Investigation | Clear Form')
    .setBackground('#1e293b').setFontColor('#e2e8f0').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(r, 30);

  freeze(sheet, 2);
}

// ─── SHEET: TREATMENT TRACKER ───────────────────────────────
// Row map (fixed — functions read/write by row number):
//   3  = section header TREATMENT SETUP
//   4  = SELECT ANALYTE (dropdown — key input)
//   5  = Starting value (auto-populated from latest result)
//   6  = Target value (AI-suggested, overridable)
//   7  = Date treatment started (defaults to today)
//   8  = Supplement / Medication
//   9  = Dose (mg/mcg/IU)
//   10 = Times per day
//   11 = Total daily dose (formula)
//   12 = section header RELEVANT FACTORS
//   13 = Menstrual / hormonal factors (shown/highlighted for iron analytes)
//   14 = Recommended lifestyle / diet (AI-populated)
//   15 = Effect of current supplements / medications (AI-populated)
//   16 = section header AI ANALYSIS
//   17 = AI Trajectory Assessment
//   18 = Expected monthly increment
//   19 = section header PROGRESS TRACKING
//   20 = column headers
//   21–40 = milestone/result rows
//   41 = footer

function buildTreatmentTrackerSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#166534');
  setWidths(sheet, [220, 200, 150, 140, 140, 90, 100, 220]);
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '📈  TREATMENT TRACKER — Monitor treatment progress vs expected trajectory', 8, C.emerald);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('① Select an analyte from the dropdown → ② Click "🏥 Populate Treatment Setup" to auto-fill with AI suggestions → ③ Adjust any values → ④ Generate Trajectory')
    .setBackground(C.emeraldLight).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.emerald)
    .setFontWeight('bold');
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 30);

  // ── SETUP SECTION ─────────────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(3, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('STEP 1 — TREATMENT SETUP  (all fields can be overridden)')
    .setBackground(C.emerald).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(3, 28);

  var setupFields = [
    // row 4
    { label: 'Select Analyte', value: '[ Choose from dropdown → ]', bg: '#bbf7d0', fg: C.emerald, h: 24, bold: true },
    // row 5
    { label: 'Latest Result (auto-populated)', value: '[ Select analyte first ]', bg: C.offWhite, fg: C.midGrey, h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 6
    { label: 'Research Target Value', value: '[ AI-suggested on populate ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 7
    { label: 'Date Treatment Started', value: '', bg: C.inputYellow, fg: C.darkGrey, h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 8
    { label: 'Supplement / Medication', value: '[ AI-suggested on populate ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 9
    { label: 'Dose (mg / mcg / IU)', value: '[ AI-suggested on populate ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 10
    { label: 'Times Per Day', value: '[ AI-suggested on populate ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 22, bold: false },
    // row 11
    { label: 'Total Daily Dose (auto)', formula: '=IF(AND(ISNUMBER(B9),ISNUMBER(B10)),B9&" × "&B10&"/day = "&(B9*B10)&" total","Enter dose and times per day above")', bg: C.greenLight, fg: C.emerald, h: 22, bold: true },
  ];

  var r = 4;
  for (var i = 0; i < setupFields.length; i++) {
    var f = setupFields[i];
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(f.label).setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    if (f.formula) {
      sheet.getRange(r, 2, 1, 7).merge().setFormula(f.formula).setBackground(f.bg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor(f.fg).setFontWeight(f.bold ? 'bold' : 'normal').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    } else {
      sheet.getRange(r, 2, 1, 7).merge().setValue(f.value).setBackground(f.bg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor(f.fg).setFontWeight(f.bold ? 'bold' : 'normal').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    }
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, f.h);
    r++;
  }

  // Date format for row 7
  sheet.getRange(7, 2).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');

  // ── RELEVANT FACTORS SECTION ──────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(12, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('STEP 2 — RELEVANT FACTORS  (review carefully — these affect expected trajectory)')
    .setBackground('#7c3aed').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(12, 28);

  var factorFields = [
    // row 13 — menstrual (highlighted when iron selected)
    { label: 'Menstrual / Hormonal Factors', value: '[ AI-populated — please review and correct ]', bg: '#fef9c3', fg: '#713f12', h: 50,
      note: 'This is CRITICAL for iron/ferritin. Describe your periods: heavy/moderate/light, how many days, how often, or none (e.g. pill, IUD, menopause, pregnant). Also note if on hormonal contraception — some types stop periods entirely.' },
    // row 14 — AI-generated lifestyle/diet recommendations
    { label: 'Recommended Lifestyle / Diet', value: '[ Auto-populated by Generate Trajectory ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 60,
      note: 'AI-generated dietary and lifestyle recommendations specific to this analyte and your profile. e.g. foods to eat/avoid, timing, absorption tips.' },
    // row 15 — AI-generated supplement/medication effect
    { label: 'Effect of Current Supplements / Medications', value: '[ Auto-populated by Generate Trajectory ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 50,
      note: 'How your current medications and supplements interact with this analyte — both helpful and harmful effects. Auto-read from your Meds & Supplements Log.' },
  ];

  r = 13;
  for (var j = 0; j < factorFields.length; j++) {
    var ff = factorFields[j];
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(ff.label).setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setNote(ff.note);
    sheet.getRange(r, 2, 1, 7).merge().setValue(ff.value).setBackground(ff.bg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor(ff.fg).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, ff.h);
    r++;
  }

  // ── AI ANALYSIS SECTION ───────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(16, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('STEP 3 — AI TRAJECTORY ANALYSIS  (generated by "Generate Treatment Trajectory")')
    .setBackground(C.orange).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(16, 28);

  var aiFields = [
    // row 17
    { label: 'AI Trajectory Assessment', value: '[ Click 🏥 menu → Generate Treatment Trajectory ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 100 },
    // row 18
    { label: 'Expected Monthly Increment', value: '[ Auto-populated ]', bg: '#fff7ed', fg: '#92400e', h: 25 },
  ];

  r = 17;
  for (var k = 0; k < aiFields.length; k++) {
    var af = aiFields[k];
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(af.label).setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 2, 1, 7).merge().setValue(af.value).setBackground(af.bg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor(af.fg).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, af.h);
    r++;
  }

  // ── PROGRESS TRACKING TABLE ───────────────────────────────
  // Row 19 = section header, 20 = col headers, 21+ = milestone/result rows
  sheet.getRange(19, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('STEP 4 — PROGRESS TRACKING  (historic results auto-loaded from TestResults | future milestones show expected range)')
    .setBackground(C.emerald).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(19, 28);

  colHeaders(sheet, 20,
    ['Milestone / Date','Target Date','Actual Test Date','Expected Range','Actual Value','Unit','On Track?','AI Comment / Action'],
    C.emerald, '#ffffff');
  sheet.setRowHeight(20, 22);

  // Pre-build 20 rows starting at 21
  for (var mr = 21; mr <= 40; mr++) {
    var mbg = (mr % 2 === 0) ? C.white : C.emeraldLight;
    sheet.getRange(mr, 1, 1, 8).setBackground(mbg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(mr, 2).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
    sheet.getRange(mr, 3).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
    sheet.getRange(mr, 4).setFontColor('#166534').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(mr, 5).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold')
      .setNote('Actual value — auto-loaded from TestResults, or enter manually.');
    sheet.getRange(mr, 7).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.setRowHeight(mr, 22);
  }

  // ── CHART AREA ────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Chart is built dynamically by generateTrackerChart() after data is populated.
  // Row 42 = chart section header, chart embedded below it.
  sheet.getRange(42, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('📊  PROGRESS CHART  (🏥 menu → Treatment Tracker → ⑤ Update Chart)')
    .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(42, 28);

  sheet.getRange(43, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('Chart will appear here after running "⑤ Update Chart" from the 🏥 menu.\nIt shows your actual results vs the expected trajectory over time.')
    .setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#1e40af').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(43, 35);

  // Footer
  sheet.getRange(44, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('🏥 Menu → Treatment Tracker:  ① Add New Tracker  |  ② Populate Setup  |  ③ Generate Trajectory  |  ④ Load Historic Results  |  ⑤ Update Chart  |  ⑥ Assess Progress')
    .setBackground('#1e293b').setFontColor('#e2e8f0').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(44, 28);

  freeze(sheet, 2);
}


// ─── SHEET: ANALYSIS ────────────────────────────────────────
function buildAnalysisSheet(sheet, ss) {
  setWidths(sheet, [160, 95, 85, 95, 120, 100, 95, 160, 280]);
  sheet.setTabColor('#7c3aed');
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '📊  ANALYSIS — All results vs AI research thresholds. See 🚨 Significant Findings for flagged only.', 9, C.navy);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 9).merge()
    .setValue('Shows every analyte. After adding results: 🏥 menu → Refresh Analysis.')
    .setBackground(C.purpleLight).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 25);
  colHeaders(sheet, 3, ['Analyte','Date','Your Value','Unit','Lab Ref Range','Lab Status','Research Assessment','Threshold Band Hit','Research Commentary'], '#6b21a8', '#ffffff');
  sheet.getRange(4, 1, 1, 9).setBackground('#ede9fe').setFontSize(8).setFontColor(C.purple)
    .setValues([['← TestResults','← Date','← Result','← Unit','← Lab range','← Lab status','← AI assessed','← Band hit','← AI commentary + common causes']]);
  sheet.setRowHeight(4, 20);
  freeze(sheet, 4);
  buildAnalysisRows(sheet, ss);
}


// ─── SHEET: SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS ────────────────────────────
// Columns 1-9: core data (built by buildSignificantFindingsRows)
// Columns 10-13: AI significance panel (built by addSignificancePanelToFindings)
//   10 = Significance Rating (1-5 badge)
//   11 = Likely Symptoms for YOU
//   12 = Ease of Correction
//   13 = How to Correct + Expected Outcome
//   14 = Research Links (HYPERLINK formulas — built by buildSignificantFindingsRows)
function buildSignificantFindingsSheet(sheet, ss) {
  sheet.setTabColor('#dc2626');
  setWidths(sheet, [180, 110, 88, 95, 160, 250, 250, 130, 110, 130, 220, 120, 280, 200]);
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 14).merge().setValue('🚨  SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS — Research-flagged results only')
    .setBackground('#7f1d1d').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(13).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(1, 40);
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 14).merge()
    .setValue('Columns A–I: test data and research flags  |  Columns J–M: AI significance panel  |  Column N: Research Links (click to open)  |  🔴 = multiple flags  🟠 = 2  🟡 = 1')
    .setBackground('#fef2f2').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#7f1d1d').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 30);
  freeze(sheet, 2);
  buildSignificantFindingsRows(sheet, ss);
}


// ─── SHARED: BUILD ANALYSIS + SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS ──────────
function buildAnalysisRows(sheet, ss) {
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!trSheet || !thSheet) return;
  var trData  = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
  var thData  = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
  var thMap   = buildThresholdMap(thData);
  var results = collectLatestResults(trData);
  // Run name mapping — never prompt in analysis build (may run from trigger context)
  var resolvedMap = runAnalyteMapping(ss, trData, thMap, false);
  var analysisRow = 5; var rowCount = 0;
  for (var aKey in results) {
    var res = results[aKey];
    // Skip wellness/lifestyle metrics — not useful in clinical analysis tab
    // These are blocked from Significant Findings but may still pass
    // isClinicalResult if they have numeric values with % units
    var ANALYSIS_SKIP = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score',
                         'nutrition score','mind (stress)','work/life','sleep score',
                         'body fat','total body water','resting heart rate',
                         'mind score','activity score','stress score'];
    var skipThis = false;
    var resLower = res.analyte.toLowerCase();
    for (var sk = 0; sk < ANALYSIS_SKIP.length; sk++) {
      if (resLower.indexOf(ANALYSIS_SKIP[sk]) >= 0) { skipThis = true; break; }
    }
    if (skipThis) continue;

    var thRows = resolveThresholdRows(res.analyte, thMap, resolvedMap);
    var parts  = assessResult(res, thRows);
    var dateDisplay = formatDate(res.date);
    var rowBg    = rowCount%2===0 ? '#f8f9ff' : '#ffffff';
    var assessBg = parts.assessment.indexOf('⚠️')>=0 ? '#fff7ed' : parts.assessment.indexOf('✅')>=0 ? '#f0fdf4' : '#f8fafc';
    var assessFg = parts.assessment.indexOf('⚠️')>=0 ? '#c2410c' : parts.assessment.indexOf('✅')>=0 ? C.green : C.midGrey;
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,1).setValue(res.analyte).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,2).setValue(dateDisplay).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,3).setValue(res.value).setBackground('#fffde7').setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold').setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,4).setValue(res.unit).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,5).setValue(res.refRange||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,6).setValue(res.status||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,7).setValue(parts.assessment).setBackground(assessBg).setFontColor(assessFg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,8).setValue(parts.bandHit).setBackground(assessBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor(assessFg);
    sheet.getRange(analysisRow,9).setValue(parts.commentary).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    var lc = parts.commentary.split('\n').length;
    sheet.setRowHeight(analysisRow, Math.max(40, Math.min(200, lc*13)));
    analysisRow++; rowCount++;
    if (rowCount % 20 === 0) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }
  if (rowCount===0) {
    sheet.getRange(5,1,1,9).merge().setValue('No numeric results found. Paste SAR data then run 🏥 → Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI.')
      .setBackground(C.amberLight).setFontSize(10).setWrap(true);
  }
}

function buildSignificantFindingsRows(sheet, ss) {
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!trSheet || !thSheet) { sheet.getRange(3,1,1,9).merge().setValue('Sheets not found. Run buildMedicalTracker first.').setBackground('#fee2e2').setFontSize(9); return; }
  var trData  = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
  var thData  = thSheet.getRange(4,1,2000,10).getValues();
  var thMap   = buildThresholdMap(thData);
  var latestRes = collectLatestResults(trData);
  var allByKey  = collectAllResults(trData);

  var resolvedMap = runAnalyteMapping(ss, trData, thMap, false);

  var suppInteractions = {};
  var suppIntDate = '';
  try {
    var stored = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS');
    if (stored) suppInteractions = JSON.parse(stored);
    suppIntDate = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS_DATE') || '';
  } catch(e) {}

  var quickInteractions = buildQuickSupplementInteractions(ss);

  var flagged = [];
  for (var aKey in latestRes) {
    var res = latestRes[aKey];
    var thRows = resolveThresholdRows(res.analyte, thMap, resolvedMap);
    if (!thRows) continue;
    var triggered = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) { if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) triggered.push(thRows[b]); }
    if (triggered.length > 0) flagged.push({ res: res, bands: triggered, history: allByKey[aKey]||[] });
  }

  var researchFlaggedKeys = {};
  for (var fi = 0; fi < flagged.length; fi++) researchFlaggedKeys[flagged[fi].res.analyte.toLowerCase()] = true;

  var SF_SKIP = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score',
                 'nutrition score','mind (stress)','work/life','sleep score',
                 'body fat','total body water','resting heart rate',
                 'mind score','activity score','stress score','height','weight'];

  var labFlagged = [];
  for (var lKey in latestRes) {
    var lRes = latestRes[lKey];
    var lNameLower = lRes.analyte.toLowerCase();
    var skipLab = false;
    for (var sk = 0; sk < SF_SKIP.length; sk++) {
      if (lNameLower.indexOf(SF_SKIP[sk]) >= 0) { skipLab = true; break; }
    }
    if (skipLab) continue;
    var labStat = (lRes.status || '').toString().toLowerCase().trim();
    var isLabAbnormal = labStat === 'abnormal' || labStat === 'high' || labStat === 'low' ||
                        labStat === 'h' || labStat === 'l' || labStat === 'above range' || labStat === 'below range';
    var lCleanKey = cleanAnalyteName(lRes.analyte).toLowerCase();
    if (isLabAbnormal && !researchFlaggedKeys[lCleanKey] && !researchFlaggedKeys[lKey]) {
      labFlagged.push({ res: lRes, history: allByKey[lKey]||[] });
    }
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow > 2) sheet.getRange(3, 1, Math.max(lastRow-2,1), 14).clearContent().clearFormat();

  if (flagged.length === 0 && labFlagged.length === 0) {
    sheet.getRange(3,1,1,9).merge().setValue('🎉  No significant findings — no research threshold flags triggered and no lab abnormals.\nIf you just added data: 🏥 menu → Refresh Analysis')
      .setBackground('#f0fdf4').setFontColor('#166534').setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.setRowHeight(3, 80); return;
  }

  flagged.sort(function(a,b){ return b.bands.length - a.bands.length; });
  var cr = 3;

  var summaryParts = [];
  if (flagged.length > 0) summaryParts.push('⚠️  ' + flagged.length + ' research threshold flag(s)');
  if (labFlagged.length > 0) summaryParts.push('🔵  ' + labFlagged.length + ' lab-flagged result(s)');
  sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge()
    .setValue(summaryParts.join('   |   ') + '   |   Columns A–I: data  |  J–M: AI significance  |  N: Research Links')
    .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

  var hdrs = ['Analyte','Your Value','Date','Lab Status','Threshold(s) Hit','What It Means','Common Causes / Context','Evidence','Trend (hover for all values)',
              'Significance Rating','Likely Symptoms for YOU','Ease of Fix','How to Correct + Timeline','📖 Research Links'];
  for (var h = 0; h < hdrs.length; h++) {
    var hBg = h >= 9 && h < 13 ? '#7c2d12' : h === 13 ? '#1e40af' : '#7f1d1d';
    sheet.getRange(cr, h+1).setValue(hdrs[h]).setBackground(hBg).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  }
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

  for (var f = 0; f < flagged.length; f++) {
    var item = flagged[f]; var res = item.res; var bands = item.bands;
var history = item.history.sort(function(a,b){
  var da = a.date instanceof Date ? a.date : new Date(a.date);
  var db = b.date instanceof Date ? b.date : new Date(b.date);
  return da - db;
});
    var severity = bands.length>=3?'high':bands.length===2?'medium':'low';
    var rowBg = severity==='high'?'#fef2f2':severity==='medium'?'#fff7ed':'#fefce8';
    var valBg = severity==='high'?'#fca5a5':severity==='medium'?'#fdba74':'#fde68a';
    var valFg = severity==='high'?'#7f1d1d':severity==='medium'?'#7c2d12':'#78350f';
    var threshText  = bands.map(function(b){ return '• '+b[1]; }).join('\n');
    var meaningText = bands.map(function(b){ return '▸ '+b[1]+':\n'+b[3]; }).join('\n\n');
    var causesText  = bands.map(function(b){ return b[9]||''; }).filter(function(c){ return c.length>0; }).join('\n\n');
    if (!causesText) causesText = 'Run AI refresh to generate common cause explanations.';

    var suppNotes = [];
    var analyteLower = res.analyte.toLowerCase();
    for (var qi = 0; qi < quickInteractions.length; qi++) {
      var qint = quickInteractions[qi];
      if (qint.analytes.some(function(a) { return analyteLower.indexOf(a) >= 0 || a.indexOf(analyteLower) >= 0; })) {
        suppNotes.push('💊 ' + qint.supplement + ': ' + qint.note);
      }
    }
    for (var suppName in suppInteractions) {
      var intText = suppInteractions[suppName].toLowerCase();
      if (intText.indexOf(analyteLower) >= 0 || intText.indexOf(res.analyte.toLowerCase()) >= 0) {
        suppNotes.push('🤖 ' + suppName + ' (AI): ' + suppInteractions[suppName].substring(0, 180));
      }
    }
    if (suppNotes.length > 0) {
      causesText = (causesText && causesText.indexOf('Run AI') < 0 ? causesText + '\n\n' : '') +
                   '── YOUR SUPPLEMENTS MAY BE A FACTOR ──\n' + suppNotes.join('\n') +
                   (suppIntDate ? '\n(AI supplement analysis: ' + suppIntDate + ')' : '\n(Run Analyse Supplements for full AI assessment)');
    }
    var evidText    = bands.map(function(b){ return (b[5]||'')+'\n'+(b[6]||''); }).join('\n---\n');
    var statusBg    = (res.status||'').toLowerCase()==='normal'?'#dcfce7':(res.status||'').toLowerCase()==='abnormal'?'#fecaca':rowBg;

    sheet.getRange(cr,1).setValue(res.analyte).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,2).setValue(res.value+' '+res.unit).setBackground(valBg).setFontColor(valFg).setFontSize(11).setFontWeight('bold').setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.getRange(cr,3).setValue(formatDate(res.date)).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,4).setValue(res.status||'Not recorded').setBackground(statusBg).setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,5).setValue(threshText).setBackground(valBg).setFontColor(valFg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(cr,6).setValue(meaningText).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,7).setValue(causesText).setBackground('#fffbeb').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#78350f');
    sheet.getRange(cr,8).setValue(evidText).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e40af');

    // ── Trend cell: sparkline + hover note listing all dated values ──────
    if (history.length >= 2) {
      var vals = history.map(function(h){ return h.value; });
      var sparkColor = vals[vals.length-1] <= vals[0] ? 'red' : 'green';
      var trendCell = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
      trendCell.setFormula('=SPARKLINE({'+vals.join(',')+'},{\"charttype\",\"line\";\"color\",\"'+sparkColor+'\";\"linewidth\",2})').setBackground(rowBg).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      // Build hover note: count + each dated result
      var histLines = history.map(function(h){
        return formatDate(h.date) + ':  ' + h.value + (h.unit ? ' ' + h.unit : '');
      }).join('\n');
      trendCell.setNote(history.length + ' result(s) captured:\n\n' + histLines);
    } else if (history.length === 1) {
      var trendCell1 = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
      trendCell1.setValue('1 result\n' + formatDate(history[0].date) + ': ' + history[0].value + ' ' + (history[0].unit||''))
        .setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      trendCell1.setNote('Only 1 result found — need 2+ for trend chart.');
    } else {
      sheet.getRange(cr,9).setValue('No history').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    }

    sheet.getRange(cr,10).setValue('[ Run Generate AI Significance Ratings ]').setBackground('#fff1f2').setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9f1239').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.getRange(cr,11).setBackground('#fff1f2');
    sheet.getRange(cr,12).setBackground('#fff1f2');
    sheet.getRange(cr,13).setBackground('#fff1f2');

    var analyteSafe = res.analyte.replace(/"/g, '').replace(/'/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
    var analyteForSearch = encodeURIComponent(analyteSafe);
    var profileCtx = '';
    try {
      var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\u{1F464} Profile');
      if (profSheet) {
        var pVals = profSheet.getRange(3,2,9,1).getValues();
        if (pVals[1][0]) profileCtx = '+' + encodeURIComponent(pVals[1][0].toString().toLowerCase());
        if (pVals[2][0]) profileCtx += '+' + encodeURIComponent(pVals[2][0].toString().toLowerCase());
      }
    } catch(e) {}
    var pubmedUrl    = 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=' + analyteForSearch + '+deficiency+symptoms' + profileCtx + '&filter=dates.2015-2025&sort=relevance';
    var labTestsUrl  = 'https://labtestsonline.org.uk/search?q=' + analyteForSearch;
    var niceUrl      = 'https://www.nice.org.uk/search?q=' + analyteForSearch;
    var patientUrl   = 'https://patient.info/search/results?q=' + analyteForSearch.replace(/%20/g, '+') + '+low';
    var linkCell = sheet.getRange(cr, 14);
    linkCell.setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    var richText = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue()
      .setText('📚 Lab Tests Online\n📖 PubMed Research\n🏥 NICE Guidelines\n👤 Patient.info\n🔍 Deep-dive (use menu)')
      .setLinkUrl(0,  19, labTestsUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(20, 38, pubmedUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(39, 57, niceUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(58, 73, patientUrl)
      .build();
    linkCell.setRichTextValue(richText);

    var maxLines = Math.max(meaningText.split('\n').length, causesText.split('\n').length, threshText.split('\n').length);
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, Math.max(80, Math.min(200, maxLines*14)));
    sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).setBorder(false,false,true,false,false,false,'#dc2626',SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_MEDIUM);
    cr++;
    if (f % 10 === 9) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  // ── Section 2: Lab-flagged results ───────────────────────────────────
  if (labFlagged.length > 0) {
    cr++;
    sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge()
      .setValue('🔵  LAB-FLAGGED RESULTS — Marked abnormal by your lab, but within research "functional" thresholds  |  These are often minor deviations. AI Significance Ratings will explain what they typically mean.')
      .setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#bfdbfe').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 30); cr++;

    var hdrs2 = ['Analyte','Your Value','Date','Lab Status','Lab Reference Range','What Abnormal Typically Means','Context & Possible Causes','Evidence','Trend (hover for all values)',
                'Significance Rating','Likely Symptoms for YOU','Ease of Fix','How to Correct + Timeline','📖 Research Links'];
    for (var h2 = 0; h2 < hdrs2.length; h2++) {
      var hBg2 = h2 >= 9 && h2 < 13 ? '#1e3a5f' : h2 === 13 ? '#1e40af' : '#1e3a5f';
      sheet.getRange(cr, h2+1).setValue(hdrs2[h2]).setBackground(hBg2).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    }
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

    for (var lf = 0; lf < labFlagged.length; lf++) {
      var lItem   = labFlagged[lf];
      var lRes    = lItem.res;
   var lHistory = lItem.history.sort(function(a,b){
  var da = a.date instanceof Date ? a.date : new Date(a.date);
  var db = b.date instanceof Date ? b.date : new Date(b.date);
  return da - db;
});
      var lStatus  = (lRes.status || '').toString();
      var rowBg2   = lf % 2 === 0 ? '#eff6ff' : '#f0f9ff';
      var valBg2   = '#bfdbfe';
      var valFg2   = '#1e3a5f';

      var lStatLow  = lStatus.toLowerCase();
      var direction = lStatLow === 'low' || lStatLow === 'l' || lStatLow === 'below range' ? 'low' :
                      lStatLow === 'high' || lStatLow === 'h' || lStatLow === 'above range' ? 'high' : 'abnormal';
      var defaultMeaning = direction === 'low'
        ? 'Your result is below the lab\'s reference range. The lab reference range reflects the middle 95% of a healthy population — being just outside it is common and may not require action, but is worth monitoring. Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.'
        : direction === 'high'
        ? 'Your result is above the lab\'s reference range. Being just above the upper limit is often a minor deviation — context matters (e.g. timing, hydration, recent activity). Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.'
        : 'Your lab marked this result as abnormal. This may be a minor deviation that requires no action, or may be worth discussing with your GP. Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.';

      var lAnalyteSafe  = lRes.analyte.replace(/"/g,'').replace(/'/g,'').toLowerCase().trim();
      var lSearchEnc    = encodeURIComponent(lAnalyteSafe);

      var lSuppNotes = [];
      var lAnalyteLower = lRes.analyte.toLowerCase();
      for (var lqi = 0; lqi < quickInteractions.length; lqi++) {
        var lqint = quickInteractions[lqi];
        if (lqint.analytes.some(function(a) { return lAnalyteLower.indexOf(a) >= 0 || a.indexOf(lAnalyteLower) >= 0; })) {
          lSuppNotes.push('💊 ' + lqint.supplement + ': ' + lqint.note);
        }
      }
      var lCausesText = direction === 'low'
        ? '• Mild lows can occur from recent diet changes, hydration, time of day, or slight natural variation\n• May resolve on retest\n• If persists, worth mentioning to GP'
        : '• Mild highs can be transient — e.g. dehydration (albumin, haematocrit), recent exercise (CK, LDH), timing (cortisol, glucose)\n• If isolated and minor, often not clinically significant\n• If persists on retest, discuss with GP';
      if (lSuppNotes.length > 0) lCausesText += '\n\n── YOUR SUPPLEMENTS MAY BE A FACTOR ──\n' + lSuppNotes.join('\n');

      sheet.getRange(cr,1).setValue(lRes.analyte).setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,2).setValue(lRes.value+' '+(lRes.unit||'')).setBackground(valBg2).setFontColor(valFg2).setFontSize(11).setFontWeight('bold').setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      sheet.getRange(cr,3).setValue(lRes.date ? formatDate(lRes.date) : '').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,4).setValue(lStatus || 'Abnormal').setBackground('#bfdbfe').setFontColor('#1e3a5f').setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setFontWeight('bold');
      sheet.getRange(cr,5).setValue(lRes.refRange || 'See lab report').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
      sheet.getRange(cr,6).setValue(defaultMeaning).setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,7).setValue(lCausesText).setBackground('#fef9c3').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#78350f');
      sheet.getRange(cr,8).setValue('Run AI Significance Ratings for evidence-based assessment').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e40af');

      // ── Trend cell for lab-flagged: sparkline + hover note ────────────
      if (lHistory.length >= 2) {
        var lVals = lHistory.map(function(h){ return h.value; });
        var lSparkColor = lVals[lVals.length-1] <= lVals[0] ? 'red' : 'green';
        var lTrendCell = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
        lTrendCell.setFormula('=SPARKLINE({'+lVals.join(',')+'},{\"charttype\",\"line\";\"color\",\"'+lSparkColor+'\";\"linewidth\",2})').setBackground(rowBg2).setVerticalAlignment('top');
        var lHistLines = lHistory.map(function(h){
          return formatDate(h.date) + ':  ' + h.value + (h.unit ? ' ' + h.unit : '');
        }).join('\n');
        lTrendCell.setNote(lHistory.length + ' result(s) captured:\n\n' + lHistLines);
      } else if (lHistory.length === 1) {
        var lTrendCell1 = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
        lTrendCell1.setValue('1 result\n' + formatDate(lHistory[0].date) + ': ' + lHistory[0].value + ' ' + (lHistory[0].unit||''))
          .setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9ca3af').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
        lTrendCell1.setNote('Only 1 result found — need 2+ for trend chart.');
      } else {
        sheet.getRange(cr,9).setValue('Need 2+ results').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9ca3af').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      }

      sheet.getRange(cr,10).setValue('[ Run Generate AI Significance Ratings ]').setBackground('#dbeafe').setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#1e3a5f').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      sheet.getRange(cr,11).setBackground('#dbeafe');
      sheet.getRange(cr,12).setBackground('#dbeafe');
      sheet.getRange(cr,13).setBackground('#dbeafe');

      var lLinkCell = sheet.getRange(cr, 14);
      lLinkCell.setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      var lRichText = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue()
        .setText('📚 Lab Tests Online\n📖 PubMed Research\n🏥 NICE Guidelines\n👤 Patient.info')
        .setLinkUrl(0,  19, 'https://labtestsonline.org.uk/search?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .setLinkUrl(20, 38, 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=' + lSearchEnc + '&filter=dates.2015-2025&sort=relevance')
        .setLinkUrl(39, 57, 'https://www.nice.org.uk/search?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .setLinkUrl(58, 73, 'https://patient.info/search/results?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .build();
      lLinkCell.setRichTextValue(lRichText);

      sheet.setRowHeight(cr, Math.max(70, Math.min(180, defaultMeaning.split('\n').length * 14 + 30)));
      sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).setBorder(false,false,true,false,false,false,'#3b82f6',SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_MEDIUM);
      cr++;
      if (lf % 10 === 9) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    }
  }

  cr++;
  sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge().setValue('KEY: 🔴 Multiple research flags | 🟠 2 flags | 🟡 1 flag | 🔵 Lab abnormal only  |  Significance: 🔴 5/5 Urgent → 🟢 1/5 Low urgency  |  Trend column: hover cell for full result history')
    .setBackground('#1e293b').setFontColor('#e2e8f0').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 35);
}



// ─── SHEET: THRESHOLDS ──────────────────────────────────────
function buildThresholdsSheet(sheet, ss) {
  setWidths(sheet, [170,120,110,250,250,150,200,120,180,280]);
  sheet.setTabColor('#6b21a8');
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '🔬  THRESHOLDS — All AI-generated. Run 🏥 Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI to populate.', 10, '#6b21a8');
  sheet.getRange(2,1,1,10).merge()
    .setValue('⚠️  STARTS EMPTY — intentional.\nRun: 🏥 Medical Tracker → Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI\nAI reads your analytes from TestResults and generates thresholds + common causes for all of them.')
    .setBackground(C.purpleLight).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 70);
  colHeaders(sheet, 3, ['Analyte','Threshold Value','Direction','Clinical Meaning','Symptoms if Outside','Confidence / Evidence','Source / Reference','Last Updated','Update Method','Common Causes / Context'], '#6b21a8', '#ffffff');
  sheet.setRowHeight(3, 28);
  sheet.getRange(4,1,1,10).merge().setValue('[ Empty — run AI refresh to populate ]').setBackground('#faf5ff').setFontColor(C.midGrey).setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(4, 30);
  freeze(sheet, 3);
}


// ─── SHEET: REFERENCE GUIDE ─────────────────────────────────
function buildReferenceGuide(sheet, ss) {
  setWidths(sheet, [190, 560]);
  sheet.setTabColor('#1e40af');
  sectionBanner(sheet, 1, '📚  REFERENCE GUIDE — Design Philosophy & Technical Detail', 2, C.navy);
  var sections = [
    ['NO HARDCODED CLINICAL DATA',
     'Zero hardcoded medical thresholds. All guidance AI-generated at refresh time from current evidence.\nCovers EVERY analyte in YOUR data — not a predetermined list.'],
    ['SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION MODULE',
     'The 🔍 Symptom Investigation sheet:\n• Reads all your test results and threshold flags\n• Reads your 💊 Meds & Supplements Log\n• Reads your Profile\n• Sends all of this as context to GPT-4o\n• AI analyses whether your symptoms are consistent with your results\n• AI assesses whether GP advice aligns with published evidence\n• Generates professional emails in both plain text and formal letter format'],
    ['TREATMENT TRACKER',
     'When an analyte is flagged and treatment started (e.g. iron for low ferritin):\n• AI models expected trajectory based on dose, absorption, and individual factors (e.g. heavy periods reduce net iron gain)\n• Chart shows expected vs actual at each milestone\n• AI assesses at each actual result whether treatment is effective\n• Suggests dose adjustment or further investigation if not on track'],
    ['THE FUNCTIONAL DEFICIENCY ZONE',
     'Lab ranges define pathology, not optimal health.\n• Ferritin: lab min ~13. Research shows fatigue <30–50 (Brownlie 2002, Verdon BMJ 2003)\n• Vit D: NHS sufficient=50.
