Endocrine Society targets 75–125 (Holick NEJM 2007)\n• TSH: lab 0.35–4.94. Wellbeing studies favour 0.5–2.0 (Larisch 2018)\n• B12: neurological symptoms in 200–350 grey zone (NICE NG239 2023)'],
    ['IMPORTANT CAVEATS',
     '1. Educational tool — not medical advice\n2. Always discuss with GP or specialist\n3. AI may hallucinate — verify citations independently\n4. Individual variation is real\n5. Context matters — ferritin+CRP, cortisol timing, sex hormones+cycle day\n6. Lab ranges differ between labs'],
  ];
  var row = 2;
  for (var i = 0; i < sections.length; i++) {
    sheet.getRange(row,1).setValue(sections[i][0]).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setBackground(i%2===0?C.navy:C.teal).setFontColor('#ffffff').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(row,2).setValue(sections[i][1]).setFontSize(9).setBackground(i%2===0?C.offWhite:C.tealLight).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(row, Math.max(80, sections[i][1].split('\n').length*15));
    row++;
  }
  borders(sheet, 2, 1, sections.length, 2);
}


// ─── AI: THRESHOLDS REFRESH ─────────────────────────────────
// When called standalone: sets up job, shows alert, schedules trigger.
// When called from chain: _setupThresholdJob() does the same without alert.
function refreshThresholdsWithAI() {
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No API key found. Check Script Properties — ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (with AI_PROVIDER=anthropic) or OPENAI_API_KEY (with AI_PROVIDER=openai).'); return; }
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!trSheet || !thSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Required sheets not found. Run buildMedicalTracker first.'); return; }

  _setupThresholdJob(ss);

  var props       = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var analyteList = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES') || '[]');

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '🔄 AI Threshold refresh queued for ' + analyteList.length + ' analyte(s).\n\n' +
    'Running in the background. The Thresholds tab will show progress.\n' +
    'This function will not block — you can keep working.'
  );
}

// ─── DIAGNOSTIC: Show analyte list ───────────────────────────────────────────
// Runs the same logic as _setupThresholdJob but just shows the result.
// Use to debug why an analyte is missing from thresholds.
function showAnalyteList() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!trSheet) { ui.alert('TestResults sheet not found.'); return; }

  var trData = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
  var accepted = [], rejected = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var a = trData[i][3]; var v = trData[i][4]; var u = trData[i][5];
    if (!a) continue;
    a = a.toString().trim();
    u = u ? u.toString().trim() : '';
    if (!a) continue;
    if (isNaN(parseFloat(v))) {
      rejected.push(a + ' [no numeric value]');
      continue;
    }
    if (!isClinicalResult(a, u)) {
      rejected.push(a + ' (unit:"' + u + '") [failed isClinicalResult]');
      continue;
    }
    var cleaned = cleanAnalyteName(a);
    if (!cleaned) {
      rejected.push(a + ' [cleanAnalyteName returned empty]');
      continue;
    }
    // Apply synonym normalisation so duplicate name variants collapse to one
    // canonical entry (same as what happens during analysis)
    cleaned = normaliseSynonyms(cleaned);
    accepted.push(cleaned);
  }

  // Deduplicate
  var seen = {}, unique = [];
  for (var j = 0; j < accepted.length; j++) {
    var k = accepted[j].toLowerCase();
    if (!seen[k]) { seen[k] = true; unique.push(accepted[j]); }
  }
  unique.sort();

  // Check which are missing from Thresholds sheet
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
  var inThresholds = {};
  if (thSheet && thSheet.getLastRow() >= 4) {
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, thSheet.getLastRow() - 3, 1).getValues();
    for (var ti = 0; ti < thData.length; ti++) {
      if (thData[ti][0]) inThresholds[thData[ti][0].toString().toLowerCase().trim()] = true;
    }
  }

  // Also consult the Name Mappings cache — an analyte resolved via AI mapping
  // counts as covered even if the name doesn't exactly match a threshold key.
  var storedMappings = loadMappings(ss);
  // Build a lookup: analyte name lower → { status, mappedTo }
  var mappingInfo = {};
  for (var mk in storedMappings) {
    mappingInfo[mk] = storedMappings[mk];
  }

  // Determine status for each analyte
  var statusMap = {}; // lower → { label, mappedTo, bg }
  var missing = [];
  for (var ui2 = 0; ui2 < unique.length; ui2++) {
    var aLower = unique[ui2].toLowerCase();
    if (inThresholds[aLower]) {
      statusMap[aLower] = { label: '✅ In Thresholds', mappedTo: '', bg: '#dcfce7' };
    } else if (mappingInfo[aLower] && mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo &&
               mappingInfo[aLower].status.indexOf('🚫') < 0 &&
               inThresholds[mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo.toLowerCase()]) {
      // Resolved via Name Mappings cache → threshold exists under a different name
      statusMap[aLower] = { label: '🔗 Mapped → ' + mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo,
                            mappedTo: mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo, bg: '#dbeafe' };
    } else if (mappingInfo[aLower] && mappingInfo[aLower].status.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0) {
      // AI suggested a mapping but it needs review
      statusMap[aLower] = { label: '⚠️ Review mapping → ' + (mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo || '?'),
                            mappedTo: mappingInfo[aLower].mappedTo || '', bg: '#fef9c3' };
      missing.push(unique[ui2]);
    } else {
      statusMap[aLower] = { label: '⚠️ MISSING — no threshold or mapping', mappedTo: '', bg: '#fef9c3' };
      missing.push(unique[ui2]);
    }
  }

  // Show in a temp sheet for readability (alert has character limit)
  var diagSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔍 Analyte Diagnostic');
  if (!diagSheet) diagSheet = ss.insertSheet('🔍 Analyte Diagnostic');
  diagSheet.clear();
  diagSheet.getRange(1,1).setValue('Analyte Diagnostic — ' + new Date().toLocaleString());
  diagSheet.getRange(2,1).setValue('ACCEPTED (' + unique.length + ')');
  diagSheet.getRange(2,2).setValue('Status');
  diagSheet.getRange(2,3).setValue('Resolved to');
  diagSheet.getRange(2,1,1,3).setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#dcfce7');
  for (var ai = 0; ai < unique.length; ai++) {
    var aL = unique[ai].toLowerCase();
    var st = statusMap[aL] || { label: '⚠️ MISSING', mappedTo: '', bg: '#fef9c3' };
    diagSheet.getRange(3+ai, 1).setValue(unique[ai]).setBackground(st.bg);
    diagSheet.getRange(3+ai, 2).setValue(st.label).setBackground(st.bg);
    diagSheet.getRange(3+ai, 3).setValue(st.mappedTo).setBackground(st.bg).setFontColor('#1e40af').setFontSize(8);
  }
  var rRow = 3 + unique.length + 1;
  diagSheet.getRange(rRow, 1).setValue('REJECTED (' + rejected.length + ')').setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#fee2e2');
  for (var ri = 0; ri < Math.min(rejected.length, 50); ri++) {
    diagSheet.getRange(rRow+1+ri, 1).setValue(rejected[ri]).setBackground('#fef2f2');
  }
  diagSheet.setColumnWidth(1, 320); diagSheet.setColumnWidth(2, 220); diagSheet.setColumnWidth(3, 280);
  ss.setActiveSheet(diagSheet);

  var msg = '✅ Diagnostic complete\n\n' + unique.length + ' analytes accepted, ' + rejected.length + ' rejected.';
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    msg += '\n\n⚠️ ' + missing.length + ' analyte(s) have no threshold or mapping:\n' +
           missing.join('\n') +
           '\n\nRun Full Refresh to generate missing thresholds.\nOr check 🔗 Name Mappings tab for stale AI-mapped entries.';
  } else {
    msg += '\n\n✅ All analytes are covered (threshold or mapping).';
  }
  msg += '\n\nSee 🔍 Analyte Diagnostic tab for full detail.';
  ui.alert('Analyte Diagnostic', msg, ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}


// Internal: sets up the threshold job in Script Properties and schedules the trigger.
// Safe to call from a triggered context (no UI alerts).
function _setupThresholdJob(ss) {
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) return;

  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!trSheet) return;

  var trData = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
  var analytes = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var a = trData[i][3]; var v = trData[i][4]; var u = trData[i][5];
    if (!a) continue;
    a = a.toString().trim();
    u = u ? u.toString().trim() : '';
    if (!a || isNaN(parseFloat(v))) continue;
    // Skip non-clinical rows (junk, fitness metrics, appointment notes etc.)
    if (!isClinicalResult(a, u)) continue;
    // Clean the name before using as threshold key — strips embedded values,
    // lab notes, status text that NHS SAR appends to analyte names.
    // This ensures threshold rows are stored under clean matchable keys.
    var cleaned = cleanAnalyteName(a);
    if (!cleaned) continue;
    analytes[cleaned.toLowerCase()] = cleaned;
  }
  var analyteList = []; for (var k in analytes) analyteList.push(analytes[k]); analyteList.sort();
  if (analyteList.length === 0) return;

  var today          = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var profileContext = getProfileContext(ss);

  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperties({
    'TH_JOB_ANALYTES': JSON.stringify(analyteList),
    'TH_JOB_PROFILE':  profileContext,
    'TH_JOB_TODAY':    today,
    'TH_JOB_START':    '0',
    'TH_JOB_RESULTS':  '[]',
    'TH_JOB_ERRORS':   '[]',
    'TH_JOB_SSID':     ss.getId()
  });

  // Remove any junk rows from Thresholds sheet before regenerating
  _cleanThresholdSheetJunk(ss);

  _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds', '0/' + analyteList.length + ' analytes done — starting...');

  // Schedule the actual work as a trigger (fresh 6-min budget)
  _clearContinuationTrigger();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('_continueThresholdRefresh').timeBased().after(60 * 1000).create();
}

// Removes non-clinical rows from the Thresholds sheet.
// Uses an explicit name-based blocklist because threshold rows have no unit
// column for isClinicalResult to check against.
function _cleanThresholdSheetJunk(ss) {
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
  if (!thSheet) return;
  var lastRow = thSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 5) return;

  // Explicit junk terms — any threshold row whose name contains one of these
  // (case-insensitive substring match) will be deleted.
  var JUNK_NAMES = [
    'fitness score', 'sleep score', 'wellbeing', 'work/life', 'alcohol score',
    'smoking score', 'nutrition score', 'mind (stress)', 'mind score',
    'activity score', 'total body water', 'body fat', 'hydration',
    'resting heart rate', 'heart rate', 'bmi', 'body mass index',
    'height', 'weight', 'steps', 'sms', 'appointment', 'referral'
  ];

  var data = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRow - 3, 1).getValues();
  var toDelete = [];
  for (var i = data.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var name = (data[i][0] || '').toString().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!name) continue;
    for (var j = 0; j < JUNK_NAMES.length; j++) {
      if (name.indexOf(JUNK_NAMES[j]) >= 0) {
        toDelete.push(4 + i);
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  for (var d = 0; d < toDelete.length; d++) {
    try { thSheet.deleteRow(toDelete[d]); } catch(e) {}
  }
  if (toDelete.length > 0) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  return toDelete.length; // return count for status message
}

// Called by trigger to continue a paused threshold refresh job
// Called by trigger to continue a paused threshold refresh job
// v5.20 fixes applied:
//   1. timeLimit increased 3.5 → 4.5 min (more work per execution)
//   2. Pre/post batch heartbeat updates (status updates 3x per batch)
//   3. _runPostThresholdStages always called inline — elapsed2 check removed
//      (restores v5.17f design intent — the conditional always fell into trigger
//       path because elapsed is always ≥3.5min by final batch completion)
// ─── THRESHOLD SKIP LIST ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Analytes matching these terms are not sent to the AI for threshold generation.
// They are clearly not blood/urine clinical markers.
// A clearly-marked placeholder row is written so they are visible and auditable.
// Add new problem analytes here if they cause hangs in future.
var THRESHOLD_SKIP_TERMS = [
  'faecal', 'fecal', 'stool', 'elastase',
  'height', 'weight', 'bmi', 'body mass',
  'wellbeing', 'alcohol score', 'smoking score', 'fitness score',
  'sleep score', 'nutrition score', 'mind (stress)', 'work/life',
  'activity score', 'steps', 'body fat', 'total body water',
  'resting heart rate', 'pulse rate',
  'read code', 'sms', 'appointment'
];

// Marker written into the threshold value cell for skipped/failed analytes.
// retrySkippedThresholds() searches for this to build the retry list.
var SKIP_MARKER    = '⏭️ SKIPPED — not sent to AI';
var FAIL_MARKER    = '⚠️ Generation failed — re-run Full Refresh';
var TIMEOUT_MARKER = '⏱️ TIMEOUT — retry individually';

function _shouldSkipForThresholds(name) {
  var n = name.toLowerCase();
  for (var si = 0; si < THRESHOLD_SKIP_TERMS.length; si++) {
    if (n.indexOf(THRESHOLD_SKIP_TERMS[si]) >= 0) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function _makeSkipPlaceholder(analyteName, reason, marker) {
  return {
    analyte:       analyteName,
    threshold:     marker || SKIP_MARKER,
    direction:     '—',
    meaning:       reason || 'Not a standard blood/urine clinical marker. If this is wrong, use 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds from the menu.',
    symptoms:      '',
    confidence:    'N/A — skipped',
    source:        'Skipped by threshold generator. Use menu → 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds to force a retry.',
    common_causes: ''
  };
}


// ─── CALLED BY TRIGGER: CONTINUE THRESHOLD REFRESH ───────────────────────────
// v5.21:
//   1. timeLimit 4.5 min per execution
//   2. Pre/post batch heartbeat updates
//   3. _runPostThresholdStages always inline
//   4. Wall clock guard on individual retries — no more indefinite hangs
//   5. Skip list — non-clinical analytes write a clear placeholder, not an AI call
function _continueThresholdRefresh() {
  var props     = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var jobRaw    = props.getProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES');
  if (!jobRaw) {
    _clearContinuationTrigger();
    return;
  }

  var analyteList    = JSON.parse(jobRaw);
  var profileContext = props.getProperty('TH_JOB_PROFILE') || '';
  var today          = props.getProperty('TH_JOB_TODAY')   || '';
  var startIdx       = parseInt(props.getProperty('TH_JOB_START') || '0');
  var allThresholds  = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TH_JOB_RESULTS') || '[]');
  var errors         = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TH_JOB_ERRORS')  || '[]');
  var ssId           = props.getProperty('TH_JOB_SSID') || '';
  var apiKey         = getApiKey();

  var ss      = ssId ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(ssId) : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');

  var batchSize    = 8;
  var startTime    = new Date().getTime();
  var timeLimit    = 4.5 * 60 * 1000;
  var totalBatches = Math.ceil(analyteList.length / batchSize);

  var idx = startIdx;
  while (idx < analyteList.length) {

    // ── Time limit check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if ((new Date().getTime() - startTime) > timeLimit) {
      props.setProperties({
        'TH_JOB_START':   idx.toString(),
        'TH_JOB_RESULTS': JSON.stringify(allThresholds),
        'TH_JOB_ERRORS':  JSON.stringify(errors)
      });
      _scheduleContinuation();
      if (allThresholds.length > 0 && thSheet) writeAllThresholds(thSheet, allThresholds, today);
      _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds',
        idx + '/' + analyteList.length + ' analytes done — pausing, will resume in ~30s...\n' +
        'Last update: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss'));
      return;
    }

    var batch    = analyteList.slice(idx, idx + batchSize);
    var batchNum = Math.floor(idx / batchSize) + 1;
    var pct      = Math.round((idx / analyteList.length) * 100);

    // ── Filter skip analytes out of batch ─────────────────────────────────
    var cleanBatch = [], skippedInBatch = [];
    for (var fi = 0; fi < batch.length; fi++) {
      if (_shouldSkipForThresholds(batch[fi])) {
        skippedInBatch.push(batch[fi]);
        allThresholds.push(_makeSkipPlaceholder(batch[fi],
          'Matched non-clinical skip list (contains: faecal/stool/elastase/height/weight etc). ' +
          'If this analyte IS a valid clinical test, use 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds from the menu.',
          SKIP_MARKER));
      } else {
        cleanBatch.push(batch[fi]);
      }
    }

    if (skippedInBatch.length > 0) {
      errors.push('Skipped (non-clinical): ' + skippedInBatch.join(', '));
    }

    if (cleanBatch.length === 0) {
      idx += batchSize;
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      continue;
    }

    // ── Pre-batch heartbeat ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds',
      '⏳ Sending to AI now: ' + cleanBatch.join(', ').substring(0, 80) +
      (cleanBatch.join(', ').length > 80 ? '...' : '') + '\n' +
      'Batch ' + batchNum + ' of ' + totalBatches + '  |  ' +
      idx + '/' + analyteList.length + ' done (' + pct + '%)  |  ' +
      allThresholds.length + ' threshold rows so far\n' +
      '⏱ ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss') +
      ' — waiting for AI response...');
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    try {
      var r = callAIForThresholds(cleanBatch, profileContext, today, apiKey);
      for (var j = 0; j < r.length; j++) allThresholds.push(r[j]);

      // ── Post-batch success heartbeat ───────────────────────────────────
      var doneIdx   = idx + batch.length;
      var donePct   = Math.round((doneIdx / analyteList.length) * 100);
      var elapsed   = Math.round((new Date().getTime() - startTime) / 1000);
      var estTotal  = (elapsed > 0 && doneIdx > startIdx)
        ? Math.round(elapsed / (doneIdx - startIdx) * analyteList.length)
        : null;
      var estRemain = estTotal ? Math.max(0, estTotal - elapsed) + 's remaining' : '';

      _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds',
        '✅ Batch ' + batchNum + ' complete: ' + cleanBatch.join(', ').substring(0, 70) + '\n' +
        doneIdx + '/' + analyteList.length + ' analytes done (' + donePct + '%)  |  ' +
        allThresholds.length + ' threshold rows generated\n' +
        '⏱ ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss') +
        (estRemain ? '  |  Est. ' + estRemain : ''));
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    } catch(e) {
      errors.push('Batch at ' + idx + ' failed (' + e.toString().substring(0, 100) + ') — retrying individually');

      for (var bi = 0; bi < cleanBatch.length; bi++) {
        var retryName = cleanBatch[bi];

        // Double-check skip list on individual retry
        if (_shouldSkipForThresholds(retryName)) {
          allThresholds.push(_makeSkipPlaceholder(retryName,
            'Skipped on individual retry — matched non-clinical skip list.',
            SKIP_MARKER));
          continue;
        }

        // Wall clock guard — if we're too close to time limit, write placeholder and bail
        var timeUsed = new Date().getTime() - startTime;
        if (timeUsed > timeLimit) {
          errors.push('Skipped individual retry for ' + retryName + ' — time limit reached');
          allThresholds.push(_makeSkipPlaceholder(retryName,
            'Execution time limit reached during individual retry. Use 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds from the menu.',
            TIMEOUT_MARKER));
          props.setProperties({
            'TH_JOB_START':   idx.toString(),
            'TH_JOB_RESULTS': JSON.stringify(allThresholds),
            'TH_JOB_ERRORS':  JSON.stringify(errors)
          });
          _scheduleContinuation();
          if (allThresholds.length > 0 && thSheet) writeAllThresholds(thSheet, allThresholds, today);
          _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds',
            'Time limit reached during retry — resuming in ~30s...\n' +
            'Last update: ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss'));
          return;
        }

        // Per-analyte retry heartbeat
        _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds',
          '🔄 Batch failed — retrying one by one\n' +
          'Current: ' + retryName + ' (' + (idx + bi + 1) + '/' + analyteList.length + ')\n' +
          '⏱ ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss'));
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();

        try {
          var single = callAIForThresholds([retryName], profileContext, today, apiKey);
          for (var si = 0; si < single.length; si++) allThresholds.push(single[si]);
        } catch(e2) {
          errors.push('Individual retry failed for ' + retryName + ': ' + e2.toString().substring(0, 100));
          allThresholds.push(_makeSkipPlaceholder(retryName,
            'API error or timeout on individual retry. Use 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds from the menu.',
            FAIL_MARKER));
        }
      }
    }

    idx += batchSize;
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  // ── All batches done ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  _clearContinuationTrigger();
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_START');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_RESULTS');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_ERRORS');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_SSID');

  if (allThresholds.length === 0) {
    var errDetail = errors.length > 0 ? ' Last error: ' + errors[0] : '';
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'error', 'No thresholds returned.' + errDetail);
    return;
  }

  if (thSheet) writeAllThresholds(thSheet, allThresholds, today);

  var chainStage = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  if (chainStage === 'waiting_thresholds') {
    props.setProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE', 'views');
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'views',
      'All ' + analyteList.length + ' analytes done — ' +
      allThresholds.length + ' threshold rows written. Rebuilding views...');
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    _clearAllChainTriggers();
    _runPostThresholdStages(ss);
  } else {
    _rebuildViews(ss);
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'complete',
      allThresholds.length + ' threshold rows written for ' + analyteList.length + ' analyte(s)');
  }
}


// ─── RETRY SKIPPED / FAILED THRESHOLDS ───────────────────────────────────────
// Menu: 🔧 Troubleshooting → 🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds
//
// Scans the Thresholds sheet for rows containing any of the placeholder markers
// (SKIPPED, TIMEOUT, or Generation Failed). Shows the user a list and lets them:
//   A) Retry ALL of them with the AI
//   B) Confirm a specific subset to retry
//   C) Cancel (leave as-is — placeholders remain visible in the sheet)
//
// Skipped analytes that the user confirms ARE valid clinical tests will be retried.
// Those confirmed as genuinely non-clinical can be deleted from the sheet manually.
function retrySkippedThresholds() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!thSheet || thSheet.getLastRow() < 4) {
    ui.alert('No Thresholds sheet found or sheet is empty. Run Full Refresh first.');
    return;
  }

  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) {
    ui.alert('No API key found. Check Script Properties — ANTHROPIC_API_KEY or OPENAI_API_KEY required.');
    return;
  }

  // ── Scan Thresholds sheet for placeholder rows ────────────────────────────
  var lastRow  = thSheet.getLastRow();
  var thData   = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRow - 3, 10).getValues();
  var problems = []; // { analyte, rowIndex, type }

  for (var i = 0; i < thData.length; i++) {
    var analyteName = (thData[i][0] || '').toString().trim();
    var threshVal   = (thData[i][1] || '').toString().trim();
    if (!analyteName) continue;

    var type = null;
    if (threshVal.indexOf('SKIPPED') >= 0)           type = '⏭️ Skipped';
    else if (threshVal.indexOf('TIMEOUT') >= 0)       type = '⏱️ Timed out';
    else if (threshVal.indexOf('Generation failed') >= 0) type = '⚠️ Failed';

    if (type) {
      problems.push({ analyte: analyteName, rowIndex: i + 4, type: type });
    }
  }

  if (problems.length === 0) {
    ui.alert(
      '✅ No skipped or failed thresholds found',
      'All analytes in the Thresholds sheet have generated threshold rows.\n\n' +
      'If you expected to see some here, run the 🔍 Analyte Diagnostic List to check for analytes ' +
      'that are in TestResults but have no Thresholds row at all.',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK
    );
    return;
  }

  // ── Show the list and ask what to do ─────────────────────────────────────
  var listLines = problems.map(function(p, idx2) {
    return '  ' + (idx2 + 1) + '. ' + p.type + '  —  ' + p.analyte;
  });

  var msg =
    problems.length + ' analyte(s) were skipped or failed during the last refresh:\n\n' +
    listLines.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'OPTIONS:\n' +
    '• YES — Retry ALL of these with the AI now (~' + Math.ceil(problems.length / 3) + ' AI calls, ~' + (problems.length * 15) + 's)\n' +
    '• NO  — Cancel, leave placeholders as-is\n\n' +
    'Note: Analytes in the skip list (faecal, stool, elastase, height, weight etc) will be ' +
    'retried but may still fail — if so they are not valid clinical blood test markers.\n\n' +
    'To retry only specific ones: delete their rows from the Thresholds sheet manually, then run this again or run Full Refresh.';

  var choice = ui.alert('🔁 Retry Skipped / Failed Thresholds', msg, ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
  if (choice !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  // ── Run AI for each problem analyte individually ──────────────────────────
  var profileContext = getProfileContext(ss).replace('\n\nPATIENT PROFILE (personalise where relevant):\n', '');
  var today          = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var succeeded = [], failed = [], stillSkipped = [];

  ui.alert(
    '⏳ Retrying ' + problems.length + ' analyte(s)...',
    'Running AI calls individually. This will take approximately ' +
    (problems.length * 15) + ' seconds.\n\nClick OK to begin — check the Thresholds sheet when done.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );

  for (var pi = 0; pi < problems.length; pi++) {
    var p = problems[pi];

    // Remove the placeholder row first so we don't get duplicates
    try { thSheet.deleteRow(p.rowIndex - pi); } catch(e) {} // adjust for deleted rows

    // Check skip list — but still attempt it (user explicitly requested retry)
    var isLikelyNonClinical = _shouldSkipForThresholds(p.analyte);

    try {
      var result = callAIForThresholds([p.analyte], profileContext, today, apiKey);
      if (result && result.length > 0) {
        // Append new threshold rows to end of sheet
        writeAllThresholds(thSheet, result, today);
        succeeded.push(p.analyte);
      } else {
        throw new Error('AI returned no rows');
      }
    } catch(e2) {
      // Write a failure placeholder back — clearly marked
      var failReason = isLikelyNonClinical
        ? 'Retry attempted but still failed — this analyte name matches the non-clinical skip list ' +
          '(faecal/stool/elastase/height/weight etc). If it IS a valid clinical test, check the ' +
          'exact analyte name in TestResults and contact support.'
        : 'Retry failed: ' + e2.toString().substring(0, 150) + '. Try again later or check API status.';

      var failRow = [
        p.analyte,
        FAIL_MARKER + ' (retry attempted ' + today + ')',
        '—',
        failReason,
        '', 'N/A — retry failed', 'Manual review needed', today, 'Retry failed', ''
      ];
      var appendRow = thSheet.getLastRow() + 1;
      thSheet.getRange(appendRow, 1, 1, failRow.length).setValues([failRow])
        .setBackground('#fef2f2').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true);

      if (isLikelyNonClinical) {
        stillSkipped.push(p.analyte);
      } else {
        failed.push(p.analyte + ' (' + e2.toString().substring(0, 80) + ')');
      }
    }

    Utilities.sleep(1000); // brief pause between calls
  }

  // ── Rebuild views so results appear in Significant Findings ──────────────
  if (succeeded.length > 0) {
    try { _rebuildViews(ss); } catch(e) {}
  }

  // ── Summary alert ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var summaryMsg = '🔁 Retry complete.\n\n';

  if (succeeded.length > 0) {
    summaryMsg += '✅ Successfully generated thresholds for ' + succeeded.length + ' analyte(s):\n' +
                  succeeded.map(function(n){ return '  • ' + n; }).join('\n') + '\n\n';
  }
  if (stillSkipped.length > 0) {
    summaryMsg += '⏭️ Still skipped (' + stillSkipped.length + ') — these are not standard clinical blood tests:\n' +
                  stillSkipped.map(function(n){ return '  • ' + n; }).join('\n') + '\n' +
                  'You can safely delete these rows from the Thresholds sheet.\n\n';
  }
  if (failed.length > 0) {
    summaryMsg += '❌ Failed to generate (' + failed.length + '):\n' +
                  failed.map(function(n){ return '  • ' + n; }).join('\n') + '\n' +
                  'Check API connection and try again.\n\n';
  }

  summaryMsg += 'Run 🔍 Show Analyte Diagnostic List to see full coverage status.';
  ui.alert('🔁 Retry Results', summaryMsg, ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}



// Schedules the next continuation trigger for threshold batch processing
// FIX: reduced from 60s to 30s — cuts dead time between executions in half
function _scheduleContinuation() {
  _clearContinuationTrigger();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('_continueThresholdRefresh')
    .timeBased()
    .after(30 * 1000)  // was 60 * 1000 — 30s is reliable in practice
    .create();
}


function _clearContinuationTrigger() {
  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var i = 0; i < triggers.length; i++) {
    if (triggers[i].getHandlerFunction() === '_continueThresholdRefresh') {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(triggers[i]);
    }
  }
}

// ─── REFRESH STATUS SHEET ─────────────────────────────
// All status written to a single dedicated tab — one write per update.
// Zero impact on other sheets. Shown during refresh, hidden when idle.

var REFRESH_STATUS_TAB = '⏳ Refresh Status';

function _getOrCreateStatusSheet(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(REFRESH_STATUS_TAB);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(REFRESH_STATUS_TAB);
    sh.setTabColor('#1e40af');
    ss.moveActiveSheet(ss.getSheets().length);
  }
  if (sh.isSheetHidden()) sh.showSheet();
  return sh;
}

function _setRefreshStatus(ss, stage, extraMsg) {
  try {
    var now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss');
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var history = {};
    try { history = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('REFRESH_STAGE_HISTORY') || '{}'); } catch(e2) {}
    if (stage === 'complete' || stage === 'error') {
      props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_STAGE_HISTORY');
    } else if (stage && stage !== 'waiting_thresholds' && !history[stage + '_time']) {
      history[stage + '_time'] = now;
      props.setProperty('REFRESH_STAGE_HISTORY', JSON.stringify(history));
    }
    if (stage === 'waiting_thresholds' && extraMsg) {
      history['thresholds_progress'] = extraMsg;
      props.setProperty('REFRESH_STAGE_HISTORY', JSON.stringify(history));
    }
    _writeRefreshStatusSheet(ss, stage, extraMsg || '', history, now);
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _writeRefreshStatusSheet(ss, stage, extraMsg, history, now) {
  try {
    var sh = _getOrCreateStatusSheet(ss);
    if (!now) now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss');
    var isComplete = (stage === 'complete');
    var isError    = (stage === 'error');
    sh.clear();
    sh.setColumnWidth(1, 28); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 200);
    sh.setColumnWidth(3, 110); sh.setColumnWidth(4, 360); sh.setColumnWidth(5, 90);

    // Header
    var hBg = isComplete ? '#dcfce7' : isError ? '#fee2e2' : '#1e3a5f';
    var hFg = isComplete ? '#166534' : isError ? '#7f1d1d' : '#ffffff';
    var hText = isComplete ? '✅  REFRESH COMPLETE — all steps finished  |  ' + now
      : isError  ? '⚠️  REFRESH ERROR — ' + (extraMsg || '') + '  |  ' + now
      : '⏳  REFRESH IN PROGRESS — ' + now + '  —  Running in the background, keep working normally.';
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).merge().setValue(hText).setBackground(hBg).setFontColor(hFg)
      .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sh.setRowHeight(1, 30);

    // Column headers
    sh.getRange(2, 1, 1, 5).setValues([['', 'Stage', 'Status', 'Detail', 'Started']])
      .setBackground('#334155').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
    sh.setRowHeight(2, 20);

    // Stage rows
    var stageOrder = ['thresholds','waiting_thresholds','views','findings','supplements','significance','guidance_diff'];
    var isWaiting  = (stage === 'waiting_thresholds');
    var currentKey = isWaiting ? 'thresholds' : stage;
    var currentIdx = stageOrder.indexOf(stage);
    var displayStages = [
      { key: 'thresholds',    n: 1, label: 'AI Thresholds' },
      { key: 'views',         n: 2, label: 'Rebuild Analysis' },
      { key: 'findings',      n: 3, label: 'Rebuild Findings' },
      { key: 'supplements',   n: 4, label: 'Supplements & Meds' },
      { key: 'significance',  n: 5, label: 'Significance Ratings' },
      { key: 'guidance_diff', n: 6, label: 'Guidance Change Log' },
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < displayStages.length; i++) {
      var ds  = displayStages[i];
      var dIdx = stageOrder.indexOf(ds.key);
      var isCurrent = (ds.key === currentKey) && !isComplete && !isError;
      var isDone = isComplete || (!isError && !isWaiting && currentIdx > dIdx) ||
                  (!isError && isWaiting && ds.key !== 'thresholds' && dIdx < stageOrder.indexOf('waiting_thresholds'));
      var icon   = isDone ? '✅' : isCurrent ? '⏳' : '○';
      var status = isDone ? 'Complete' : isCurrent ? 'Running...' : 'Pending';
      var detail = isCurrent ? (extraMsg || '') : (ds.key === 'thresholds' && history['thresholds_progress'] ? history['thresholds_progress'] : '');
      var time   = history[ds.key + '_time'] || '';
      var rowBg  = isDone ? '#f0fdf4' : isCurrent ? '#fefce8' : '#f8fafc';
      var rowFg  = isDone ? '#166534' : isCurrent ? '#713f12' : '#94a3b8';
      var rw = 3 + i;
      sh.getRange(rw, 1).setValue(icon).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(11).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
      sh.getRange(rw, 2).setValue('Step ' + ds.n + '/5  ' + ds.label).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(rowFg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
      sh.getRange(rw, 3).setValue(status).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(rowFg).setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
      sh.getRange(rw, 4).setValue(detail).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(rowFg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
      sh.getRange(rw, 5).setValue(time).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor('#64748b').setFontSize(8).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
      sh.setRowHeight(rw, 22);
    }

    // Footer
    sh.getRange(8, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue(isComplete
        ? '✅ Last full refresh completed at ' + now + '.  Check 🚨 Significant Findings and 📊 Analysis for updated results.  Next: run Quick Refresh after adding new test results.'
        : isError ? '⚠️ Refresh stopped with an error. Try running Full Refresh again from the 🏥 menu.'
        : 'Typically takes 3–6 minutes total.  |  🏥 menu → Check Refresh Status to monitor progress at any time.')
      .setBackground(isComplete ? '#dcfce7' : isError ? '#fee2e2' : '#f1f5f9')
      .setFontColor(isComplete ? '#166534' : isError ? '#7f1d1d' : '#64748b')
      .setFontSize(8).setWrap(true);
    sh.setRowHeight(8, 24);
    // Tip row
    sh.getRange(9, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue('💡 Tip: Run 🏥 → Generate Personal Health Narrative for a plain-English summary of your results with a prioritised action plan.')
      .setBackground('#fffbeb').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontStyle('italic');
    sh.setRowHeight(9, 20);
    sh.setFrozenRows(2);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    // Do NOT hide on complete — keep visible as a persistent last-refresh log
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _clearRefreshStatus(ss) {
  try { var sh = ss.getSheetByName(REFRESH_STATUS_TAB); if (sh) sh.hideSheet(); } catch(e) {}
}

// Backwards-compat wrapper
function _setThresholdStatus(ss, msg) {
  if (!msg) { _clearRefreshStatus(ss); return; }
  var stg = msg.indexOf('✅') >= 0 ? 'complete' : msg.indexOf('⚠') >= 0 ? 'error' : 'waiting_thresholds';
  _setRefreshStatus(ss, stg, msg);
}

// ─── SHOW VERSION HISTORY (menu item) ────────────────
function showVersionHistory() {
  var ss   = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var name = '📋 Version History';
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  var sh = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(name);
  sh.clear(); sh.clearFormats();
  sh.setTabColor('#334155');
  if (sh.isSheetHidden()) sh.showSheet();
  ss.setActiveSheet(sh);

  // Column widths
  sh.setColumnWidth(1, 70);
  sh.setColumnWidth(2, 110);
  sh.setColumnWidth(3, 180);
  sh.setColumnWidth(4, 480);

  // Title
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).merge()
    .setValue('Medical Tracker — Version History')
    .setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#ffffff')
    .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(13).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sh.setRowHeight(1, 32);

  // Subtitle
  sh.getRange(2, 1, 1, 4).merge()
    .setValue('To roll back: paste the corresponding versioned .gs file into Apps Script in full, then run buildMedicalTracker.')
    .setBackground('#334155').setFontColor('#94a3b8').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
  sh.setRowHeight(2, 22);

  // Column headers
  sh.getRange(3, 1, 1, 4).setValues([['Version', 'Date', 'Title', 'Changes']])
    .setBackground('#475569').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
  sh.setRowHeight(3, 20);

  // Version data — mirrors the header comment exactly
  var versions = [
    ['v5.9', '19 Feb 2026', 'Version logging',
      '+ Version history in script header\n+ showVersionHistory() function — readable sheet tab\n+ Versioned output file naming for rollback'],
    ['v5.8', '19 Feb 2026', 'Dedicated Refresh Status sheet',
      '+ Replaced multi-sheet banners with single status tab\n+ Zero impact on other sheets during refresh\n+ Per-step table: pending / running / complete per stage\n+ Sheet auto-hides on completion\n+ Stage start times tracked in Script Properties'],
    ['v5.7', '19 Feb 2026', 'Refresh UX improvements',
      '+ Confirmation alert shows timing estimate (3-6 min)\n+ Clear messaging: background process, keep working\n+ Status banner extended to all main sheets (superseded by v5.8)'],
    ['v5.6', '19 Feb 2026', 'Threshold batch performance',
      '+ Batch size increased 8 → 15 analytes per API call\n+ Status updates after every batch (not just on timeout)\n+ Continuation trigger delay reduced 60s → 5s'],
    ['v5.5', '19 Feb 2026', 'BMI formula fix',
      '+ BMI category formula no longer uses emoji/en-dashes (caused #ERROR)\n+ Restore skips Calculated BMI and BMI Category rows\n+ Both fixProfileSheet and backup restore protect formula cells'],
    ['v5.4', '19 Feb 2026', 'Backup retention',
      '+ Only one backup per sheet retained (previous deleted on new backup)\n+ Avoids tab proliferation on repeated rebuilds'],
    ['v5.3', '19 Feb 2026', 'Profile validation fix',
      '+ Stale dropdown bug fixed (old schema overwriting new fields)\n+ Dynamic row tracking for dropdowns (no hardcoded row numbers)\n+ BMI formula uses dynamic row refs\n+ fixProfileSheet menu function added\n+ breakApart() on rebuild to clear stale merges'],
    ['v5.2', '19 Feb 2026', 'Refresh status banner',
      '+ Progress banner on Thresholds + Findings during refresh\n+ Check Refresh Status menu item\n+ Stage-by-stage progress display'],
    ['v5.1', '19 Feb 2026', 'Timeout & lab flags',
      '+ Refresh chain timeout fix (triggers, not inline)\n+ Lab-flagged results section in Significant Findings\n+ Profile restore label-matching improvement'],
    ['v5.0', '18 Feb 2026', 'Initial v5 release',
      '+ Medication & Supplement Log tab\n+ Symptom Investigation tab (AI-powered)\n+ Investigation Records table (save/reload)\n+ Email generator (pre-GP and post-GP versions)\n+ Treatment Tracker with AI trajectory and charts'],
  ];

  for (var i = 0; i < versions.length; i++) {
    var r  = 4 + i;
    var v  = versions[i];
    var isLatest = (i === 0);
    var rowBg = isLatest ? '#f0fdf4' : (i % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff');
    var vFg   = isLatest ? '#166534' : '#1e293b';
    sh.getRange(r, 1).setValue(v[0]).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(vFg).setFontWeight(isLatest?'bold':'normal').setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(r, 2).setValue(v[1]).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor('#64748b').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(r, 3).setValue(v[2]).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(vFg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top').setWrap(true);
    sh.getRange(r, 4).setValue(v[3]).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor('#374151').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    var lineCount = v[3].split('\n').length;
    sh.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(22, lineCount * 14 + 6));
  }

  sh.setFrozenRows(3);
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Version History sheet ready.\n\nCurrent version: v5.10 (19 Feb 2026)\nTo roll back: paste any older versioned .gs file into Apps Script in full, then run buildMedicalTracker.');
}

// ─── GUIDANCE DIFF: compare new thresholds vs stored ────────
// Called as final stage of Full Refresh (no AI call — pure comparison).
// Compares newly written Thresholds sheet against stored previous snapshot.
// Logs significant changes to 📜 Guidance Changes tab.
// Cross-references against patient's current results, meds, supplements
// to assign a relevance tier:
//   🔴 High   — affects analyte with current flagged result or active med/supplement
//   🟡 Medium — analyte has been tested but currently in range
//   ⚪ Low    — analyte not in patient's test history
function _runGuidanceDiff(ss) {
  try {
    var props   = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
    if (!thSheet) return;
    var lastRow = thSheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 4) return;
    var newData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRow - 3, 10).getValues();

    // Build map of new thresholds: analyte_lc -> array of row objects
    var newMap = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < newData.length; i++) {
      var row = newData[i];
      if (!row[0]) continue;
      var key = row[0].toString().toLowerCase().trim();
      if (!newMap[key]) newMap[key] = [];
      newMap[key].push({
        analyte: row[0].toString(), threshold: row[1].toString(),
        direction: row[2].toString(), meaning: row[3].toString(),
        symptoms: row[4].toString(), confidence: row[5].toString(), source: row[6].toString()
      });
    }

    // Load previous snapshot
    var prevMap = {};
    try { prevMap = JSON.parse(props.getProperty('TH_PREV_SNAPSHOT') || '{}'); } catch(e) {}

    // Collect patient context for relevance tiering
    var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
    var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\u{1F48A} Meds & Supplements Log');
    var testedAnalytes = {}, flaggedAnalytes = {}, activeMeds = {};

    if (trSheet) {
      var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
      var latestRes = collectLatestResults(trData);
      for (var k in latestRes) {
        testedAnalytes[k] = true;
        var isFlagged = latestRes[k].status && latestRes[k].status.toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('NORMAL') < 0 &&
                        latestRes[k].status.toString().trim() !== '';
        var thRows = newMap[k] || [];
        for (var t = 0; t < thRows.length; t++) {
          if (isTriggered(latestRes[k].value, thRows[t])) { isFlagged = true; break; }
        }
        if (isFlagged) flaggedAnalytes[k] = true;
      }
    }
    if (medSheet) {
      var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
      for (var m = 0; m < mData.length; m++) {
        if (mData[m][0] && !mData[m][6]) {
          activeMeds[mData[m][0].toString().toLowerCase().trim()] = true;
        }
      }
    }

    var changes = [];
    var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm');
    var cautionWords = ['cancer','carcinoma','toxic','contraindicated','avoid','dangerous','harmful',
                        'kidney','liver','overdose','risk','warning','caution','interaction'];

    for (var aKey in newMap) {
      var newRows  = newMap[aKey];
      var prevRows = prevMap[aKey] || [];

      // Relevance tier
      var tier = '\u26aa Low'; var tierNote = 'Not in your test history';
      if (testedAnalytes[aKey])  { tier = '\U0001f7e1 Medium'; tierNote = 'In your test history'; }
      if (flaggedAnalytes[aKey]) { tier = '\U0001f534 High';   tierNote = 'Currently flagged in your results'; }
      for (var mk in activeMeds) {
        if (mk.indexOf(aKey) >= 0 || aKey.indexOf(mk) >= 0) {
          tier = '\U0001f534 High'; tierNote = 'Matches an active medication or supplement'; break;
        }
      }

      var prevText = prevRows.map(function(r){ return r.threshold+'|'+r.direction+'|'+r.meaning; }).join(';;');
      var newText  = newRows.map(function(r){ return r.threshold+'|'+r.direction+'|'+r.meaning; }).join(';;');
      if (prevText === newText) continue; // no change

      if (prevText === '' && !testedAnalytes[aKey]) continue; // new analyte not in patient history

      var prevMeanings = prevRows.map(function(r){ return (r.meaning+' '+r.symptoms+' '+r.confidence).toLowerCase(); }).join(' ');
      var newMeanings  = newRows.map(function(r){ return (r.meaning+' '+r.symptoms+' '+r.confidence).toLowerCase(); }).join(' ');
      var newCautions  = cautionWords.filter(function(w){ return newMeanings.indexOf(w) >= 0 && prevMeanings.indexOf(w) < 0; });

      var prevNums = prevText.match(/\d+\.?\d*/g) || [];
      var newNums  = newText.match(/\d+\.?\d*/g) || [];
      var rangeChanged = false;
      for (var ni = 0; ni < Math.min(prevNums.length, newNums.length); ni++) {
        var pn = parseFloat(prevNums[ni]), nn = parseFloat(newNums[ni]);
        if (pn > 0 && Math.abs(nn - pn) / pn > 0.15) { rangeChanged = true; break; }
      }

      if (newCautions.length > 0 || rangeChanged || tier.indexOf('\U0001f534') >= 0 || prevText === '') {
        var changeType = [];
        if (prevText === '') changeType.push('New thresholds added');
        if (newCautions.length > 0) changeType.push('New caution: ' + newCautions.join(', '));
        if (rangeChanged) changeType.push('Threshold range shifted >15%');
        changes.push({
          date: today, analyte: newRows[0].analyte,
          type: changeType.join(' | ') || 'Guidance updated',
          previous: prevRows.length > 0 ? prevRows.map(function(r){ return r.threshold+' '+r.direction+': '+r.meaning.substring(0,100); }).join('\n') : '(no previous data)',
          current:  newRows.map(function(r){ return r.threshold+' '+r.direction+': '+r.meaning.substring(0,100); }).join('\n'),
          tier: tier, tierNote: tierNote
        });
      }
    }

    // Save current as snapshot for next comparison
    props.setProperty('TH_PREV_SNAPSHOT', JSON.stringify(newMap));

    if (changes.length === 0) return;
    _writeGuidanceChanges(ss, changes);
    _surfaceHighGuidanceChanges(ss, changes);

  } catch(e) {} // non-fatal
}

function _writeGuidanceChanges(ss, changes) {
  var tabName = '\U0001f4dc Guidance Changes';
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
  var isNew = !sh || sh.getLastRow() < 3;
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(tabName);
    sh.setTabColor('#7c3aed');
    var sheets = ss.getSheets();
    for (var i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++) {
      if (sheets[i].getName() === '\U0001f6a8 Significant Findings') { ss.moveSheet(sh, i + 2); break; }
    }
  }
  if (isNew) {
    sh.clear();
    sh.setColumnWidths(1, 7, 1);
    sh.setColumnWidth(1, 100); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 140); sh.setColumnWidth(3, 200);
    sh.setColumnWidth(4, 320); sh.setColumnWidth(5, 320); sh.setColumnWidth(6, 90); sh.setColumnWidth(7, 180);
    sh.getRange(1,1,1,7).merge()
      .setValue('\U0001f4dc  GUIDANCE CHANGES  \u2014  Significant changes detected during Full Refresh. Newest first.')
      .setBackground('#4c1d95').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sh.setRowHeight(1, 28);
    sh.getRange(2,1,1,7).setValues([['Date','Analyte','Change Type','Previous Guidance','New Guidance','Relevance','Why Relevant']])
      .setBackground('#6d28d9').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
    sh.setRowHeight(2, 20);
    sh.setFrozenRows(2);
  }
  sh.insertRowsBefore(3, changes.length);
  for (var i = 0; i < changes.length; i++) {
    var c = changes[i]; var r = 3 + i;
    var isHigh = c.tier.indexOf('\U0001f534') >= 0, isMed = c.tier.indexOf('\U0001f7e1') >= 0;
    var rowBg = isHigh ? '#fef2f2' : isMed ? '#fefce8' : '#f8fafc';
    var rowFg = isHigh ? '#7f1d1d' : isMed ? '#713f12' : '#64748b';
    sh.getRange(r,1).setValue(c.date).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(r,2).setValue(c.analyte).setBackground(rowBg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(r,3).setValue(c.type).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor(rowFg);
    sh.getRange(r,4).setValue(c.previous).setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#92400e');
    sh.getRange(r,5).setValue(c.current).setBackground('#f0fdf4').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#166534');
    sh.getRange(r,6).setValue(c.tier).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(rowFg).setFontSize(10).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.getRange(r,7).setValue(c.tierNote).setBackground(rowBg).setFontColor(rowFg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sh.setRowHeight(r, Math.max(40, Math.max(c.previous.split('\n').length, c.current.split('\n').length) * 14 + 10));
  }
}

function _surfaceHighGuidanceChanges(ss, changes) {
  var highChanges = changes.filter(function(c){ return c.tier.indexOf('\U0001f534') >= 0; });
  if (highChanges.length === 0) return;
  var fSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\U0001f6a8 Significant Findings');
  if (!fSheet) return;
  fSheet.insertRowsBefore(1, highChanges.length + 1);
  fSheet.getRange(1,1,1,9).merge()
    .setValue('\u26a0\ufe0f  ' + highChanges.length + ' GUIDANCE CHANGE' + (highChanges.length > 1 ? 'S' : '') +
      ' detected affecting your results or supplements \u2014 see \U0001f4dc Guidance Changes tab for full detail.')
    .setBackground('#7f1d1d').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  fSheet.setRowHeight(1, 28);
  for (var i = 0; i < highChanges.length; i++) {
    var c = highChanges[i];
    fSheet.getRange(2+i,1,1,9).merge()
      .setValue('\U0001f534  '+c.analyte+'  |  '+c.type+'  |  '+c.current.substring(0,120).replace(/\n/g,' '))
      .setBackground('#fef2f2').setFontColor('#7f1d1d').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    fSheet.setRowHeight(2+i, 24);
  }
}

// ─── CHECK REFRESH STATUS (menu item) ──────────────────────────
// ── STAGE_LABELS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Maps REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE property values to human-readable progress labels.
// Used by checkRefreshStatus() to show step number and description.
var STAGE_LABELS = {
  'thresholds':         { step: 1, label: 'Generating AI thresholds for all analytes' },
  'waiting_thresholds': { step: 1, label: 'Waiting for threshold AI batch to complete' },
  'views':              { step: 2, label: 'Building Analysis and Results views' },
  'findings':           { step: 3, label: 'Building Significant Findings sheet' },
  'supplements':        { step: 4, label: 'Running supplement/medication analysis' },
  'significance':       { step: 5, label: 'Generating AI significance ratings' }
};

function checkRefreshStatus() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var stage = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  var thRaw = props.getProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES');
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  var pending = triggers.filter(function(t) {
    var fn = t.getHandlerFunction();
    return fn === '_runRefreshChain' || fn === '_continueThresholdRefresh';
  });

  if (!stage && !thRaw && pending.length === 0) {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
    var lastStatus = thSheet ? thSheet.getRange(1, 1).getValue().toString() : '';
    if (lastStatus && lastStatus.indexOf('✅') >= 0) {
      ui.alert('✅ Last Refresh Completed', lastStatus + '\n\nNo refresh currently running.', ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    } else if (lastStatus && lastStatus.indexOf('⚠') >= 0) {
      ui.alert('⚠️ Last Refresh Had an Error', lastStatus + '\n\nTry Refresh Everything again from the menu.', ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    } else {
      ui.alert('ℹ️ No Refresh Running', 'No background refresh is active.\n\nRun: 🏥 menu → Refresh Everything to start one.', ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    }
    return;
  }

  var info = STAGE_LABELS[stage] || {};
  var thStart = parseInt(props.getProperty('TH_JOB_START') || '0');
  var thTotal = thRaw ? JSON.parse(thRaw).length : 0;
  var pct = (thTotal > 0 && (stage === 'waiting_thresholds' || stage === 'thresholds'))
    ? ' (' + thStart + '/' + thTotal + ' analytes = ' + Math.round(thStart / thTotal * 100) + '%)' : '';
  var currentStep = info.step || 1;
  var bar = '';
  for (var i = 1; i <= 5; i++) bar += (i <= currentStep ? '█' : '░');

  var msg = '🔄 REFRESH IN PROGRESS  [' + bar + ']\n\n';
  msg += 'Current step (' + currentStep + '/5):  ' + (info.label || stage) + pct + '\n';
  if (pending.length > 0) msg += 'Next trigger fires in < 60 seconds.\n';
  msg += '\nRemaining steps:\n';
  var nextStages = ['views', 'findings', 'supplements', 'significance'];
  var fromIdx = Math.max(0, nextStages.indexOf(
    (stage === 'thresholds' || stage === 'waiting_thresholds') ? 'views' : stage
  ) + 1);
  for (var j = fromIdx; j < nextStages.length; j++) {
    var ns = STAGE_LABELS[nextStages[j]];
    if (ns) msg += '  • Step ' + ns.step + ': ' + ns.label + '\n';
  }
  msg += '\nNothing to do — runs automatically. Watch the banner at the top of Thresholds or Significant Findings.';

  ui.alert('🔄 Refresh Status', msg, ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}

// Strip markdown code fences and extract first JSON array from AI response
function _extractJSONArray(text) {
  if (!text) throw new Error('Empty response from AI');
  // Remove ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ``` fences
text = text.replace(/```json/gi, '').replace(/```/g, '').trim();
  var m = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*\]/);
  if (!m) throw new Error('No JSON array in response: ' + text.substring(0, 200));
  // Try direct parse first
  try {
    return JSON.parse(m[0]);
  } catch(e1) {
    // If direct parse fails, try cleaning common Claude JSON issues:
    // - Smart quotes → straight quotes
    // - Unescaped newlines inside strings
    try {
      var cleaned = m[0]
        .replace(/[\u2018\u2019]/g, "'")   // smart single quotes
        .replace(/[\u201C\u201D]/g, '"')   // smart double quotes
        .replace(/\n/g, ' ')               // newlines inside strings
        .replace(/\r/g, ' ');              // carriage returns
      return JSON.parse(cleaned);
    } catch(e2) {
      throw new Error('JSON parse failed: ' + e1.message + ' | Cleaned attempt: ' + e2.message);
    }
  }
}
function callAIForThresholds(analyteList, profileContext, today, apiKey) {
  // apiKey param kept for backwards compatibility but routing via callAI()
  //
  // NAMING STRATEGY: We tell the AI to echo back the exact name we sent,
  // then enforce it post-parse. This means the Thresholds sheet always uses
  // the same name as TestResults — no synonym guessing required.
  var nameList = analyteList.join('\n');
  var prompt = 'You are a specialist in clinical pathology. Today is '+today+'.\n\n'
    + 'Generate research-based clinical thresholds for these analytes based on current UK guidelines and published research.\n'
    + profileContext + '\n\n'
    + 'REQUIREMENTS:\n'
    + '1. 2-5 threshold rows per analyte\n'
    + '2. CRITICAL: Every analyte MUST have at least one actionable flagging row with direction "Above" or "Below" (not just Optimal Zone).\n'
    + '3. Cover: lab reference boundary (Above/Below), functional concern zone (Above/Below), optimal zone\n'
    + '4. UK guidelines preferred (NICE, BTA, BCSH, KDIGO, BSH, ESC)\n'
    + '5. Cite specific papers\n'
    + '6. common_causes: plain English bullet points covering diet, supplements, medications, lifestyle, lab artefacts\n\n'
    + 'CRITICAL — ANALYTE NAMING: The "analyte" field in each JSON object MUST be copied '
    + 'character-for-character from the list below. Do NOT rename, expand, abbreviate, or '
    + 'reformat it. "Vitamin B12" must stay "Vitamin B12". "Red blood cell (RBC) count" must '
    + 'stay "Red blood cell (RBC) count". Any deviation will break the lookup.\n\n'
    + 'ANALYTES:\n' + nameList + '\n\n'
    + 'Return ONLY a valid JSON array. Each element:\n'
    + '{"analyte":"EXACT name from the list above — copy it verbatim",'
    + '"threshold":"e.g. < 30 µg/L or > 4.0 mmol/L",'
    + '"direction":"Below or Above or Optimal Zone",'
    + '"meaning":"1-3 sentences",'
    + '"symptoms":"associated symptoms",'
    + '"confidence":"~80% — evidence summary",'
    + '"source":"specific paper or guideline",'
    + '"common_causes":"• reason • reason • reason"}\n\n'
    + 'Reflect most current evidence as of ' + today + '.';

  var text = callAI(prompt, 7000, 'Threshold Refresh');
  var results = _extractJSONArray(text);

  // Post-parse enforcement: overwrite d.analyte with the name we sent,
  // in case the AI ignored the instruction. Exact case-insensitive match first,
  // then substring containment as fallback for stubborn renames.
  var sentIndex = {};
  for (var s = 0; s < analyteList.length; s++) {
    sentIndex[analyteList[s].toLowerCase()] = analyteList[s];
  }
  for (var ri = 0; ri < results.length; ri++) {
    var aiName = (results[ri].analyte || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (sentIndex[aiName]) {
      results[ri].analyte = sentIndex[aiName];
    } else {
      for (var k in sentIndex) {
        if (aiName.indexOf(k) >= 0 || k.indexOf(aiName) >= 0) {
          results[ri].analyte = sentIndex[k];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return results;
}

function writeAllThresholds(sheet, thresholds, dateStr) {
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow >= 4) sheet.getRange(4, 1, Math.max(lastRow-3,1), 10).clearContent().clearFormat();
  // Always use a live timestamp so the column shows exactly when this write happened
  var ts = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss');
  for (var i = 0; i < thresholds.length; i++) {
    var r = 4+i; var d = thresholds[i]; var rowBg = i%2===0?'#f0e6ff':'#faf5ff';
    sheet.getRange(r,1).setValue(d.analyte||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(r,2).setValue(d.threshold||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(r,3).setValue(d.direction||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(r,4).setValue(d.meaning||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r,5).setValue(d.symptoms||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#7c2d12');
    sheet.getRange(r,6).setValue(d.confidence||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(r,7).setValue(d.source||'').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#1e40af');
    sheet.getRange(r,8).setValue(ts).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.getRange(r,9).setValue('AI — ' + getAIProvider()).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#6b21a8').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(r,10).setValue(d.common_causes||'').setBackground('#fffbeb').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#78350f');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 55);
  }
  if (thresholds.length > 0) borders(sheet, 3, 1, thresholds.length+1, 10);
}

function getInvestigationSheet(ss) {
  var active = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var name = active.getName();
  // Check for any investigation tab by text content
  if (name.indexOf('Symptom Investigation') >= 0) return active;
  // Fallback to original name
  return ss.getSheetByName('🔍 Symptom Investigation') || null;
}

// ─── AI: SYMPTOM INVESTIGATION ───────────────────────────────
function runSymptomAnalysis() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
var sheet = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!sheet) { ui.alert('Symptom Investigation sheet not found.'); return; }

  // Read form values
  var symptoms  = sheet.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString();
  var invDateRaw = sheet.getRange(5, 2).getValue();
  var duration  = sheet.getRange(7, 2).getValue().toString();
  var severity  = sheet.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString();
  var changes   = sheet.getRange(9, 2).getValue().toString();

  if (!symptoms || symptoms.length < 5) { ui.alert('Please enter your symptoms in the form first.'); return; }

  // ── Date cutoff logic ────────────────────────────────────────
  // If the investigation date is set and is in the past (more than 1 day ago),
  // treat it as a historic investigation and filter all context to that date.
  var invDate    = (invDateRaw instanceof Date && !isNaN(invDateRaw.getTime())) ? invDateRaw : null;
  if (!invDate && invDateRaw) {
    var parsed = new Date(invDateRaw);
    if (!isNaN(parsed.getTime())) invDate = parsed;
  }

  var today         = new Date();
  var cutoffDate    = null;
  var isHistoric    = false;
  var historicLabel = '';

  if (invDate) {
    var daysDiff = Math.round((today - invDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    if (daysDiff > 1) {
      isHistoric  = true;
      cutoffDate  = invDate;
      historicLabel = Utilities.formatDate(invDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
    }
  }

  // Warn the user about historic filtering before proceeding
  if (isHistoric) {
    var proceed = ui.alert(
      '📅 Historic Investigation Detected',
      'The investigation date is ' + historicLabel + ' — ' +
      Math.round((today - invDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) + ' days ago.\n\n' +
      'To give an accurate analysis for that date, the AI will ONLY see:\n' +
      '  • Test results dated on or before ' + historicLabel + '\n' +
      '  • GP records dated on or before ' + historicLabel + '\n' +
      '  • Medications started on or before ' + historicLabel + '\n' +
      '  • Previous investigations dated on or before ' + historicLabel + '\n\n' +
      'Results, records, and investigations from AFTER that date will be excluded — ' +
      'otherwise the analysis would be contaminated by information that did not exist yet.\n\n' +
      'Continue with date-filtered analysis?',
      ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
    );
    if (proceed !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  }

  // Build context — with or without date cutoff
  var context = buildFullContext(ss, cutoffDate);
  var todayStr = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var invDateStr = invDate ? Utilities.formatDate(invDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy') : todayStr;
  var currentRecordId = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();

  var historicNote = isHistoric
    ? '\n\nIMPORTANT: This investigation has a historic date of ' + invDateStr + '. ' +
      'Your analysis must reflect what was clinically knowable on THAT date only. ' +
      'Do not reference any test results, records, diagnoses, or treatments that occurred after ' + invDateStr + '.'
    : '';

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical AI assistant helping a patient understand their symptoms in the context of their full medical history and test results. Today is ' + todayStr + '.\n' +
    'Investigation date: ' + invDateStr + '.' + historicNote + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT SYMPTOMS (this investigation):\n' +
    'Symptoms: ' + symptoms + '\n' +
    'Duration: ' + duration + '\n' +
    'Severity (1-10): ' + severity + '\n' +
    'Recent changes: ' + changes + '\n' +
    (currentRecordId ? 'Record ID: ' + currentRecordId + '\n' : '') + '\n' +
    context + '\n\n' +
    'INSTRUCTIONS — use the full context above including:\n' +
    '• PREVIOUS SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS: Look for recurring patterns, previously identified contributing factors, and whether past issues were resolved or are persisting.\n' +
    '• RESULT TRENDS: Consider whether values are rising or falling over time, not just the latest snapshot.\n' +
    '• GP / CONSULTATION RECORDS: Consider what GPs have previously assessed or prescribed.\n' +
    '• MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS: Have any been started or stopped that might explain current symptoms?\n\n' +
    'Please provide:\n' +
    '1. ASSESSMENT: How do these symptoms relate to the patient\'s test results, threshold flags, and medical history?\n' +
    '2. CONTRIBUTING FACTORS: Which specific results, trends, medications, supplements, or historical factors are most likely contributing? Cite actual values and dates.\n' +
    '3. PATTERN ANALYSIS: Is this a new issue or a recurring/persisting problem?\n' +
    '4. SUGGESTED TESTS: What additional tests would help clarify the picture?\n' +
    '5. CONFIDENCE: How confident are you in this assessment (%) and why?\n\n' +
    'Be specific and cite actual values and dates from the history. Write in plain English.\n\n' +
    'Format response with clear headings: ASSESSMENT, CONTRIBUTING FACTORS, PATTERN ANALYSIS, SUGGESTED TESTS, CONFIDENCE LEVEL';

  try {
    ui.alert(
      (isHistoric ? '📅 Running historic analysis for ' + historicLabel : 'Running AI analysis') +
      '... this takes 15–30 seconds. Click OK to begin.'
    );
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 2500, 'analyseGPAdvice');

    var assessment  = extractSection(response, 'ASSESSMENT', 'CONTRIBUTING FACTORS');
    var factors     = extractSection(response, 'CONTRIBUTING FACTORS', 'PATTERN ANALYSIS');
    var pattern     = extractSection(response, 'PATTERN ANALYSIS', 'SUGGESTED TESTS');
    var tests       = extractSection(response, 'SUGGESTED TESTS', 'CONFIDENCE LEVEL');
    var confidence  = extractSection(response, 'CONFIDENCE LEVEL', null);

    // Prepend a clear historic notice to the assessment if applicable
    if (isHistoric) {
      var historicBanner = '📅 HISTORIC ANALYSIS — data filtered to ' + historicLabel + '\n' +
        'Only test results, records, and investigations from on or before this date were used.\n\n';
      assessment = historicBanner + (assessment || response);
    }

    sheet.getRange(12, 2).setValue(assessment || response).setWrap(true);
    var factorsAndPattern = (factors || '') + (pattern ? '\n\nPATTERN ANALYSIS:\n' + pattern : '');
    sheet.getRange(13, 2).setValue(factorsAndPattern).setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(14, 2).setValue(tests || '').setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(15, 2).setValue(confidence || '').setWrap(true);

    var ar = [12, 13, 14, 15];
    for (var ri = 0; ri < ar.length; ri++) {
      var cellText = sheet.getRange(ar[ri], 2).getValue().toString();
      var lcount   = cellText.split('\n').length;
      sheet.setRowHeight(ar[ri], Math.max(40, Math.min(350, lcount * 14 + 10)));
    }

    // Auto-set date if empty
    if (!sheet.getRange(5, 2).getValue()) sheet.getRange(5, 2).setValue(new Date()).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');

    ui.alert(
      '✅ AI analysis complete.' +
      (isHistoric ? '\n\n📅 Historic mode: only data from on or before ' + historicLabel + ' was used.' : '') +
      '\n\nScroll down to review the assessment, then:\n• Generate Pre-GP Email\n• Or enter your GP appointment details in Step 4'
    );
  } catch(e) { ui.alert('Error during analysis:\n' + e.toString()); }
}

function analyseGPAdvice() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Symptom Investigation sheet not found.'); return; }

  // STEP 4 rows: 17=section, 18=appt date, 19=GP name, 20=GP advice, 21=tests ordered, 22=prescription
  var gpAdvice     = sheet.getRange(20, 2).getValue().toString();
  var testsOrdered = sheet.getRange(21, 2).getValue().toString();
  var prescription = sheet.getRange(22, 2).getValue().toString();
  var symptoms     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var aiAssessment = sheet.getRange(12, 2).getValue().toString();
  var invDateRaw   = sheet.getRange(5,  2).getValue();  // investigation date (Step 1)
  var apptDateRaw  = sheet.getRange(18, 2).getValue(); // GP appointment date (Step 4)

  if (!gpAdvice || gpAdvice.length < 5) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please enter the GP/Consultant advice in Step 4 first.'); return; }

  // Use the GP appointment date as the context cutoff if it's historic,
  // otherwise fall back to the investigation date.
  // This ensures the AI only sees data that existed at the time of the appointment.
  var cutoffDate = null;
  var isHistoric = false;
  var cutoffLabel = '';
  var today = new Date();

  var apptDate = null;
  if (apptDateRaw instanceof Date && !isNaN(apptDateRaw.getTime())) apptDate = apptDateRaw;
  else if (apptDateRaw) { var pd = new Date(apptDateRaw); if (!isNaN(pd.getTime())) apptDate = pd; }

  var invDate = null;
  if (invDateRaw instanceof Date && !isNaN(invDateRaw.getTime())) invDate = invDateRaw;
  else if (invDateRaw) { var pid = new Date(invDateRaw); if (!isNaN(pid.getTime())) invDate = pid; }

  // Prefer GP appointment date as cutoff; fall back to investigation date
  var referenceDateForCutoff = apptDate || invDate;
  if (referenceDateForCutoff) {
    var daysDiff = Math.round((today - referenceDateForCutoff) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    if (daysDiff > 1) {
      cutoffDate = referenceDateForCutoff;
      isHistoric = true;
      cutoffLabel = Utilities.formatDate(referenceDateForCutoff, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
    }
  }

  var context = buildFullContext(ss, cutoffDate);
  var todayStr = Utilities.formatDate(today, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  var historicNote = isHistoric
    ? '\n\nIMPORTANT: This appointment occurred on ' + cutoffLabel + '. ' +
      'Your analysis must only reference data that existed on or before that date. ' +
      'Do not refer to any results or records that came after ' + cutoffLabel + '.'
    : '';

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical AI assistant helping a patient evaluate advice they received from their GP or consultant. Today is ' + todayStr + '.' + historicNote + '\n\n' +
    'ORIGINAL SYMPTOMS:\n' + symptoms + '\n\n' +
    'PREVIOUS AI ASSESSMENT:\n' + aiAssessment + '\n\n' +
    'GP / CONSULTANT ADVICE:\n' + gpAdvice + '\n' +
    'Tests ordered: ' + testsOrdered + '\n' +
    'Prescription / Treatment: ' + prescription + '\n\n' +
    context + '\n\n' +
    'Please provide:\n' +
    '1. ASSESSMENT OF GP ADVICE: Does this advice align with current published evidence and the patient\'s specific results? What is strong about the advice? What might be missing?\n' +
    '2. RESEARCH ALIGNMENT: Cite specific guidelines or papers that support or question aspects of the advice.\n' +
    '3. CONFIDENCE IN GP ADVICE: How confident are you that this advice is appropriate (%) and why?\n' +
    '4. SUGGESTED FOLLOW-UP: What questions should the patient ask at their next appointment?\n\n' +
    'Be balanced and fair — GPs operate with limited time and information. Write in plain English.\n' +
    'Format with clear headings: ASSESSMENT OF GP ADVICE, RESEARCH ALIGNMENT, CONFIDENCE IN GP ADVICE, SUGGESTED FOLLOW-UP';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Analysing GP advice... click OK to begin (15–30 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 2500);

    var gpAssessment2 = extractSection(response, 'ASSESSMENT OF GP ADVICE', 'RESEARCH ALIGNMENT');
    var alignment     = extractSection(response, 'RESEARCH ALIGNMENT', 'CONFIDENCE IN GP ADVICE');
    var gpConfidence  = extractSection(response, 'CONFIDENCE IN GP ADVICE', 'SUGGESTED FOLLOW-UP');
    var followUp      = extractSection(response, 'SUGGESTED FOLLOW-UP', null);

    if (isHistoric) {
      var banner = '📅 HISTORIC ANALYSIS — context filtered to ' + cutoffLabel + '\n\n';
      gpAssessment2 = banner + (gpAssessment2 || response);
    }

    sheet.getRange(24, 2).setValue(gpAssessment2 || response).setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(25, 2).setValue(alignment || '').setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(26, 2).setValue(gpConfidence || '').setWrap(true);
    sheet.getRange(27, 2).setValue(followUp || '').setWrap(true);

    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      '✅ GP advice analysis complete.' +
      (isHistoric ? '\n\n📅 Historic mode: only data from on or before ' + cutoffLabel + ' was used.' : '') +
      '\n\nReview the assessment, then generate Post-GP Email if needed.'
    );
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error:\n' + e.toString()); }
}

function generatePreGPEmail() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!sheet) return;

  var symptoms    = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var duration    = sheet.getRange(7,  2).getValue().toString();
  var aiAssessment= sheet.getRange(12, 2).getValue().toString();
  var suggestedTests = sheet.getRange(14, 2).getValue().toString();
  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today       = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  var prompt =
    'Write a professional, polite email from a patient to their GP requesting an appointment or tests. Today is '+today+'.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: '+profile+'\n' +
    'SYMPTOMS: '+symptoms+'\n' +
    'DURATION: '+duration+'\n' +
    'AI-identified potential contributing factors and suggested tests:\n'+aiAssessment+'\n\nSuggested tests: '+suggestedTests+'\n\n' +
    'Requirements for the email:\n' +
    '1. Professional but warm tone — patient is advocating for themselves, not demanding\n' +
    '2. Clearly describe symptoms and duration\n' +
    '3. Mention that they have been monitoring their test results and have some specific concerns\n' +
    '4. Request specific tests by name where suggested — include clinical justification briefly\n' +
    '5. Acknowledge the GP\'s expertise and request their guidance\n' +
    '6. End with a specific request (appointment, test referral, phone call)\n\n' +
    'Write the full email including Subject line. Also provide a shorter version suitable for an online GP message/appointment request form (max 250 words).\n\n' +
    'Format:\nFULL EMAIL:\n[full email here]\n\nSHORT VERSION (for online forms):\n[short version here]';

  try {
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 1500, 'GP Letter');
    // Row 16 = section header, row 17 = email field (STEP 3)
    sheet.getRange(17, 2).setValue(response).setWrap(true);
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Pre-GP email generated.\n\nScroll to Step 3 to copy the email text.');
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error generating email:\n'+e.toString()); }
}

function generatePostGPEmail() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!sheet) return;

  var symptoms     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var gpAdvice     = sheet.getRange(20, 2).getValue().toString();
  var gpAssessment = sheet.getRange(24, 2).getValue().toString();
  var followUp     = sheet.getRange(27, 2).getValue().toString();
  var gpName       = sheet.getRange(19, 2).getValue().toString();
  var profile      = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today        = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  if (!gpAdvice || gpAdvice.length < 5) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please enter GP advice (Step 4) and run Analyse GP Advice first.'); return; }

  var prompt =
    'Write a professional, polite email from a patient to their GP following an appointment. Today is '+today+'.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: '+profile+'\n' +
    'GP/CONSULTANT: '+gpName+'\n' +
    'ORIGINAL SYMPTOMS: '+symptoms+'\n' +
    'GP ADVICE RECEIVED: '+gpAdvice+'\n\n' +
    'POINTS TO RAISE (from AI analysis of GP advice — patient is asking politely about these):\n'+gpAssessment+'\n\nSuggested follow-up questions:\n'+followUp+'\n\n' +
    'Requirements:\n' +
    '1. Thank the GP for the appointment — warm and respectful tone\n' +
    '2. Confirm understanding of the advice given\n' +
    '3. Politely raise any concerns or questions — NOT aggressive or accusatory\n' +
    '4. Reference specific research or guidelines briefly where relevant (e.g. "I read that NICE guidelines suggest...")\n' +
    '5. Request any additional tests or considerations as questions, not demands\n' +
    '6. End positively — the patient values the GP\'s expertise and is seeking collaborative care\n\n' +
    'Write the full email including Subject line. Also provide a shorter version (max 250 words).\n\n' +
    'Format:\nFULL EMAIL:\n[full email here]\n\nSHORT VERSION:\n[short version here]';

  try {
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 1500);
    // Row 30 = email field (STEP 6)
    sheet.getRange(30, 2).setValue(response).setWrap(true);
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Post-GP email generated.\n\nScroll to Step 6 to copy the email text.');
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error generating email:\n'+e.toString()); }
}

function saveInvestigation() {
  var ss     = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet  = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Investigation Records');
  if (!sheet || !recSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Required sheets not found.'); return; }

  var existingId = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  var recordId   = existingId || 'INV-' + new Date().getTime().toString().slice(-6);
  var today      = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy');

  var symptoms     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var aiAssessment = sheet.getRange(12, 2).getValue().toString();
  var gpAdvice     = sheet.getRange(20, 2).getValue().toString();
  var gpResponse   = sheet.getRange(24, 2).getValue().toString();
  var status       = sheet.getRange(32, 2).getValue().toString() || 'Draft';
  var notes        = sheet.getRange(33, 2).getValue().toString();
  var invDate      = sheet.getRange(5,  2).getValue();

  // Check if record ID exists — update if so
  var lastRow = recSheet.getLastRow();
  var targetRow = -1;
  if (lastRow >= 4) {
    var ids = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRow-3, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      if (ids[i][0].toString() === recordId) { targetRow = 4+i; break; }
    }
  }
  if (targetRow === -1) targetRow = Math.max(lastRow+1, 4);
