  var dateStr = invDate instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(invDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy') : today;
  var rowData = [
    recordId, dateStr,
    symptoms.substring(0, 100)+(symptoms.length>100?'...':''),
    aiAssessment.substring(0, 150)+(aiAssessment.length>150?'...':''),
    gpAdvice.length > 3 ? 'Yes' : 'No',
    gpAdvice.substring(0, 100)+(gpAdvice.length>100?'...':''),
    gpResponse.substring(0, 150)+(gpResponse.length>150?'...':''),
    status, notes, today
  ];

  recSheet.getRange(targetRow, 1, 1, 10).setValues([rowData]).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
  recSheet.setRowHeight(targetRow, 40);
  sheet.getRange(4, 2).setValue(recordId);

  // Also store the full investigation text in Script Properties for AI context
  // Key: INV_FULL_<recordId>  Value: JSON with full symptom, AI, GP, and notes text
  // Script Properties has a 9KB per-value limit — truncate if needed but keep most important parts
  var fullData = {
    id:         recordId,
    date:       dateStr,
    symptoms:   symptoms.substring(0, 800),
    duration:   sheet.getRange(7, 2).getValue().toString().substring(0, 100),
    severity:   sheet.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString(),
    aiAssess:   aiAssessment.substring(0, 1200),
    gpAdvice:   gpAdvice.substring(0, 400),
    aiOnGP:     sheet.getRange(24, 2).getValue().toString().substring(0, 600),
    status:     status,
    notes:      notes.substring(0, 200)
  };
  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('INV_FULL_' + recordId, JSON.stringify(fullData));
    // Keep an index of all saved investigation IDs
    var invIndex = [];
    try { invIndex = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]'); } catch(e) {}
    if (invIndex.indexOf(recordId) < 0) invIndex.push(recordId);
    // Keep only latest 20 to avoid property storage limits
    if (invIndex.length > 20) invIndex = invIndex.slice(invIndex.length - 20);
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('INV_INDEX', JSON.stringify(invIndex));
  } catch(propErr) {}  // silently fail — sheet record is the source of truth

  // Auto quick-refresh: rebuild Analysis+Findings so records stay current (no AI call)
  _quickRefreshSilent(ss);

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Investigation saved.\nRecord ID: '+recordId+'\n\nView in 📋 Investigation Records tab.');
}

function loadInvestigation() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var result = ui.prompt('Load Investigation','Enter the Record ID to load (e.g. INV-123456).\nFind IDs in the 📋 Investigation Records tab, column A.',ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (result.getSelectedButton()!==ui.Button.OK) return;
  var recordId = result.getResponseText().trim();
  if (!recordId) return;

  var ss       = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Investigation Records');
  var sheet    = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!recSheet || !sheet) { ui.alert('Required sheets not found.'); return; }

  var lastRow = recSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 4) { ui.alert('No records found.'); return; }
  var data = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRow-3, 10).getValues();
  var found = null;
  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { if (data[i][0].toString()===recordId) { found = data[i]; break; } }
  if (!found) { ui.alert('Record ID "'+recordId+'" not found in Investigation Records.'); return; }

  // Populate form with saved data (summaries — user will see the investigation was loaded)
  sheet.getRange(4,  2).setValue(found[0]);
  sheet.getRange(6,  2).setValue(found[2].replace('...',''));
  sheet.getRange(20, 2).setValue(found[5].replace('...',''));
  sheet.getRange(32, 2).setValue(found[7]);
  sheet.getRange(33, 2).setValue(found[8]);

  ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);
  ui.alert('✅ Record '+recordId+' loaded.\n\nNote: Full AI analysis text is not stored in the records table (only summaries). Re-run AI Analysis to regenerate full assessments if needed.');
}

function clearInvestigationForm() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var confirm = ui.alert('Clear Form','This will clear all fields in the Symptom Investigation form.\n\nMake sure you have saved this investigation first (🏥 menu → Save Investigation).\n\nContinue?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = getInvestigationSheet(ss);
  if (!sheet) return;
  var clearRows = [4,5,6,7,8,9,12,13,14,15,17,18,19,20,21,22,24,25,26,27,30,32,33];
  for (var i = 0; i < clearRows.length; i++) {
    var cell = sheet.getRange(clearRows[i], 2);
    var bg   = cell.getBackground();
    if (bg === C.inputYellow || bg === '#fffde7') { cell.setValue(''); }
    else { cell.setValue(i < 12 ? '' : '[ Run AI from 🏥 menu ]'); }
  }
  ui.alert('✅ Form cleared. Ready for a new investigation.');
}


// ─── AI: TREATMENT TRACKER ───────────────────────────────────
// ─── ADD NEW TREATMENT TRACKER ──────────────────────────────
// Creates a new Treatment Tracker tab named after the selected analyte.
// Multiple trackers can coexist — e.g. "📈 Ferritin", "📈 Vitamin D".
function addNewTreatmentTracker() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // Build analyte list
  var trSheet   = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!trSheet) { ui.alert('TestResults sheet not found.'); return; }
  var trData    = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
  var latestRes = collectLatestResults(trData);
  var analyteNames = [];
  for (var k in latestRes) analyteNames.push(latestRes[k].analyte);
  analyteNames.sort();

  if (analyteNames.length === 0) { ui.alert('No test results found. Paste SAR data into TestResults first.'); return; }

  var result = ui.prompt(
    '➕ New Treatment Tracker',
    'Enter the analyte name to create a tracker for.\n\n' +
    'Available analytes:\n' + analyteNames.slice(0, 25).join('\n') +
    (analyteNames.length > 25 ? '\n... and ' + (analyteNames.length - 25) + ' more' : ''),
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
  );
  if (result.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var analyte = result.getResponseText().trim();
  if (!analyte) return;

  var tabName = '📈 ' + analyte;
  // Check if already exists
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
  if (existing) {
    var overwrite = ui.alert('Tab "' + tabName + '" already exists.\n\nSwitch to it?', ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
    if (overwrite === ui.Button.YES) ss.setActiveSheet(existing);
    return;
  }

  // Insert after the last 📈 tab, or after 📋 Investigation Records
  var sheets = ss.getSheets();
  var insertAfterIdx = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < sheets.length; i++) {
    if (sheets[i].getName().indexOf('📈') === 0 || sheets[i].getName() === '📋 Investigation Records') {
      insertAfterIdx = i;
    }
  }
  var newSheet = ss.insertSheet(tabName, insertAfterIdx + 1);
  buildTreatmentTrackerSheet(newSheet, ss);

  // Pre-select the analyte
  newSheet.getRange(4, 2).setValue(analyte);
  ss.setActiveSheet(newSheet);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ New tracker created: "' + tabName + '"\n\n' +
    'Analyte pre-selected: ' + analyte + '\n\n' +
    'Next: 🏥 menu → Treatment Tracker → ① Populate Treatment Setup'
  );
}

// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: POPULATE SETUP ─────────────────────
// Called after user selects an analyte from the dropdown.
// Reads latest result, looks up thresholds, reads medication log,
// calls AI to suggest supplement/dose/target, populates the form.
function populateTreatmentSetup() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  // Work on the active sheet if it's a treatment tracker, otherwise use default
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker sheet found. Use 🏥 → Add New Treatment Tracker first.'); return; }

  // Ask user to select analyte via prompt (dropdown in sheet is visual only)
  // Build list of available analytes from TestResults
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!trSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('TestResults sheet not found.'); return; }

  var trData   = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
  var latestRes = collectLatestResults(trData);
  var analyteNames = [];
  for (var k in latestRes) analyteNames.push(latestRes[k].analyte);
  analyteNames.sort();

  if (analyteNames.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No test results found. Paste SAR data into TestResults first.');
    return;
  }

  // Show current value in row 4 col 2 as the selected analyte
  var selectedAnalyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();

  // If nothing selected yet, prompt user to type one
  if (!selectedAnalyte || selectedAnalyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    var ui     = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
    var result = ui.prompt(
      'Select Analyte to Track',
      'Enter the analyte name exactly as it appears in your TestResults.\n\n' +
      'Your analytes with results:\n' + analyteNames.slice(0, 20).join('\n') +
      (analyteNames.length > 20 ? '\n... and ' + (analyteNames.length - 20) + ' more' : ''),
      ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
    );
    if (result.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
    selectedAnalyte = result.getResponseText().trim();
    if (!selectedAnalyte) return;
    sheet.getRange(4, 2).setValue(selectedAnalyte);
  }

  // Find the latest result for this analyte (case-insensitive)
  var aKey = selectedAnalyte.toLowerCase();
  var res  = latestRes[aKey];
  if (!res) {
    // Try partial match
    for (var k2 in latestRes) {
      if (k2.indexOf(aKey) >= 0 || aKey.indexOf(k2) >= 0) { res = latestRes[k2]; break; }
    }
  }
  if (!res) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No result found for "' + selectedAnalyte + '".\nCheck the name matches your TestResults exactly.');
    return;
  }

  // Populate latest result (row 5) and date (row 7)
  sheet.getRange(5, 2).setValue(res.value + ' ' + res.unit + '  (tested ' + formatDate(res.date) + ')')
    .setBackground('#dcfce7').setFontColor('#166534').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10);
  sheet.getRange(7, 2).setValue(new Date()).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy')
    .setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontColor(C.darkGrey);

  // Read thresholds for this analyte to include in AI prompt
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  var thresholdContext = '';
  if (thSheet) {
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var thRows = thMap[aKey] || findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap);
    if (thRows && thRows.length > 0) {
      thresholdContext = 'RESEARCH THRESHOLDS FOR THIS ANALYTE:\n' +
        thRows.map(function(t){ return t[1] + ' (' + t[2] + '): ' + t[3]; }).join('\n');
    }
  }

  // Read medication log for absorption-affecting meds
  var medContext = getMedicationContext(ss);

  // Get profile
  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var profileFull = getProfileContext(ss);
  var today       = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  // Call AI to suggest treatment plan
  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical specialist helping set up a treatment monitoring plan. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'ANALYTE TO TREAT: ' + res.analyte + '\n' +
    'CURRENT VALUE: ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' (tested ' + formatDate(res.date) + ')\n' +
    'LAB REFERENCE RANGE: ' + (res.refRange || 'not available') + '\n' +
    'LAB STATUS: ' + (res.status || 'not recorded') + '\n\n' +
    thresholdContext + '\n\n' +
    medContext + '\n\n' +
    'Please provide a treatment setup recommendation in EXACTLY this JSON format (no other text):\n' +
    '{\n' +
    '  "target_value": "e.g. 75 µg/L — the research-optimal target, not just lab minimum",\n' +
    '  "target_rationale": "1-2 sentences explaining why this target",\n' +
    '  "supplement_name": "e.g. Ferrous Fumarate 210mg tablets",\n' +
    '  "dose_amount": 210,\n' +
    '  "dose_unit": "mg",\n' +
    '  "times_per_day": 2,\n' +
    '  "dosing_notes": "e.g. Take with Vitamin C, avoid tea/coffee within 1hr, take on empty stomach if tolerated",\n' +
    '  "menstrual_question": "A plain-English question to ask the patient about their periods/hormonal status — ONLY if this analyte is affected by menstrual blood loss (iron, ferritin, haemoglobin etc). Leave empty string if not relevant.",\n' +
    '  "diet_factors": "key dietary factors affecting this analyte",\n' +
    '  "absorption_meds": "any medications from the patient profile known to affect this analyte",\n' +
    '  "other_factors": "other relevant factors (inflammation, B12 for iron absorption, Vit K2/Mg for Vit D etc)",\n' +
    '  "monitoring_frequency": "e.g. Retest at 3 months minimum"\n' +
    '}';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Getting AI treatment suggestions for ' + res.analyte + '...\nClick OK to begin (10–20 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 800, 'Significance Rating');

    // Parse JSON
    var jsonMatch = response.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    if (!jsonMatch) throw new Error('AI did not return valid JSON. Response: ' + response.substring(0, 200));
    var ai = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);

    // Populate target value (row 6)
    sheet.getRange(6, 2)
      .setValue((ai.target_value || '') + (ai.target_rationale ? '\n\n(' + ai.target_rationale + ')' : ''))
      .setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(6, 55);

    // Populate supplement (row 8)
    sheet.getRange(8, 2)
      .setValue((ai.supplement_name || '') + (ai.dosing_notes ? '\n\nTips: ' + ai.dosing_notes : ''))
      .setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(8, 55);

    // Populate dose (row 9) and times (row 10)
    if (ai.dose_amount) sheet.getRange(9, 2).setValue(ai.dose_amount).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontColor(C.darkGrey).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold');
    if (ai.times_per_day) sheet.getRange(10, 2).setValue(ai.times_per_day).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontColor(C.darkGrey).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold');

    // ── Relevant Factors section ─────────────────────────────

    // Row 13: Menstrual/hormonal — only populate with a specific question if relevant
    if (ai.menstrual_question && ai.menstrual_question.length > 5) {
      sheet.getRange(13, 2)
        .setValue('⚠️ PLEASE COMPLETE: ' + ai.menstrual_question + '\n\n' +
                  'Examples:\n' +
                  '• Heavy periods, 5–7 days, every 28 days\n' +
                  '• Light periods on combined pill, 3 days every 28 days\n' +
                  '• No periods — on Mirena IUD / Depo / continuous pill\n' +
                  '• Post-menopausal — no periods for 3+ years\n' +
                  '• Pregnant / breastfeeding\n\n' +
                  'This significantly affects the expected rate of improvement.')
        .setBackground('#fef08a').setFontColor('#713f12').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontWeight('bold');
      sheet.setRowHeight(13, 110);
    } else {
      sheet.getRange(13, 2)
        .setValue('Not applicable for this analyte.')
        .setBackground(C.offWhite).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setFontSize(9);
      sheet.setRowHeight(13, 22);
    }

    // Row 14: Lifestyle/diet recommendations (AI-populated)
    sheet.getRange(14, 2)
      .setValue(ai.diet_factors || 'No specific dietary or lifestyle recommendations identified for this analyte.')
      .setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(14, 60);

    // Row 15: Effect of current supplements/medications (AI-populated)
    var medText = ai.absorption_meds || '';
    if (!medText || medText.length < 5) medText = 'No medications or supplements from your log appear to significantly affect ' + res.analyte + '.';
    sheet.getRange(15, 2)
      .setValue(medText)
      .setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(15, 50);

    // Set baseline row in milestone table (row 21 — first data row)
    sheet.getRange(21, 5).setValue(res.value);
    sheet.getRange(21, 6).setValue(res.unit);
    sheet.getRange(21, 7).setValue('—').setFontColor(C.midGrey);
    sheet.getRange(21, 3).setValue(res.date).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
    sheet.getRange(21, 4).setValue(res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' (baseline)').setFontColor('#166534');
    sheet.getRange(21, 2).setValue(new Date()).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');

    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      '✅ Treatment setup populated for: ' + res.analyte + '\n\n' +
      'PLEASE CHECK:\n' +
      (ai.menstrual_question ? '⚠️  Complete the MENSTRUAL/HORMONAL FACTORS field — this affects the trajectory significantly\n' : '') +
      '• Verify the suggested supplement and dose match what your GP prescribed\n' +
      '• Adjust target value if your GP suggested a different goal\n' +
      '• Add any other relevant factors in the yellow fields\n\n' +
      'Then: 🏥 menu → Treatment Tracker → Generate Treatment Trajectory'
    );

  } catch(e) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error getting AI suggestions:\n' + e.toString() +
      '\n\nYou can fill in the fields manually.');
  }
}




// ─── SUPPLEMENT ↔ RESULT QUICK CROSS-REFERENCE ───────────────
// Rule-based lookup of well-established supplement/med → analyte
// interactions. Runs instantly (no AI call). Used by buildSignificantFindingsRows.
// Returns array of { supplement, analytes: [...], note } objects
// filtered to what the patient is actually taking.
function buildQuickSupplementInteractions(ss) {
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  if (!medSheet) return [];

  var medData  = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 3).getValues();
  var taking   = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < medData.length; i++) {
    if (medData[i][0]) taking.push(medData[i][0].toString().toLowerCase().trim());
  }
  if (taking.length === 0) return [];

  // Known high-confidence interactions (analyte names in lowercase)
  // Format: { keywords: ['match if any in supplement name'], analytes: ['match if any in analyte name'], note }
  var RULES = [
    // Biotin
    { kw: ['biotin'],
      analytes: ['tsh','t3','t4','thyroid','thyroxine','triiodothyronine'],
      note: 'Biotin can cause falsely LOW TSH, T3, T4 in immunoassay tests (competitive biotin-streptavidin interference). Stop biotin 3–5 days before thyroid tests for accurate results.' },
    { kw: ['biotin'],
      analytes: ['troponin','cortisol','ferritin','b12','vitamin b12'],
      note: 'High-dose biotin can interfere with many immunoassay tests — may cause falsely high or low results depending on assay type. Discuss with GP if taking >5mg/day.' },
    // Saffron
    { kw: ['saffron'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','alp','bilirubin','liver','ggt','alanine','aspartate','alkaline phosphatase'],
      note: 'High-dose saffron (>1.5g/day) has been associated with hepatotoxicity. If taking saffron supplements, elevated liver enzymes should be considered potentially supplement-related.' },
    { kw: ['saffron'],
      analytes: ['tsh','thyroid'],
      note: 'Saffron may have mild thyroid-modulating effects in some studies. Flagged for awareness.' },
    // Ginseng (Panax, Korean, Asian, American)
    { kw: ['ginseng'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','alp','ggt','liver','bilirubin','alanine','aspartate','alkaline phosphatase'],
      note: 'Ginseng has been reported to cause hepatotoxicity (raised liver enzymes) in some cases, particularly with high doses or prolonged use. May explain elevated liver markers.' },
    { kw: ['ginseng'],
      analytes: ['glucose','hba1c','insulin'],
      note: 'Ginseng has hypoglycaemic properties and may lower blood glucose. Could explain lower glucose readings and interact with diabetic medications.' },
    { kw: ['ginseng'],
      analytes: ['inr','prothrombin','warfarin','coagulation'],
      note: 'Ginseng can affect coagulation and may interact with warfarin — if on anticoagulants, discuss with GP.' },
    // Iron
    { kw: ['iron','ferrous','ferric','spatone'],
      analytes: ['ferritin','iron','tibc','transferrin','haemoglobin','haematocrit','mcv','mchc'],
      note: 'Iron supplementation directly raises serum iron, ferritin, and haemoglobin. Results may reflect supplementation rather than dietary status — timing of last dose before test matters (acute iron spike vs stored ferritin).' },
    // Vitamin D
    { kw: ['vitamin d','vit d','cholecalciferol','ergocalciferol','d3','d2'],
      analytes: ['25-oh','vitamin d','vit d','calcium','phosphate'],
      note: 'Vitamin D supplementation directly raises 25-OH Vitamin D. High-dose (>4000IU/day) may raise calcium — monitor both. Result reflects both supplementation and sun exposure.' },
    // B12
    { kw: ['b12','vitamin b12','cyanocobalamin','methylcobalamin','hydroxocobalamin'],
      analytes: ['b12','vitamin b12','cobalamin'],
      note: 'B12 supplementation will raise serum B12 — timing of last dose before test matters significantly. Active B12 (holotranscobalamin) is a better marker of functional status than total B12.' },
    // Folic acid / folate
    { kw: ['folic','folate','methylfolate'],
      analytes: ['folate','folic','homocysteine'],
      note: 'Folate supplementation raises red cell and serum folate. Also lowers homocysteine (often to normal range even when functional deficiency exists). May mask true folate status.' },
    // Fish oil / Omega-3
    { kw: ['fish oil','omega','epa','dha','cod liver'],
      analytes: ['triglyceride','cholesterol','ldl','hdl'],
      note: 'Omega-3/fish oil significantly lowers triglycerides (expected effect). May also slightly lower LDL. Results should be interpreted knowing the patient is taking omega-3.' },
    { kw: ['fish oil','omega','epa','dha','cod liver'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','liver'],
      note: 'Very high dose fish oil (>3g/day EPA+DHA) rarely associated with mild liver enzyme elevation.' },
    // Metformin
    { kw: ['metformin'],
      analytes: ['b12','vitamin b12','cobalamin'],
      note: 'Metformin reduces B12 absorption in ~30% of long-term users (NICE-recommended monitoring). Low B12 in a metformin user is very likely medication-related. Discuss with GP.' },
    // PPIs / antacids
    { kw: ['omeprazole','lansoprazole','pantoprazole','esomeprazole','rabeprazole','ranitidine','ppi','antacid','gaviscon'],
      analytes: ['iron','ferritin','b12','vitamin b12','magnesium'],
      note: 'PPIs reduce stomach acid, significantly impairing absorption of iron, B12, and magnesium with long-term use. Low levels of these in a PPI user is very likely absorption-related.' },
    // Statins
    { kw: ['statin','simvastatin','atorvastatin','rosuvastatin','pravastatin','fluvastatin'],
      analytes: ['ck','creatine kinase','alt','ast','liver'],
      note: 'Statins can cause myopathy (raised CK) and rarely hepatotoxicity (raised liver enzymes). Elevated CK or liver enzymes in a statin user should be discussed with GP.' },
    { kw: ['statin','simvastatin','atorvastatin','rosuvastatin','pravastatin'],
      analytes: ['coq10','vitamin d'],
      note: 'Statins reduce endogenous CoQ10 synthesis. Some evidence they also impair Vitamin D synthesis — low Vit D in statin users may be partially medication-related.' },
    // Magnesium
    { kw: ['magnesium'],
      analytes: ['magnesium','calcium','potassium'],
      note: 'Magnesium supplementation raises serum magnesium. Also works synergistically with Vitamin D and calcium metabolism — relevant if any of these are flagged.' },
    // Zinc
    { kw: ['zinc'],
      analytes: ['copper','zinc'],
      note: 'High-dose zinc supplementation (>25mg/day long-term) depletes copper by competing for absorption. Low copper or raised zinc should be considered supplement-related.' },
    // Vitamin A (high dose)
    { kw: ['vitamin a','retinol','retinyl'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','alp','liver','bilirubin'],
      note: 'High-dose Vitamin A (>10,000IU/day) is hepatotoxic and can significantly raise liver enzymes. If taking Vit A supplements and liver markers are flagged, this is a priority concern.' },
    // Ashwagandha
    { kw: ['ashwagandha','withania'],
      analytes: ['tsh','thyroid','t3','t4'],
      note: 'Ashwagandha stimulates thyroid function and can raise T3 and T4 levels, sometimes causing them to appear optimal when they would otherwise be flagged low — or rarely causing subclinical hyperthyroidism.' },
    { kw: ['ashwagandha','withania'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','liver'],
      note: 'Ashwagandha has been associated with rare cases of hepatotoxicity. If liver enzymes are elevated and patient is taking ashwagandha, this should be raised with GP.' },
    // Turmeric / Curcumin
    { kw: ['turmeric','curcumin'],
      analytes: ['alt','ast','liver','bilirubin'],
      note: 'High-dose curcumin/turmeric supplements have been associated with hepatotoxicity in some reports. Standard culinary amounts are safe but supplement doses (1g+/day) may affect liver enzymes.' },
    { kw: ['turmeric','curcumin'],
      analytes: ['inr','prothrombin'],
      note: 'Curcumin has anticoagulant properties — may affect INR/coagulation tests, particularly if on warfarin.' },
    // St John\'s Wort
    { kw: ["st john", "st. john", "hypericum"],
      analytes: ['cyclosporin','warfarin','contraceptive','oestrogen'],
      note: "St John's Wort is a potent CYP450 inducer — it significantly reduces blood levels of many medications including warfarin, combined pill, and antidepressants. Discuss with GP if taking with any prescription medications." },
    // DHEA
    { kw: ['dhea','dehydroepiandrosterone'],
      analytes: ['testosterone','oestradiol','estradiol','dhea','dheas'],
      note: 'DHEA supplementation raises DHEA-S and can convert to testosterone and oestrogen — directly affects results of sex hormone tests. Results should be interpreted knowing patient is taking DHEA.' },
    // Creatine
    { kw: ['creatine'],
      analytes: ['creatinine','gfr','egfr'],
      note: 'Creatine supplementation raises serum creatinine and can lower eGFR estimates — this is not kidney damage but a pharmacological effect on creatinine metabolism. May cause falsely low eGFR.' },
    // Green tea / EGCG
    { kw: ['green tea','egcg','epigallocatechin'],
      analytes: ['iron','ferritin'],
      note: 'Green tea extract (EGCG) inhibits iron absorption when taken with or near meals/iron supplements. May contribute to low ferritin even in patients taking iron supplements.' },
  ];

  var matches = [];
  for (var r = 0; r < RULES.length; r++) {
    var rule = RULES[r];
    // Check if patient is taking anything matching the keyword list
    var matchingSupplement = '';
    for (var t = 0; t < taking.length; t++) {
      for (var k = 0; k < rule.kw.length; k++) {
        if (taking[t].indexOf(rule.kw[k]) >= 0) {
          matchingSupplement = taking[t]; break;
        }
      }
      if (matchingSupplement) break;
    }
    if (matchingSupplement) {
      matches.push({
        supplement: matchingSupplement,
        analytes:   rule.analytes,
        note:       rule.note
      });
    }
  }
  return matches;
}



// ─── ANALYSE SUPPLEMENTS & MEDICATIONS ──────────────────────
// For each item in the Meds & Supplements Log, calls AI to assess it
// against the patient's full profile and test results.
// Writes personalised analysis to col 10 (J) of the med log.
// Also flags any supplement that may be contributing to flagged findings.
function analyseSupplements() {
  var ss       = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui       = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  if (!medSheet) { ui.alert('Meds & Supplements Log sheet not found.'); return; }

  var medData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
  var items   = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < medData.length; i++) {
    if (!medData[i][0]) continue;
    var stopped = medData[i][6] ? true : false;
    items.push({
      row:      i + 5,
      name:     medData[i][0].toString(),
      type:     medData[i][1].toString(),
      dose:     medData[i][2].toString(),
      freq:     medData[i][3].toString(),
      total:    medData[i][4].toString(),
      started:  medData[i][5],
      stopped:  stopped,
      notes:    medData[i][8].toString()
    });
  }

  if (items.length === 0) {
    ui.alert('No medications or supplements found in the Meds & Supplements Log.\n\nAdd them first, then run this analysis.');
    return;
  }

  // Build full patient context
  var profile    = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today      = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  // Auto-detect conditions from across the workbook (records, meds indications,
  // profile, saved investigations) so AI is never blind to diagnoses like POTS.
  // A GP record saying "confirmed POTS" is picked up automatically — no manual
  // entry required from the user.
  var detectedConditions = [];
  try {
    detectedConditions = _sweepWorkbookForConditions(ss);
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log('analyseSupplements: condition sweep failed — ' + e);
  }
  var conditionsStr = detectedConditions.length > 0
    ? detectedConditions.join(', ')
    : 'None auto-detected — treat with caution, patient may have conditions not listed';

  // Collect all current test results and flags for context
  var trSheet    = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet    = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  var resultsStr = '';
  var flagsStr   = '';
  if (trSheet && thSheet) {
    var trData   = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var thData   = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    var thMap    = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var results  = collectLatestResults(trData);
    var resLines = [], flagLines = [];
    for (var aKey in results) {
      var res = results[aKey];
      resLines.push(res.analyte + ': ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' (lab: ' + res.status + ')');
      var thRows = thMap[aKey] || findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap);
      if (thRows) {
        for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) {
          if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) {
            flagLines.push(res.analyte + ' ' + res.value + ' ' + res.unit + ' — ' + thRows[b][1]);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    resultsStr = resLines.join('\n');
    flagsStr   = flagLines.join('\n');
  }

  // Build the list of items for the AI prompt
  var itemList = items.map(function(it, idx) {
    return (idx + 1) + '. ' + it.name +
           (it.dose ? ' — ' + it.dose : '') +
           (it.freq ? ' × ' + it.freq + '/day' : '') +
           (it.total ? ' = ' + it.total + ' total' : '') +
           (it.type ? ' [' + it.type + ']' : '') +
           (it.stopped ? ' [STOPPED]' : ' [CURRENT]') +
           (it.notes ? ' — Note: ' + it.notes : '');
  }).join('\n');

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical pharmacist and nutritional specialist reviewing a patient\'s medications and supplements. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'DETECTED CONDITIONS (auto-swept from GP records, meds indications, profile, saved investigations):\n' + conditionsStr + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT TEST RESULTS:\n' + (resultsStr || 'No results available') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED RESULTS (values outside research thresholds):\n' + (flagsStr || 'None flagged') + '\n\n' +
    'MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS TO ASSESS:\n' + itemList + '\n\n' +
    'For EACH numbered item above, provide a JSON object. Return a JSON ARRAY in the same order.\n' +
    'Each object MUST have EXACTLY these fields:\n' +
    '{\n' +
    '  "item_number": 1,\n' +
    '  "name": "exact name as provided",\n' +
    '  "overall_verdict": "one of: ✅ Good choice for your profile | ⚠️ Use with caution | 🔄 Consider alternatives | ❌ Recommend stopping | ℹ️ Prescription — discuss with GP",\n' +
    '  "suitability_for_profile": "Is this a good idea for THIS patient? Consider their age, sex, hormonal status, conditions, other supplements. 2-3 sentences.",\n' +
    '  "interactions_with_results": "Does this supplement/medication likely explain any of their flagged or abnormal results? e.g. biotin causes falsely low TSH/T4, ginseng can raise liver enzymes, high-dose Vit A causes raised ALT, omega-3 can lower triglycerides. State NONE if not relevant.",\n' +
    '  "interactions_with_other_items": "Does this interact with anything else in their list? e.g. iron + calcium compete, Vit D + K2 work synergistically, some herbs affect cytochrome P450. NONE if not relevant.",\n' +
    '  "dose_assessment": "Is their dose appropriate for their profile and goals? Too low / appropriate / too high? Suggest adjustment if warranted.",\n' +
    '  "timing_advice": "When and how should this be taken for best effect? e.g. iron on empty stomach with Vit C away from tea/coffee",\n' +
    '  "cautions": "Any specific cautions for this patient given their profile and results? NONE if no specific cautions.",\n' +
    '  "recommendation": "Specific actionable recommendation in 1-2 sentences."\n' +
    '}\n\n' +
    'IMPORTANT: Apply condition-appropriate clinical reasoning for every detected condition listed above. Do not assess supplements in isolation from the patient\'s diagnoses — a supplement that looks concerning in a healthy person may be essential management for a diagnosed condition, and vice versa.\n' +
    'Be honest — if something is likely causing a flagged result, say so clearly. If a supplement is genuinely inappropriate for this patient, say so.\n' +
    'Return ONLY the JSON array, no other text.';

  try {
    ui.alert('Analysing ' + items.length + ' medication(s)/supplement(s) against your profile and test results...\\nClick OK to begin (20–40 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 3500, 'Treatment Trajectory');

  var ratings;
try {
  ratings = _extractJSONArray(response);
} catch(e) {
  throw new Error('AI did not return a JSON array.\n\nResponse preview: ' + response.substring(0, 300));
}
    if (!Array.isArray(ratings)) throw new Error('AI returned invalid data structure.');

    var warnings = []; // Collect items flagged as potentially explaining test results

    for (var r = 0; r < ratings.length && r < items.length; r++) {
      var rating = ratings[r];
      var item   = items[r];
      var cell   = medSheet.getRange(item.row, 10);

      // Determine cell background by verdict
      var verdict = rating.overall_verdict || '';
      var cellBg  = verdict.indexOf('✅') >= 0 ? '#f0fdf4'
                  : verdict.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0 ? '#fef9c3'
                  : verdict.indexOf('🔄') >= 0 ? '#eff6ff'
                  : verdict.indexOf('❌') >= 0 ? '#fef2f2'
                  :                              '#f5f5f5';
      var cellFg  = verdict.indexOf('✅') >= 0 ? '#166534'
                  : verdict.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0 ? '#713f12'
                  : verdict.indexOf('🔄') >= 0 ? '#1e40af'
                  : verdict.indexOf('❌') >= 0 ? '#7f1d1d'
                  :                              '#374151';

      // Build cell text
      var lines = [verdict];
      if (rating.suitability_for_profile) lines.push('\n' + rating.suitability_for_profile);
      var intResults = rating.interactions_with_results || '';
      if (intResults && intResults.toUpperCase() !== 'NONE' && intResults.length > 4) {
        lines.push('\n⚠️ RESULT INTERACTION: ' + intResults);
        warnings.push('• ' + item.name + ': ' + intResults);
      }
      var intOther = rating.interactions_with_other_items || '';
      if (intOther && intOther.toUpperCase() !== 'NONE' && intOther.length > 4) {
        lines.push('\n🔗 Interaction with other items: ' + intOther);
      }
      if (rating.dose_assessment) lines.push('\n💊 Dose: ' + rating.dose_assessment);
      if (rating.timing_advice)   lines.push('\n⏰ Timing: ' + rating.timing_advice);
      var cautions = rating.cautions || '';
      if (cautions && cautions.toUpperCase() !== 'NONE' && cautions.length > 4) {
        lines.push('\n⚠️ Caution: ' + cautions);
      }
      if (rating.recommendation) lines.push('\n✅ Recommendation: ' + rating.recommendation);

      cell.setValue(lines.join('')).setBackground(cellBg).setFontColor(cellFg)
        .setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      medSheet.setRowHeight(item.row, Math.max(80, Math.min(220, lines.join('').split('\n').length * 14)));
    }

    // Store supplement-result interactions for use by buildSignificantFindingsRows
    // Store as a script property so it's available without re-running the analysis
    var interactionStore = {};
    for (var w = 0; w < ratings.length; w++) {
      var ir = ratings[w].interactions_with_results || '';
      if (ir && ir.toUpperCase() !== 'NONE' && ir.length > 4) {
        interactionStore[items[w].name] = ir;
      }
    }
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
      'SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS',
      JSON.stringify(interactionStore)
    );
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(
      'SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS_DATE',
      today
    );

    ss.setActiveSheet(medSheet);

    var alertMsg = '✅ AI analysis complete for ' + ratings.length + ' item(s).\n\n' +
                   'Column J now shows personalised assessment for each supplement/medication.\n\n';
    if (warnings.length > 0) {
      alertMsg += '⚠️ POSSIBLE RESULT INTERACTIONS DETECTED:\n' + warnings.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
                  'These may be explaining some of your flagged test results.\n' +
                  'Run 🏥 → Refresh Everything to update Significant Findings with these interactions.';
    } else {
      alertMsg += 'No supplement-result interactions detected for your current results.';
    }
    ui.alert(alertMsg);

  } catch(e) {
    ui.alert('Error analysing supplements:\n' + e.toString().substring(0, 300));
  }
}


function getMedicationContext(ss) {
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  if (!medSheet) return '';
  var medData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 500, 9).getValues();
  var lines   = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < medData.length; i++) {
    if (!medData[i][0]) continue;
    var stopped = medData[i][6] ? ' (stopped ' + formatDate(medData[i][6]) + ')' : ' (current)';
    var notes   = medData[i][8] ? ' — Indication/notes: ' + medData[i][8].toString().trim() : '';
    lines.push(medData[i][0] + ' ' + medData[i][2] + ' x ' + medData[i][3] + '/day' + stopped + notes);
  }
  return lines.length > 0 ? 'PATIENT MEDICATIONS / SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + lines.join('\n') : '';
}


// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: GENERATE TRAJECTORY ─────────────────
function generateTreatmentTrajectory() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker sheet found. Use 🏥 → Add New Treatment Tracker first.'); return; }

  // Fixed row map — matches buildTreatmentTrackerSheet
  var analyte    = sheet.getRange(4,  2).getValue().toString();
  var starting   = sheet.getRange(5,  2).getValue().toString();
  var target     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var startDate  = sheet.getRange(7,  2).getValue();
  var supplement = sheet.getRange(8,  2).getValue().toString();
  var dose       = sheet.getRange(9,  2).getValue().toString();
  var timesDay   = sheet.getRange(10, 2).getValue().toString();
  var menstrual  = sheet.getRange(13, 2).getValue().toString();
  var dietFact   = sheet.getRange(14, 2).getValue().toString();
  var medFact    = sheet.getRange(15, 2).getValue().toString();

  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please select an analyte and run Populate Treatment Setup first.');
    return;
  }
  if (!supplement || supplement.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please run Populate Treatment Setup first, or manually fill in the Supplement and Dose fields.');
    return;
  }

  var totalDose = '';
  try { totalDose = sheet.getRange(11, 2).getValue().toString(); } catch(e) {}

  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var startStr = startDate instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(startDate, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy') : 'today';

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical pharmacology specialist. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'Generate a detailed treatment trajectory for a patient.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT: ' + profile + '\n' +
    'ANALYTE: ' + analyte + '\n' +
    'Starting value: ' + starting + '\n' +
    'Target value: ' + target + '\n' +
    'Treatment started: ' + startStr + '\n' +
    'Supplement/Medication: ' + supplement + '\n' +
    'Dose: ' + dose + '\n' +
    'Times per day: ' + timesDay + '\n' +
    'Total daily dose: ' + totalDose + '\n\n' +
    'RELEVANT FACTORS:\n' +
    'Menstrual/hormonal: ' + menstrual + '\n' +
    'Diet/absorption: ' + dietFact + '\n' +
    'Medications: ' + medFact + '\n\n' +
    'Provide:\n' +
    '1. TRAJECTORY ASSESSMENT: Realistic expected improvement considering ALL the relevant factors above. ' +
    'Be specific — e.g. heavy periods significantly reduce net iron gain; quantify this if possible.\n' +
    '2. EXPECTED MONTHLY INCREMENT: Expected rise/fall per month as a range.\n' +
    '3. EXPECTED VALUES AT MILESTONES as a JSON array (include baseline month 0):\n' +
    '   [{"month":0,"label":"Baseline","expected_low":X,"expected_high":X},{"month":1,"label":"1 Month","expected_low":X,"expected_high":Y},...up to month 12]\n' +
    '4. MONITORING GUIDANCE: Red flags, when to retest, what to do if not on track at 3 months.\n' +
    '5. CAVEATS: Factors that could make response faster or slower.\n\n' +
    'Include the JSON array exactly as specified.';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Generating trajectory for ' + analyte + '... click OK to begin (15–30 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 2500);

    var trajectoryText = extractSection(response, 'TRAJECTORY ASSESSMENT', 'EXPECTED MONTHLY INCREMENT');
    var increment      = extractSection(response, 'EXPECTED MONTHLY INCREMENT', 'EXPECTED VALUES AT MILESTONES');
    var caveats        = extractSection(response, 'CAVEATS', null);
    var monitoring     = extractSection(response, 'MONITORING GUIDANCE', 'CAVEATS');

    // Write to AI section rows 17, 18
    sheet.getRange(17, 2).setValue(trajectoryText || response).setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setWrap(true).setFontSize(9);
    sheet.setRowHeight(17, Math.max(100, trajectoryText.split('\n').length * 14));
    sheet.getRange(18, 2).setValue(increment || '').setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setWrap(true).setFontSize(9);
    // Monitoring notes & caveats go into row 14 (Lifestyle/Diet) and row 15 (Supplement effects)
    // Only update if they contain relevant content and cells still show placeholder
    var monCav = (monitoring || '') + (caveats ? '\n\nCaveats:\n' + caveats : '');
    if (monCav.trim().length > 5) {
      var cur14 = sheet.getRange(14, 2).getValue().toString();
      var cur15 = sheet.getRange(15, 2).getValue().toString();
      if (cur14.indexOf('[') >= 0 || cur14.indexOf('Auto-populated') >= 0) {
        sheet.getRange(14, 2).setValue(monCav).setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setWrap(true).setFontSize(9);
        sheet.setRowHeight(14, Math.max(60, monCav.split('\n').length * 13));
      }
      if (cur15.indexOf('[') >= 0 || cur15.indexOf('Auto-populated') >= 0) {
        sheet.getRange(15, 2).setValue(monCav).setBackground('#fff7ed').setFontColor('#92400e').setWrap(true).setFontSize(9);
        sheet.setRowHeight(15, Math.max(50, monCav.split('\n').length * 13));
      }
    }

    // Parse milestone JSON and populate expected values in table (rows 21+)
    var jsonMatch = response.match(/\[[\s\S]*?\]/);
    if (jsonMatch) {
      try {
        var milestones = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
        // Get the unit from the starting value text
        var unitMatch = starting.match(/[a-zA-Z\/µ%]+/);
        var unit = unitMatch ? unitMatch[0] : '';

        for (var m = 0; m < milestones.length && m < 9; m++) {
          var tableRow = 21 + m;
          var ms = milestones[m];
          var expStr = ms.expected_low === ms.expected_high
            ? ms.expected_low + ' ' + unit
            : ms.expected_low + '–' + ms.expected_high + ' ' + unit;
          sheet.getRange(tableRow, 4).setValue(expStr).setBackground('#dcfce7').setFontColor('#166534').setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold');

          // Set target dates based on start date + month offset
          if (startDate instanceof Date && ms.month > 0) {
            var targetDate = new Date(startDate);
            targetDate.setMonth(targetDate.getMonth() + ms.month);
            sheet.getRange(tableRow, 2).setValue(targetDate).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
          }
        }
      } catch(jsonErr) {
        // silently ignore JSON parse failure — text assessment still written
      }
    }

    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      '✅ Trajectory generated for ' + analyte + '.\n\n' +
      'Expected values are now shown in the green column of the milestone table.\n\n' +
      'Enter actual test results in the yellow cells as you receive them, then use:\n' +
      '🏥 menu → Treatment Tracker → Assess Current Progress'
    );
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error:\n' + e.toString()); }
}

// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: LOAD HISTORIC RESULTS ───────────────
// Reads ALL results for the selected analyte from TestResults and
// populates the milestone table chronologically.
// Historic results = actual values already tested.
// Future milestone slots show expected range targets from AI trajectory.
function loadHistoricResultsIntoTracker() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker found. Use 🏥 → Add New Treatment Tracker first.'); return; }

  var analyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please select an analyte first (run Populate Treatment Setup).');
    return;
  }

  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (!trSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('TestResults sheet not found.'); return; }

  var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
  var aKey   = analyte.toLowerCase().trim();

  // Collect ALL results for this analyte, sorted by date ascending
  var history = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var row = trData[i];
    if (!row[3] || !row[1]) continue;
    var rowAnalyte = row[3].toString().toLowerCase().trim();
    if (rowAnalyte !== aKey && rowAnalyte.indexOf(aKey) < 0 && aKey.indexOf(rowAnalyte) < 0) continue;
    var numVal = parseFloat(row[4]);
    if (isNaN(numVal)) continue;
    history.push({ date: row[1], value: numVal, unit: row[5] || '', refRange: row[6] || '', status: row[7] || '' });
  }

  if (history.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No results found for "' + analyte + '" in TestResults.\n\nCheck the analyte name matches exactly.');
    return;
  }

  history.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.date > b.date ? 1 : a.date < b.date ? -1 : 0); });

  // Get treatment start date to determine which are historic vs future
  var startDate = sheet.getRange(7, 2).getValue();
  var startTime = startDate instanceof Date ? startDate.getTime() : 0;

  // Clear existing milestone table rows 21–40
  sheet.getRange(21, 1, 20, 8).clearContent().clearFormat();
  for (var r = 21; r <= 40; r++) {
    var mbg = r % 2 === 0 ? C.white : C.emeraldLight;
    sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).setBackground(mbg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 2).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
    sheet.getRange(r, 3).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
    sheet.getRange(r, 4).setFontColor('#166534').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(r, 5).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(r, 7).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22);
  }

  var tableRow = 21;

  // ── Section 1: HISTORIC RESULTS (all from TestResults) ────
  // Single banner row only — no sub-headers (main col header at row 20 already covers all columns)
  sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('HISTORIC RESULTS — from TestResults')
    .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9)
    .setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 20);
  tableRow++;

  // Get the current target string to compare against
  var targetStr  = sheet.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString();
  var targetNums = targetStr.match(/[\d.]+/);
  var targetNum  = targetNums ? parseFloat(targetNums[0]) : null;

  var unit = '';
  for (var hi = 0; hi < history.length; hi++) {
    var h_item = history[hi];
    if (tableRow > 40) break; // safety

    var isPreTreatment = startTime > 0 && (h_item.date instanceof Date ? h_item.date.getTime() : 0) <= startTime;
    var rowBg = isPreTreatment ? '#eff6ff' : C.emeraldLight;

    var onTrack = '';
    if (targetNum !== null) {
      onTrack = h_item.value >= targetNum ? '✅' : h_item.value >= targetNum * 0.75 ? '⚠️' : '🔴';
    }

    // Column mapping matches main header row 20:
    // col 1 = Milestone/Date  col 2 = Target Date (blank for historic)
    // col 3 = Actual Test Date  col 4 = Expected Range (— for historic)
    // col 5 = Actual Value  col 6 = Unit  col 7 = On Track?  col 8 = AI Comment
    var label = isPreTreatment ? 'Pre-treatment' : 'Historic result';
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1).setValue(label).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor(isPreTreatment ? '#1e40af' : '#166534');
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 2).setValue('').setBackground(rowBg);  // Target Date — not applicable for historic
    var hDate = h_item.date instanceof Date ? h_item.date : (function(d){ var s=d.toString().split('/'); return s.length===3 ? new Date(s[2],s[1]-1,s[0]) : new Date(d); })(h_item.date);
sheet.getRange(tableRow, 3).setValue(hDate).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 4).setValue('—').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontColor(C.midGrey);
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 5).setValue(h_item.value).setBackground('#dcfce7').setFontSize(11).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor('#166634').setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 6).setValue(h_item.unit).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
    unit = h_item.unit;
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 7).setValue(onTrack).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(14).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 8).setValue(h_item.status ? 'Lab: ' + h_item.status : '').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontColor(C.midGrey);
    sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 22); tableRow++;
  }

  // ── Section 2: FUTURE MILESTONES ──────────────────────────
  tableRow++;
  if (tableRow <= 40) {
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1, 1, 8).merge()
      .setValue('FUTURE MILESTONES — expected ranges from AI trajectory (run Generate Trajectory first)')
      .setBackground(C.emerald).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9)
      .setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 20); tableRow++;

    if (tableRow <= 40) {
      var futHdrs = ['Milestone','Target Date','Actual Date (fill when tested)','Expected Range','Actual Value (fill when tested)','Unit','On Track?','AI Comment'];
      for (var fh = 0; fh < futHdrs.length; fh++) {
        sheet.getRange(tableRow, fh + 1).setValue(futHdrs[fh])
          .setBackground('#059669').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(8);
      }
      sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 18); tableRow++;
    }

    var milestones = [
      { label: '1 Month',  months: 1 }, { label: '2 Months', months: 2 },
      { label: '3 Months', months: 3 }, { label: '4 Months', months: 4 },
      { label: '6 Months', months: 6 }, { label: '9 Months', months: 9 },
      { label: '12 Months', months: 12 }
    ];

    for (var m = 0; m < milestones.length && tableRow <= 40; m++) {
      var ms   = milestones[m];
      var mbg2 = m % 2 === 0 ? C.emeraldLight : C.white;

      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1).setValue(ms.label).setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor(C.emerald);
      if (startDate instanceof Date) {
        var tDate = new Date(startDate);
        tDate.setMonth(tDate.getMonth() + ms.months);
        sheet.getRange(tableRow, 2).setValue(tDate).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy').setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#166534');
      } else {
        sheet.getRange(tableRow, 2).setValue('Set start date').setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(8).setFontColor(C.midGrey);
      }
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 3).setBackground(mbg2);  // user fills actual date
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 4).setValue('Run Generate Trajectory').setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(8).setFontColor(C.midGrey);
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 5).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold')
        .setNote('Enter actual result when you receive it, then run Assess Current Progress.');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 6).setValue(unit).setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(9);
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 7).setBackground(mbg2).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 8).setBackground(mbg2).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true);
      sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 24); tableRow++;
    }
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '✅ Loaded ' + history.length + ' historic result(s) for ' + analyte + '.\n\n' +
    'Historic results shown in blue/green above.\n' +
    'Future milestone rows shown below — run Generate Trajectory to populate expected ranges.\n\n' +
    'When you receive new test results, they will automatically appear here next time you run this function.'
  );
}


// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: ASSESS PROGRESS ─────────────────────
function assessTreatmentProgress() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker found. Use 🏥 → Add New Treatment Tracker first.'); return; }

  var analyte    = sheet.getRange(4,  2).getValue().toString();
  var starting   = sheet.getRange(5,  2).getValue().toString();
  var target     = sheet.getRange(6,  2).getValue().toString();
  var supplement = sheet.getRange(8,  2).getValue().toString();
  var dose       = sheet.getRange(9,  2).getValue().toString();
  var timesDay   = sheet.getRange(10, 2).getValue().toString();
  var menstrual  = sheet.getRange(13, 2).getValue().toString();
  var trajectory = sheet.getRange(17, 2).getValue().toString();

  // Collect all actual values from milestone table rows 21–40
  var milestoneData = [];
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var scanTo  = Math.min(lastRow, 40);
  for (var r = 21; r <= scanTo; r++) {
    var rowVals = sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).getValues()[0];
    var actual  = rowVals[4];
    if (actual && !isNaN(parseFloat(actual.toString()))) {
      milestoneData.push({
        label:    rowVals[0].toString(),
        date:     rowVals[2] instanceof Date ? formatDate(rowVals[2]) : '',
        expected: rowVals[3].toString(),
        actual:   parseFloat(actual),
        unit:     rowVals[5].toString()
      });
    }
  }

  if (milestoneData.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No actual values found.\n\nEither:\n• Run Load Historic Results to pull from TestResults\n• Enter values manually in the yellow "Actual Value" cells');
    return;
  }

  var profile  = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today    = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var msStr    = milestoneData.map(function(m) {
    return (m.label || 'Result') + (m.date ? ' (' + m.date + ')' : '') +
           ': actual=' + m.actual + ' ' + m.unit +
           (m.expected && m.expected !== '—' ? ', expected=' + m.expected : '');
  }).join('\n');

  var prompt =
    'Assess treatment progress for a patient. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT: ' + profile + '\n' +
    'ANALYTE: ' + analyte + '\n' +
    'Starting value: ' + starting + '  |  Target: ' + target + '\n' +
    'Treatment: ' + supplement + ' ' + dose + ' × ' + timesDay + '/day\n' +
    'Menstrual/hormonal factors: ' + menstrual + '\n' +
    'Original trajectory summary: ' + trajectory.substring(0, 500) + '\n\n' +
    'ALL RESULTS (historic + milestone actual values):\n' + msStr + '\n\n' +
    'Please assess:\n' +
    '1. ON TRACK?: Is the trend in the right direction and at the expected rate?\n' +
    '2. EXPLANATION: If behind target, what are the most likely reasons?\n' +
    '3. RECOMMENDATION: Continue / increase dose / additional testing / investigate absorption / refer?\n' +
    '   If increasing dose, suggest specific new dose.\n' +
    '4. NEXT MILESTONE TARGET: What value should we expect at the next test?\n\n' +
    'Be specific, practical and encouraging. Write in plain English.';

  try {
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 1000, 'Supplement Interactions');

    // Find last row with an actual value and write AI comment there
    var lastActualRow = 22;
    for (var rr = 22; rr <= scanTo; rr++) {
      if (!isNaN(parseFloat(sheet.getRange(rr, 5).getValue()))) lastActualRow = rr;
    }
    sheet.getRange(lastActualRow, 8).setValue(response)
      .setBackground('#dcfce7').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(lastActualRow, Math.max(80, response.split('\n').length * 12));

    // Auto on-track indicators for future milestone rows with actual values
    for (var rr2 = 22; rr2 <= scanTo; rr2++) {
      var actVal  = parseFloat(sheet.getRange(rr2, 5).getValue());
      var expCell = sheet.getRange(rr2, 4).getValue().toString();
      if (!isNaN(actVal) && expCell && expCell !== '—' && expCell.indexOf('Run') < 0) {
        var expNums = expCell.match(/[\d.]+/g);
        if (expNums && expNums.length >= 1) {
          var expLow  = parseFloat(expNums[0]);
          var expHigh = expNums.length >= 2 ? parseFloat(expNums[expNums.length - 1]) : expLow * 1.15;
          var ot = actVal >= expLow ? '✅' : actVal >= expLow * 0.8 ? '⚠️' : '🔴';
          sheet.getRange(rr2, 7).setValue(ot).setFontSize(14).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
        }
      }
    }

    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Progress assessed.\n\nSee the AI Comment on the last result row.\n✅ on track  ⚠️ slightly behind  🔴 significantly behind');
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error:\n' + e.toString()); }
}


// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: GENERATE CHART ──────────────────────
// Builds a line chart on the active treatment tracker sheet showing:
//   - Expected trajectory (from col D, rows 21–40)
//   - Actual results (from col E, rows 21–40)
// Chart is embedded starting at row 43 (below the progress table).
function generateTrackerChart() {
  var ss          = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet       = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker found. Navigate to a 📈 tracker tab first.'); return; }

  var analyte   = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  var targetVal = sheet.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please run Setup & Generate Trajectory first.'); return;
  }

  // Collect data rows 21–40:
  //   col A = label, col D = expected range text, col E = actual value, col F = unit
  // We also extract the low/high of the expected range to build a corridor band.
  var labels = [], expected = [], expLow = [], expHigh = [], actual = [], unit = '';
  var targetNum = parseFloat(targetVal);

  for (var r = 21; r <= 40; r++) {
    var rowVals = sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 6).getValues()[0];
    var lbl = rowVals[0] ? rowVals[0].toString().trim() : '';
    var exp = rowVals[3] ? rowVals[3].toString().trim() : '';
    var act = rowVals[4];
    var u   = rowVals[5] ? rowVals[5].toString().trim() : '';

    if (!lbl || lbl.indexOf('HISTORIC') >= 0 || lbl.indexOf('FUTURE') >= 0) continue;
    if (!exp && isNaN(parseFloat(act))) continue;

    // Extract midpoint AND low/high of expected range
    var expMid = null, expL = null, expH = null;
    if (exp) {
      var nums = exp.match(/[\d.]+/g);
      if (nums && nums.length >= 2) {
        expL = parseFloat(nums[0]);
        expH = parseFloat(nums[nums.length - 1]);
        expMid = (expL + expH) / 2;
      } else if (nums && nums.length === 1) {
        expMid = parseFloat(nums[0]);
        // No corridor if only single value
        expL = expMid * 0.92;  // ~8% corridor below
        expH = expMid * 1.08;  // ~8% corridor above
      }
    }

    var actNum = !isNaN(parseFloat(act)) ? parseFloat(act) : null;
    if (expMid === null && actNum === null) continue;

    labels.push(lbl.length > 14 ? lbl.substring(0, 14) : lbl);
    expected.push(expMid);
    expLow.push(expL);
    expHigh.push(expH);
    actual.push(actNum);
    if (u && !unit) unit = u;
  }

  if (labels.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No data to chart yet.\n\nRun Setup & Generate Trajectory first, then try again.');
    return;
  }

  var dataStartRow = 46;
  var dataRows = labels.length;

  // Clear old charts and data area
  try {
    var charts = sheet.getCharts();
    for (var c = 0; c < charts.length; c++) sheet.removeChart(charts[c]);
  } catch(e) {}

  // Clear wider area to accommodate 6 columns (label + 5 data series)
  sheet.getRange(dataStartRow - 2, 1, dataRows + 6, 6).clearContent().clearFormat();

  // Target reference note above chart data
  if (!isNaN(targetNum)) {
    sheet.getRange(dataStartRow - 2, 1, 1, 6).merge()
      .setValue('🎯 Research Target: ' + targetNum + ' ' + unit + '  |  Green line = target reference  |  Blue band = expected trajectory corridor')
      .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#92400e').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.setRowHeight(dataStartRow - 2, 18);
  }

  // Column headers for chart data table
  // Cols: Milestone | Exp.Low (corridor) | Expected (midpoint) | Exp.High (corridor) | Actual | Target
  var hdrLabels = ['Milestone', 'Exp.Low', 'Expected', 'Exp.High', 'Actual', 'Target'];
  var hdrBgs    = ['#1e40af','#93c5fd','#3b82f6','#93c5fd','#16a34a','#d97706'];
  for (var h = 0; h < hdrLabels.length; h++) {
    sheet.getRange(dataStartRow, h + 1).setValue(hdrLabels[h]).setFontWeight('bold')
      .setBackground(hdrBgs[h]).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontSize(8).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
  }

  for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
    var dr = dataStartRow + 1 + i;
    sheet.getRange(dr, 1).setValue(labels[i]).setFontSize(8);
    if (expLow[i]   !== null) sheet.getRange(dr, 2).setValue(expLow[i]).setFontSize(8);
    if (expected[i] !== null) sheet.getRange(dr, 3).setValue(expected[i]).setFontSize(8);
    if (expHigh[i]  !== null) sheet.getRange(dr, 4).setValue(expHigh[i]).setFontSize(8);
    if (actual[i]   !== null) sheet.getRange(dr, 5).setValue(actual[i]).setFontSize(8);
    if (!isNaN(targetNum))    sheet.getRange(dr, 6).setValue(targetNum).setFontSize(8);
    sheet.setRowHeight(dr, 18);
  }

  // Build chart
  // Series: 0=Exp.Low (corridor floor), 1=Expected (midpoint), 2=Exp.High (corridor ceiling), 3=Actual, 4=Target
  var dataRange    = sheet.getRange(dataStartRow, 1, dataRows + 1, 6);
  var chartBuilder = sheet.newChart()
    .setChartType(Charts.ChartType.LINE)
    .addRange(dataRange)
    .setPosition(43, 1, 0, 0);

  chartBuilder.setOption('title', analyte + ' Treatment Progress' + (unit ? ' (' + unit + ')' : ''));
  chartBuilder.setOption('titleTextStyle', { fontSize: 13, bold: true, color: '#1e293b' });
  chartBuilder.setOption('width', 920);
  chartBuilder.setOption('height', 380);
  chartBuilder.setOption('useFirstColumnAsDomain', true);
  chartBuilder.setOption('legend', { position: 'bottom', textStyle: { fontSize: 9 } });
  chartBuilder.setOption('hAxis', {
    title: 'Milestone', slantedText: true, slantedTextAngle: 30, textStyle: { fontSize: 9 }
  });
  chartBuilder.setOption('vAxis', {
    title: analyte + (unit ? ' (' + unit + ')' : ''), textStyle: { fontSize: 9 },
    viewWindowMode: 'pretty'
  });
  chartBuilder.setOption('series', {
    0: { color: '#bfdbfe', lineWidth: 0, pointSize: 0, visibleInLegend: false },  // Exp.Low — corridor floor (invisible line)
    1: { color: '#60a5fa', lineWidth: 2, lineDashStyle: [6, 3], pointSize: 3 },   // Expected — dashed blue midpoint
    2: { color: '#bfdbfe', lineWidth: 0, pointSize: 0, visibleInLegend: false },  // Exp.High — corridor ceiling (invisible line)
    3: { color: '#16a34a', lineWidth: 3, pointSize: 7, pointShape: 'circle' },    // Actual — solid green dots
    4: { color: '#f59e0b', lineWidth: 1, lineDashStyle: [4, 4], pointSize: 0 }    // Target — amber dashed flat line
  });
  chartBuilder.setOption('backgroundColor', '#f8fafc');
  chartBuilder.setOption('chartArea', { left: 75, top: 45, width: '78%', height: '62%' });
  chartBuilder.setOption('interpolateNulls', true);

  sheet.insertChart(chartBuilder.build());

  // Update chart section header
  sheet.getRange(42, 1, 1, 8).setValue(
    '📊  PROGRESS CHART — ' + analyte +
    '  |  Blue dashed = expected midpoint  |  Light blue band = corridor  |  Green dots = actual results  |  Amber = target  |  Updated: ' +
    Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy')
  ).setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(42, 28);

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '\u2705 Chart updated for ' + analyte + '.\n\n' +
    'Blue dashed line = expected trajectory (midpoint)\n' +
    'Light blue band = expected corridor (acceptable range)\n' +
    'Green dots = your actual results\n' +
    'Amber dashed = research target\n\n' +
    'Re-run after adding new results to keep the chart current.'
  );
}


// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: COMBINED SETUP + TRAJECTORY ───────
// Combines the old steps 1 (Populate Setup), 2 (Generate Trajectory),
// and 3 (Load Historic Results) into a single action.
// No AI call: NO  |  OpenAI calls: 2 (setup + trajectory)
// Run once when creating a new tracker for an analyte.
function setupAndGenerateTrajectory() {
  var ss          = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet       = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker found.\nUse 🏥 menu → Treatment Tracker → Add New Treatment Tracker first.'); return; }

  // Step 1: Populate Setup (reuse existing function logic)
  populateTreatmentSetup();

  // Check setup actually populated before continuing
  var analyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) return; // user cancelled or error in setup

  var supplement = sheet.getRange(8, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!supplement || supplement.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Setup did not complete — cannot generate trajectory.\nFill in the yellow fields manually then run Generate Trajectory separately.');
    return;
  }

  // Step 2: Load historic results silently (no alert)
  _loadHistoricResultsSilent(ss, sheet);

  // Step 3: Generate trajectory
  generateTreatmentTrajectory();
}

// Silent version of loadHistoricResultsIntoTracker — no alert on completion,
// used when called as part of the combined setup flow.
function _loadHistoricResultsSilent(ss, sheet) {
  try {
    var analyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
    if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) return;
    var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
    if (!trSheet) return;
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var aKey   = analyte.toLowerCase().trim();
    var history = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
      var row = trData[i];
      if (!row[3] || !row[1]) continue;
      var rowAnalyte = row[3].toString().toLowerCase().trim();
      if (rowAnalyte !== aKey && rowAnalyte.indexOf(aKey) < 0 && aKey.indexOf(rowAnalyte) < 0) continue;
      var numVal = parseFloat(row[4]);
      if (isNaN(numVal)) continue;
      history.push({ date: row[1], value: numVal, unit: row[5] || '', refRange: row[6] || '', status: row[7] || '' });
    }
    if (history.length === 0) return; // no history yet — fine for a new tracker
    history.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.date > b.date ? 1 : a.date < b.date ? -1 : 0); });

    var startDate = sheet.getRange(7, 2).getValue();
    var startTime = startDate instanceof Date ? startDate.getTime() : 0;
    var targetStr  = sheet.getRange(6, 2).getValue().toString();
    var targetNums = targetStr.match(/[\d.]+/);
    var targetNum  = targetNums ? parseFloat(targetNums[0]) : null;
    var unit = '';

    // Clear milestone rows 21–40 and reset formatting
    sheet.getRange(21, 1, 20, 8).clearContent().clearFormat();
    for (var r = 21; r <= 40; r++) {
      var mbg = r % 2 === 0 ? C.white : C.emeraldLight;
      sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).setBackground(mbg).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(r, 2).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
      sheet.getRange(r, 3).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy');
      sheet.getRange(r, 4).setFontColor('#166534').setFontWeight('bold');
      sheet.getRange(r, 5).setBackground(C.inputYellow).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold');
      sheet.getRange(r, 7).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
      sheet.setRowHeight(r, 22);
    }

    var tableRow = 21;
    // Banner only — no sub-headers (main col header at row 20 covers all columns)
    sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1, 1, 8).merge()
      .setValue('HISTORIC RESULTS — from TestResults')
      .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 20); tableRow++;

    for (var hi = 0; hi < history.length; hi++) {
      var h_item = history[hi];
      if (tableRow > 40) break;
      if (!unit && h_item.unit) unit = h_item.unit;
      var isPreTreatment = startTime > 0 && (h_item.date instanceof Date ? h_item.date.getTime() : 0) <= startTime;
      var rowBg = isPreTreatment ? '#eff6ff' : C.emeraldLight;
      var onTrack = '';
      if (targetNum !== null) {
        onTrack = h_item.value >= targetNum ? '✅' : h_item.value >= targetNum * 0.75 ? '⚠️' : '🔴';
      }
      // Column mapping matches main header row 20:
      // col 1 = Milestone/Date  col 2 = Target Date (blank)
      // col 3 = Actual Test Date  col 4 = Expected Range (—)
      // col 5 = Actual Value  col 6 = Unit  col 7 = On Track?  col 8 = AI Comment
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 1).setValue(isPreTreatment ? 'Pre-treatment' : 'Historic result').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor(isPreTreatment ? '#1e40af' : '#166534');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 2).setValue('').setBackground(rowBg);
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 3).setValue(h_item.date instanceof Date ? h_item.date : new Date(h_item.date)).setNumberFormat('dd/mm/yyyy').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9);
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 4).setValue('—').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#94a3b8');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 5).setValue(h_item.value).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold').setFontColor('#1e293b');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 6).setValue(h_item.unit).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontColor('#64748b');
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 7).setValue(onTrack).setFontSize(12).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setBackground(rowBg);
      sheet.getRange(tableRow, 8).setValue(h_item.status ? 'Lab: ' + h_item.status : '').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#64748b');
      sheet.setRowHeight(tableRow, 22); tableRow++;
    }
  } catch(e) {
    // Silent fail — historic load is best-effort during combined setup
  }
}


// ─── TREATMENT TRACKER: COMBINED ASSESS + UPDATE CHART ────
// Combines old steps 4 (Assess Progress) and 5 (Update Chart) into one.
// OpenAI call: YES (1 call for progress assessment)
// Run after entering a new actual result in the yellow Actual Value cell.
function assessAndUpdateChart() {
  var ss          = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var activeSheet = ss.getActiveSheet();
  var sheet       = (activeSheet.getName().indexOf('📈') === 0) ? activeSheet : ss.getSheetByName('📈 Treatment Tracker');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No Treatment Tracker found.'); return; }

  var analyte = sheet.getRange(4, 2).getValue().toString();
  if (!analyte || analyte.indexOf('[') >= 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Please run Setup & Generate Trajectory first.'); return;
  }

  // First reload historic results silently so chart is always current
  _loadHistoricResultsSilent(ss, sheet);

  // Then run assessment (AI call)
  assessTreatmentProgress();

  // Then update chart (no AI, fast)
  generateTrackerChart();
}


// ─── SHARED HELPERS FOR SIGNIFICANCE RATINGS ─────────────────
function _buildSignificancePrompt(profile, today, medContext, findingsForPrompt, count) {
  return 'You are a clinical specialist assessing the importance of flagged blood test results for a specific patient. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    medContext + '\n\n' +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS TO ASSESS:\n' + findingsForPrompt + '\n\n' +
    'For EACH numbered finding above, provide a JSON object. Return a JSON ARRAY with one object per finding, in the same order.\n' +
    'Each object must have EXACTLY these fields:\n' +
    '{\n' +
    '  "analyte": "name",\n' +
    '  "significance_score": 1-5,\n' +
    '  "significance_label": "one of: Commonly low / rarely serious | Monitor — may be contributing | Address soon | Significant — prioritise | Urgent — seek medical advice",\n' +
    '  "significance_reasoning": "2-3 sentences: why this score for THIS patient specifically",\n' +
    '  "likely_symptoms": "Plain English list of symptoms this level is likely causing for THIS patient right now.",\n' +
    '  "life_impact": "one of: Minimal impact | Mild — noticeable but manageable | Moderate — affecting daily life | Significant — limiting normal activities | Severe — substantially limiting",\n' +
    '  "ease_of_correction": "one of: Very easy — lifestyle only | Easy — OTC supplement | Moderate — GP prescription needed | Complex — specialist or investigation needed | Difficult — underlying condition",\n' +
    '  "correction_plan": "Specific, quantified plan with cofactors, timing, what to avoid.",\n' +
    '  "expected_outcome": "Specific numbers and timeline if patient follows plan.",\n' +
    '  "gp_needed": true/false\n' +
    '}\n\n' +
    'Score: 1=common/low urgency, 2=worth addressing, 3=address soon, 4=prioritise/GP useful, 5=urgent/GP within days.\n' +
    'Be personalised — a finding that is score 2 for a healthy 25yo male could be score 4 for a peri-menopausal woman.\n' +
    'Return ONLY the JSON array, no other text.';
}

function _writeSignificanceRatings(sfSheet, ratings, dataRows) {
  for (var r = 0; r < ratings.length && r < dataRows.length; r++) {
    var rating  = ratings[r];
    var dataRow = dataRows[r];
    var shRow   = dataRow.sheetRow;

    var score = parseInt(rating.significance_score) || 3;
    var badge = '', badgeBg, badgeFg;
    if (score >= 5) { badge = '🔴 '+score+'/5\nURGENT'; badgeBg='#7f1d1d'; badgeFg='#ffffff'; }
    else if (score===4) { badge='🟠 '+score+'/5\nPRIORITISE'; badgeBg='#7c2d12'; badgeFg='#ffffff'; }
    else if (score===3) { badge='🟡 '+score+'/5\nADDRESS SOON'; badgeBg='#78350f'; badgeFg='#ffffff'; }
    else if (score===2) { badge='🔵 '+score+'/5\nMONITOR'; badgeBg='#1e3a5f'; badgeFg='#ffffff'; }
    else                { badge='🟢 '+score+'/5\nLOW URGENCY'; badgeBg='#14532d'; badgeFg='#ffffff'; }

    sfSheet.getRange(shRow, 10)
      .setValue(badge+'\n\n'+(rating.significance_label||'')+'\n\n'+(rating.significance_reasoning||''))
      .setBackground(badgeBg).setFontColor(badgeFg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');

    var sympBg = score>=4?'#fef2f2':score===3?'#fff7ed':'#fffbeb';
    sfSheet.getRange(shRow, 11)
      .setValue((rating.likely_symptoms||'')+'\n\n📊 Life impact: '+(rating.life_impact||''))
      .setBackground(sympBg).setFontColor('#1e293b').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');

    var ease = rating.ease_of_correction||'';
    var easeBg = ease.indexOf('Very easy')>=0?'#f0fdf4':ease.indexOf('Easy')>=0?'#dcfce7':ease.indexOf('Moderate')>=0?'#fef9c3':ease.indexOf('Complex')>=0?'#fff7ed':'#fef2f2';
    var easeFg = ease.indexOf('Very easy')>=0||ease.indexOf('Easy')>=0?'#166534':ease.indexOf('Moderate')>=0?'#713f12':'#7f1d1d';
    sfSheet.getRange(shRow, 12)
      .setValue(ease+(rating.gp_needed?'\n\n⚕️ GP involvement recommended':'\n\n✅ Self-manageable'))
      .setBackground(easeBg).setFontColor(easeFg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');

    var correctionText = (rating.correction_plan||'')+'\n\n📈 Expected: '+(rating.expected_outcome||'');
    sfSheet.getRange(shRow, 13)
      .setValue(correctionText)
      .setBackground('#f0f9ff').setFontColor('#1e293b').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');

    var maxLines = Math.max(
      (rating.significance_reasoning||'').split('\n').length+5,
      (rating.likely_symptoms||'').split('\n').length+3,
      (correctionText||'').split('\n').length+2
    );
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(shRow, Math.max(120, Math.min(300, maxLines*14)));
  }
}


// ─── SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS: AI SIGNIFICANCE RATINGS ──────────
function addSignificancePanelToFindings() {
  var ss      = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Significant Findings sheet not found. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  var lastRow = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 6) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No significant findings yet. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  var sheetData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 9).getValues();
  var dataRows = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sheetData.length; i++) {
    var row    = sheetData[i];
    var label  = row[0] ? row[0].toString().trim() : '';
    var value  = row[1] ? row[1].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!label || label.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || label.indexOf('AI FORWARD') >= 0 ||
        label.indexOf('GP LETTER') >= 0 || label.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0) continue;
    if (value && value.length > 0) {
      dataRows.push({ sheetRow: i + 1, analyte: label, value: value,
                      date: row[2], labStatus: row[3], thresholds: row[4],
                      meaning: row[5], causes: row[6] });
    }
  }

  if (dataRows.length === 0) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No flagged findings found in sheet. Run Refresh Everything first.');
