    return;
  }

  var profile    = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today      = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var medContext = getMedicationContext(ss);

  var findingsForPrompt = dataRows.map(function(d, idx) {
    return (idx + 1) + '. ANALYTE: ' + d.analyte +
           '\n   Value: ' + d.value + ' (tested ' + (d.date ? formatDate(d.date) : '?') + ')' +
           '\n   Lab status: ' + (d.labStatus || 'not recorded') +
           '\n   Research flags: ' + (d.thresholds || '').substring(0, 200) +
           '\n   Clinical meaning: ' + (d.meaning || '').substring(0, 300);
  }).join('\n\n');

  var prompt = _buildSignificancePrompt(profile, today, medContext, findingsForPrompt, dataRows.length);

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Generating personalised significance ratings for ' + dataRows.length + ' finding(s)...\nClick OK to begin (15–30 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 4000, 'Deep Dive');

    // FIX: use _extractJSONArray which strips ```json fences before parsing
    var ratings;
    try {
      ratings = _extractJSONArray(response);
    } catch(e) {
      throw new Error('AI did not return a JSON array.\n\nResponse preview: ' + response.substring(0, 300));
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(ratings) || ratings.length === 0) throw new Error('AI returned empty or invalid data.');

    _writeSignificanceRatings(sfSheet, ratings, dataRows);

    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
      '✅ AI Significance Ratings added for ' + ratings.length + ' finding(s).\n\n' +
      'Columns J–M now show:\n' +
      '• J: Significance Rating (1–5 with colour coding)\n' +
      '• K: Likely symptoms for YOU specifically\n' +
      '• L: Ease of correction\n' +
      '• M: Specific correction plan + expected outcome\n\n' +
      'Ratings are personalised to your profile — age, sex, hormonal status, medications, and other results are all factored in.'
    );
  } catch(e) {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error generating significance ratings:\n' + e.toString().substring(0, 300));
  }
}


function buildSignificantFindingsRows(sheet, ss) {
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!trSheet || !thSheet) { sheet.getRange(3,1,1,9).merge().setValue('Sheets not found. Run buildMedicalTracker first.').setBackground('#fee2e2').setFontSize(9); return; }
  var trData  = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
  var thData  = thSheet.getRange(4,1,2000,10).getValues();
  var thMap   = buildThresholdMap(thData);
  var latestRes = collectLatestResults(trData);
  var allByKey  = collectAllResults(trData);

  var resolvedMap = runAnalyteMapping(ss, trData, thMap, false);

  var suppInteractions = {};
  var suppIntDate = '';
  try {
    var stored = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS');
    if (stored) suppInteractions = JSON.parse(stored);
    suppIntDate = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS_DATE') || '';
  } catch(e) {}

  var quickInteractions = buildQuickSupplementInteractions(ss);

  var flagged = [];
  for (var aKey in latestRes) {
    var res = latestRes[aKey];
    var thRows = resolveThresholdRows(res.analyte, thMap, resolvedMap);
    if (!thRows) continue;
    var triggered = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) { if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) triggered.push(thRows[b]); }
    if (triggered.length > 0) flagged.push({ res: res, bands: triggered, history: allByKey[aKey]||[] });
  }

  var researchFlaggedKeys = {};
  for (var fi = 0; fi < flagged.length; fi++) researchFlaggedKeys[flagged[fi].res.analyte.toLowerCase()] = true;

  var SF_SKIP = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score',
                 'nutrition score','mind (stress)','work/life','sleep score',
                 'body fat','total body water','resting heart rate',
                 'mind score','activity score','stress score','height','weight'];

  var labFlagged = [];
  for (var lKey in latestRes) {
    var lRes = latestRes[lKey];
    var lNameLower = lRes.analyte.toLowerCase();
    var skipLab = false;
    for (var sk = 0; sk < SF_SKIP.length; sk++) {
      if (lNameLower.indexOf(SF_SKIP[sk]) >= 0) { skipLab = true; break; }
    }
    if (skipLab) continue;
    var labStat = (lRes.status || '').toString().toLowerCase().trim();
    var isLabAbnormal = labStat === 'abnormal' || labStat === 'high' || labStat === 'low' ||
                        labStat === 'h' || labStat === 'l' || labStat === 'above range' || labStat === 'below range';
    var lCleanKey = cleanAnalyteName(lRes.analyte).toLowerCase();
    if (isLabAbnormal && !researchFlaggedKeys[lCleanKey] && !researchFlaggedKeys[lKey]) {
      labFlagged.push({ res: lRes, history: allByKey[lKey]||[] });
    }
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow > 2) sheet.getRange(3, 1, Math.max(lastRow-2,1), 14).clearContent().clearFormat();

  if (flagged.length === 0 && labFlagged.length === 0) {
    sheet.getRange(3,1,1,9).merge().setValue('🎉  No significant findings — no research threshold flags triggered and no lab abnormals.\nIf you just added data: 🏥 menu → Refresh Analysis')
      .setBackground('#f0fdf4').setFontColor('#166534').setFontSize(10).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.setRowHeight(3, 80); return;
  }

  flagged.sort(function(a,b){ return b.bands.length - a.bands.length; });
  var cr = 3;

  var summaryParts = [];
  if (flagged.length > 0) summaryParts.push('⚠️  ' + flagged.length + ' research threshold flag(s)');
  if (labFlagged.length > 0) summaryParts.push('🔵  ' + labFlagged.length + ' lab-flagged result(s)');
  sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge()
    .setValue(summaryParts.join('   |   ') + '   |   Columns A–I: data  |  J–M: AI significance  |  N: Research Links')
    .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontColor('#92400e').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

  var hdrs = ['Analyte','Your Value','Date','Lab Status','Threshold(s) Hit','What It Means','Common Causes / Context','Evidence','Trend (hover for all values)',
              'Significance Rating','Likely Symptoms for YOU','Ease of Fix','How to Correct + Timeline','📖 Research Links'];
  for (var h = 0; h < hdrs.length; h++) {
    var hBg = h >= 9 && h < 13 ? '#7c2d12' : h === 13 ? '#1e40af' : '#7f1d1d';
    sheet.getRange(cr, h+1).setValue(hdrs[h]).setBackground(hBg).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  }
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

  for (var f = 0; f < flagged.length; f++) {
    var item = flagged[f]; var res = item.res; var bands = item.bands;
 
var history = item.history.sort(function(a,b){ return parseDMY(a.date) - parseDMY(b.date); });
    var severity = bands.length>=3?'high':bands.length===2?'medium':'low';
    var rowBg = severity==='high'?'#fef2f2':severity==='medium'?'#fff7ed':'#fefce8';
    var valBg = severity==='high'?'#fca5a5':severity==='medium'?'#fdba74':'#fde68a';
    var valFg = severity==='high'?'#7f1d1d':severity==='medium'?'#7c2d12':'#78350f';
    var threshText  = bands.map(function(b){ return '• '+b[1]; }).join('\n');
    var meaningText = bands.map(function(b){ return '▸ '+b[1]+':\n'+b[3]; }).join('\n\n');
    var causesText  = bands.map(function(b){ return b[9]||''; }).filter(function(c){ return c.length>0; }).join('\n\n');
    if (!causesText) causesText = 'Run AI refresh to generate common cause explanations.';

    var suppNotes = [];
    var analyteLower = res.analyte.toLowerCase();
    for (var qi = 0; qi < quickInteractions.length; qi++) {
      var qint = quickInteractions[qi];
      if (qint.analytes.some(function(a) { return analyteLower.indexOf(a) >= 0 || a.indexOf(analyteLower) >= 0; })) {
        suppNotes.push('💊 ' + qint.supplement + ': ' + qint.note);
      }
    }
    for (var suppName in suppInteractions) {
      var intText = suppInteractions[suppName].toLowerCase();
      if (intText.indexOf(analyteLower) >= 0 || intText.indexOf(res.analyte.toLowerCase()) >= 0) {
        suppNotes.push('🤖 ' + suppName + ' (AI): ' + suppInteractions[suppName].substring(0, 180));
      }
    }
    if (suppNotes.length > 0) {
      causesText = (causesText && causesText.indexOf('Run AI') < 0 ? causesText + '\n\n' : '') +
                   '── YOUR SUPPLEMENTS MAY BE A FACTOR ──\n' + suppNotes.join('\n') +
                   (suppIntDate ? '\n(AI supplement analysis: ' + suppIntDate + ')' : '\n(Run Analyse Supplements for full AI assessment)');
    }
    var evidText    = bands.map(function(b){ return (b[5]||'')+'\n'+(b[6]||''); }).join('\n---\n');
    var statusBg    = (res.status||'').toLowerCase()==='normal'?'#dcfce7':(res.status||'').toLowerCase()==='abnormal'?'#fecaca':rowBg;

    sheet.getRange(cr,1).setValue(res.analyte).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,2).setValue(res.value+' '+res.unit).setBackground(valBg).setFontColor(valFg).setFontSize(11).setFontWeight('bold').setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.getRange(cr,3).setValue(formatDate(res.date)).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,4).setValue(res.status||'Not recorded').setBackground(statusBg).setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,5).setValue(threshText).setBackground(valBg).setFontColor(valFg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontWeight('bold');
    sheet.getRange(cr,6).setValue(meaningText).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(cr,7).setValue(causesText).setBackground('#fffbeb').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#78350f');
    sheet.getRange(cr,8).setValue(evidText).setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e40af');

    // ── Trend cell: sparkline + hover note listing all dated values ──────
    if (history.length >= 2) {
      var vals = history.map(function(h){ return h.value; });
      var sparkColor = vals[vals.length-1] <= vals[0] ? 'red' : 'green';
      var trendCell = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
      trendCell.setFormula('=SPARKLINE({'+vals.join(',')+'},{\"charttype\",\"line\";\"color\",\"'+sparkColor+'\";\"linewidth\",2})').setBackground(rowBg).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      // Build hover note: count + each dated result
      var histLines = history.map(function(h){
        return formatDate(h.date) + ':  ' + h.value + (h.unit ? ' ' + h.unit : '');
      }).join('\n');
      trendCell.setNote(history.length + ' result(s) captured:\n\n' + histLines);
    } else if (history.length === 1) {
      var trendCell1 = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
      trendCell1.setValue('1 result\n' + formatDate(history[0].date) + ': ' + history[0].value + ' ' + (history[0].unit||''))
        .setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      trendCell1.setNote('Only 1 result found — need 2+ for trend chart.');
    } else {
      sheet.getRange(cr,9).setValue('No history').setBackground(rowBg).setFontSize(7).setFontColor(C.midGrey).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    }

    sheet.getRange(cr,10).setValue('[ Run Generate AI Significance Ratings ]').setBackground('#fff1f2').setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9f1239').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sheet.getRange(cr,11).setBackground('#fff1f2');
    sheet.getRange(cr,12).setBackground('#fff1f2');
    sheet.getRange(cr,13).setBackground('#fff1f2');

    var analyteSafe = res.analyte.replace(/"/g, '').replace(/'/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
    var analyteForSearch = encodeURIComponent(analyteSafe);
    var profileCtx = '';
    try {
      var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\u{1F464} Profile');
      if (profSheet) {
        var pVals = profSheet.getRange(3,2,9,1).getValues();
        if (pVals[1][0]) profileCtx = '+' + encodeURIComponent(pVals[1][0].toString().toLowerCase());
        if (pVals[2][0]) profileCtx += '+' + encodeURIComponent(pVals[2][0].toString().toLowerCase());
      }
    } catch(e) {}
    var pubmedUrl    = 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=' + analyteForSearch + '+deficiency+symptoms' + profileCtx + '&filter=dates.2015-2025&sort=relevance';
    var labTestsUrl  = 'https://labtestsonline.org.uk/search?q=' + analyteForSearch;
    var niceUrl      = 'https://www.nice.org.uk/search?q=' + analyteForSearch;
    var patientUrl   = 'https://patient.info/search/results?q=' + analyteForSearch.replace(/%20/g, '+') + '+low';
    var linkCell = sheet.getRange(cr, 14);
    linkCell.setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    var richText = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue()
      .setText('📚 Lab Tests Online\n📖 PubMed Research\n🏥 NICE Guidelines\n👤 Patient.info\n🔍 Deep-dive (use menu)')
      .setLinkUrl(0,  19, labTestsUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(20, 38, pubmedUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(39, 57, niceUrl)
      .setLinkUrl(58, 73, patientUrl)
      .build();
    linkCell.setRichTextValue(richText);

    var maxLines = Math.max(meaningText.split('\n').length, causesText.split('\n').length, threshText.split('\n').length);
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, Math.max(80, Math.min(200, maxLines*14)));
    sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).setBorder(false,false,true,false,false,false,'#dc2626',SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_MEDIUM);
    cr++;
    if (f % 10 === 9) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  // ── Section 2: Lab-flagged results ───────────────────────────────────
  if (labFlagged.length > 0) {
    cr++;
    sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge()
      .setValue('🔵  LAB-FLAGGED RESULTS — Marked abnormal by your lab, but within research "functional" thresholds  |  These are often minor deviations. AI Significance Ratings will explain what they typically mean.')
      .setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#bfdbfe').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 30); cr++;

    var hdrs2 = ['Analyte','Your Value','Date','Lab Status','Lab Reference Range','What Abnormal Typically Means','Context & Possible Causes','Evidence','Trend (hover for all values)',
                'Significance Rating','Likely Symptoms for YOU','Ease of Fix','How to Correct + Timeline','📖 Research Links'];
    for (var h2 = 0; h2 < hdrs2.length; h2++) {
      var hBg2 = h2 >= 9 && h2 < 13 ? '#1e3a5f' : h2 === 13 ? '#1e40af' : '#1e3a5f';
      sheet.getRange(cr, h2+1).setValue(hdrs2[h2]).setBackground(hBg2).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
    }
    sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 24); cr++;

    for (var lf = 0; lf < labFlagged.length; lf++) {
      var lItem   = labFlagged[lf];
      var lRes    = lItem.res;
     var lHistory = lItem.history.sort(function(a,b){ return parseDMY(a.date) - parseDMY(b.date); });
      var lStatus  = (lRes.status || '').toString();
      var rowBg2   = lf % 2 === 0 ? '#eff6ff' : '#f0f9ff';
      var valBg2   = '#bfdbfe';
      var valFg2   = '#1e3a5f';

      var lStatLow  = lStatus.toLowerCase();
      var direction = lStatLow === 'low' || lStatLow === 'l' || lStatLow === 'below range' ? 'low' :
                      lStatLow === 'high' || lStatLow === 'h' || lStatLow === 'above range' ? 'high' : 'abnormal';
      var defaultMeaning = direction === 'low'
        ? 'Your result is below the lab\'s reference range. The lab reference range reflects the middle 95% of a healthy population — being just outside it is common and may not require action, but is worth monitoring. Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.'
        : direction === 'high'
        ? 'Your result is above the lab\'s reference range. Being just above the upper limit is often a minor deviation — context matters (e.g. timing, hydration, recent activity). Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.'
        : 'Your lab marked this result as abnormal. This may be a minor deviation that requires no action, or may be worth discussing with your GP. Run AI Significance Ratings for a personalised assessment.';

      var lAnalyteSafe  = lRes.analyte.replace(/"/g,'').replace(/'/g,'').toLowerCase().trim();
      var lSearchEnc    = encodeURIComponent(lAnalyteSafe);

      var lSuppNotes = [];
      var lAnalyteLower = lRes.analyte.toLowerCase();
      for (var lqi = 0; lqi < quickInteractions.length; lqi++) {
        var lqint = quickInteractions[lqi];
        if (lqint.analytes.some(function(a) { return lAnalyteLower.indexOf(a) >= 0 || a.indexOf(lAnalyteLower) >= 0; })) {
          lSuppNotes.push('💊 ' + lqint.supplement + ': ' + lqint.note);
        }
      }
      var lCausesText = direction === 'low'
        ? '• Mild lows can occur from recent diet changes, hydration, time of day, or slight natural variation\n• May resolve on retest\n• If persists, worth mentioning to GP'
        : '• Mild highs can be transient — e.g. dehydration (albumin, haematocrit), recent exercise (CK, LDH), timing (cortisol, glucose)\n• If isolated and minor, often not clinically significant\n• If persists on retest, discuss with GP';
      if (lSuppNotes.length > 0) lCausesText += '\n\n── YOUR SUPPLEMENTS MAY BE A FACTOR ──\n' + lSuppNotes.join('\n');

      sheet.getRange(cr,1).setValue(lRes.analyte).setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setFontWeight('bold').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,2).setValue(lRes.value+' '+(lRes.unit||'')).setBackground(valBg2).setFontColor(valFg2).setFontSize(11).setFontWeight('bold').setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      sheet.getRange(cr,3).setValue(lRes.date ? formatDate(lRes.date) : '').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,4).setValue(lStatus || 'Abnormal').setBackground('#bfdbfe').setFontColor('#1e3a5f').setFontSize(9).setHorizontalAlignment('center').setFontWeight('bold');
      sheet.getRange(cr,5).setValue(lRes.refRange || 'See lab report').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(9).setWrap(true);
      sheet.getRange(cr,6).setValue(defaultMeaning).setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      sheet.getRange(cr,7).setValue(lCausesText).setBackground('#fef9c3').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#78350f');
      sheet.getRange(cr,8).setValue('Run AI Significance Ratings for evidence-based assessment').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e40af');

      // ── Trend cell for lab-flagged: sparkline + hover note ────────────
      if (lHistory.length >= 2) {
        var lVals = lHistory.map(function(h){ return h.value; });
        var lSparkColor = lVals[lVals.length-1] <= lVals[0] ? 'red' : 'green';
        var lTrendCell = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
        lTrendCell.setFormula('=SPARKLINE({'+lVals.join(',')+'},{\"charttype\",\"line\";\"color\",\"'+lSparkColor+'\";\"linewidth\",2})').setBackground(rowBg2).setVerticalAlignment('top');
        var lHistLines = lHistory.map(function(h){
          return formatDate(h.date) + ':  ' + h.value + (h.unit ? ' ' + h.unit : '');
        }).join('\n');
        lTrendCell.setNote(lHistory.length + ' result(s) captured:\n\n' + lHistLines);
      } else if (lHistory.length === 1) {
        var lTrendCell1 = sheet.getRange(cr,9);
        lTrendCell1.setValue('1 result\n' + formatDate(lHistory[0].date) + ': ' + lHistory[0].value + ' ' + (lHistory[0].unit||''))
          .setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9ca3af').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
        lTrendCell1.setNote('Only 1 result found — need 2+ for trend chart.');
      } else {
        sheet.getRange(cr,9).setValue('Need 2+ results').setBackground(rowBg2).setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#9ca3af').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      }

      sheet.getRange(cr,10).setValue('[ Run Generate AI Significance Ratings ]').setBackground('#dbeafe').setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#1e3a5f').setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
      sheet.getRange(cr,11).setBackground('#dbeafe');
      sheet.getRange(cr,12).setBackground('#dbeafe');
      sheet.getRange(cr,13).setBackground('#dbeafe');

      var lLinkCell = sheet.getRange(cr, 14);
      lLinkCell.setBackground('#eff6ff').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      var lRichText = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue()
        .setText('📚 Lab Tests Online\n📖 PubMed Research\n🏥 NICE Guidelines\n👤 Patient.info')
        .setLinkUrl(0,  19, 'https://labtestsonline.org.uk/search?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .setLinkUrl(20, 38, 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=' + lSearchEnc + '&filter=dates.2015-2025&sort=relevance')
        .setLinkUrl(39, 57, 'https://www.nice.org.uk/search?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .setLinkUrl(58, 73, 'https://patient.info/search/results?q=' + lSearchEnc)
        .build();
      lLinkCell.setRichTextValue(lRichText);

      sheet.setRowHeight(cr, Math.max(70, Math.min(180, defaultMeaning.split('\n').length * 14 + 30)));
      sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).setBorder(false,false,true,false,false,false,'#3b82f6',SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID_MEDIUM);
      cr++;
      if (lf % 10 === 9) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    }
  }


  cr++;
  sheet.getRange(cr,1,1,14).merge().setValue('KEY: 🔴 Multiple research flags | 🟠 2 flags | 🟡 1 flag | 🔵 Lab abnormal only  |  Significance: 🔴 5/5 Urgent → 🟢 1/5 Low urgency  |  Trend column: hover cell for full result history')
    .setBackground('#1e293b').setFontColor('#e2e8f0').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
  sheet.setRowHeight(cr, 35);
}


// ─── SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS: PERSONALISED DEEP-DIVE ────────────
// Generates a rich, personalised research briefing for a specific
// flagged analyte. Writes it to a new sheet tab so it's persistent
// and printable. Includes curated research context, personalised
// interpretation, and clickable source links.
function deepDiveOnFinding() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // Get list of flagged analytes from SF sheet
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) { ui.alert('Significant Findings sheet not found. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  var lastRow   = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  var sfData    = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 5).getValues();
  var analytes  = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sfData.length; i++) {
    var lbl = sfData[i][0] ? sfData[i][0].toString().trim() : '';
    var val = sfData[i][1] ? sfData[i][1].toString().trim() : '';
    if (lbl && val && lbl.indexOf('KEY:') < 0 && lbl.indexOf('AI') < 0 && lbl.indexOf('⚠️') < 0) {
      analytes.push({ analyte: lbl, value: val, sheetRow: i + 1 });
    }
  }

  if (analytes.length === 0) { ui.alert('No flagged findings found. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  // Prompt user to select which analyte
  var listStr = analytes.map(function(a, i) { return (i+1) + '. ' + a.analyte + '  (' + a.value + ')'; }).join('\n');
  var result  = ui.prompt(
    '🔬 Deep-dive — Select Analyte',
    'Enter the number or name of the finding to deep-dive into:\n\n' + listStr,
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
  );
  if (result.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var input    = result.getResponseText().trim();
  var selected = null;

  // Match by number or name
  var numMatch = input.match(/^\d+$/);
  if (numMatch) {
    var idx = parseInt(input) - 1;
    if (idx >= 0 && idx < analytes.length) selected = analytes[idx];
  } else {
    for (var a = 0; a < analytes.length; a++) {
      if (analytes[a].analyte.toLowerCase().indexOf(input.toLowerCase()) >= 0) {
        selected = analytes[a]; break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!selected) { ui.alert('Could not find "' + input + '" in flagged findings.'); return; }

  // Read full row data for selected finding
  var rowData    = sfSheet.getRange(selected.sheetRow, 1, 1, 14).getValues()[0];
  var analyte    = rowData[0].toString();
  var value      = rowData[1].toString();
  var date       = rowData[2] ? formatDate(rowData[2]) : '';
  var labStatus  = rowData[3].toString();
  var thresholds = rowData[4].toString();
  var meaning    = rowData[5].toString();
  var causes     = rowData[6].toString();
  var sigRating  = rowData[9] ? rowData[9].toString() : '';
  var symptoms   = rowData[10] ? rowData[10].toString() : '';
  var correction = rowData[12] ? rowData[12].toString() : '';

  // Read threshold detail from Thresholds sheet
  var thSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  var thContext = '';
  if (thSheet) {
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
    var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var aKey   = analyte.toLowerCase().trim();
    var thRows = thMap[aKey] || findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap);
    if (thRows) {
      thContext = thRows.map(function(t) {
        return 'Threshold: ' + t[1] + ' (' + t[2] + ')\nMeaning: ' + t[3] +
               (t[4] ? '\nSymptoms: ' + t[4] : '') +
               (t[5] ? '\nEvidence: ' + t[5] : '') +
               (t[6] ? '\nSource: ' + t[6] : '');
      }).join('\n\n');
    }
  }

  var profile    = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var medContext = getMedicationContext(ss);
  var today      = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical specialist writing a detailed, personalised research briefing for a patient about one of their flagged blood test results. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    medContext + '\n\n' +
    'FINDING TO DEEP-DIVE:\n' +
    'Analyte: ' + analyte + '\n' +
    'Patient value: ' + value + ' (tested ' + date + ')\n' +
    'Lab status: ' + labStatus + '\n' +
    'Research thresholds triggered: ' + thresholds + '\n' +
    'Clinical meaning: ' + meaning.substring(0, 400) + '\n' +
    (thContext ? 'Threshold detail from research:\n' + thContext.substring(0, 800) + '\n\n' : '') +
    (sigRating ? 'Significance: ' + sigRating.substring(0, 200) + '\n' : '') +
    (symptoms ? 'Likely symptoms: ' + symptoms.substring(0, 300) + '\n' : '') + '\n' +
    'Write a comprehensive, personalised briefing with these sections. Use plain English — write for an intelligent non-medical person.\n\n' +
    'WHAT IS [ANALYTE] AND WHAT DOES IT DO?\n' +
    'Explain clearly what this analyte is, what it does in the body, and why the level matters.\n\n' +
    'WHAT DOES YOUR SPECIFIC VALUE MEAN?\n' +
    'Interpret ' + value + ' specifically in the context of this patient. Reference their age, sex, hormonal status, conditions, and other relevant factors. Is this mildly below, significantly below, borderline? How does their specific profile affect how seriously to take this level?\n\n' +
    'WHAT IS LIKELY CAUSING THIS?\n' +
    'For this specific patient — given their profile and medications — what are the most probable causes? Rank by likelihood. Include any interactions with their current medications/supplements.\n\n' +
    'WHAT SYMPTOMS MIGHT THIS BE CAUSING RIGHT NOW?\n' +
    'Be specific and honest — what would a person at this level with this profile typically be experiencing? Distinguish between what is likely vs possible.\n\n' +
    'WHAT THE RESEARCH SAYS (PLAIN ENGLISH):\n' +
    'Summarise the key research evidence. What studies are relevant? What do they say about this level in someone with this profile? Mention specific papers/guidelines where relevant (e.g. "The Verdon BMJ 2003 trial showed that ferritin <30 caused fatigue even in women within normal lab ranges").\n\n' +
    'HOW DOES THIS INTERACT WITH YOUR OTHER RESULTS?\n' +
    'Are there any connections between this finding and the patient\'s other medications, supplements, or conditions that are clinically relevant?\n\n' +
    'WHAT TO DO NEXT:\n' +
    'Practical next steps in priority order. Be specific — exact supplement names, doses, timing, cofactors, what to avoid. Include what to discuss with GP, and what to say.\n\n' +
    'WHAT TO LOOK FOR GOING FORWARD:\n' +
    'What symptoms should improve first? At what timeline? What does a good response look like? When should they retest?\n\n' +
    'Write clearly, thoroughly, and practically. This is the most important section for the patient.';

  try {
    ui.alert('Generating deep-dive briefing for ' + analyte + '...\nThis is a detailed analysis — click OK to begin (20–40 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 4000);

    // Create or replace a deep-dive sheet tab
    var tabName  = '🔬 ' + analyte.substring(0, 25) + ' Deep-dive';
    var existing = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
    var ddSheet  = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(tabName);
    ddSheet.clear();
    ddSheet.setTabColor('#1e40af');
    setWidths(ddSheet, [220, 640]);

    // Header
    ddSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 2).merge()
      .setValue('🔬  DEEP-DIVE: ' + analyte.toUpperCase() + '  |  Your value: ' + value + '  |  ' + today)
      .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(12)
      .setHorizontalAlignment('center').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    ddSheet.setRowHeight(1, 36);

    // Sub-header with result context
    ddSheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 2).merge()
      .setValue('Lab status: ' + labStatus + '  |  Research flags: ' + thresholds.substring(0, 120) +
                '  |  Tested: ' + date + '  |  Patient: ' + profile.substring(0, 100))
      .setBackground('#dbeafe').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#1e3a5f');
    ddSheet.setRowHeight(2, 30);

    // Research links row
    var analyteSafe = analyte.replace(/['"]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
    var enc         = encodeURIComponent(analyteSafe);
    var richLinks = SpreadsheetApp.newRichTextValue()
      .setText('RESEARCH LINKS (click to open):    📚 Lab Tests Online    📖 PubMed    🏥 NICE Guidelines    👤 Patient.info    🎓 Google Scholar')
      .setLinkUrl(36, 52, 'https://labtestsonline.org.uk/search?q=' + enc)
      .setLinkUrl(56, 64, 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=' + enc + '+clinical+significance&sort=relevance&filter=dates.2015-2025')
      .setLinkUrl(68, 83, 'https://www.nice.org.uk/search?q=' + enc)
      .setLinkUrl(87, 99, 'https://patient.info/search/results?q=' + enc.replace(/%20/g,'+'))
      .setLinkUrl(103, 117, 'https://scholar.google.com/scholar?q=' + enc + '+clinical+reference+range')
      .build();
    ddSheet.getRange(3, 1, 1, 2).merge()
      .setRichTextValue(richLinks)
      .setBackground('#f0f9ff').setFontSize(9).setWrap(false).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    ddSheet.setRowHeight(3, 28);

    // Parse and write sections
    var sections = [
      'WHAT IS', 'WHAT DOES YOUR SPECIFIC VALUE MEAN', 'WHAT IS LIKELY CAUSING',
      'WHAT SYMPTOMS', 'WHAT THE RESEARCH SAYS', 'HOW DOES THIS INTERACT',
      'WHAT TO DO NEXT', 'WHAT TO LOOK FOR'
    ];

    var sectionColors = ['#1e3a5f','#1e3a5f','#1e3a5f','#7c3aed','#166534','#78350f','#166534','#166534'];
    var sectionBgs    = ['#dbeafe','#dbeafe','#dbeafe','#ede9fe','#dcfce7','#fef9c3','#dcfce7','#dcfce7'];

    var writeRow = 4;
    var responseUpper = response.toUpperCase();

    for (var s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
      var secKey   = sections[s];
      var startIdx = responseUpper.indexOf(secKey);
      if (startIdx < 0) continue;

      // Find section title line
      var titleEnd = response.indexOf('\n', startIdx);
      var secTitle = response.substring(startIdx, titleEnd > 0 ? titleEnd : startIdx + 80).trim();

      // Find content (until next section or end)
      var nextIdx = response.length;
      for (var ns = 0; ns < sections.length; ns++) {
        if (ns === s) continue;
        var ni = responseUpper.indexOf(sections[ns], startIdx + secKey.length);
        if (ni > startIdx && ni < nextIdx) nextIdx = ni;
      }
      var secContent = response.substring(titleEnd > 0 ? titleEnd : startIdx + 80, nextIdx).trim();

      // Section header row
      ddSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 2).merge()
        .setValue(secTitle).setBackground(sectionColors[s]).setFontColor('#ffffff')
        .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10).setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
      ddSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, 28); writeRow++;

      // Content row
      var lineCount = secContent.split('\n').length;
      ddSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 2).merge()
        .setValue(secContent).setBackground(sectionBgs[s])
        .setFontSize(10).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e293b');
      ddSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, Math.max(60, Math.min(400, lineCount * 16 + 20)));
      writeRow++;
    }

    // If section parsing failed, just dump the full response
    if (writeRow <= 5) {
      ddSheet.getRange(4, 1, 1, 2).merge()
        .setValue(response).setFontSize(10).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      var lc = response.split('\n').length;
      ddSheet.setRowHeight(4, Math.max(200, Math.min(600, lc * 14)));
    }

    // Footer
    ddSheet.getRange(writeRow + 1, 1, 1, 2).merge()
      .setValue('⚠️  This briefing is AI-generated for personal research purposes. Always discuss with your GP or specialist before making clinical decisions. AI may make errors — verify critical facts via the research links above.')
      .setBackground('#fef2f2').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#7f1d1d').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    ddSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow + 1, 40);

    ss.setActiveSheet(ddSheet);
    ui.alert(
      '✅ Deep-dive briefing created for ' + analyte + '.\n\n' +
      'A new tab "' + tabName + '" has been created with:\n' +
      '• Plain-English explanation of what the analyte does\n' +
      '• Personalised interpretation of YOUR value\n' +
      '• Likely causes given your specific profile\n' +
      '• What symptoms it may be causing\n' +
      '• Key research evidence in plain language\n' +
      '• Specific action steps\n' +
      '• Clickable links to Lab Tests Online, PubMed, NICE, Patient.info\n\n' +
      'The tab stays until you delete it or rebuild the tracker.'
    );

  } catch(e) {
    ui.alert('Error generating deep-dive:\n' + e.toString().substring(0, 300));
  }
}


// ─── SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS: AI FORWARD ADVICE ─────────────────
function generateSignificantFindingsAdvice() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Significant Findings sheet not found. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  var lastRow = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 5) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No significant findings data yet. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  // Collect flagged findings from the sheet
  var context  = buildFullContext(ss);
  var profile  = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today    = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  // Read significant findings rows (cols 1–8, skip header rows 1-5)
  var sfData   = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 9).getValues();
  var findings = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sfData.length; i++) {
    var row = sfData[i];
    if (!row[0] || row[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || row[0].toString().indexOf('HISTORIC') >= 0) continue;
    if (row[1]) findings.push({
      analyte:   row[0], value: row[1], date: row[2],
      labStatus: row[3], thresholds: row[4], meaning: row[5], causes: row[6]
    });
  }

  if (findings.length === 0) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No flagged findings found. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  var findingsStr = findings.map(function(f) {
    return f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' (tested ' + f.date + ', lab: ' + f.labStatus + ')\n  Flags: ' + f.thresholds + '\n  Meaning: ' + (f.meaning || '').substring(0, 200);
  }).join('\n\n');

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical specialist reviewing a patient\'s flagged blood test results. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS (research threshold breaches):\n' + findingsStr + '\n\n' +
    'MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (buildFullContext(ss).split('MEDICATIONS')[1] || 'See profile').substring(0, 600) + '\n\n' +
    'Please provide a comprehensive FORWARD ACTION PLAN:\n\n' +
    '1. PRIORITY ACTIONS: What should the patient do first? Rank by urgency.\n' +
    '2. SELF-MANAGEMENT STEPS: What can the patient do themselves? (diet, lifestyle, supplements, timing of supplements, cofactors needed)\n' +
    '   Be very specific — e.g. "Take iron with 500mg Vitamin C, avoid tea/coffee within 1 hour" not just "improve diet"\n' +
    '3. GP ACTIONS NEEDED: What should be discussed with or requested from the GP?\n' +
    '   Which of these findings warrants GP involvement vs self-management?\n' +
    '4. FURTHER TESTING: What additional tests would complete the picture? Why?\n' +
    '5. INTERACTIONS & CONNECTIONS: Do any of these findings interact? (e.g. low B12 reduces iron absorption, low Vit D affects thyroid etc)\n' +
    '6. TIMELINE: What is a realistic timeline for improvement if the patient takes these actions?\n\n' +
    'Write in clear, plain English a non-medical person can understand and act on. Be specific and practical.';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Generating AI action plan for ' + findings.length + ' flagged findings...\nClick OK to begin (20–30 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 3000, 'Significant Findings Advice');

    // Write advice to a new section at the bottom of the Significant Findings sheet
    var writeRow = lastRow + 2;
    sfSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 9).merge()
      .setValue('🤖  AI FORWARD ACTION PLAN — generated ' + today)
      .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11)
      .setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, 32); writeRow++;

    sfSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 9).merge()
      .setValue('ℹ️  This is AI-generated guidance based on your results. Always discuss with your GP before making significant changes. Refresh Everything will clear this — copy it first if needed.')
      .setBackground('#fef3c7').setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#92400e').setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, 30); writeRow++;

    sfSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 9).merge()
      .setValue(response)
      .setBackground('#f0f9ff').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e293b');
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, Math.max(200, Math.min(500, response.split('\n').length * 13)));

    // Store the advice in a named range / property for PDF use
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('LAST_SF_ADVICE', response);
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('LAST_SF_ADVICE_DATE', today);

    ss.setActiveSheet(sfSheet);
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ AI Action Plan generated.\n\nScroll to the bottom of 🚨 Significant Findings to read it.\n\nYou can also generate:\n• A GP letter (🏥 menu → Significant Findings → Generate GP Letter)\n• A PDF summary report (🏥 menu → Significant Findings → Generate PDF Report)');
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error:\n' + e.toString()); }
}


// ─── SIGNIFICANT FINDINGS: GP LETTER ────────────────────────
function generateGPLetter() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Significant Findings sheet not found.'); return; }

  var lastRow = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 5) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No significant findings yet. Run Refresh Everything first.'); return; }

  // Read findings
  var sfData   = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 9).getValues();
  var findings = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sfData.length; i++) {
    var row = sfData[i];
    if (!row[0] || !row[1] || row[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || row[0].toString().indexOf('AI FORWARD') >= 0) continue;
    findings.push({ analyte: row[0], value: row[1], date: row[2], labStatus: row[3], thresholds: row[4] });
  }

  if (findings.length === 0) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('No flagged findings found.'); return; }

  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  // Get patient name from profile if available, else generic
  var profileSheet = ss.getSheetByName('👤 Profile');
  var patientName  = 'My Patient';
  var gpSurgery    = '';
  if (profileSheet) {
    var pData = profileSheet.getRange(3, 1, 11, 2).getValues(); // 11 rows covers all profile fields incl. Home Town
    for (var p = 0; p < pData.length; p++) {
      if (pData[p][0].toString().indexOf('GP Surgery') >= 0) gpSurgery = pData[p][1].toString();
    }
  }

  var findingsStr = findings.map(function(f) {
    return '  • ' + f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' (tested ' + f.date + ') — ' + f.thresholds;
  }).join('\n');

  var existingAdvice = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LAST_SF_ADVICE') || '';

  var prompt =
    'Write a professional letter from a patient to their GP. Today is ' + today + '.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n' +
    (gpSurgery ? 'GP Surgery: ' + gpSurgery + '\n' : '') + '\n' +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS:\n' + findingsStr + '\n\n' +
    (existingAdvice ? 'AI ACTION PLAN (for context — do not reproduce verbatim):\n' + existingAdvice.substring(0, 800) + '\n\n' : '') +
    'Write a formal but warm letter that:\n' +
    '1. Opens politely — patient is seeking GP support, not demanding\n' +
    '2. Briefly explains they have been monitoring their test results\n' +
    '3. Lists the specific findings of concern with values and dates\n' +
    '4. Explains why these are concerning (research thresholds vs lab normal — briefly, not lecturing)\n' +
    '5. Requests specific actions — choose the most appropriate from:\n' +
    '   • Further testing (list specific tests with justification)\n' +
    '   • Prescription for supplement/medication (with suggested dose if evidence-based)\n' +
    '   • Referral (if warranted)\n' +
    '   • Discussion/appointment to review\n' +
    '6. References published guidelines briefly where relevant (e.g. "NICE NG239 2023 suggests...")\n' +
    '7. Closes respectfully — values GP expertise, seeks collaborative care\n\n' +
    'Format:\nFULL LETTER:\n[letter with Dear Dr [Name], ... Yours sincerely, [Patient]]\n\n' +
    'SHORT VERSION (for online GP message form, max 200 words):\n[short version]';

  try {
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Generating GP letter... click OK to begin (15–25 seconds).');
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 1500);

    // Write GP letter to bottom of Significant Findings
    var writeRow = sfSheet.getLastRow() + 2;
    sfSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 9).merge()
      .setValue('📧  GP LETTER — generated ' + today + '  (copy and paste into email or GP messaging portal)')
      .setBackground(C.emerald).setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, 32); writeRow++;

    sfSheet.getRange(writeRow, 1, 1, 9).merge()
      .setValue(response)
      .setBackground('#ecfdf5').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setFontColor('#1e293b');
    sfSheet.setRowHeight(writeRow, Math.max(200, Math.min(600, response.split('\n').length * 13)));

    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('LAST_GP_LETTER', response);

    ss.setActiveSheet(sfSheet);
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ GP Letter generated.\n\nScroll to the bottom of 🚨 Significant Findings.\n\nThe letter includes a full version and a short version for online GP forms.\n\nGenerate PDF Report includes this letter.');
  } catch(e) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Error:\n' + e.toString()); }
}


// ─── PDF REPORT ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Creates a formatted Google Doc report, then exports as PDF to Drive.
// Returns a Drive file URL the user can click to download.
function generatePDFReport() {
  var ss      = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui      = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (!sfSheet) { ui.alert('Significant Findings sheet not found.'); return; }

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');

  // ── Collect data ──────────────────────────────────────────
  var profile     = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var sfData      = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 13).getValues();
  var aiAdvice    = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LAST_SF_ADVICE') || '';
  var gpLetter    = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LAST_GP_LETTER') || '';
  var adviceDate  = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('LAST_SF_ADVICE_DATE') || today;

  var findings = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sfData.length; i++) {
    var row = sfData[i];
    if (!row[0] || !row[1]) continue;
    var lbl = row[0].toString();
    if (lbl.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || lbl.indexOf('AI FORWARD') >= 0 || lbl.indexOf('GP LETTER') >= 0 || lbl.indexOf('FUTURE') >= 0) break;
    findings.push({
      analyte: lbl, value: row[1], date: row[2], labStatus: row[3],
      thresholds: row[4], meaning: row[5], causes: row[6],
      // AI significance panel (cols 10-13, index 9-12)
      sigRating:   row[9]  ? row[9].toString()  : '',
      symptoms:    row[10] ? row[10].toString() : '',
      easeOfFix:   row[11] ? row[11].toString() : '',
      correction:  row[12] ? row[12].toString() : ''
    });
  }

  // ── Create Google Doc ─────────────────────────────────────
  var docName = 'Medical Summary Report — ' + todayFs;
  var doc     = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body    = doc.getBody();

  // Style helpers
  function addHeading(text, level) {
    var style = level === 1 ? DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1
              : level === 2 ? DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2
              :                DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING3;
    var p = body.appendParagraph(text);
    p.setHeading(style);
    return p;
  }
  function addPara(text, bold) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(text || '');
    if (bold) p.setBold(true);
    p.setFontSize(10);
    return p;
  }
  function addRule() {
    body.appendHorizontalRule();
  }

  // ── Cover ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var titleP = body.appendParagraph('Medical Test Results — Summary Report');
  titleP.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);

  body.appendParagraph('Generated: ' + today).setFontSize(9).setItalic(true);
  body.appendParagraph('This report is for personal use and to support conversations with your GP or specialist. It is not a medical diagnosis. All AI-generated content should be discussed with a qualified clinician.').setFontSize(9).setItalic(true);
  addRule();

  // ── Patient Profile ───────────────────────────────────────
  addHeading('Patient Profile', 2);
  var profileSheet = ss.getSheetByName('👤 Profile');
  if (profileSheet) {
    var pData = profileSheet.getRange(3, 1, 9, 2).getValues();
    for (var p = 0; p < pData.length; p++) {
      if (pData[p][0] && pData[p][1]) {
        body.appendParagraph(pData[p][0] + ': ' + pData[p][1]).setFontSize(10);
      }
    }
  }
  addRule();

  // ── Significant Findings Table ────────────────────────────
  addHeading('Significant Findings (' + findings.length + ' flagged)', 1);
  addPara('Results below are within NHS laboratory reference ranges but trigger research-based threshold flags, suggesting they may be contributing to symptoms even when the lab reports them as "Normal".', false);
  body.appendParagraph('');

  if (findings.length > 0) {
    // One section per finding — gives space for all the AI content
    for (var f = 0; f < findings.length; f++) {
      var fd = findings[f];
      // Extract score number from sigRating string for background colour
      var scoreMatch = fd.sigRating.match(/(\d)\/5/);
      var score      = scoreMatch ? parseInt(scoreMatch[1]) : 0;
      var scoreBg    = score >= 5 ? '#fef2f2' : score === 4 ? '#fff7ed' : score === 3 ? '#fefce8' : '#f0fdf4';

      // Finding heading with score badge
      var scoreLabel = fd.sigRating ? fd.sigRating.split('\n')[0] : '';
      var fHeading = body.appendParagraph((f + 1) + '.  ' + fd.analyte + '   ' + fd.value + '  (tested ' + (fd.date ? formatDate(fd.date) : '?') + ')');
      fHeading.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);

      // Core data table (compact)
      var coreTable = body.appendTable();
      var coreRows  = [
        ['Lab Status', fd.labStatus || 'Not recorded', 'Research Flags', (fd.thresholds || '').substring(0, 120)],
      ];
      for (var cr = 0; cr < coreRows.length; cr++) {
        var ctRow = coreTable.appendTableRow();
        for (var cc = 0; cc < coreRows[cr].length; cc++) {
          var ctCell = ctRow.appendTableCell(coreRows[cr][cc]);
          ctCell.getChild(0).asParagraph().setFontSize(9);
          if (cc % 2 === 0) ctCell.getChild(0).asParagraph().setBold(true);
        }
      }

      // AI significance panel (if populated)
      if (fd.sigRating || fd.symptoms || fd.correction) {
        body.appendParagraph('');
        var aiTable = body.appendTable();

        if (fd.sigRating) {
          var sr = aiTable.appendTableRow();
          sr.appendTableCell('Significance').getChild(0).asParagraph().setBold(true).setFontSize(9);
          sr.appendTableCell(fd.sigRating.replace(/🔴|🟠|🟡|🔵|🟢/g, '').trim().substring(0, 300)).getChild(0).asParagraph().setFontSize(9);
        }
        if (fd.symptoms) {
          var sym = aiTable.appendTableRow();
          sym.appendTableCell('Likely Symptoms').getChild(0).asParagraph().setBold(true).setFontSize(9);
          sym.appendTableCell(fd.symptoms.substring(0, 400)).getChild(0).asParagraph().setFontSize(9);
        }
        if (fd.easeOfFix) {
          var ef = aiTable.appendTableRow();
          ef.appendTableCell('Ease of Fix').getChild(0).asParagraph().setBold(true).setFontSize(9);
          ef.appendTableCell(fd.easeOfFix.replace(/⚕️|✅/g, '').trim()).getChild(0).asParagraph().setFontSize(9);
        }
        if (fd.correction) {
          var co = aiTable.appendTableRow();
          co.appendTableCell('Correction Plan').getChild(0).asParagraph().setBold(true).setFontSize(9);
          co.appendTableCell(fd.correction.substring(0, 500)).getChild(0).asParagraph().setFontSize(9);
        }
      } else {
        body.appendParagraph('  Significance ratings not yet generated. Run 🏥 → Significant Findings → Generate AI Significance Ratings.')
          .setFontSize(8).setItalic(true);
      }

      body.appendParagraph('');
      if (f < findings.length - 1) addRule();
    }
  } else {
    addPara('No flagged findings found. Run Refresh Everything to populate.', false);
  }

  addRule();

  // ── AI Forward Action Plan ────────────────────────────────
  addHeading('AI Forward Action Plan', 1);
  if (aiAdvice && aiAdvice.length > 10) {
    addPara('Generated: ' + adviceDate, false).setItalic(true).setFontSize(9);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    // Split by numbered sections for readability
    var sections = aiAdvice.split(/\n(?=\d\.|\*\*\d)/);
    for (var s = 0; s < sections.length; s++) {
      var sec = sections[s].trim();
      if (!sec) continue;
      if (sec.match(/^\d\./)) {
        var lines = sec.split('\n');
        var secTitle = lines[0];
        addPara(secTitle, true).setFontSize(10);
        for (var l = 1; l < lines.length; l++) {
          if (lines[l].trim()) body.appendParagraph(lines[l]).setFontSize(10);
        }
      } else {
        body.appendParagraph(sec).setFontSize(10);
      }
      body.appendParagraph('');
    }
  } else {
    addPara('No AI Action Plan generated yet. Run: 🏥 menu → Significant Findings → Generate AI Advice & Action Plan', false);
  }

  addRule();

  // ── GP Letter ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (gpLetter && gpLetter.length > 10) {
    addHeading('GP Letter', 1);
    addPara('Copy and paste this into an email or GP online messaging portal.', false).setItalic(true).setFontSize(9);
    body.appendParagraph('');
    var gpLines = gpLetter.split('\n');
    for (var gl = 0; gl < gpLines.length; gl++) {
      body.appendParagraph(gpLines[gl] || '').setFontSize(10);
    }
    addRule();
  }

  // ── Disclaimer ────────────────────────────────────────────
  addHeading('Important Notice', 2);
  body.appendParagraph('This report is generated by an AI-assisted personal health tracking tool. It is intended to support informed conversations with your GP or specialist, not to replace clinical advice. AI-generated threshold values and recommendations should be verified with a qualified clinician. Nothing in this report constitutes a medical diagnosis or treatment plan.').setFontSize(9).setItalic(true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  // ── Export as PDF ─────────────────────────────────────────
  try {
    var docFile  = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
    var pdfBlob  = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
    pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');

    // Save PDF to same folder as the spreadsheet, or root if not in a folder
    var ssFile    = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
    var parents   = ssFile.getParents();
    var pdfFile;
    if (parents.hasNext()) {
      pdfFile = parents.next().createFile(pdfBlob);
    } else {
      pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
    }

    // Delete the intermediate Google Doc (keep only the PDF)
    docFile.setTrashed(true);

    var pdfUrl = pdfFile.getUrl();
    ui.alert(
      '✅ PDF Report generated!\n\n' +
      'File: ' + docName + '.pdf\n' +
      'Saved to: ' + (parents.hasNext() ? 'same folder as this spreadsheet' : 'Google Drive root') + '\n\n' +
      'To open it:\n' +
      '1. Go to Google Drive\n' +
      '2. Find "' + docName + '.pdf"\n' +
      '3. Open and download\n\n' +
      'Or find it at:\n' + pdfUrl
    );
  } catch(pdfErr) {
    // If PDF export fails (permissions), offer the Google Doc instead
    var docUrl = doc.getUrl();
    ui.alert(
      '⚠️ PDF export requires Drive permissions.\n\n' +
      'A Google Doc report was created instead:\n' + docUrl + '\n\n' +
      'You can:\n' +
      '• Open the Doc → File → Download → PDF Document\n' +
      '• Share the Doc link directly\n\n' +
      'Error: ' + pdfErr.toString().substring(0, 100)
    );
  }
}


// ─── CONTEXT BUILDERS ───────────────────────────────────────
// cutoffDate (optional Date): when provided, ALL data sources are filtered
// to only include entries BEFORE this date. Used when a symptom investigation
// has a historic date — ensures the AI only sees what was knowable at the time,
// not results or records that came after.
function buildFullContext(ss, cutoffDate) {
  var parts = [];

  // Normalise cutoff — strip to midnight so same-day results are included
  var cutoff = null;
  if (cutoffDate instanceof Date && !isNaN(cutoffDate.getTime())) {
    cutoff = new Date(cutoffDate.getFullYear(), cutoffDate.getMonth(), cutoffDate.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
  }

  // Helper: returns true if a row date is on or before the cutoff
  function beforeCutoff(rowDate) {
    if (!cutoff) return true;
    if (!rowDate) return false;
    var d = rowDate instanceof Date ? rowDate : new Date(rowDate);
    return d <= cutoff;
  }

  // Preamble note for AI when filtering is active
  if (cutoff) {
    parts.push(
      'DATA CUTOFF NOTICE: This investigation has a historic date. ' +
      'ALL data below (test results, GP records, medications, prior investigations) ' +
      'has been filtered to only include information available on or before ' +
      Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy') + '. ' +
      'Do NOT make any reference to events, results, or records that would have occurred after this date. ' +
      'Your assessment must reflect what was clinically knowable at that time only.'
    );
  }

  // ── 1. Profile ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  parts.push('PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + getProfileSummary(ss));

  // ── 2. Test results — filtered to cutoff ─────────────────────
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  if (trSheet && thSheet) {
    var trData   = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
    var thData   = thSheet.getRange(4,1,2000,10).getValues();
    var thMap    = buildThresholdMap(thData);

    // Filter trData to cutoff before building result maps
    var trDataFiltered = cutoff
      ? trData.filter(function(row){ return row[1] && beforeCutoff(row[1]); })
      : trData;

    var results  = collectLatestResults(trDataFiltered);
    var allByKey = collectAllResults(trDataFiltered);
    var resultLines = []; var flagLines = []; var trendLines = [];
    var excludedCount = cutoff ? (trData.filter(function(r){ return r[1] && !beforeCutoff(r[1]); }).length) : 0;

    for (var aKey in results) {
      var res    = results[aKey];
      var thRows = thMap[aKey] || findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap);
      resultLines.push(res.analyte+': '+res.value+' '+res.unit+
        ' (lab range: '+res.refRange+', lab status: '+res.status+', tested: '+formatDate(res.date)+')');

      if (thRows) {
        for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) {
          if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) {
            flagLines.push('⚠️ '+res.analyte+' '+res.value+' '+res.unit+
              ' — '+thRows[b][1]+' ('+thRows[b][3]+')');
          }
        }
      }

      var history = allByKey[aKey] || [];
      if (history.length >= 2) {
     history.sort(function(a,b){ return parseDMY(a.date) - parseDMY(b.date); });
        var oldest    = history[0];
        var newest    = history[history.length - 1];
        var change    = newest.value - oldest.value;
        var pct       = oldest.value !== 0 ? Math.round((change / Math.abs(oldest.value)) * 100) : 0;
        var direction = change > 0 ? '↑ rising' : change < 0 ? '↓ falling' : '→ stable';
        var histStr   = history.map(function(h){ return formatDate(h.date)+': '+h.value; }).join(' → ');
        trendLines.push(res.analyte+': '+direction+' ('+pct+'% over '+history.length+' readings: '+histStr+')');
      }
    }

    var resultsHeader = 'LATEST TEST RESULTS' + (cutoff ? ' (on or before '+Utilities.formatDate(cutoff,Session.getScriptTimeZone(),'dd MMM yyyy')+')' : '') + ':';
    if (excludedCount > 0) resultsHeader += '\n[Note: ' + excludedCount + ' result row(s) dated after the investigation date were excluded from this analysis]';

    if (resultLines.length > 0) parts.push(resultsHeader+'\n'+resultLines.join('\n'));
    else if (cutoff) parts.push(resultsHeader+'\n[No test results found before this date]');
    if (flagLines.length  > 0) parts.push('RESEARCH THRESHOLD FLAGS:\n'+flagLines.join('\n'));
    if (trendLines.length > 0) parts.push('RESULT TRENDS (historical trajectory):\n'+trendLines.join('\n'));
  }

  // ── 3. Medications & supplements — filtered to cutoff ────────
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  if (medSheet) {
    var medData = medSheet.getRange(5,1,500,9).getValues();
    var medLines = []; var excludedMeds = 0;
    for (var m = 0; m < medData.length; m++) {
      if (!medData[m][0]) continue;
      var startDate = medData[m][5];
      // Exclude meds that hadn't been started yet at the cutoff
      if (cutoff && startDate && !beforeCutoff(startDate)) { excludedMeds++; continue; }
      // For meds that were stopped AFTER the cutoff, show as current (they were active at cutoff)
      var stoppedDate = medData[m][6];
      var stoppedAtCutoff = cutoff && stoppedDate && !beforeCutoff(stoppedDate);
      var stopped = stoppedDate
        ? (stoppedAtCutoff ? ' (current as of investigation date)' : ' (stopped: '+formatDate(stoppedDate)+')')
        : ' (current)';
      medLines.push(medData[m][0]+' — '+medData[m][2]+'×'+medData[m][3]+'/day'+
        ', started: '+formatDate(startDate)+stopped+
        (medData[m][8] ? ', note: '+medData[m][8] : ''));
    }
    var medHeader = 'MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS' + (cutoff ? ' (active as of investigation date)' : ' (current and recent)') + ':';
    if (excludedMeds > 0) medHeader += '\n[Note: '+excludedMeds+' item(s) started after the investigation date were excluded]';
    if (medLines.length > 0) parts.push(medHeader+'\n'+medLines.join('\n'));
  }

  // ── 4. GP / consultation records — filtered to cutoff ────────
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  if (recSheet) {
    var recData = recSheet.getRange(5,1,500,7).getValues();
    var recLines = []; var excludedRecs = 0;
    for (var r = 0; r < recData.length; r++) {
      if (!recData[r][1]) continue;
      if (cutoff && !beforeCutoff(recData[r][1])) { excludedRecs++; continue; }
      var recType = recData[r][2] ? recData[r][2].toString() : '';
      var title   = recData[r][3] ? recData[r][3].toString().substring(0,80) : '';
      var details = recData[r][4] ? recData[r][4].toString().substring(0,200) : '';
      var doctor  = recData[r][5] ? recData[r][5].toString() : '';
      if (!title && !details) continue;
      recLines.push(formatDate(recData[r][1]) + (recType?' ['+recType+']':'') +
        (doctor?' ('+doctor+')':'')+': ' + title + (details?' — '+details:''));
    }
    var recHeader = 'GP / CONSULTATION RECORDS' + (cutoff ? ' (on or before investigation date)' : ' (most recent 30)') + ':';
    if (excludedRecs > 0) recHeader += '\n[Note: '+excludedRecs+' record(s) dated after the investigation date were excluded]';
    if (recLines.length > 0) {
      var recLimited = recLines.slice(Math.max(0, recLines.length - 30));
      parts.push(recHeader+'\n'+recLimited.join('\n'));
    } else if (cutoff) {
      parts.push(recHeader+'\n[No GP records found before this date]');
    }
  }

  // ── 5. Previous symptom investigations — filtered to cutoff ──
  var histContext = getInvestigationHistory(ss, cutoff);
  if (histContext) parts.push(histContext);

  return parts.join('\n\n');
}



// ─── VIEW FULL AI CONTEXT ─────────────────────────────────────
// Shows the user exactly what context the AI receives when analysing
// their symptoms — so they can verify history is being used correctly
// and understand what the AI "knows" about them.
function viewAIContext() {
  var ss      = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui      = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');

  var context = buildFullContext(ss);

  // Count what's included
  var hasHistory  = context.indexOf('PREVIOUS SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS') >= 0;
  var hasTrends   = context.indexOf('RESULT TRENDS') >= 0;
  var hasGPRecs   = context.indexOf('GP / CONSULTATION RECORDS') >= 0;
  var hasMeds     = context.indexOf('MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS') >= 0;
  var hasFlags    = context.indexOf('RESEARCH THRESHOLD FLAGS') >= 0;

  // Count investigations
  var invCount = 0;
  try {
    var invIndex = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    invCount = invIndex.length;
  } catch(e) {}

  // Write context to a sheet tab for easy reading
  var tabName  = '🔍 AI Context View';
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
  var ctxSheet = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(tabName);
  ctxSheet.clear();
  ctxSheet.setTabColor('#1e40af');
  ctxSheet.setColumnWidth(1, 860);

  ctxSheet.getRange(1, 1)
    .setValue('🤖  FULL AI CONTEXT — exactly what the AI receives when analysing your symptoms  |  Generated: ' + today)
    .setBackground('#1e40af').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11)
    .setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  ctxSheet.setRowHeight(1, 32);

  ctxSheet.getRange(2, 1)
    .setValue(
      'Context summary:  ' +
      '✅ Profile  |  ' +
      '✅ Latest results  |  ' +
      (hasFlags   ? '✅' : '⬜') + ' Research flags  |  ' +
      (hasTrends  ? '✅' : '⬜') + ' Historical trends  |  ' +
      (hasMeds    ? '✅' : '⬜') + ' Medications & supplements  |  ' +
      (hasGPRecs  ? '✅' : '⬜') + ' GP/consultation records  |  ' +
      (hasHistory ? '✅ ' + invCount + ' past investigation(s)' : '⬜ No saved investigations yet') + '\n' +
      'Total context length: ' + Math.round(context.length / 1000) + 'k characters' + '\n' +
      'To add investigation history: run 🏥 → Symptom Investigation → Run AI Analysis, then Save Investigation.'
    )
    .setBackground('#dbeafe').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#1e3a5f').setVerticalAlignment('top');
  ctxSheet.setRowHeight(2, 65);

  ctxSheet.getRange(3, 1)
    .setValue(context)
    .setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top').setBackground('#f8fafc');
  var contextLines = context.split('\n').length;
  ctxSheet.setRowHeight(3, Math.min(2000, Math.max(200, contextLines * 14)));

  ctxSheet.getRange(4, 1)
    .setValue('ℹ️  This view is read-only. Edit data in the relevant sheets. Delete this tab when done — it regenerates on demand.')
    .setBackground('#fef9c3').setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#713f12').setWrap(true);
  ctxSheet.setRowHeight(4, 28);

  ss.setActiveSheet(ctxSheet);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ AI Context view created.\n\n' +
    'Context includes:\n' +
    (hasHistory ? '✅ ' + invCount + ' saved investigation(s)\n' : '⬜ No saved investigations (run & save some first)\n') +
    (hasTrends  ? '✅ Historical result trends\n' : '⬜ No trend data (need 2+ results per analyte)\n') +
    (hasGPRecs  ? '✅ GP/consultation records from Records sheet\n' : '⬜ No GP records in Records sheet\n') +
    (hasMeds    ? '✅ Medications & supplements\n' : '⬜ No medications logged\n') + '\n' +
    'Tab "🔍 AI Context View" created — scroll to read the full context.\n' +
    'Delete it when done.'
  );
}


// ─── INVESTIGATION HISTORY BUILDER ──────────────────────────
// Reads past saved investigations from Script Properties and
// summarises them for inclusion in AI context. Includes:
// - previous symptoms and when they occurred
// - what the AI said at the time
// - what the GP said
// - whether issues were resolved
// cutoffDate (optional Date): only include investigations saved BEFORE this date.
function getInvestigationHistory(ss, cutoffDate) {
  var lines = [];

  // Normalise cutoff to end-of-day
  var cutoff = null;
  if (cutoffDate instanceof Date && !isNaN(cutoffDate.getTime())) {
    cutoff = new Date(cutoffDate.getFullYear(), cutoffDate.getMonth(), cutoffDate.getDate(), 23, 59, 59);
  }

  // Helper: parse a dd/MM/yyyy string into a Date
  function parseInvDate(ds) {
    if (!ds) return null;
    var parts = ds.toString().split('/');
    if (parts.length === 3) {
      return new Date(parseInt(parts[2]), parseInt(parts[1])-1, parseInt(parts[0]));
    }
    var d = new Date(ds);
    return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
  }

  // Source 1: Script Properties (full text, stored by saveInvestigation)
  try {
    var invIndex = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    var propInvestigations = [];
    var excludedInvCount = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < invIndex.length; i++) {
      var stored = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIndex[i]);
      if (stored) {
        try {
          var inv = JSON.parse(stored);
          // Apply cutoff filter — exclude investigations dated after the cutoff
          if (cutoff) {
            var invDate = parseInvDate(inv.date);
            if (invDate && invDate > cutoff) { excludedInvCount++; continue; }
          }
          propInvestigations.push(inv);
        } catch(e) {}
      }
    }
    propInvestigations.sort(function(a,b){ return (a.date > b.date ? -1 : a.date < b.date ? 1 : 0); });

    if (propInvestigations.length > 0) {
      var invLines = [];
      for (var p = 0; p < propInvestigations.length; p++) {
        var inv2 = propInvestigations[p];
        var line = '[' + inv2.id + ' — ' + inv2.date + '] ' +
          'Symptoms: ' + (inv2.symptoms || '').substring(0, 200) +
          (inv2.severity ? ' (severity ' + inv2.severity + '/10)' : '') +
          (inv2.duration ? ', duration: ' + inv2.duration : '') + '.';
        if (inv2.aiAssess && inv2.aiAssess.length > 20) {
          line += ' AI assessment: ' + inv2.aiAssess.substring(0, 300) + '.';
        }
        if (inv2.gpAdvice && inv2.gpAdvice.length > 5) {
          line += ' GP said: ' + inv2.gpAdvice.substring(0, 150) + '.';
        }
        if (inv2.status) line += ' Status: ' + inv2.status + '.';
        invLines.push(line);
      }
      var invHeader = 'PREVIOUS SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS (' + invLines.length + ', most recent first)';
      if (excludedInvCount > 0) invHeader += '\n[Note: ' + excludedInvCount + ' investigation(s) dated after the investigation date were excluded]';
      invHeader += ':';
      lines.push(invHeader + '\n' + invLines.join('\n'));
    } else if (cutoff && excludedInvCount > 0) {
      lines.push('PREVIOUS SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n[All ' + excludedInvCount + ' saved investigation(s) are dated after the investigation date and were excluded from this historic analysis]');
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Source 2: Investigation Records sheet (fallback — summary only)
  if (lines.length === 0) {
    try {
      var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Investigation Records');
      if (recSheet) {
        var lastRow  = recSheet.getLastRow();
        if (lastRow >= 4) {
          var invData  = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, Math.min(lastRow - 3, 20), 10).getValues();
          var sheetLines = []; var sheetExcluded = 0;
          for (var s = invData.length - 1; s >= 0; s--) {
            if (!invData[s][0]) continue;
            if (cutoff) {
              var sd = parseInvDate(invData[s][1] ? invData[s][1].toString() : '');
              if (sd && sd > cutoff) { sheetExcluded++; continue; }
            }
            var sLine = '[' + invData[s][0] + ' — ' + invData[s][1] + '] ' +
              'Symptoms: ' + (invData[s][2] || '').toString().substring(0, 150) + '. ' +
              'AI: ' + (invData[s][3] || '').toString().substring(0, 200) + '. ' +
              (invData[s][4] === 'Yes' ? 'GP seen. GP said: ' + (invData[s][5] || '').toString().substring(0, 100) + '. ' : '') +
              'Status: ' + (invData[s][7] || '') + '.';
            sheetLines.push(sLine);
          }
          if (sheetLines.length > 0) {
            var shHeader = 'PREVIOUS SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS (summary from records, ' + sheetLines.length + ' records)';
            if (sheetExcluded > 0) shHeader += '\n[Note: ' + sheetExcluded + ' record(s) dated after the investigation date were excluded]';
            shHeader += ':';
            lines.push(shHeader + '\n' + sheetLines.join('\n'));
          }
        }
      }
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  return lines.join('\n\n');
}

function getProfileSummary(ss) {
  var profileSheet = ss.getSheetByName('👤 Profile');
  if (!profileSheet) return 'Profile not available';
  // Rows 3–11 are the 9 data fields (label in col A, value in col B)
  var pData = profileSheet.getRange(3, 1, 9, 2).getValues();
  var parts = [];
  var yearOfBirth = null;
  for (var p = 0; p < pData.length; p++) {
    if (!pData[p][0] || !pData[p][1]) continue;
    var label = pData[p][0].toString();
    var value = pData[p][1].toString();
    parts.push(label + ': ' + value);
    if (label.indexOf('Year of Birth') >= 0) {
      var yob = parseInt(value);
      if (!isNaN(yob)) yearOfBirth = yob;
    }
  }
  // Inject explicit calculated age so AI never derives it inconsistently
  if (yearOfBirth) {
    var currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    parts.push('Age (calculated): ' + (currentYear - yearOfBirth) + ' years old');
  }
  // Also include calculated BMI (row 13 = r+2 after the BMI section header at row 12)
  try {
    var bmi = profileSheet.getRange(13, 2).getValue();
    if (bmi && !isNaN(parseFloat(bmi))) parts.push('BMI: ' + bmi);
  } catch(e) {}
  return parts.join(', ');
}

function getProfileContext(ss) {
  var summary = getProfileSummary(ss);
  return summary.length > 10 ? '\n\nPATIENT PROFILE (personalise where relevant):\n'+summary : '';
}

function extractSection(text, startHeading, endHeading) {
  if (!text) return '';
  var startIdx = text.indexOf(startHeading);
  if (startIdx === -1) return '';
  startIdx += startHeading.length;
  // Skip past any punctuation/newlines after heading
  while (startIdx < text.length && (text[startIdx]===':'||text[startIdx]==='\n'||text[startIdx]===' ')) startIdx++;
  var endIdx = endHeading ? text.indexOf(endHeading, startIdx) : text.length;
  if (endIdx === -1) endIdx = text.length;
  return text.substring(startIdx, endIdx).trim();
}


// ─── QUICK REFRESH ─────────────────────────────────
// Rebuilds Analysis + Significant Findings from existing data.
// NO AI CALL. NO OpenAI cost. NO threshold re-run.
// Uses whatever thresholds were last generated by Full Refresh.
// Run after: pasting new test results, adding records.
// Time: ~10 seconds.
function quickRefresh() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // ── Run analyte mapping prompt here — the only safe place for getUi() ──
  // buildSignificantFindingsRows runs mapping silently (trigger-safe).
  // We do the user-facing prompt here so the stored mappings are ready
  // before the rebuild runs.
  try {
    var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
    var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
    if (trSheet && thSheet) {
      var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
      var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();
      var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
      runAnalyteMapping(ss, trData, thMap, true); // prompt=true: safe here
    }
  } catch(e) {} // never block the refresh

  _rebuildViews(ss);
  ui.alert(
    '✅ Quick Refresh complete\n\n' +
    'Analysis and Significant Findings have been rebuilt from your current TestResults and Thresholds.\n\n' +
    '⚠️ This did NOT update AI thresholds or re-run any AI analysis.\n' +
    'Run Full Refresh from the Data Refresh menu to update guidance, supplement ratings and significance scores (3 AI calls, 3–6 min).'
  );
}

// Silent version — called automatically after saveInvestigation so context stays current
function _quickRefreshSilent(ss) {
  try { _rebuildViews(ss); } catch(e) {}
}

// ─── REFRESH ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Fully trigger-based chain. Each stage is a separate execution.
// refreshEverything just saves intent and schedules the first stage.
function refreshEverything() {
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  // Show last full refresh timestamp if available
  var lastRun = props.getProperty('FULL_REFRESH_LAST_RUN') || 'Never';
  var lastRunTxt = lastRun === 'Never' ? 'Last full refresh: Never' : 'Last full refresh: ' + lastRun;

  var choice = ui.alert(
    '🔄 Full Refresh  —  3 AI calls, 3–6 minutes',
    lastRunTxt + '\n\n' +
    'WHAT THIS DOES:\n' +
    '  Step 1: Ask OpenAI to regenerate thresholds for all your analytes  [AI call]\n' +
    '  Step 2: Rebuild Analysis + Significant Findings from new thresholds  [no AI]\n' +
    '  Step 3: Ask OpenAI to review all supplements & medications  [AI call]\n' +
    '  Step 5: Compare new guidance vs previous \u2014 log any significant changes  [no AI]\n\n' +
    'WHAT THIS DOES NOT DO:\n' +
    '  \u2713 Does not run symptom analysis (do that manually after filling symptoms)\n' +
    '  \u2713 Does not change your test results or records\n\n' +
    'Run monthly, or after significant new results / new supplements.\n\n' +
    'Press YES to start (3\u20136 min, runs in background).\n' +
    'Press NO to cancel.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (choice !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  // Clear any old chain state — also check for orphaned triggers
  var existingTriggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  var stuckTriggers = existingTriggers.filter(function(t) {
    var fn = t.getHandlerFunction();
    return fn === '_runRefreshChain' || fn === '_continueThresholdRefresh';
  });
  if (stuckTriggers.length > 0) {
    var warnChoice = ui.alert(
      '⚠️ Previous refresh may still be running',
      stuckTriggers.length + ' trigger(s) from a previous refresh are still scheduled.\n\n' +
      'This usually means a previous Full Refresh timed out or got stuck.\n\n' +
      'Click YES to clear them and start fresh.\n' +
      'Click NO to cancel (use 🚨 Clear Stuck Threshold Job if needed).',
      ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
    );
    if (warnChoice !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  }
  _clearAllChainTriggers();

  // Record start time
  var startedAt = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm');
  props.setProperties({
    'REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE':   'thresholds',
    'REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID':    ss.getId(),
    'FULL_REFRESH_STARTED':  startedAt
  });

  // Schedule first stage as a trigger — do NOT run inline
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('_runRefreshChain').timeBased().after(60 * 1000).create();

  ui.alert(
    '🔄 Full Refresh started\n\n' +
    'Expected time: 3–5 minutes\n\n' +
    'How it works:\n' +
    '  • ~60s: scheduler starts (Apps Script minimum delay)\n' +
    '  • ~2–3 min: AI generates thresholds for ' + (function(){try{return JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES')||'[]').length}catch(e){return'your'}}()) + ' analytes\n' +
    '  • ~30s: Analysis, Findings, Supplements & Significance all rebuild\n\n' +
    '✅ You can keep working normally — check the ⏳ Refresh Status tab for live progress.\n\n' +
    '⏱ How it works:\n' +
    '  1. AI generates thresholds (~2–3 min)\n' +
    '  2. Analysis, Findings, Supplements & Significance rebuild (~90s)\n' +
    'Total: typically 4–6 minutes. Only 2–3 trigger waits of ~60s each.\n\n' +
    'The ⏳ Refresh Status tab will show live progress.\n' +
    'Menu → Check Refresh Status to check at any time.'
  );


}
// ─── REFRESH CHAIN RUNNER ────────────────────────────────────
// Simplified two-phase design:
//   Phase 1: _runRefreshChain sets up threshold job → schedules _continueThresholdRefresh
//   Phase 2: when thresholds complete, _runPostThresholdStages() runs ALL remaining
//            stages inline in one execution (views, findings, supplements,
//            significance, guidance_diff) — no more per-stage triggers.
// Total time: ~2-4 min (threshold AI batches) + <60s (post-threshold steps)
function _runRefreshChain() {
  _clearAllChainTriggers();
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var stage = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  var ssId  = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID') || '';
  if (!stage) return;
  var ss = ssId ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(ssId) : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  try {
    if (stage === 'thresholds') {
      props.setProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE', 'waiting_thresholds');
      _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'waiting_thresholds');
      _setupThresholdJob(ss); // schedules _continueThresholdRefresh
      return;
    }
    // Fallback: if somehow called with a post-threshold stage, run inline
    if (stage === 'views' || stage === 'findings' || stage === 'supplements' ||
