        stage === 'significance' || stage === 'guidance_diff') {
      _runPostThresholdStages(ss);
      return;
    }
  } catch(e) {
    props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'error', 'Failed at stage "' + stage + '": ' + e.toString().substring(0, 120));
  }
}

// Runs all post-threshold stages inline in one execution.
// Called directly from _continueThresholdRefresh once thresholds are written.
// Total runtime typically 30-90 seconds — well within 6-min budget.
// ─── MANUAL POST-THRESHOLD COMPLETION ────────────────────────────────────────
// Runs if the auto trigger for post-threshold stages failed or got stuck.
// Safe to run at any time after thresholds have been written.
// Menu: 🔄 Complete Refresh (post-threshold only)
function completeRefreshPostThreshold() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  // Check thresholds exist
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
  if (!thSheet || thSheet.getLastRow() < 4) {
    ui.alert('No threshold data found. Run Full Refresh first to generate thresholds.');
    return;
  }

  var confirm = ui.alert(
    '▶️ Complete Refresh — post-threshold steps',
    'This runs the steps that follow threshold generation:\n\n' +
    '  • Rebuild Analysis\n' +
    '  • Rebuild Significant Findings\n' +
    '  • Supplements & Medications AI review\n' +
    '  • Significance Ratings\n' +
    '  • Guidance Change Log\n\n' +
    'Use this if a Full Refresh got stuck after thresholds were written.\n\n' +
    'Takes ~60–90 seconds. Continue?',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  // Clear any stuck state
  _clearAllChainTriggers();
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE', 'views');
  if (!props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID')) {
    props.setProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID', ss.getId());
  }

  ui.alert('▶️ Running post-threshold steps now. This will take ~60–90 seconds.\n\nThe ⏳ Refresh Status tab will update as each step completes.');

  _runPostThresholdStages(ss);
}


function _runPostThresholdStages(ss) {
  // Clear the trigger that launched us
  _clearAllChainTriggers();
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var ssId  = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID') || '';
  if (!ss) ss = ssId ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(ssId) : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  // Seed confirmed NHS SAR name variant pairs into the mapping cache
  // before rebuilding views, so lookups resolve correctly first time.
  try { _seedKnownMappings(ss); } catch(e) { Logger.log("_seedKnownMappings error: " + e); }
  // Each step wrapped individually — one failure won't kill the whole run
  try { _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'views'); _rebuildAnalysis(ss); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); }
  catch(e) { Logger.log('_rebuildAnalysis error: ' + e); }

  try { _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'findings'); _rebuildFindings(ss); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); }
  catch(e) { Logger.log('_rebuildFindings error: ' + e); }

  try { _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'supplements'); _runAnalyseSupplemensBackground(ss); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); }
  catch(e) { Logger.log('_runAnalyseSupplements error: ' + e); }

  try { _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'significance'); _runSignificanceRatingsBackground(ss); SpreadsheetApp.flush(); }
  catch(e) { Logger.log('_runSignificanceRatings error: ' + e); }

  try { _runGuidanceDiff(ss); } catch(e) { Logger.log('_runGuidanceDiff error: ' + e); }

  // Done — mark complete regardless of individual step errors
  try {
    var completedAt = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm');
    props.setProperty('FULL_REFRESH_LAST_RUN', completedAt);
    props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
    props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_SSID');
    props.deleteProperty('FULL_REFRESH_STARTED');
    _setRefreshStatus(ss, 'complete');
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _clearAllChainTriggers() {
  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var i = 0; i < triggers.length; i++) {
    var fn = triggers[i].getHandlerFunction();
    if (fn === '_runRefreshChain' || fn === '_continueThresholdRefresh' || fn === '_runPostThresholdStages') {
      try { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(triggers[i]); } catch(e) {}
    }
  }
}

// Nuclear option — deletes ALL project triggers, not just chain ones.
// Use if refresh is permanently stuck and clearStuckThresholdJob doesn't help.
function clearAllTriggers() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var confirm = ui.alert(
    '🚨 Clear ALL Triggers',
    'This will delete every scheduled trigger in this script project — ' +
    'including any stuck refresh, threshold, or chain triggers.\n\n' +
    'Use this if a Full Refresh is stuck and won\'t clear.\n\n' +
    'Also clears the REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE property so a fresh refresh can start.\n\n' +
    'Continue?',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (confirm !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  var count = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < triggers.length; i++) {
    try { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(triggers[i]); count++; } catch(e) {}
  }
  // Also clear stuck chain state
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_ANALYTES');
  props.deleteProperty('TH_JOB_START');
  props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_STAGE_HISTORY');

  ui.alert(
    '✅ All triggers cleared.',
    count + ' trigger(s) deleted.\n\nRefresh chain state reset.\n\nYou can now run a fresh Full Refresh from the 🏥 menu.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// Background (no UI alerts) version of analyseSupplements
function _runAnalyseSupplemensBackground(ss) {
  var apiKey  = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) return;
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  if (!medSheet) return;
  var medData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
  var items = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < medData.length; i++) {
    if (!medData[i][0]) continue;
    items.push({
      row: i + 5, name: medData[i][0].toString(), type: medData[i][1].toString(),
      dose: medData[i][2].toString(), freq: medData[i][3].toString(),
      total: medData[i][4].toString(), started: medData[i][5],
      stopped: medData[i][6] ? true : false, notes: medData[i][8].toString()
    });
  }
  if (items.length === 0) return;

  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
  var resultsStr = '', flagsStr = '';
  if (trSheet && thSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5,1,1996,10).getValues();
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4,1,2000,10).getValues();
    var thMap  = buildThresholdMap(thData);
    var results = collectLatestResults(trData);
    var resLines = [], flagLines = [];
    for (var aKey in results) {
      var res = results[aKey];
      resLines.push(res.analyte+': '+res.value+' '+res.unit+' (lab: '+res.status+')');
      var thRows = thMap[aKey] || findThresholdFuzzy(aKey, thMap);
      if (thRows) {
        for (var b = 0; b < thRows.length; b++) {
          if (isTriggered(res.value, thRows[b])) flagLines.push(res.analyte+' '+res.value+' — '+thRows[b][1]);
        }
      }
    }
    resultsStr = resLines.join('\n');
    flagsStr   = flagLines.join('\n');
  }

  var itemList = items.map(function(it, idx) {
    return (idx+1)+'. '+it.name+(it.dose?' — '+it.dose:'')+(it.freq?' ×'+it.freq+'/day':'')+(it.stopped?' [STOPPED]':' [CURRENT]');
  }).join('\n');

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical pharmacist reviewing a patient\'s medications and supplements.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT TEST RESULTS:\n' + (resultsStr||'No results') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED RESULTS:\n' + (flagsStr||'None') + '\n\n' +
    'MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + itemList + '\n\n' +
    'For EACH item, return a JSON object. Return a JSON ARRAY.\n' +
    'Each object: {"item_number":1,"name":"...","overall_verdict":"✅ Good choice|⚠️ Use with caution|🔄 Consider adjusting|❌ Review urgently",' +
    '"suitability_for_profile":"...","interactions_with_results":"...","interactions_with_other_items":"...",' +
    '"dose_assessment":"...","timing_advice":"...","cautions":"...","recommendation":"..."}\n' +
    'Return ONLY the JSON array.';

  try {
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 3500);
    var ratings;
    try { ratings = _extractJSONArray(response); } catch(e) { return; }
    if (!Array.isArray(ratings)) return;

    var suppInteractionMap = {};
    for (var ri = 0; ri < ratings.length && ri < items.length; ri++) {
      var rating = ratings[ri]; var item = items[ri];
      var verdict = rating.overall_verdict || '';
      var cellBg = verdict.indexOf('✅')>=0?'#f0fdf4':verdict.indexOf('⚠️')>=0?'#fef9c3':verdict.indexOf('🔄')>=0?'#eff6ff':verdict.indexOf('❌')>=0?'#fef2f2':'#f5f5f5';
      var cellFg = verdict.indexOf('✅')>=0?'#166534':verdict.indexOf('⚠️')>=0?'#713f12':verdict.indexOf('🔄')>=0?'#1e40af':verdict.indexOf('❌')>=0?'#7f1d1d':'#374151';
      var lines = [verdict];
      if (rating.suitability_for_profile) lines.push('\n'+rating.suitability_for_profile);
      var intResults = rating.interactions_with_results||'';
      if (intResults && intResults.toUpperCase()!=='NONE' && intResults.length>4) lines.push('\n⚠️ RESULT INTERACTION: '+intResults);
      var intOther = rating.interactions_with_other_items||'';
      if (intOther && intOther.toUpperCase()!=='NONE' && intOther.length>4) lines.push('\n🔗 Interaction: '+intOther);
      if (rating.dose_assessment) lines.push('\n💊 Dose: '+rating.dose_assessment);
      if (rating.timing_advice)   lines.push('\n⏰ Timing: '+rating.timing_advice);
      var cautions = rating.cautions||'';
      if (cautions && cautions.toUpperCase()!=='NONE' && cautions.length>4) lines.push('\n⚠️ Caution: '+cautions);
      if (rating.recommendation)  lines.push('\n✅ Recommendation: '+rating.recommendation);
      var cellText = lines.join('');
      medSheet.getRange(item.row, 10).setValue(cellText).setBackground(cellBg).setFontColor(cellFg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
      medSheet.setRowHeight(item.row, Math.max(80, Math.min(220, lines.join('').split('\n').length * 14)));
      if (intResults && intResults.toUpperCase()!=='NONE' && intResults.length>4) suppInteractionMap[item.name] = intResults;
    }

    var today2 = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperties({
      'SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS': JSON.stringify(suppInteractionMap),
      'SUPPLEMENT_INTERACTIONS_DATE': today2,
      'SUPPLEMENT_ANALYSIS_DATE': today2
    });
  } catch(e) {}
}

// Background version of addSignificancePanelToFindings (no UI alerts)
function _runSignificanceRatingsBackground(ss) {
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) return;
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) return;
  var lastRow = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 5) return;

  var sheetData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, lastRow, 9).getValues();
  var dataRows = [];
  for (var i = 4; i < sheetData.length; i++) {
    var row = sheetData[i];
    var label = row[0] ? row[0].toString().trim() : '';
    var value = row[1] ? row[1].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!label || label.indexOf('KEY:')>=0 || label.indexOf('AI FORWARD')>=0 ||
        label.indexOf('GP LETTER')>=0 || label.indexOf('⚠️')>=0) continue;
    if (value) dataRows.push({ sheetRow: i+1, analyte: label, value: value,
      date: row[2], labStatus: row[3], thresholds: row[4], meaning: row[5], causes: row[6] });
  }
  if (dataRows.length === 0) return;

  var profile    = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var today      = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var medContext = getMedicationContext(ss);

  var MAX_SIG_BATCH = 10;
  dataRows = dataRows.slice(0, MAX_SIG_BATCH);
  var findingsForPrompt = dataRows.map(function(d, idx) {
    return (idx+1)+'. ANALYTE: '+d.analyte+'\n   Value: '+d.value+
           '\n   Lab status: '+(d.labStatus||'not recorded')+
           '\n   Research flags: '+(d.thresholds||'').substring(0,120)+
           '\n   Clinical meaning: '+(d.meaning||'').substring(0,150);
  }).join('\n\n');

  var prompt = _buildSignificancePrompt(profile, today, medContext, findingsForPrompt, dataRows.length);

  try {
    var response = callOpenAI(prompt, 4000);

    // FIX: use _extractJSONArray which strips ```json fences before parsing
    var ratings;
    try {
      ratings = _extractJSONArray(response);
    } catch(e) {
      return; // background — fail silently
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(ratings)) return;
    _writeSignificanceRatings(sfSheet, ratings, dataRows);
  } catch(e) {}
}


// Internal — rebuilds Analysis and Significant Findings sheets.
// Called by refreshEverything and also at end of refreshThresholdsWithAI.
// Internal — rebuilds Analysis sheet only.
function _rebuildAnalysis(ss) {
  var anSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📊 Analysis');
  if (!anSheet) return;
  var lr = anSheet.getLastRow();
  if (lr > 4) anSheet.getRange(5, 1, lr - 4, 9).clearContent().clearFormat();
  buildAnalysisRows(anSheet, ss);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
}

// Internal — rebuilds Significant Findings sheet only.
function _rebuildFindings(ss) {
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  if (!sfSheet) return;
  var sfLr = sfSheet.getLastRow();
  if (sfLr > 2) sfSheet.getRange(3, 1, sfLr - 2, 9).clearContent().clearFormat();
  buildSignificantFindingsRows(sfSheet, ss);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
}

// Legacy wrapper.
function _rebuildViews(ss) {
  _rebuildAnalysis(ss);
  _rebuildFindings(ss);
}


// ─── MENU ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ─── FIX PROFILE SHEET ──────────────────────────────────────
// Rebuilds only the Profile tab — clears ALL old validation first,
// rewrites the correct dropdowns, preserves any values the user entered.
function fixProfileSheet() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName('\u{1F464} Profile');
  if (!sheet) { SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('Profile sheet not found.'); return; }

  // 1. Save existing user values (col B, rows 3 onwards) keyed by label in col A
  var lastRow  = sheet.getLastRow();
  var savedValues = {};
  if (lastRow >= 3) {
    var existing = sheet.getRange(3, 1, lastRow - 2, 2).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < existing.length; i++) {
      var lbl = existing[i][0] ? existing[i][0].toString().trim() : '';
      var val = existing[i][1];
      if (lbl && val !== '') savedValues[lbl] = val;
    }
  }

  // 2. Nuke ALL content, formatting, merges AND validation on the sheet
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), sheet.getMaxColumns()).breakApart();
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), sheet.getMaxColumns()).clearDataValidations();
  sheet.clearFormats();
  sheet.clearContents();
  sheet.clearNotes();

  // 3. Rebuild the profile sheet fresh
  buildProfileSheet(sheet, ss);

  // 4. Restore saved values by matching labels — skip auto-calculated rows
  var skipLabels = { 'calculated bmi': true, 'bmi category': true, 'healthy bmi range': true, 'bmi  (auto-calculated)': true };
  var newLastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (newLastRow >= 3) {
    var newLabels = sheet.getRange(3, 1, newLastRow - 2, 1).getValues();
    for (var j = 0; j < newLabels.length; j++) {
      var newLbl = newLabels[j][0] ? newLabels[j][0].toString().trim() : '';
      if (!newLbl) continue;
      if (skipLabels[newLbl.toLowerCase()]) continue; // never overwrite formula cells
      if (savedValues[newLbl] !== undefined) {
        sheet.getRange(j + 3, 2).setValue(savedValues[newLbl]);
      }
    }
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '\u2705 Profile tab fixed!\n\n' +
    'All old validation cleared and correct dropdowns reapplied.\n' +
    'Your entered values have been preserved.\n\n' +
    'Check each field is showing the right dropdown options now.'
  );
}

      .addItem('🗑 Delete Old Backups (keep latest)',       'deleteOldBackups'))
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('📤 Get ChatGPT Extraction Template',          'exportExtractionTemplate')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('🏗️ Rebuild Entire Tracker',                   'buildMedicalTracker')
    .addItem('🔄 Reset for New User  — wipe patient data, keep thresholds & mappings', 'resetForNewUser')
    .addItem('🔧 Fix Profile Sheet (repair dropdowns)',      'fixProfileSheet')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('📋 Version History & Rollback Guide',           'showVersionHistory')
    .addItem('🧠 Rebuild Operations Reference Tab',          'rebuildOperationsReference')
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem('🔬 Build Clinical Summary (run after adding new records)', 'buildClinicalSummary')
    .addItem('▶️ Continue Clinical Summary (if previous run timed out)', '_continueClinicalSummary')
    .addToUi();
}

// ─── CHATGPT EXTRACTION TEMPLATE ────────────────────────────
// Creates a sheet tab with copy-paste prompts for extracting
// NHS SAR data via ChatGPT into the correct column format.
function exportExtractionTemplate() {
  var ss      = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tabName = '📤 ChatGPT Extraction Guide';
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName(tabName);
  var sheet    = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(tabName);
  sheet.clear();
  sheet.setTabColor('#0369a1');
  setWidths(sheet, [160, 900]);

  // ── Header ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 2).merge()
    .setValue('📤  ChatGPT Extraction Guide — copy the prompts below to extract your NHS SAR data')
    .setBackground('#0369a1').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11)
    .setVerticalAlignment('middle').setWrap(true);
  sheet.setRowHeight(1, 32);

  sheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 2).merge()
    .setValue('How to use: (1) Copy the prompt from the yellow cell  (2) Paste into ChatGPT  (3) Upload your SAR PDF or paste the text  (4) Copy the CSV output  (5) Paste into the matching sheet in this tracker starting at row 5, columns B onwards (skip column A — that auto-fills)')
    .setBackground('#e0f2fe').setFontSize(9).setWrap(true).setFontColor('#0c4a6e');
  sheet.setRowHeight(2, 50);

  var sections = [

    // ── TEST RESULTS ────────────────────────────────────────
    { label: '🩸 TEST RESULTS\nPrompt', bg: '#fef9c3', fg: '#713f12', height: 520,
      text:
'You are a medical data extraction assistant.\n\n' +
'I will give you NHS SAR (Subject Access Request) data containing blood test results. ' +
'Extract ALL test results and return them as CSV with EXACTLY these columns in this order:\n\n' +
'date,panel,analyte,value,unit,ref_range,status,doctor,page\n\n' +
'COLUMN RULES:\n' +
'• date         — test date in DD/MM/YYYY format (e.g. 15/03/2023)\n' +
'• panel        — the test panel/group name (e.g. "Full Blood Count", "Thyroid Function", "Lipid Profile"). Use the section heading from the document.\n' +
'• analyte      — the individual test name exactly as written (e.g. "Haemoglobin", "TSH", "Ferritin", "Serum Sodium")\n' +
'• value        — numeric result only, no units (e.g. 45.2)\n' +
'• unit         — units only (e.g. "g/L", "µg/L", "mmol/L", "IU/L"). Use µ not u for micro.\n' +
'• ref_range    — reference range as written (e.g. "13.0-17.0", "0.35-4.94", ">200")\n' +
'• status       — lab flag if present: "Normal", "Low", "High", "Abnormal". Leave blank if not stated.\n' +
'• doctor       — requesting doctor name if shown, otherwise blank\n' +
'• page         — page number in the document if shown, otherwise blank\n\n' +
'IMPORTANT:\n' +
'• One row per individual analyte result\n' +
'• Do NOT include header row — data rows only\n' +
'• Do NOT include calculated fields or notes\n' +
'• If a result has multiple components (e.g. "Glucose fasting/random") use the most specific name\n' +
'• Use plain straight quotes for CSV, not curly quotes\n' +
'• Dates must be DD/MM/YYYY — convert if the source uses another format\n' +
'• If the same analyte appears multiple times on different dates, include ALL occurrences as separate rows\n\n' +
'Return ONLY the CSV rows. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks.\n\n' +
'Example output:\n' +
'15/03/2023,Full Blood Count,Haemoglobin,138,g/L,130-170,Normal,Dr Smith,4\n' +
'15/03/2023,Full Blood Count,Platelets,245,10^9/L,150-400,,Dr Smith,4\n' +
'15/03/2023,Iron Studies,Ferritin,22,µg/L,13-150,Normal,Dr Smith,5\n\n' +
'[PASTE YOUR SAR TEST RESULTS TEXT OR UPLOAD PDF BELOW THIS LINE]\n'
    },

    // ── RECORDS ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    { label: '📋 RECORDS\nPrompt', bg: '#dcfce7', fg: '#166534', height: 440,
      text:
'You are a medical data extraction assistant.\n\n' +
'I will give you NHS SAR (Subject Access Request) data containing clinical records — ' +
'consultations, referrals, letters, imaging reports, and other correspondence. ' +
'Extract ALL records and return them as CSV with EXACTLY these columns in this order:\n\n' +
'date,record_type,title,details,doctor,page\n\n' +
'COLUMN RULES:\n' +
'• date         — record date in DD/MM/YYYY format\n' +
'• record_type  — ONE of: Consultation, Referral form, Imaging report, Test Panel, Test Request, Read code, Letter, Other\n' +
'• title        — short descriptive title (max 80 characters), e.g. "GP Consultation — fatigue and low iron", "Referral to Haematology", "MRI Lumbar Spine Report"\n' +
'• details      — key clinical content summarised concisely (max 300 characters): presenting complaint, findings, diagnosis, plan, outcome. Use plain English.\n' +
'• doctor       — clinician name if shown, otherwise blank\n' +
'• page         — page number if shown, otherwise blank\n\n' +
'IMPORTANT:\n' +
'• One row per distinct clinical encounter or document\n' +
'• Do NOT include test result rows here — those go in the Test Results extraction\n' +
'• Do NOT include header row — data rows only\n' +
'• Use plain straight quotes, not curly quotes\n' +
'• Dates must be DD/MM/YYYY\n' +
'• Keep details concise but clinically meaningful — include diagnosis, key findings, prescribed treatment, and follow-up plan if present\n\n' +
'Return ONLY the CSV rows. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks.\n\n' +
'Example output:\n' +
'12/01/2023,Consultation,"GP Consultation — fatigue and breathlessness","Presenting with 3 months fatigue. FBC requested. Iron deficiency suspected. Ferrous fumarate 210mg BD prescribed pending results.",Dr Patel,12\n' +
'03/02/2023,Letter,"Haematology Outpatient Letter","Iron deficiency anaemia confirmed. Recommends 6 months oral iron then recheck. No further investigations at this stage.",Dr Williams,18\n' +
'15/02/2023,Imaging report,"Chest X-Ray","Normal chest X-ray. No acute cardiopulmonary abnormality identified.",Dr Jones,22\n\n' +
'[PASTE YOUR SAR RECORDS TEXT OR UPLOAD PDF BELOW THIS LINE]\n'
    },

    // ── PASTE INSTRUCTIONS ──────────────────────────────────
    { label: '📋 After\nExtracting', bg: '#f1f5f9', fg: '#1e293b', height: 160,
      text:
'Once ChatGPT returns the CSV rows:\n\n' +
'FOR TEST RESULTS:\n' +
'1. Go to the TestResults sheet in this tracker\n' +
'2. Click cell B5 (NOT A5 — column A is auto-generated)\n' +
'3. Paste the CSV rows (Ctrl+Shift+V or Edit → Paste Special → Values only)\n' +
'4. Column A (UniqueKey) will auto-fill — do not edit it\n\n' +
'FOR RECORDS:\n' +
'1. Go to the Records sheet\n' +
'2. Click cell B5\n' +
'3. Paste the CSV rows\n\n' +
'TIPS:\n' +
'• If dates paste as numbers, select column B and format as DD/MM/YYYY\n' +
'• Run 🔄 Refresh Everything after pasting to update all analysis\n' +
'• You can paste multiple extractions — duplicate UniqueKeys are ignored in analysis'
    }
  ];

  var r = 3;
  for (var i = 0; i < sections.length; i++) {
    var s = sections[i];
    sheet.getRange(r, 1).setValue(s.label)
      .setBackground(s.bg).setFontColor(s.fg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9)
      .setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.getRange(r, 2).setValue(s.text)
      .setBackground(s.bg).setFontColor(s.fg).setFontSize(9)
      .setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, s.height);
    r++;
    // Spacer
    sheet.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).setBackground('#f8fafc');
    sheet.setRowHeight(r, 8);
    r++;
  }

  freeze(sheet, 2);

  // Navigate to the new sheet
  ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);

  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '📤 ChatGPT Extraction Guide created!\n\n' +
    'You are now on the "📤 ChatGPT Extraction Guide" tab.\n\n' +
    'Copy the yellow prompt → paste into ChatGPT → upload your SAR PDF → ' +
    'copy the CSV output → paste into TestResults or Records at row 5 column B.\n\n' +
    'Run 🔄 Refresh Everything after pasting to update all analysis.'
  );
}

// ─── END ────────────────────────────────────────────────────


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ANALYTE NAME MAPPING ENGINE  (v5.16)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// NHS SAR exports embed values, lab notes, and status text directly in the
// analyte name field (e.g. "Serum creatinine Please note change of adult
// reference range..."), and truncate names mid-word ("Calculated LDL
// cholesterol lev").  This engine:
//
//   1. cleanAnalyteName()     — strips embedded noise from raw SAR names
//   2. resolveAnalyteName()   — maps cleaned name → Thresholds sheet key,
//                               using stored mappings sheet first, then AI
//   3. runAnalyteMapping()    — full reconciliation pass (called on refresh
//                               when user consents); prompts before AI call
//   4. viewMappingsReport()   — menu item: show/open the mappings sheet
//   5. Mappings stored in "🔗 Name Mappings" sheet (auditable, editable)
//
// MAPPING SHEET COLUMNS:
//   A: Raw name (from TestResults)
//   B: Mapped to (canonical Thresholds key, or blank if excluded/unresolved)
//   C: Status  ✅ Exact match | 🤖 AI-mapped | ✏️ Manual | 🚫 Excluded | ⚠️ Review
//   D: Confidence (AI-provided, or "exact" / "manual")
//   E: Reasoning (AI-provided explanation or manual note)
//   F: Date resolved
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── SYNONYM NORMALISER ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// NHS SARs use multiple names for the same measurement. This maps them all
// to a single canonical form so they deduplicate correctly and match a single
// Thresholds row rather than creating multiple entries for the same result.
//
// Rules are applied AFTER cleanAnalyteName() so inputs are already stripped
// of embedded values and lab notes.
//
// Add new synonyms here as encountered — format is:
//   canonicalName (lowercase): [array of variant names to match (lowercase substrings)]
function normaliseSynonyms(cleaned) {
  var SYNONYMS = [
    // Blood pressure — systolic
    {
      canonical: 'Systolic blood pressure',
      match: ['systolic arterial pressure','blood pressure (systolic)',
              'systolic bp','bp systolic']
    },
    // Blood pressure — diastolic
    {
      canonical: 'Diastolic blood pressure',
      match: ['diastolic arterial pressure','blood pressure (diastolic)',
              'diastolic bp','bp diastolic']
    },
    // HbA1c — various NHS SAR and OCR corruption variants
    {
      canonical: 'HbA1c levi - IFCC standardised', // matches actual Thresholds key (OCR typo preserved)
      match: ['hba1c ifcc-standardised','hba1c level - ifcc standardised',
              'hba\\1c levi - ifcc standardised','hba1c levi - ifcc standardised',
              'hba1c level - ifcc','glycated haemoglobin','hba1c ifcc']
    },
    // MCH — canonical = abbreviated form that AI stored in Thresholds sheet
    {
      canonical: 'Mean corpusc. haemoglobin (MCH)',
      match: ['mean corpusc. haemoglobin(mch)',
              'mean corpuscular haemoglobin (mch)',
              'mean corpuscular haemoglobin(mch)',
              'mean corpusc. haemoglobin(mch)',
              'mch (mean corpuscular haemoglobin)']
              // NOTE: bare 'mch' removed — would substring-match MCHC names
    },
    // MCHC — canonical = abbreviated form in Thresholds sheet
    {
      canonical: 'Mean corpusc. Hb. conc. (MCHC)',
      match: ['mean corpusc. hb. conc. (mchc)',
              'mean corpusc. hb conc. (mchc)',
              'mean corpusc. hb conc (mchc)',
              'mean corpuscular hb conc (mchc)',
              'mean corpuscular haemoglobin concentration (mchc)',
              'mean corpuscular haemoglobin concentration(mchc)',
              'mchc (mean corpusc. hb. conc.)',
              'mchc']
    },
    // LDL — truncated SAR name
    {
      canonical: 'Calculated LDL Cholesterol Level', // matches actual Thresholds key
      match: ['calculated ldl cholesterol lev', 'calculated ldl cholesterol level']
    },
    // HDL — variant names
    {
      canonical: 'HDL cholesterol',
      match: ['serum hdl cholesterol level','hdl-cholesterol']
      // NOTE: 'hdl chol' removed — would substring-match 'Non-HDL cholesterol'
    },
    // Non-HDL cholesterol — case variant
    {
      canonical: 'Non-HDL cholesterol',
      match: ['non-hdl cholesterol']  // handles case since we compare lowercase
    },
    // Bilirubin
    // RBC, WBC, Haemoglobin, Vitamin B12: no synonym entry needed here.
    // callAIForThresholds enforces exact name echo-back, so the Thresholds
    // sheet will always use the same name as TestResults for these.
    {
      canonical: 'Serum total bilirubin level',
      match: ['serum bilirubin','total bilirubin','bilirubin total']
    },
    // Tissue transglutaminase — truncated/typo variant
    {
      canonical: 'Tissu transglutaminase IgA level', // matches actual Thresholds key (typo preserved)
      match: ['tissu transglutaminase iga lev','tissue transglutaminase iga lev',
              'ttg iga','anti-ttg iga']
    },
    // TSH — lab code suffix
    {
      canonical: 'Serum TSH level',
      match: ['serum tsh level (toc9375)','serum tsh']
    },
    // Adjusted Calcium — keep separate from Serum Calcium (different analyte)
    // "Adjusted calcium" accounts for albumin; "Serum calcium" is raw. Do not merge.
    {
      canonical: 'Adjusted Calcium',
      match: ['adjusted calcium']
    },
    // Creatinine
    {
      canonical: 'Serum creatinine',
      match: ['serum creatinine']  // catches the cleaned version of the "Please note..." variant
    },
  ];

  var cLower = cleaned.toLowerCase().trim();

  for (var i = 0; i < SYNONYMS.length; i++) {
    var rule = SYNONYMS[i];
    // EXACT match only — never substring. Substring matching causes false positives,
    // e.g. 'mch' would match 'mchc', 'hdl chol' would match 'non-hdl cholesterol'.
    for (var j = 0; j < rule.match.length; j++) {
      if (cLower === rule.match[j]) {
        return rule.canonical;
      }
    }
    // Also check if the canonical name matches (idempotent — already canonical)
    if (cLower === rule.canonical.toLowerCase()) return rule.canonical;
  }

  return cleaned; // no synonym found — return as-is
}

// ── 1. NAME CLEANER ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Strips the noise that NHS SAR exports append to analyte names.
// Returns a cleaned string suitable for threshold key lookup.
function cleanAnalyteName(raw) {
  if (!raw) return '';
  var s = raw.toString().trim();

  // Remove embedded numeric value + unit that SAR appends, e.g.:
  //   "Haematocrit, 0.450 L/L"  →  "Haematocrit"
  //   "Mean corpuscular volume (MCV), 94.0 fL"  →  "Mean corpuscular volume (MCV)"
  //   "Serum HDL cholesterol level, 1.0 mmol/L"  →  "Serum HDL cholesterol level"
  s = s.replace(/,\s*[\d.]+\s*[a-zA-Z\/µμ%^0-9]+\s*$/g, '');

  // Remove lab notes appended after the analyte name, e.g.:
  //   "Serum creatinine Please note change of adult reference range..."
  //   "Serum calcium Please note change of adult reference range as of"
  s = s.replace(/\s+please note.*/i, '');
  s = s.replace(/\s+note:.*/i, '');

  // Remove status text appended after name, e.g.:
  //   "Serum TSH level (TOC9375) - Normal"
  //   "Serum TSH level (TOC9375) - Abnormal"
  s = s.replace(/\s+-\s*(normal|abnormal|high|low|borderline|satisfactory|not done|pending)$/i, '');

  // Remove trailing lab reference codes in parentheses that aren't part of
  // the canonical name — but KEEP codes that ARE part of the name like (TOC9375)
  // Strategy: remove only if preceded by "level" or "result" or similar
  s = s.replace(/\s+\([A-Z]{2,6}\d{3,8}\)\s*$/g, '');

  // Normalise whitespace
  s = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();

  return s;
}

// ── 2. MAPPINGS SHEET LOADER / WRITER ────────────────────────────────────
var MAPPINGS_SHEET_NAME = '🔗 Name Mappings';

function getMappingsSheet(ss, createIfMissing) {
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(MAPPINGS_SHEET_NAME);
  if (sheet) return sheet;
  if (!createIfMissing) return null;

  sheet = ss.insertSheet(MAPPINGS_SHEET_NAME);
  sheet.setTabColor('#0f766e');
  sheet.hideSheet(); // hidden by default — accessible via menu

  // Headers
  var hdrs = ['Raw Name (from TestResults)', 'Maps To (Thresholds key)',
              'Status', 'Confidence', 'Reasoning / Notes', 'Date Resolved'];
  var hdrRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, hdrs.length);
  hdrRange.setValues([hdrs])
    .setBackground('#0f766e').setFontColor('#ffffff')
    .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
  setWidths(sheet, [280, 200, 100, 80, 320, 100]);
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  return sheet;
}

function loadMappings(ss) {
  // Returns object: { cleanedLower: { mappedTo, status, confidence } }
  var sheet = getMappingsSheet(ss, false);
  if (!sheet) return {};
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return {};
  var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 6).getValues();
  var map = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    var raw     = data[i][0] ? data[i][0].toString().trim() : '';
    var mappedTo = data[i][1] ? data[i][1].toString().trim() : '';
    var status  = data[i][2] ? data[i][2].toString().trim() : '';
    var conf    = data[i][3] ? data[i][3].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!raw) continue;
    var key = cleanAnalyteName(raw).toLowerCase();
    map[key] = { mappedTo: mappedTo, status: status, confidence: conf, sheetRow: i + 2 };
  }
  return map;
}

function saveMappings(ss, newMappings, today) {
  // newMappings: array of { raw, mappedTo, status, confidence, reasoning }
  var sheet = getMappingsSheet(ss, true);
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();

  // Build index of existing raw names to avoid duplicates
  var existing = {};
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var existData = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var e = 0; e < existData.length; e++) {
      if (existData[e][0]) existing[existData[e][0].toString().trim().toLowerCase()] = e + 2;
    }
  }

  for (var i = 0; i < newMappings.length; i++) {
    var m = newMappings[i];
    var rawKey = m.raw.toLowerCase();
    var rowData = [m.raw, m.mappedTo || '', m.status || '', m.confidence || '', m.reasoning || '', today];

    if (existing[rawKey]) {
      // Update existing row — but don't overwrite manual entries
      var existRow = existing[rawKey];
      var existStatus = sheet.getRange(existRow, 3).getValue().toString();
      if (existStatus.indexOf('✏️') >= 0) continue; // never overwrite manual
      sheet.getRange(existRow, 1, 1, 6).setValues([rowData]);
    } else {
      // Append new row
      var appendRow = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;
      sheet.getRange(appendRow, 1, 1, 6).setValues([rowData]);
      existing[rawKey] = appendRow;
    }

    // Colour-code by status
    var statusRow = existing[rawKey] || sheet.getLastRow();
    var bg = m.status.indexOf('✅') >= 0 ? '#f0fdf4' :
             m.status.indexOf('🤖') >= 0 ? '#eff6ff' :
             m.status.indexOf('✏️') >= 0 ? '#fefce8' :
             m.status.indexOf('🚫') >= 0 ? '#fef2f2' : '#fff7ed';
    sheet.getRange(statusRow, 1, 1, 6).setBackground(bg).setFontSize(8).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('top');
  }
  if (sheet.getLastRow() > 1) sheet.setRowHeightsForced(2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 36);
}

// ── 3. MAIN RECONCILIATION PASS ──────────────────────────────────────────
// ── KNOWN NHS SAR VARIANT SEED ──────────────────────────────────────────
// Seeds the Name Mappings cache with confirmed exact name pairs.
// "raw" = name as it appears in TestResults (after cleanAnalyteName)
// "canonical" = exact name in Thresholds sheet col A
// Only pairs confirmed from real data -- never guesses.
// To add: verify Thresholds col A spelling exactly, then add here.
function _seedKnownMappings(ss) {
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');
  if (!thSheet || thSheet.getLastRow() < 4) return;
  var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, thSheet.getLastRow() - 3, 1).getValues();
  var thKeys = {};
  for (var t = 0; t < thData.length; t++) {
    if (thData[t][0]) thKeys[thData[t][0].toString().trim().toLowerCase()] = thData[t][0].toString().trim();
  }
  // Confirmed pairs from real NHS SAR data
  var KNOWN_PAIRS = [
    { raw: 'Mean corpusc. Hb. conc. (MCHC)',                      canonical: 'Mean corpusc. Hb conc. (MCHC)' },
    { raw: 'Total white cell count (WBC)',                         canonical: 'Total white cell count' },
    { raw: 'Red blood cell count (RBC)',                           canonical: 'Red blood cell (RBC) count' },
    { raw: 'Mean corpuscular haemoglobin (MCH)',                   canonical: 'Mean corpusc. haemoglobin (MCH)' },
    { raw: 'Mean corpuscular haemoglobin concentration (MCHC)',    canonical: 'Mean corpusc. Hb conc. (MCHC)' },
    { raw: 'Haemoglobin estimation',                               canonical: 'Haemoglobin' },
  ];
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy');
  var toSave = [];
  var existing = loadMappings(ss);
  for (var i = 0; i < KNOWN_PAIRS.length; i++) {
    var pair = KNOWN_PAIRS[i];
    var rawKey    = cleanAnalyteName(pair.raw).toLowerCase();
    var canonLower = pair.canonical.toLowerCase();
    if (!thKeys[canonLower]) continue;  // threshold not generated yet
    if (existing[rawKey] && existing[rawKey].status && existing[rawKey].status.indexOf('manual') >= 0) continue;  // user-edited
    if (existing[rawKey] && existing[rawKey].mappedTo &&
        existing[rawKey].mappedTo.toLowerCase() === canonLower &&
        existing[rawKey].confidence === 'seeded') continue;  // already seeded correctly
    toSave.push({ raw: pair.raw, mappedTo: thKeys[canonLower],
      status: 'Exact match', confidence: 'seeded',
      reasoning: 'Confirmed NHS SAR variant - seeded from known pairs' });
  }
  if (toSave.length > 0) saveMappings(ss, toSave, today);
}

// Called during Quick Refresh / Full Refresh.
// Returns a resolved mapping: { cleanedLower → thresholdKey }
// that buildSignificantFindingsRows and buildAnalysisRows use for lookups.
function runAnalyteMapping(ss, trData, thMap, promptUser) {
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy');
  var ui    = promptUser ? SpreadsheetApp.getUi() : null;

  // Step A: collect all unique clinical analyte names from TestResults
  var rawNames = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < trData.length; i++) {
    var row = trData[i];
    if (!row[1] || !row[3]) continue;
    if (isNaN(parseFloat(row[4]))) continue;
    var raw  = row[3].toString().trim();
    var unit = row[5] ? row[5].toString().trim() : '';
    if (!isClinicalResult(raw, unit)) continue;
    rawNames[raw] = true;
  }

  // Step B: load existing stored mappings
  var storedMappings = loadMappings(ss);

  // Step C: for each raw name, try to resolve to a Thresholds key
  var resolved    = {};  // cleanedLower → thresholdKey (string)
  var needsAI     = [];  // raw names that couldn't be resolved
  var newMappings = [];  // entries to persist

  var thKeys = Object.keys(thMap); // all lowercase threshold keys

  for (var raw in rawNames) {
    var cleaned    = cleanAnalyteName(raw);
    var cleanedKey = cleaned.toLowerCase();

    // Already in thMap? (exact match after cleaning)
    if (thMap[cleanedKey]) {
      resolved[cleanedKey] = cleanedKey;
      // Record as exact match if not already stored
      if (!storedMappings[cleanedKey]) {
        newMappings.push({ raw: raw, mappedTo: cleaned,
          status: '✅ Exact match', confidence: 'exact', reasoning: 'Cleaned name matches threshold key directly' });
      }
      continue;
    }

    // In stored mappings?
    if (storedMappings[cleanedKey]) {
      var stored = storedMappings[cleanedKey];
      if (stored.status.indexOf('🚫') >= 0) continue; // excluded
      if (stored.mappedTo) {
        resolved[cleanedKey] = stored.mappedTo.toLowerCase();
      }
      continue;
    }

    // Couldn't resolve — needs AI
    needsAI.push({ raw: raw, cleaned: cleaned });
  }

  // Step D: AI resolution for unmatched names
  if (needsAI.length > 0) {
    var doAI = true;

    if (promptUser && ui) {
      var nameList = needsAI.map(function(n) { return '  • ' + n.raw; }).join('\n');
      var resp = ui.alert(
        '🔗 ' + needsAI.length + ' analyte name(s) need mapping',
        'The following analyte names from your TestResults don\'t exactly match ' +
        'any entry in the Thresholds sheet:\n\n' + nameList +
        '\n\nRun a quick AI call to resolve these mappings? ' +
        '(Takes ~10 seconds. Mappings are saved so this only runs once per new analyte.)\n\n' +
        'Click CANCEL to skip for now — these analytes won\'t be matched this refresh.',
        ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
      );
      doAI = (resp === ui.Button.OK);
    }

    if (doAI) {
      try {
        var aiMappings = _callAIForNameMappings(needsAI, thKeys);
        for (var a = 0; a < aiMappings.length; a++) {
          var am = aiMappings[a];
          var amKey = cleanAnalyteName(am.raw).toLowerCase();

          if (am.canonical && am.canonical !== 'null' && am.canonical !== '') {
            var canonLower = am.canonical.toLowerCase();
            // Verify AI suggestion actually exists in thMap
            if (thMap[canonLower]) {
              resolved[amKey] = canonLower;
              newMappings.push({ raw: am.raw, mappedTo: am.canonical,
                status: am.confidence >= 0.8 ? '🤖 AI-mapped' : '⚠️ Review',
                confidence: (Math.round((am.confidence || 0) * 100)) + '%',
                reasoning: am.reasoning || '' });
            } else {
              // AI suggested something not in thMap — flag for review
              newMappings.push({ raw: am.raw, mappedTo: am.canonical,
                status: '⚠️ Review — suggested key not in Thresholds',
                confidence: (Math.round((am.confidence || 0) * 100)) + '%',
                reasoning: (am.reasoning || '') + ' [NOTE: suggested key "' + am.canonical + '" not found in Thresholds sheet — may need threshold refresh first]' });
            }
          } else {
            // AI says this is not a clinical analyte → exclude
            newMappings.push({ raw: am.raw, mappedTo: '',
              status: '🚫 Excluded — ' + (am.reasoning || 'not a clinical analyte'),
              confidence: (Math.round((am.confidence || 0) * 100)) + '%',
              reasoning: am.reasoning || 'AI identified as non-clinical (appointment note, metric, junk)' });
          }
        }
      } catch(e) {
        // AI call failed — log but don't break the refresh
        if (promptUser && ui) {
          ui.alert('⚠️ Name mapping AI call failed:\n' + e.toString().substring(0, 200) +
                   '\n\nRefresh will continue — unmatched analytes will be skipped this run.');
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Step E: persist new mappings to sheet
  if (newMappings.length > 0) {
    saveMappings(ss, newMappings, today);
  }

  return resolved; // { cleanedLower → thresholdKey }
}

// ── 4. AI CALL FOR NAME MAPPINGS ─────────────────────────────────────────
function _callAIForNameMappings(unmatchedList, thKeys) {
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) throw new Error('No API key found. Set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY + AI_PROVIDER=anthropic, or OPENAI_API_KEY + AI_PROVIDER=openai in Script Properties.');

  var rawNames    = unmatchedList.map(function(n) { return n.raw; });
  var thKeysForAI = thKeys.slice(0, 80).join('\n'); // send up to 80 keys as context

  var prompt =
    'You are reconciling NHS SAR export analyte names with a clinical threshold database.\n\n' +
    'NHS SAR exports often corrupt analyte names by:\n' +
    '- Truncating mid-word: "Calculated LDL cholesterol lev" = "Calculated LDL cholesterol level"\n' +
    '- Appending values: "Haematocrit, 0.450 L/L" = "Haematocrit"\n' +
    '- Appending lab notes: "Serum creatinine Please note change of adult reference range..."\n' +
    '- Appending status: "Serum TSH level (TOC9375) - Normal"\n' +
    '- Appending lab codes: "Serum TSH level (TOC9375)" where TOC9375 is a lab code\n' +
    '- Including appointment/SMS notifications that are NOT analytes\n\n' +
    'AVAILABLE THRESHOLD KEYS (these are the valid canonical names):\n' +
    thKeysForAI + '\n\n' +
    'For each raw name below, return the best matching canonical threshold key, or null if it is ' +
    'not a clinical blood/urine test analyte (e.g. appointment notifications, SMS messages, ' +
    'fitness scores, weight, height, lifestyle metrics).\n\n' +
    'RAW NAMES TO MAP:\n' +
    rawNames.map(function(n, i) { return (i+1) + '. ' + n; }).join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'Return ONLY a JSON array, one object per raw name, in the same order:\n' +
    '[{"raw":"exact raw name","canonical":"matching threshold key or null","confidence":0.0-1.0,' +
    '"reasoning":"brief explanation"}]\n\n' +
    'If the name is clearly a clinical analyte but no threshold key matches, still return your ' +
    'best guess canonical name (it may need a threshold refresh) — do not return null just ' +
    'because the key is missing.\n' +
    'Return ONLY the JSON array, no other text.';

  var text = callAI(prompt, 2000, 'Name Mapping');
  return _extractJSONArray(text);
}

// ── 5. RESOLVED MAP LOOKUP HELPER ────────────────────────────────────────
// Given a raw analyte name and the resolved mapping, returns the threshold
// rows to use (or null if none).  Used by both buildSignificantFindingsRows
// and buildAnalysisRows as a drop-in replacement for the old thMap[aKey] call.
function resolveThresholdRows(rawName, thMap, resolvedMap) {
  var cleaned    = cleanAnalyteName(rawName);
  var cleanedKey = cleaned.toLowerCase();

  // Direct exact match (name was already clean)
  if (thMap[cleanedKey]) return thMap[cleanedKey];

  // Via resolved mapping
  if (resolvedMap && resolvedMap[cleanedKey]) {
    var mappedKey = resolvedMap[cleanedKey];
    if (thMap[mappedKey]) return thMap[mappedKey];
  }

  return null;
}

// ── 6. VIEW MAPPINGS REPORT (menu item) ──────────────────────────────────
function viewMappingsReport() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sheet = getMappingsSheet(ss, false);

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    ui.alert('No Mapping Data Yet',
      'No analyte name mappings have been generated yet.\n\n' +
      'Run Quick Refresh — when prompted about unmatched analytes, click OK to run the AI mapping.\n\n' +
      'Mappings are saved permanently and only need to run once per new analyte.',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    return;
  }

  // Unhide and activate the sheet
  sheet.showSheet();
  ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);

  // Count by status
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var data    = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 3).getValues();
  var counts  = { exact: 0, ai: 0, manual: 0, excluded: 0, review: 0, other: 0 };
  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    var st = data[i][2] ? data[i][2].toString() : '';
    if (st.indexOf('✅') >= 0)      counts.exact++;
    else if (st.indexOf('🤖') >= 0) counts.ai++;
    else if (st.indexOf('✏️') >= 0) counts.manual++;
    else if (st.indexOf('🚫') >= 0) counts.excluded++;
    else if (st.indexOf('⚠️') >= 0) counts.review++;
    else counts.other++;
  }

  ui.alert('🔗 Name Mappings Report',
    'Total mappings stored: ' + (lastRow - 1) + '\n\n' +
    '✅ Exact match (clean name found directly):  ' + counts.exact + '\n' +
    '🤖 AI-mapped (AI resolved the name):         ' + counts.ai + '\n' +
    '✏️ Manual (you edited this mapping):          ' + counts.manual + '\n' +
    '🚫 Excluded (identified as non-clinical):    ' + counts.excluded + '\n' +
    '⚠️ Needs review (low confidence or missing): ' + counts.review + '\n\n' +
    'The 🔗 Name Mappings sheet is now visible.\n' +
    'You can manually edit the "Maps To" column to correct any mapping.\n' +
    'Set Status to "✏️ Manual" for any row you edit — manual entries are never overwritten.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}

// ── 7. CLEAR MAPPINGS (force re-run) ─────────────────────────────────────
function clearAnalyteMappings() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var resp = ui.alert('🗑 Clear All Name Mappings?',
    'This will delete all stored analyte name mappings.\n\n' +
    'The next Quick Refresh will re-run the AI mapping for all unmatched names.\n\n' +
    'Use this if your Thresholds sheet has been substantially updated and you want ' +
    'mappings to be recalculated from scratch.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (resp !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var sheet = getMappingsSheet(ss, false);
  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 6).clearContent().clearFormat();
  }
  ui.alert('✅ Mappings cleared. Run Quick Refresh to regenerate.');
}

// ── Emergency: clear a stuck threshold job ───────────────────────────────
// If threshold refresh gets stuck (exceeded execution time error), use this
// to clear the job state so a fresh run can start cleanly.
function cleanThresholdSheetJunkManual() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var deleted = _cleanThresholdSheetJunk(ss);
  if (deleted > 0) {
    ui.alert('✅ Cleaned ' + deleted + ' junk row(s) from Thresholds sheet.\n\n' +
             'Removed: Fitness score, Sleep score, Wellbeing, Work/Life balance, ' +
             'Total body water, Body fat, Mind (stress) score etc.\n\n' +
             'Run Quick Refresh to rebuild views.');
  } else {
    ui.alert('✅ No junk rows found in Thresholds sheet — already clean.');
  }
}


// ─── AI PROVIDER SWITCHER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function setProviderOpenAI() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('AI_PROVIDER', 'openai');
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ AI Provider set to OpenAI (GPT-4o)\n\nMake sure OPENAI_API_KEY is set in Script Properties.');
}

function setProviderAnthropic() {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('AI_PROVIDER', 'anthropic');
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ AI Provider set to Anthropic (Claude Sonnet 4.5)\n\nMake sure ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set in Script Properties.\n\nModel: claude-sonnet-4-5-20251001');
}

function checkAIProvider() {
  var provider = getAIProvider();
  var key = getApiKey();
  var keyProp = provider === 'anthropic' ? 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' : 'OPENAI_API_KEY';
  var keyStatus = key ? '✅ ' + keyProp + ' found (' + key.substring(0,8) + '...)' : '❌ ' + keyProp + ' NOT FOUND';
  var model = provider === 'anthropic' ? 'claude-sonnet-4-5' : 'gpt-4o';
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(
    '🤖 Active AI Provider',
    'Provider:  ' + provider.toUpperCase() + '\n' +
    'Model:     ' + model + '\n' +
    'API Key:   ' + keyStatus + '\n\n' +
    (key ? '' : '⚠️ ACTION NEEDED: Add ' + keyProp + ' to Script Properties\n\n') +
    'To switch: 🏥 menu → 🤖 AI Provider → select provider\n' +
    'To set key: Extensions → Apps Script → Project Settings → Script Properties',
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK
  );
}


// ─── AI USAGE LOGGING ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Appends one row per AI call to the hidden "📊 AI Usage Log" sheet.
// Columns: Timestamp | Date | Event | Model | Prompt tokens | Completion tokens
//          | Total tokens | Est. cost USD | Notes
//
// GPT-4o pricing (as of early 2025): $2.50/1M input, $10.00/1M output
// Claude Sonnet 3.5 pricing:         $3.00/1M input, $15.00/1M output
// Claude Sonnet 3.7 pricing:         $3.00/1M input, $15.00/1M output
// (Prices shown in dashboard for reference — update if pricing changes)
var AI_USAGE_SHEET = '📊 AI Usage Log';

function logAIUsage(eventLabel, model, promptTokens, completionTokens) {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(AI_USAGE_SHEET);
    if (!sheet) sheet = _buildUsageLogSheet(ss);
    if (!sheet) return;

    var now = new Date();
    var dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd/MM/yyyy');
    var timeStr = Utilities.formatDate(now, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss');
    var totalTok = (promptTokens || 0) + (completionTokens || 0);

    // Cost calculation — GPT-4o
    var costUsd = 0;
    if (model && model.indexOf('gpt-4o') >= 0) {
      costUsd = ((promptTokens || 0) / 1000000 * 2.50) +
                ((completionTokens || 0) / 1000000 * 10.00);
    } else if (model && (model.indexOf('claude') >= 0 || model.indexOf('sonnet') >= 0)) {
      costUsd = ((promptTokens || 0) / 1000000 * 3.00) +
                ((completionTokens || 0) / 1000000 * 15.00);
    }

    sheet.appendRow([
      timeStr, dateStr, eventLabel || 'Unknown', model || 'gpt-4o',
      promptTokens || 0, completionTokens || 0, totalTok,
      Math.round(costUsd * 10000) / 10000  // 4 decimal places
    ]);
  } catch(e) {
    // Never crash the main function due to logging failure
    Logger.log('logAIUsage error: ' + e.message);
  }
}

function _buildUsageLogSheet(ss) {
  try {
    var sheet = ss.insertSheet(AI_USAGE_SHEET);
    sheet.hideSheet();

    // Header row
    var headers = ['Time', 'Date', 'Event', 'Model',
                   'Prompt Tokens', 'Completion Tokens', 'Total Tokens', 'Est. Cost (USD)'];
    var hRow = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length);
    hRow.setValues([headers]);
    hRow.setBackground('#1e3a5f').setFontColor('#ffffff').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(9);
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(1, 70);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(2, 85);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(3, 180);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(4, 100);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(5, 100);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(6, 120);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(7, 90);
    sheet.setColumnWidth(8, 110);
    return sheet;
  } catch(e) {
    Logger.log('_buildUsageLogSheet error: ' + e.message);
    return null;
  }
}

// ─── BUILD / REFRESH AI USAGE DASHBOARD ──────────────────────────────────────
function buildAIUsageDashboard() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui;
  try { ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(); } catch(e) {}

  // Ensure log sheet exists
  var logSheet = ss.getSheetByName(AI_USAGE_SHEET);
  if (!logSheet) {
    if (ui) ui.alert('No AI usage data yet. The log is built automatically when you run any AI feature.');
    return;
  }

  var dashName = '💰 AI Usage Dashboard';
  var dash = ss.getSheetByName(dashName);
  if (dash) ss.deleteSheet(dash);
  dash = ss.insertSheet(dashName);

  // Pull log data
  var logData = logSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (logData.length <= 1) {
    dash.getRange(1,1).setValue('No AI usage data recorded yet.');
    return;
  }

  var rows = logData.slice(1); // skip header
  // rows: [time, date, event, model, promptTok, completionTok, totalTok, costUsd]

  // ── Pricing reference (per 1M tokens) ───────────────────────────────────
  var PRICES = {
    'gpt-4o':            { input: 2.50,  output: 10.00 },
    'gpt-4o-mini':       { input: 0.15,  output: 0.60  },
    'claude-sonnet-3-5': { input: 3.00,  output: 15.00 },
    'claude-sonnet-3-7': { input: 3.00,  output: 15.00 },
    'claude-opus-4':     { input: 15.00, output: 75.00 },
  };

  var GBP_RATE = 0.79; // approximate USD→GBP — update periodically

  // ── Aggregations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var totalCalls = rows.length;
  var totalPrompt = 0, totalCompletion = 0, totalCost = 0;
  var byDate = {}, byEvent = {};

  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    var r = rows[i];
    var dateKey = r[1] ? r[1].toString() : 'Unknown';
    var event   = r[2] ? r[2].toString() : 'Unknown';
    var pTok    = Number(r[4]) || 0;
    var cTok    = Number(r[5]) || 0;
    var cost    = Number(r[7]) || 0;

    totalPrompt     += pTok;
    totalCompletion += cTok;
    totalCost       += cost;

    if (!byDate[dateKey])  byDate[dateKey]  = { calls:0, tokens:0, cost:0 };
    byDate[dateKey].calls++;
    byDate[dateKey].tokens += pTok + cTok;
    byDate[dateKey].cost   += cost;

    if (!byEvent[event]) byEvent[event] = { calls:0, tokens:0, cost:0 };
    byEvent[event].calls++;
    byEvent[event].tokens += pTok + cTok;
    byEvent[event].cost   += cost;
  }

  // Monthly rollup
  var byMonth = {};
  for (var dk in byDate) {
    var parts = dk.split('/');
    var mKey = parts.length === 3 ? parts[1] + '/' + parts[2] : dk;
    if (!byMonth[mKey]) byMonth[mKey] = { calls:0, tokens:0, cost:0 };
    byMonth[mKey].calls  += byDate[dk].calls;
    byMonth[mKey].tokens += byDate[dk].tokens;
    byMonth[mKey].cost   += byDate[dk].cost;
  }

  // ── Write dashboard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var C = { navy:'#1e3a5f', teal:'#0d6e6e', amber:'#b45309', purple:'#6b21a8',
            green:'#166534', red:'#991b1b', white:'#ffffff', lightgrey:'#f8fafc',
            lightyellow:'#fffbeb', lightgreen:'#f0fdf4', lightpurple:'#faf5ff' };

  function banner(row, txt, bg, cols) {
    dash.getRange(row, 1, 1, cols||8).merge()
      .setValue(txt).setBackground(bg).setFontColor(C.white)
      .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(11).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    dash.setRowHeight(row, 28);
  }
  function subhdr(row, vals, bg) {
    var r = dash.getRange(row, 1, 1, vals.length);
    r.setValues([vals]).setBackground(bg||'#e2e8f0').setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(8);
  }
  function dataRow(row, vals, bg) {
    dash.getRange(row, 1, 1, vals.length).setValues([vals])
      .setBackground(bg||C.lightgrey).setFontSize(9);
  }
  function gap(row) { dash.setRowHeight(row, 8); }

  var r = 1;

  // Title
  dash.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('💰  AI Usage Dashboard  —  Medical Tracker')
    .setBackground(C.navy).setFontColor(C.white)
    .setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(14).setHorizontalAlignment('center');
  dash.setRowHeight(r, 36); r++;

  dash.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('Automatically updated each time an AI feature runs.  All costs are estimates based on published model pricing.')
    .setFontSize(8).setFontColor('#64748b').setFontStyle('italic').setHorizontalAlignment('center');
  r++; gap(r); r++;

  // ── Summary totals ──────────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '📊  All-Time Summary', C.teal, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Metric', 'Value', '', 'Metric', 'Value', '', 'Metric', 'Value']); r++;

  var avgCostPerCall = totalCalls > 0 ? totalCost / totalCalls : 0;
  dataRow(r, ['Total AI calls', totalCalls, '',
              'Total tokens used', (totalPrompt + totalCompletion).toLocaleString(), '',
              'Total cost (£)', '£' + (totalCost * GBP_RATE).toFixed(4)], C.lightgreen); r++;
  dataRow(r, ['Prompt tokens', totalPrompt.toLocaleString(), '',
              'Completion tokens', totalCompletion.toLocaleString(), '',
              'Avg cost / call (£)', '£' + (avgCostPerCall * GBP_RATE).toFixed(5)]); r++;
  dataRow(r, ['Avg tokens / call', totalCalls > 0 ? Math.round((totalPrompt+totalCompletion)/totalCalls).toLocaleString() : '0', '',
              '', '', '',
              '', ''], C.lightgreen); r++;
  gap(r); r++;

  // ── Pricing reference ───────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '💡  Model Pricing Reference  (per 1M tokens)', C.purple, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Model', 'Input (per 1M)', 'Output (per 1M)', 'Typical call cost*',
             '', 'Model', 'Input (per 1M)', 'Output (per 1M)']); r++;

  var priceRows = [
    ['GPT-4o (current)',        '$2.50', '$10.00', '~$0.003–0.015', '', 'Claude Sonnet 3.5/3.7', '$3.00', '$15.00'],
    ['GPT-4o mini',             '$0.15', '$0.60',  '~$0.0002–0.001','', 'Claude Haiku 3.5',      '$0.80', '$4.00'],
    ['GPT-4 Turbo',             '$10.00','$30.00', '~$0.01–0.06',   '', 'Claude Opus 4',          '$15.00','$75.00'],
  ];
  for (var pi = 0; pi < priceRows.length; pi++) {
    dataRow(r, priceRows[pi], pi % 2 === 0 ? C.lightpurple : C.white); r++;
  }
  dash.getRange(r, 1, 1, 8).merge()
    .setValue('* Typical call cost based on ~1,500 prompt + ~500 completion tokens per average feature call.')
    .setFontSize(7).setFontColor('#64748b').setFontStyle('italic');
  r++; gap(r); r++;

  // ── By event type ───────────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '🔍  Usage by Feature / Event', C.amber, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Feature', 'Calls', 'Prompt Tokens', 'Completion Tokens', 'Total Tokens', 'Est. Cost (£)', 'Avg Cost / Call (£)', '']); r++;
  var eventKeys = Object.keys(byEvent).sort(function(a,b){ return byEvent[b].cost - byEvent[a].cost; });
  for (var ei = 0; ei < eventKeys.length; ei++) {
    var ev = byEvent[eventKeys[ei]];
    // Recalculate prompt/completion split from raw log
    var evPrompt = 0, evCompl = 0;
    for (var ri2 = 0; ri2 < rows.length; ri2++) {
      if (rows[ri2][2] === eventKeys[ei]) {
        evPrompt += Number(rows[ri2][4]) || 0;
        evCompl  += Number(rows[ri2][5]) || 0;
      }
    }
    dataRow(r, [
      eventKeys[ei], ev.calls,
      evPrompt.toLocaleString(), evCompl.toLocaleString(), ev.tokens.toLocaleString(),
      '£' + (ev.cost * GBP_RATE).toFixed(4),
      '£' + (ev.calls > 0 ? ((ev.cost/ev.calls) * GBP_RATE).toFixed(5) : '0'),
      ''
    ], ei % 2 === 0 ? C.lightyellow : C.white); r++;
  }
  gap(r); r++;

  // ── By date ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '📅  Usage by Date  (newest first)', C.teal, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Date', 'Calls', 'Total Tokens', 'Est. Cost (£)', '', '', '', '']); r++;
  var dateKeys = Object.keys(byDate).sort().reverse();
  for (var di = 0; di < dateKeys.length; di++) {
    var dd = byDate[dateKeys[di]];
    dataRow(r, [
      dateKeys[di], dd.calls, dd.tokens.toLocaleString(),
      '£' + (dd.cost * GBP_RATE).toFixed(4),
      '', '', '', ''
    ], di % 2 === 0 ? C.lightgrey : C.white); r++;
  }
  gap(r); r++;

  // ── By month ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '📆  Monthly Summary', C.navy, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Month', 'Calls', 'Total Tokens', 'Est. Cost (£)', '', '', '', '']); r++;
  var monthKeys = Object.keys(byMonth).sort().reverse();
  for (var mi = 0; mi < monthKeys.length; mi++) {
    var mm = byMonth[monthKeys[mi]];
    dataRow(r, [
      monthKeys[mi], mm.calls, mm.tokens.toLocaleString(),
      '£' + (mm.cost * GBP_RATE).toFixed(4),
      '', '', '', ''
    ], mi % 2 === 0 ? C.lightgreen : C.white); r++;
  }
  gap(r); r++;

  // ── Recent calls log ────────────────────────────────────────
  banner(r, '🕐  Recent Calls  (last 30)', C.purple, 8); r++;
  subhdr(r, ['Time', 'Date', 'Event', 'Model', 'Prompt Tok', 'Completion Tok', 'Total Tok', 'Cost (USD)']); r++;
  var recentRows = rows.slice(-30).reverse();
  for (var rci = 0; rci < recentRows.length; rci++) {
    var rc = recentRows[rci];
    dataRow(r, [
      rc[0], rc[1], rc[2], rc[3],
      rc[4], rc[5], rc[6],
      '£' + ((Number(rc[7]) || 0) * GBP_RATE).toFixed(5)
    ], rci % 2 === 0 ? C.lightpurple : C.white); r++;
  }

  // Format column widths
  [120, 85, 180, 100, 100, 100, 100, 110].forEach(function(w, i) {
    dash.setColumnWidth(i+1, w);
  });

  dash.setFrozenRows(0);

  // Move to visible position (after Analysis)
  try {
    var analysisSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udcca Analysis');
    if (analysisSheet) {
      var aIdx = analysisSheet.getIndex();
      ss.moveActiveSheet && ss.setActiveSheet(dash);
      ss.moveActiveSheet(aIdx + 1);
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  ss.setActiveSheet(dash);
}


// ─── PERSONAL HEALTH NARRATIVE ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Generates a rich, plain-English personal health document in the style of
// a thoughtful health advisor: story so far, what results actually mean,
// honest medication/supplement assessment, prioritised action plan with
// expected impact ratings. Produces a Word-compatible Google Doc → PDF.
//
// Structure mirrors the attached reference document:
//  1. The Story So Far (patient overview, key history)
//  2. Blood Results — What Actually Matters (flagged + context analytes)
//  3. Medications & Supplements — Honest Assessment
//  4. Key Concerns & Underlying Drivers
//  5. Realistic Expected Impact by Action
//  6. Prioritised Next Steps (Priority 1/2/3 table)
//  7. The Bigger Picture (prognosis, outlook)
function generatePersonalHealthNarrative() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the report include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: medication interactions, treatment history, and clinical analysis are unaffected.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  ui.alert('⏳ Generating Personal Health Narrative...\n\nThis takes ~45-60 seconds.');

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  // ── Gather data ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc8a Meds & Supplements Log');
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var thSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');

  // Flagged results with full threshold context
  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var r = sfData[sf];
      if (!r[0] || !r[1]) continue;
      if (r[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skip = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var nm = r[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var isSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skip.length; sk++) { if (nm.indexOf(skip[sk]) >= 0) { isSkip = true; break; } }
      if (isSkip) continue;
      var sig = (r[9] || '').toString().toLowerCase();
      if (sig.indexOf('clinically irrelevant') >= 0 || sig.indexOf('not clinically significant') >= 0) continue;
      flaggedContext.push({ analyte: r[0], value: r[1], unit: r[3]||'', labStatus: r[2]||'', threshold: r[4]||'', meaning: r[5]||'', significance: r[9]||'' });
    }
  }

  // All latest results (clinical only) for context
  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var key = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[key]) {
        seen[key] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') +
          (trData[tri][7] ? ' (' + trData[tri][7] + ')' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  // Medications and supplements — include indication/notes so AI knows WHY each is prescribed
  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' (stopped ' + mData[mi][6] + ')' : ' (current)';
      var notes   = mData[mi][8] ? ' — Indication: ' + mData[mi][8].toString().trim() : '';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + ' ' + (mData[mi][2]||'') +
        (mData[mi][3] ? ' x' + mData[mi][3] + '/day' : '') +
        (mData[mi][1] ? ' [' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') +
        stopped + notes);
    }
  }

  // Key GP records (consultations, imaging, diagnoses)
var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built — run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated (records have changed since last build — ' + cs.builtDate + ').\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  // Saved investigations
  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx2 = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx2.length; ii++) {
      var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx2[ii]);
      if (raw) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,100) +
            (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,80) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // ── AI CALL 1: Core narrative sections 1-5 ───────────────────────────────
 var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);
  var prompt1 =
    'You are a knowledgeable personal health advisor writing a detailed personal health reference document for a patient. ' +
    'Your tone is warm, honest, and expert — like the best kind of GP who actually has time to explain things properly. ' +
    'You explain medical concepts in plain English without being condescending. ' +
    'You are honest about uncertainty and evidence quality. You never give false reassurance. ' +
    'You write in a direct, personal style — addressing the patient as "you". ' +
    'You never instruct a patient to stop or reduce prescribed medications.\n' +
    'However, you MUST provide full, accurate, evidence-based assessment of medication\n' +
    'effects — including absorption interactions, pharmacological effects on gut motility,\n' +
    'platelet function, neurotransmitter synthesis, and any other clinically relevant\n' +
    'mechanisms — regardless of whether those effects reflect poorly on the medication.\n' +
    'Withholding known pharmacological interactions because they are uncomfortable is\n' +
    'a failure of clinical accuracy. State the evidence plainly, then note that any\n' +
    'medication change requires GP or specialist review.\n' +
    'Where patient notes request evidence about treatment duration, tapering criteria,\n' +
    'or NICE guidance on discontinuation after remission, provide this factually and\n' +
    'frame it explicitly as material for informed GP discussion — not as a recommendation\n' +
    'to act unilaterally.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: This report is for home/family discussion. Do not include explicit\n' +
    'references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents, or crisis presentations by name.\n' +
    'You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health challenges" where clinically\n' +
    'relevant, but do not describe specific incidents or methods. Medication history,\n' +
    'interactions, and all clinical analysis are unaffected — discuss fluoxetine, its\n' +
    'pharmacological effects, and treatment duration fully as normal.\n\n' : '') +
   'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS (research thresholds exceeded):\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.map(function(f) {
      return '• ' + f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' ' + f.unit + ' (' + f.labStatus + ') — flags: ' + f.threshold + ' — ' + f.meaning;
    }).join('\n') : 'None currently flagged') + '\n\n' +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.slice(0,40).join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS (IMPORTANT: the Indication field shows WHY each is prescribed ' +
    '— do NOT recommend something the patient is already on. If they are on the contraceptive pill, ' +
    'do NOT suggest contraception to reduce bleeding. If they are on propranolol for POTS, ' +
    'do NOT ask why they are on it.):\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None logged') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None saved') + '\n\n' +
    'Where the PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES above contain relevant symptom descriptions, treatment experiences, ' +
    'or questions, weave these naturally into your analysis where clinically relevant. ' +
    'For example: if the patient describes a specific bleeding pattern, incorporate it into the menstrual/iron discussion. ' +
    'If the patient asks a specific question, answer it within the relevant section rather than ignoring it. ' +
    'Do not create a separate section for patient notes — integrate them into the clinical narrative.\n\n' +
    '## 1. THE STORY SO FAR\n' +
    'A concise but complete narrative of this patient\'s health history — key events, timeline, current status. ' +
    'Connect the dots between different findings. Write 2-4 paragraphs.\n\n' +
    '## 2. BLOOD RESULTS — WHAT ACTUALLY MATTERS\n' +
    'For each flagged result (and any near-borderline results worth discussing), write a dedicated subsection. ' +
    'Explain what the test measures, why the current value matters (or doesn\'t), what the research actually says ' +
    'about this level, and what it might mean for this specific patient given their profile. ' +
    'Be honest about lab "normal" ranges vs research-optimal ranges where they differ. ' +
    'Write 2-3 paragraphs per analyte. Include: what the number means, what causes it, what it might explain.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Write in flowing prose. Be specific to this patient. Do not be generic. ' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1100 words for sections 1-2. Complete all analytes — do not truncate.';

  var part1a = callAI(prompt1, NARRATIVE_TOKENS_PART1A, 'Personal Health Narrative (1-2)');

  // ── AI CALL 1b: Sections 3-5 ──────────────────────────────────────────
  var prompt1b_std =
    'You are continuing a Personal Health Narrative for the same patient. ' +
    'Sections 1 and 2 have already been written. Now write section 3.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS:\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.map(function(f) {
      return '• ' + f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' ' + f.unit + ' (' + f.labStatus + ') — ' + f.threshold + ' — ' + f.meaning;
    }).join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS (IMPORTANT: the Indication field shows WHY each is prescribed ' +
    '— do NOT recommend something the patient is already on):\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY:\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None saved') + '\n\n' +
   'IMPORTANT: Do not discuss any result that is flagged as "Clinically Irrelevant" or described as a minor lab variance. Skip these entirely — do not mention them by name.\n\n' +
    '## 3. MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS — HONEST ASSESSMENT\n' +
    'For each current medication and supplement, write: what it does, how well it works, ' +
    'known interactions or concerns given this patient\'s specific results, whether the evidence supports continuing it. ' +
    'Be honest if evidence is weak or mixed. Flag any interactions with their blood results. ' +
    'If there are potentially better alternatives, explain the pharmacological reasoning briefly. ' +
    'Always note that changes require GP/specialist review.\n\n' +
        NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Write in flowing prose where indicated. Be specific to this patient. Do not be generic. ' +
    'Total length: approximately 600-900 words for section 3. Be thorough — do not truncate.';

  var part1b = callAI(prompt1b_std, NARRATIVE_TOKENS_PART1B, 'Personal Health Narrative (3-5)');

  // ── AI CALL 2: Action plan and bigger picture ─────────────────────────────
  var prompt2 =
    'You are continuing a Personal Health Narrative document for the same patient. ' +
    'Use the full context below — the same data as sections 1-4 — to write sections 5, 6, and 7. ' +
    'Do NOT be generic. Every sentence should be specific to this patient.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS (full detail):\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.map(function(f) {
      return '• ' + f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' ' + f.unit + ' (' + f.labStatus + ') — ' + f.threshold + ' — ' + f.meaning;
    }).join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.slice(0,40).join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'Write the following sections:\n\n' +
    '## 5. IF YOU DO THESE THINGS — REALISTIC EXPECTED IMPACT\n' +
    'For each actionable recommendation, give a specific expected impact estimate based on research evidence. ' +
    'Format as: **[Action]** — [1-2 sentence evidence-based impact statement with specific numbers where possible]. ' +
    'Rate each as: HIGH IMPACT / GOOD IMPACT / MODERATE IMPACT / UNCERTAIN. ' +
    'Be honest — do not oversell. Include both supplement/lifestyle and GP/referral actions.\n\n' +
    '## 6. PRIORITISED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'Create a clear prioritised action table with three tiers:\n' +
    'PRIORITY 1 — Start this week, no GP appointment needed\n' +
    'PRIORITY 2 — Start soon, low effort or short GP interaction\n' +
    'PRIORITY 3 — Requires GP appointment, referral, or sustained effort\n\n' +
    'For each action provide: what to do (specific, not vague), why it matters for this patient, ' +
    'where to get it / how to action it, and effort level. ' +
    'Be specific to THIS patient\'s results — not generic advice. ' +
    'Include 2-3 actions per priority tier. Format each action clearly.\n\n' +
    '## 7. THE BIGGER PICTURE\n' +
    'Write 3-4 paragraphs covering:\n' +
    '- The honest prognosis: what is likely to improve, on what timescale, with what conditions\n' +
    '- What "getting better" will realistically look like (not sudden recovery, but gradual shifts)\n' +
    '- Why so many things may have happened together (if applicable)\n' +
    '- One paragraph of genuine encouragement — based on the actual evidence, not platitudes\n\n' +
