    'End with a 1-sentence disclaimer noting this is AI-assisted personal reference, not clinical advice.\n\n' +
   NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Write in direct, personal prose. Address the patient as "you". Be honest and specific. ' +
    'Total length: approximately 600-900 words for sections 6-7.';
  var part2 = callAI(prompt2, NARRATIVE_TOKENS_PART2, 'Personal Health Narrative (6-7)');

  // ── Build Google Doc ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
var docName = 'Personal Health Narrative — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  var C = { navy: '#1e3a5f', teal: '#0d6e6e', amber: '#92400e', charcoal: '#1e293b' };

  function docTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(22); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function h1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function h2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function para(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
    return p;
  }
  function gap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(5); }
  function rule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  // ── Cover ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  docTitle('Personal Health Summary & Action Guide');
  para('Prepared ' + today + '  |  AI-assisted personal health reference', 9, true);
  gap();

  // Profile box
  var profLines = profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profLines.join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9).setBold(false).setItalic(false);
  gap();

  para('⚠️  This document is an AI-assisted personal health reference. It does not constitute medical advice. ' +
       'All medication changes, test requests and treatment decisions require review by a qualified clinician.', 8, true);
  rule();

if (cs.stale || cs.missing) {
    para('⚠️ Clinical Summary ' + (cs.missing ? 'has not been built yet' : 'may be outdated — records changed since ' + cs.builtDate) +
         '. Run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.', 8, true);
  }

  // ── Parse and render AI output ───────────────────────────────
  function renderAISection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    // Clean markdown bold markers in headings and strip ** from body
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { gap(); continue; }
      // Section header: ## N. TITLE
      if (line.match(/^##\s+\d+\.\s+/)) {
        rule();
        h1(line.replace(/^##\s+/, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      // Subsection: **Title** or ### Title
      if (line.match(/^###\s+/) || (line.match(/^\*\*[^*]+\*\*\s*$/) && line.length < 80)) {
        h2(line.replace(/^###\s+/, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      // Priority header lines
      if (line.match(/^PRIORITY [123]/i)) {
        var pp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { pp.setFontSize(10); pp.setBold(true); pp.setItalic(false); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      // Impact rating lines
      if (line.match(/^(HIGH IMPACT|GOOD IMPACT|MODERATE IMPACT|UNCERTAIN|POTENTIALLY)/i)) {
        var ip = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { ip.setFontSize(9); ip.setBold(true); ip.setItalic(true); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      // Bullet points
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      // Regular paragraph — strip residual **
      para(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderAISection(part1a);
  renderAISection(part1b);
  renderAISection(part2);

  // ── Annex A: Threshold Evidence Audit ────────────────────────────────────
  rule();
  h1('Annex A — Threshold Evidence Audit');
  para(
    'This annex lists every analyte that generated an action or finding in this document, ' +
    'showing the NHS lab reference range alongside all AI-generated research thresholds used. ' +
    'Check confidence levels and sources before acting on any recommendation. ' +
    'Low-confidence thresholds should be verified with a clinician or deleted from the ' +
    'Thresholds sheet (they regenerate on next Full Refresh).', 9, true);
  gap();

  // Collect analytes from Significant Findings
  var nxAnalytes = {};
  if (sfSheet) {
    var nxSfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 13).getValues();
    for (var nxSf = 4; nxSf < nxSfData.length; nxSf++) {
      var nxRow = nxSfData[nxSf];
      if (!nxRow[0] || !nxRow[1]) continue;
      var nxLbl = nxRow[0].toString();
      if (nxLbl === 'Analyte') continue;  // skip header row
      if (nxLbl.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || nxLbl.indexOf('GUIDANCE CHANGE') >= 0) break;
      var nxSkip = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat',
                    'total body water','height','weight','resting heart'];
      var nxIsSkip = false;
      for (var nxSk = 0; nxSk < nxSkip.length; nxSk++) {
        if (nxLbl.toLowerCase().indexOf(nxSkip[nxSk]) >= 0) { nxIsSkip = true; break; }
      }
      if (nxIsSkip) continue;
      nxAnalytes[nxLbl.toLowerCase()] = nxLbl;
    }
  }

  // Build latest-result lookup and add lab-abnormal analytes
  var nxLatest = {};
  if (trSheet) {
    var nxTrData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    for (var nxTri = 0; nxTri < nxTrData.length; nxTri++) {
      var nxTr = nxTrData[nxTri];
      if (!nxTr[3] || isNaN(parseFloat(nxTr[4]))) continue;
      var nxAKey = cleanAnalyteName(nxTr[3].toString()).toLowerCase();
      if (!nxLatest[nxAKey] || nxTr[1] > nxLatest[nxAKey].date) {
        nxLatest[nxAKey] = {
          analyte: nxTr[3].toString(), value: nxTr[4],
          unit: nxTr[5] || '', refRange: nxTr[6] || '',
          labStatus: nxTr[7] || '', date: nxTr[1]
        };
      }
      if (nxTr[7] && nxTr[7].toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('ABNORMAL') >= 0) {
        nxAnalytes[nxAKey] = nxTr[3].toString();
      }
    }
  }

  // Group Thresholds rows by analyte key — normalise same way as buildThresholdMap
  var nxThByAnalyte = {};
  if (thSheet) {
    var nxThData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, 2000, 10).getValues();  // use fixed size like buildThresholdMap
    for (var nxTh = 0; nxTh < nxThData.length; nxTh++) {
      if (!nxThData[nxTh][0]) continue;
      var nxThRaw  = nxThData[nxTh][0].toString().toLowerCase().trim();
      var nxThNorm = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(nxThData[nxTh][0].toString())).toLowerCase();
      if (!nxThByAnalyte[nxThRaw])  nxThByAnalyte[nxThRaw]  = [];
      nxThByAnalyte[nxThRaw].push(nxThData[nxTh]);
      if (nxThNorm !== nxThRaw) {
        if (!nxThByAnalyte[nxThNorm]) nxThByAnalyte[nxThNorm] = [];
        nxThByAnalyte[nxThNorm].push(nxThData[nxTh]);
      }
    }


  } else {
  }

  var nxKeys = Object.keys(nxAnalytes).sort();

  if (nxKeys.length === 0) {
    para('No flagged analytes to audit — run Full Refresh to generate Significant Findings.', 10, true);
  } else {
    para(nxKeys.length + ' analyte(s) audited below.', 9);
    gap();

    for (var nxAk = 0; nxAk < nxKeys.length; nxAk++) {
      var nxKey    = nxKeys[nxAk];
      var nxName   = nxAnalytes[nxKey];
      var nxClean  = cleanAnalyteName(nxName).toLowerCase();
      var nxRes    = nxLatest[nxKey] || nxLatest[nxClean] || null;
      var nxNormKey = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(nxName)).toLowerCase();
      var nxThRows = nxThByAnalyte[nxKey] || nxThByAnalyte[nxClean] || nxThByAnalyte[nxNormKey] || [];

      // Analyte sub-heading
      h2(nxName);

      // Latest result line with date
      if (nxRes) {
        var nxDateStr = nxRes.date instanceof Date
          ? Utilities.formatDate(nxRes.date, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy')
          : (nxRes.date||'').toString();
        var nxLabLine =
          'Result date: ' + nxDateStr + '     ' +
          'Value: ' + nxRes.value + ' ' + nxRes.unit + '     ' +
          'NHS lab range: ' + (nxRes.refRange || 'not provided') + '     ' +
          'Lab status: ' + (nxRes.labStatus || 'not provided');
        var nxLabPara = para(nxLabLine, 9);
        if (nxRes.labStatus && nxRes.labStatus.toString().toUpperCase().indexOf('ABNORMAL') >= 0) {
          nxLabPara.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#991b1b').setBold(true);
        } else {
          nxLabPara.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#1e3a5f');
        }
      } else {
        para('No test result found in TestResults.', 9, true);
      }

      // AI thresholds table
      if (nxThRows.length === 0) {
        para('No AI thresholds generated. Run Full Refresh, or check Name Mappings for a variant name.', 9, true);
      } else {
        var nxTbl = body.appendTable();
        var nxHdr = nxTbl.appendTableRow();
        ['Threshold', 'Direction', 'Clinical meaning', 'Confidence / Evidence', 'Source / Guideline'].forEach(function(nxCol) {
          var nxC = nxHdr.appendTableCell(nxCol);
          nxC.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
          nxC.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#ffffff').setBold(true).setFontSize(7);
        });

        for (var nxThr = 0; nxThr < nxThRows.length; nxThr++) {
          var nxTRow  = nxThRows[nxThr];
          var nxConf  = (nxTRow[5]||'').toString();
          var nxConfL = nxConf.toLowerCase();
          var nxConfBg = (nxConfL.indexOf('low') >= 0 || nxConfL.indexOf('weak') >= 0 ||
                          nxConfL.indexOf('limited') >= 0 || nxConfL.indexOf('insufficient') >= 0)
                         ? '#fef2f2'
                         : (nxConfL.indexOf('moderate') >= 0 || nxConfL.indexOf('mixed') >= 0)
                         ? '#fffbeb'
                         : '#f0fdf4';
          var nxRowBg = nxThr % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff';
          var nxTblRow = nxTbl.appendTableRow();
          var nxCellVals = [
            (nxTRow[1]||'').toString(),
            (nxTRow[2]||'').toString(),
            (nxTRow[3]||'').toString().substring(0, 150),
            nxConf.substring(0, 100),
            (nxTRow[6]||'').toString().substring(0, 100)
          ];
          nxCellVals.forEach(function(nxVal, nxCi) {
            var nxC2 = nxTblRow.appendTableCell(nxVal);
            nxC2.setBackgroundColor(nxCi === 3 ? nxConfBg : nxRowBg);
            nxC2.editAsText().setFontSize(8).setForegroundColor('#111111');
          });
        }
        var nxLeg = para('Confidence: green = high evidence  |  yellow = moderate  |  red = low/weak — verify before acting', 7, true);
        nxLeg.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#6b7280');
      }
      gap();
    }
  }

  var nxNote = para(
    'AI thresholds are generated from published research and guidelines (NICE, BSH, BCSH, ESC) ' +
    'but are not clinically validated. Suspicious or implausible ranges should be raised with your ' +
    'clinician. Delete bad rows from the Thresholds sheet — they regenerate on the next Full Refresh.', 8, true);
  nxNote.editAsText().setForegroundColor('#374151');
  rule();

  // ── Footer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  rule();
  para('Generated ' + today + ' using AI-assisted personal health tracking (Medical Tracker v5). ' +
       'For personal reference only. Not a clinical document. All AI content should be reviewed with a qualified clinician.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  // ── Convert to PDF ─────────────────────────────────────────
  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Personal Health Narrative saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Contains:\n' +
    '• Plain-English explanation of your results\n' +
    '• Honest medication & supplement assessment\n' +
    '• Key underlying health drivers\n' +
    '• Expected impact by action\n' +
    '• Prioritised next steps (Priority 1 / 2 / 3)\n' +
    '• Bigger picture & prognosis\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}


// ─── PERSONAL HEALTH NARRATIVE — RICH (PRE-REASONING) ───────────────────────
// Identical to generatePersonalHealthNarrative except it runs a silent
// pre-reasoning AI call first. That reasoning summary is then fed into both
// writing calls as additional context, surfacing niche clinical insights
// (functional deficiencies, medication CNS effects, cross-system patterns)
// that one-shot prompts frequently miss.
// Total: 3 AI calls (~90-120 seconds). Falls back gracefully if call 0 fails.
function generatePersonalHealthNarrativeRich() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();


  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the report include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: medication interactions, treatment history, and clinical analysis are unaffected by this choice.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  _scheduleNarrativeJob('rich', omitMHDetail);
  ui.alert('⏳ Generating Personal Health Narrative',
    'Running in the background — takes approximately 10-12 minutes.\n\n' +
    'Progress shown in the ⏳ Refresh Status tab — check it anytime.\n\n' +
    'Your PDF will appear in Google Drive when complete. You can keep working normally.',
    SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK);
  return;

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  // ── Gather data (identical to standard version) ────────────────────────
  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc8a Meds & Supplements Log');
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var thSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var r = sfData[sf];
      if (!r[0] || !r[1]) continue;
      if (r[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skip = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var nm = r[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var isSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skip.length; sk++) { if (nm.indexOf(skip[sk]) >= 0) { isSkip = true; break; } }
      if (isSkip) continue;
      var sig = (r[9] || '').toString().toLowerCase();
      if (sig.indexOf('clinically irrelevant') >= 0 || sig.indexOf('not clinically significant') >= 0) continue;
      flaggedContext.push({ analyte: r[0], value: r[1], unit: r[3]||'', labStatus: r[2]||'', threshold: r[4]||'', meaning: r[5]||'', significance: r[9]||'' });
    }
  }

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var key = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[key]) {
        seen[key] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' (' + trData[tri][7] + ')' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' (stopped ' + mData[mi][6] + ')' : ' (current)';
      var notes   = mData[mi][8] ? ' — Indication: ' + mData[mi][8].toString().trim() : '';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + ' ' + (mData[mi][2]||'') + (mData[mi][3] ? ' x' + mData[mi][3] + '/day' : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + notes);
    }
  }

var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built — run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated (records have changed since last build — ' + cs.builtDate + ').\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx2 = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx2.length; ii++) {
      var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx2[ii]);
      if (raw) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,100) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,80) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // ── Shared data block (reused in all three prompts) ────────────────────
var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

 var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built — run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated (records have changed since last build — ' + cs.builtDate + ').\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS (research thresholds exceeded):\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.map(function(f) {
      return '\u2022 ' + f.analyte + ': ' + f.value + ' ' + f.unit + ' (' + f.labStatus + ') \u2014 flags: ' + f.threshold + ' \u2014 ' + f.meaning;
    }).join('\n') : 'None currently flagged') + '\n\n' +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.slice(0,40).join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS (Indication field shows WHY each is prescribed):\n' +
    (medLines.join('\n') || 'None logged') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None saved');

  // ── AI CALL 0: Silent pre-reasoning pass ───────────────────────────────
  // This never appears in the PDF — it generates clinical reasoning that is
  // fed into calls 1 and 2 as additional context.
  var priorReasoning = '';
  try {
var promptReason =
  'You are a highly experienced clinician reviewing a patient\'s health data.\n' +
  'Your task is NOT to write a report. Your task is to THINK through the clinical picture\n' +
  'and produce a reasoning summary that will be handed to a colleague who will write the report.\n\n' +
  'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
  dataBlock + '\n\n' +
  'Work through the following questions IN ORDER. For each, reason carefully before answering.\n' +
  'Cite specific values from the data. Be honest about uncertainty.\n\n' +
  '1. PATIENT CONTEXT FIRST — BEFORE LOOKING AT ANY NUMBERS\n' +
  'Set the blood results aside completely. Based only on the patient profile, family history,\n' +
  'medication history, symptom history, and clinical records: what is your clinical impression\n' +
  'of this patient? What kind of patient is this, and what does their whole context suggest\n' +
  'about the most likely underlying drivers? What does the treatment response trajectory tell\n' +
  'you — what has worked, what has failed, and what the treatment response trajectory suggests\n' +
  'about underlying mechanisms? Does the family history suggest a heritable or shared pattern\n' +
  'rather than an acquired or incidental one? Does any medication history change the clinical\n' +
  'picture — is there evidence that a prescribed treatment is failing to control the problem\n' +
  'it was intended for?\n\n' +
  '2. FUNCTIONAL DEFICIENCY SCREEN\n' +
  'Now look at the results. Are any analytes lab-normal but potentially functionally deficient\n' +
  'in the context of this patient\'s conditions and clinical impression from step 1?\n' +
  'Consider: serum B12 vs functional B12 (holotranscobalamin, MMA), ferritin in the context\n' +
  'of symptoms and loss rate, Vitamin D in neurological/autonomic conditions, iron stores vs\n' +
  'iron deficiency erythropoiesis markers (CHR, hypochromic cells, MCHC), magnesium.\n' +
  'List each with clinical reasoning.\n\n' +
  '3. MEDICATION & TREATMENT EFFECTS SCREEN\n' +
  'For each current medication, what are the known CNS, autonomic, metabolic, or haematological\n' +
  'effects specifically relevant given this patient\'s conditions and symptoms?\n' +
  'Is there evidence in this patient\'s records that any prescribed treatment is failing to\n' +
  'control the problem it was intended for? Does the documented treatment response history\n' +
  'tell you something diagnostically that the blood numbers alone do not?\n\n' +
  '4. CROSS-SYSTEM PATTERN ANALYSIS\n' +
  'What patterns connect multiple findings across systems? What unifying mechanisms might\n' +
  'explain clusters of seemingly unrelated findings? Identify 2-4 candidate mechanisms.\n\n' +
  '5. NICHE INVESTIGATIONS NOT YET DONE\n' +
  'Based on the clinical impression from step 1 and the patterns identified, what specific\n' +
  'investigations are recommended in specialist literature that have not yet been done?\n' +
  'Be specific: test name, what it measures, why it matters here.\n\n' +
  '6. CLINICAL RECORDS MINING\n' +
  'Read the GP consultation records carefully for specific clinical\n' +
  'details that are diagnostically significant but may not appear in\n' +
  'blood results. Look specifically for:\n' +
  '• Exact nature and site of any reported bleeding — is it PV (vaginal),\n' +
  '  GI, or other? This distinction is diagnostically critical.\n' +
  '• Precise temporal relationships — what started or stopped when,\n' +
  '  particularly in relation to starting or stopping medications\n' +
  '• Patient-reported treatment responses — what worked, what did not,\n' +
  '  what caused unexpected symptoms\n' +
  '• Symptoms that began or resolved in direct temporal relation to\n' +
  '  a specific medication, procedure, or event\n' +
  '• Referral outcomes — what was investigated, what was found,\n' +
  '  what was referred but outcome not recorded\n' +
  '• Any clinical observation in the records that refines or contradicts\n' +
  '  the headline diagnosis or treatment plan\n' +
  'Cite specific consultation dates and exact wording where relevant.\n\n' +
  'Apply these grey zones in your reasoning — do not call these values simply "normal":\n' +
    '• B12: 350-500 ng/L is a functional deficiency grey zone\n' +
  '• Ferritin: <30-50 µg/L may cause symptoms even above lab minimum\n' +
  '• Vitamin D: <75 nmol/L is suboptimal even above lab threshold of 50\n' +
  '• TSH: >2.0 is worth noting even within lab range\n' +
  '• MCV: >95 fL warrants B12/folate investigation even below 100 fL macrocytosis threshold\n\n' +
  'Check result interactions:\n' +
  '• High MCV + low iron/ferritin: masking — B12/folate deficiency may be worse than MCV suggests\n' +
  '• Rising haematocrit + rising ferritin: likely iron recovery, not pathological\n' +
  '• Low ferritin + normal Hb: non-anaemic iron deficiency — still clinically significant\n\n' +
  'CRITICAL: Only assert facts that are explicitly stated in the data provided.\n' +
  'Do not infer, extrapolate, or combine patient notes with clinical data to create\n' +
  'conclusions that go beyond what is directly evidenced. If something is plausible\n' +
  'but not confirmed, label it explicitly as HYPOTHESIS not as fact.\n' +
  'Patient notes are anecdotal self-report and may be inaccurate — never treat them as\n' +
  'equivalent to clinical records or test results.\n' +
  'When inferring patterns from family history, always consider the base rate of the\n' +
  'finding in the general population before concluding a familial pattern is significant.\n' +
  'Many common conditions affect 15-30% of the population — multiple affected family\n' +
  'members may reflect population prevalence rather than inherited pathology.\n' +
  'Label any family pattern inference as HYPOTHESIS unless supported by specialist\n' +
  'clinical confirmation.\n\n' +
  'Output your reasoning as structured notes — not a patient-facing document.\n' +
  'Be specific to this patient\'s actual data values. Total length: 800-1100 words.';
    priorReasoning = callAI(promptReason, 2500, 'Narrative Rich — Pre-reasoning');
  } catch(e) {
    // Pre-reasoning failed — log but continue, report will still generate
    priorReasoning = '[Pre-reasoning pass failed: ' + e.message + ' — report generated without enriched context]';
  }

  var reasoningContext = priorReasoning
    ? '\n\nPRIOR CLINICAL REASONING (from pre-analysis pass — use this to enrich your writing,\n' +
      'but do not reproduce it verbatim; weave insights into your prose):\n' + priorReasoning + '\n'
    : '';

  // ── AI CALL 1: Core narrative sections 1-5 (enriched) ─────────────────
  var prompt1 =
    'You are a knowledgeable personal health advisor writing a detailed personal health reference document for a patient. ' +
    'Your tone is warm, honest, and expert — like the best kind of GP who actually has time to explain things properly. ' +
    'You explain medical concepts in plain English without being condescending. ' +
    'You are honest about uncertainty and evidence quality. You never give false reassurance. ' +
    'You write in a direct, personal style — addressing the patient as "you". ' +
    'You never instruct a patient to stop or reduce prescribed medications.\n' +
    'However, you MUST provide full, accurate, evidence-based assessment of medication\n' +
    'effects — including absorption interactions, pharmacological effects on gut motility,\n' +
    'platelet function, neurotransmitter synthesis, and any other clinically relevant\n' +
    'mechanisms — regardless of whether those effects reflect poorly on the medication.\n' +
    'Withholding known pharmacological interactions because they are uncomfortable is\n' +
    'a failure of clinical accuracy. State the evidence plainly, then note that any\n' +
    'medication change requires GP or specialist review.\n' +
    'Where patient notes request evidence about treatment duration, tapering criteria,\n' +
    'or NICE guidance on discontinuation after remission, provide this factually and\n' +
    'frame it explicitly as material for informed GP discussion — not as a recommendation\n' +
    'to act unilaterally.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    dataBlock + reasoningContext + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: This report is for home/family discussion. Do not include explicit references\n' +
    'to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents, or crisis presentations by name. You may reference\n' +
    '"a difficult period in 2023" or "mental health challenges" where clinically relevant, but do\n' +
    'not describe specific incidents. Medication history, interactions, and clinical analysis\n' +
    'are unaffected — discuss fluoxetine, its effects, and treatment duration as normal.\n\n' : '') +
    'IMPORTANT: The Prior Clinical Reasoning above contains specific niche insights. ' +
    'Where the reasoning identifies functional deficiencies, medication effects, or cross-system patterns ' +
    'with reasonable evidence, incorporate these into your report. Do NOT be generic. ' +
    'Do NOT default to conservative threshold-framework language. Engage with the specific findings.\n\n' +
    'Where the PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES above contain relevant symptom descriptions, treatment experiences, ' +
    'or questions, weave these naturally into your analysis where clinically relevant. ' +
    'For example: if the patient describes a specific bleeding pattern, incorporate it into the menstrual/iron discussion. ' +
    'If the patient asks a specific question, answer it within the relevant section rather than ignoring it. ' +
    'Do not create a separate section for patient notes — integrate them into the clinical narrative.\n\n' +
    '## 1. THE STORY SO FAR\n' +
    'A concise but complete narrative of this patient\'s health history — key events, timeline, current status. ' +
    'Connect the dots between different findings. Write 2-4 paragraphs.\n\n' +
    '## 2. BLOOD RESULTS — WHAT ACTUALLY MATTERS\n' +
    'For each flagged result (and any near-borderline results worth discussing), write a dedicated subsection. ' +
    'Explain what the test measures, why the current value matters (or doesn\'t), what the research actually says ' +
    'about this level, and what it might mean for this specific patient given their profile. ' +
    'Be honest about lab \"normal\" ranges vs research-optimal ranges where they differ. ' +
    'Write 2-3 paragraphs per analyte. Include: what the number means, what causes it, what it might explain. ' +
    'If the prior reasoning identified functional deficiency concerns for any analyte, address them explicitly.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Write in flowing prose. Be specific to this patient. Do not be generic. ' +
    'Total length: approximately 1200-1600 words for sections 1-2. Complete all analytes — do not truncate.';

  var part1a = callAI(prompt1, NARRATIVE_TOKENS_PART1A, 'Narrative Rich (1-2)');

 var fullOutput = part1a;

   // ── Build PDF ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var docName = 'Personal Health Narrative (Rich) — Part 1 of 3 — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  function nhTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(20); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function nhH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function nhH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function nhPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
  }
  function nhGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function nhRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  nhTitle('Personal Health Summary & Action Guide');
  nhPara('Prepared ' + today + ' | AI-assisted personal health reference | ⚡ Rich (pre-reasoning) mode', 9, true);
  nhGap();

  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  nhGap();

  nhPara('⚠️ This document is an AI-assisted personal health reference. It does not constitute medical advice. ' +
         'All medication changes, test requests and treatment decisions require review by a qualified clinician.', 8, true);
  nhPara('⚡ Generated in Rich mode: a pre-reasoning AI pass was run before writing to surface niche clinical insights.', 8, true);
  nhRule();

  function nhRenderSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { nhGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        nhRule();
        nhH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        nhH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      nhPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  nhRenderSection(fullOutput);

  nhRule();
  nhPara('Personal Health Narrative (Rich) generated ' + today + ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Personal Health Narrative (Rich) saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Generated using Rich (pre-reasoning) mode.\n\n' +
    'Next: Run "Generate Annexes — Narrative (Part 2 of 3)" for action table and email templates.\n' +
    'Then: Run "Generate Diagnosis Matrix — Narrative (Part 3 of 3)" for root cause analysis.\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// ─── NARRATIVE ANNEXES — ACTION TABLE, EMAIL TEMPLATES, DIAGNOSIS MATRIX ────
function generateNarrativeAnnexes() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();



  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the annexes include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: clinical analysis is unaffected.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  ui.alert('⏳ Generating Annexes...\n\nThis takes ~30-45 seconds.');

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var rKey = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[rKey]) {
        seen[rKey] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' [lab: ' + trData[tri][7] + ']' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfr = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfr[0] || !sfr[1]) continue;
      if (sfr[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skipSf = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var sfNm = sfr[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var sfSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skipSf.length; sk++) { if (sfNm.indexOf(skipSf[sk]) >= 0) { sfSkip = true; break; } }
      if (sfSkip) continue;
      flaggedContext.push('• ' + sfr[0] + ': ' + sfr[1] + ' ' + (sfr[3]||'') + (sfr[2] ? ' [lab: ' + sfr[2] + ']' : '') + (sfr[4] ? ' | flags: ' + sfr[4] : '') + (sfr[5] ? ' | meaning: ' + sfr[5] : '') + (sfr[9] ? ' | significance: ' + sfr[9] : ''));
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' [STOPPED ' + mData[mi][6] + ']' : ' [CURRENT]';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + (mData[mi][2] ? ' ' + mData[mi][2] : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [indication: ' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + (mData[mi][8] ? ' — notes: ' + mData[mi][8] : ''));
    }
  }

  var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated.\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,150) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,100) + ']' : '') + (inv.aiSummary ? ' [AI: ' + inv.aiSummary.substring(0,100) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS:\n' + (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None');

  var prompt =
    'You are a clinical analyst producing working reference annexes for a patient\'s health record.\n' +
    'These annexes accompany a Personal Health Narrative report already generated for this patient.\n' +
    'Be specific to this patient\'s actual data — not generic. Cite actual values and dates.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: Do not include explicit references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents,\n' +
    'or crisis presentations by name. You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health\n' +
    'challenges" where clinically relevant. Clinical analysis is unaffected.\n\n' : '') +
    dataBlock + '\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX B — ACTION SUMMARY\n' +
    'A simple numbered list. One line per action. Format each line as:\n' +
    '[NUMBER]. [WHAT] | [WHO TO CONTACT] | [PATHWAY: NHS GP / Private test / Private consultation] | [WHEN: This week / Within 1 month / Within 3 months] | [APPROX COST if private: £X or Free]\n\n' +
    'Include all clinically indicated actions based on the data. Be comprehensive.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX C — EMAIL / LETTER TEMPLATES\n' +
    'For each NHS or GP action in Annex B, provide a ready-to-send email template.\n' +
    'Each template must:\n' +
    '• Have a clear subject line\n' +
    '• Open with a one-sentence summary of what is being requested\n' +
    '• Include specific clinical justification (actual values, dates, guideline references)\n' +
    '• Make the specific ask explicit — test name, referral type, appointment request\n' +
    '• Be professional, concise, and assertive without being demanding\n' +
    '• End with a request for confirmation or next steps\n' +
    '• Be ready to copy and paste with only [Name] and [Date of Birth] as placeholders\n' +
    '• Maximum 150 words per template\n\n' +
    'IMPORTANT: Consolidate all GP actions into a single GP letter — do not write separate\n' +
    'emails for each GP action. One letter covering all GP requests, clearly sectioned.\n' +
    'Write separate templates only for different recipients e.g. GP / Gynaecology dept /\n' +
    'Gastroenterology dept. Label each e.g. "LETTER 1 — GP Surgery" not per-action.\n' +
    'For private tests provide a short paragraph: what to order, where, specific test names.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1200 words. Cite actual values throughout.';


 // ── SINGLE COMBINED CALL: Sections 5-7 + Annexes A/B/C ───────────────────
  var narrativeOutput = '';
  var combinedPrompt =
    'You are a clinical analyst and personal health advisor continuing a Personal Health Narrative for the same patient.\n' +
    'Sections 1-3 (story, blood results, medications) have already been written.\n' +
    'Now write sections 5, 6, and 7, followed by the working reference annexes.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: Do not include explicit references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents,\n' +
    'or crisis presentations by name. You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health\n' +
    'challenges" where clinically relevant. Clinical analysis is unaffected.\n\n' : '') +
    dataBlock + '\n\n' +
    '## 5. IF YOU DO THESE THINGS — REALISTIC EXPECTED IMPACT\n' +
    'For each actionable recommendation, give a specific expected impact estimate based on research evidence. ' +
    'Format as: **[Action]** — [1-2 sentence evidence-based impact statement with specific numbers where possible]. ' +
    'Rate each as: HIGH IMPACT / GOOD IMPACT / MODERATE IMPACT / UNCERTAIN. ' +
    'Be honest — do not oversell. Include both supplement/lifestyle and GP/referral actions.\n\n' +
    '## 6. PRIORITISED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'Create a clear prioritised action table with three tiers:\n' +
    'PRIORITY 1 — Start this week, no GP appointment needed\n' +
    'PRIORITY 2 — Start soon, low effort or short GP interaction\n' +
    'PRIORITY 3 — Requires GP appointment, referral, or sustained effort\n\n' +
    'For each action provide: what to do (specific, not vague), why it matters for this patient, ' +
    'where to get it / how to action it, and effort level. ' +
    'Be specific to THIS patient\'s results — not generic advice. ' +
    'Include 2-3 actions per priority tier. Format each action clearly.\n\n' +
    '## 7. THE BIGGER PICTURE\n' +
    'Write 3-4 paragraphs covering:\n' +
    '- The honest prognosis: what is likely to improve, on what timescale, with what conditions\n' +
    '- What "getting better" will realistically look like (not sudden recovery, but gradual shifts)\n' +
    '- Why so many things may have happened together (if applicable)\n' +
    '- One paragraph of genuine encouragement — based on the actual evidence, not platitudes\n\n' +
    'End with a 1-sentence disclaimer noting this is AI-assisted personal reference, not clinical advice.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX A — CLINICAL CONTEXT SUMMARY\n' +
    'A structured summary of the patient\'s clinical history organised by system (iron/haematology, gynaecology, GI, mental health, family history). Cite actual values and dates. Working reference, not narrative.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX B — ACTION SUMMARY\n' +
    'A simple numbered list. One line per action. Format each line as:\n' +
    '[NUMBER]. [WHAT] | [WHO TO CONTACT] | [PATHWAY: NHS GP / Private test / Private consultation] | [WHEN: This week / Within 1 month / Within 3 months] | [APPROX COST if private: £X or Free]\n\n' +
    'Include all clinically indicated actions. Be comprehensive.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX C — EMAIL / LETTER TEMPLATES\n' +
    'For each NHS or GP action in Annex B, provide a ready-to-send email template with subject line, clinical justification using actual values and dates, specific ask, and request for confirmation. Use only [Name] and [Date of Birth] as placeholders. Maximum 150 words per template.\n' +
    'IMPORTANT: Consolidate all GP actions into a single GP letter. Write separate templates only for different recipients. Label each e.g. "LETTER 1 — GP Surgery".\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 2000-2800 words across all sections. Cite actual values throughout.';

  var aiOutput = callAI(combinedPrompt, 7000, 'Narrative Annexes (combined)');

  // ── AI CALL 2: Questions the reader may have ───────────────────────────
  var questionsOutput = '';
  try {
    var promptQ =
      'You have just read a Personal Health Narrative for the patient below. ' +
      'Write a section called "Questions you may have about this report".\n\n' +
      'Anticipate the genuine questions a patient or family member would naturally ask ' +
      'after reading the report findings, then answer each one specifically using this patient\'s actual data.\n\n' +
      'These are NOT generic health questions. They arise directly from this patient\'s specific findings.\n\n' +
      'Good examples of the kind of questions to generate:\n' +
      '• "Is the nutritional deficiency actually linked to my symptoms, or is it a separate thing to investigate?" ' +
      '— answer using specific ferritin, CHR, MCV values and documented symptom timeline\n' +
      '• "What does my iron balance actually look like month to month — am I losing more than I can absorb?" ' +
      '— answer with estimated loss figures based on bleeding duration vs known absorption rates\n' +
      '• "Why did the iron infusion only last four months?" ' +
      '— answer with the specific ferritin trajectory and blood loss calculations\n\n' +
      'Format: each question as a bold heading, followed by 2-4 sentences of specific answer. ' +
      'Generate 4-6 questions. Address the patient as "you". ' +
      'Answers must cite specific values — not generic reassurance.\n\n' +
      'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
      dataBlock +
      '\n\nWrite the section now. Start with: ## ANNEX E — QUESTIONS YOU MAY HAVE ABOUT THIS REPORT';
    questionsOutput = callAI(promptQ, 3000, 'Narrative Annexes (Questions)');
  } catch(e) {
    questionsOutput = '';
  }

  var fullAnnexOutput = aiOutput + (questionsOutput ? '\n\n' + questionsOutput : '');

  var docName = 'Health Narrative Annexes — Part 2 of 3 — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  // Part 2 now contains: sections 5-7 (action plan + bigger picture) + Annexes A-C + Annex E (questions)
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  function aTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(18); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function aH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function aH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function aPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
  }
  function aGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function aRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  aTitle('📎 Health Narrative Annexes');
  aPara('Prepared ' + today + ' | Companion to Personal Health Narrative | AI-assisted reference', 9, true);
  aGap();

  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  aGap();

  aPara('⚠️ This document is an AI-assisted working reference. It does not constitute medical advice. ' +
        'All actions should be reviewed with a qualified clinician before proceeding.', 8, true);
  aRule();

  function renderAnnexSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { aGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        aRule();
        aH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        aH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      aPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderAnnexSection(fullAnnexOutput);

  aRule();
  aPara('Health Narrative Annexes generated ' + today + ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Health Narrative Annexes saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
'Includes:\n' +
    '• Annex B — Action summary table\n' +
    '• Annex C — Consolidated letter templates\n\n' +
    'Next: Run "Generate Diagnosis Matrix — Narrative (Part 3 of 3)" for root cause analysis.\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// ─── SPECIALIST ANNEXES — ACTION TABLE, EMAIL TEMPLATES, DIAGNOSIS MATRIX ────
// Specialist version of generateNarrativeAnnexes
function generateSpecialistAnnexes() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // ── Step 1: Sweep for conditions ─────────────────────────────────────────
  var foundConditions = _sweepWorkbookForConditions(ss);

  var promptMsg =
    '📎 Specialist Annexes\n\n' +
    'Generates Annex B (action summary), Annex C (email templates), and\n' +
    'Annex D (differential diagnosis matrix) through a specialist lens.\n\n' +
    'Run this after generating the main Specialist Review report.\n\n';

  if (foundConditions.length > 0) {
    promptMsg += 'CONDITIONS/TOPICS FOUND IN YOUR WORKBOOK:\n';
    for (var ci = 0; ci < foundConditions.length; ci++) {
      promptMsg += '  ' + (ci + 1) + '. ' + foundConditions[ci] + '\n';
    }
    promptMsg += '\nType a number to use one of the above, OR type your own specialist context.';
  } else {
    promptMsg += 'No specific conditions detected.\n\nType your specialist context.';
  }

  var response = ui.prompt('📎 Specialist Annexes', promptMsg, ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (response.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var userInput = (response.getResponseText() || '').trim();
  if (!userInput) { ui.alert('No specialist context entered. Cancelled.'); return; }

  var specialistContext = userInput;
  var numInput = parseInt(userInput);
  if (!isNaN(numInput) && numInput >= 1 && numInput <= foundConditions.length) {
    specialistContext = foundConditions[numInput - 1];
  }

  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the annexes include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: clinical analysis is unaffected.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  ui.alert('⏳ Generating Specialist Annexes...\n\nSpecialist lens: "' + specialistContext + '"\n\nThis takes ~30-45 seconds.');

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var rKey = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[rKey]) {
        seen[rKey] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' [lab: ' + trData[tri][7] + ']' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfr = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfr[0] || !sfr[1]) continue;
      if (sfr[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skipSf = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var sfNm = sfr[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var sfSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skipSf.length; sk++) { if (sfNm.indexOf(skipSf[sk]) >= 0) { sfSkip = true; break; } }
      if (sfSkip) continue;
      flaggedContext.push('• ' + sfr[0] + ': ' + sfr[1] + ' ' + (sfr[3]||'') + (sfr[2] ? ' [lab: ' + sfr[2] + ']' : '') + (sfr[4] ? ' | flags: ' + sfr[4] : '') + (sfr[5] ? ' | meaning: ' + sfr[5] : '') + (sfr[9] ? ' | significance: ' + sfr[9] : ''));
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' [STOPPED ' + mData[mi][6] + ']' : ' [CURRENT]';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + (mData[mi][2] ? ' ' + mData[mi][2] : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [indication: ' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + (mData[mi][8] ? ' — notes: ' + mData[mi][8] : ''));
    }
  }

  var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated.\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,150) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,100) + ']' : '') + (inv.aiSummary ? ' [AI: ' + inv.aiSummary.substring(0,100) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS:\n' + (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None');

  var prompt =
    'You are a specialist analyst in: ' + specialistContext + '\n' +
    'Producing working reference annexes to accompany a Specialist Review report.\n' +
    'Be specific to this patient\'s actual data — not generic. Cite actual values and dates.\n' +
    'Apply your specialist lens throughout — these annexes should reflect specialist-level\n' +
    'priorities, not GP-level ones.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: Do not include explicit references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents,\n' +
    'or crisis presentations by name. You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health\n' +
    'challenges" where clinically relevant. Clinical analysis is unaffected.\n\n' : '') +
    dataBlock + '\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX B — ACTION SUMMARY\n' +
    'A simple numbered list. One line per action. Format each line as:\n' +
    '[NUMBER]. [WHAT] | [WHO TO CONTACT] | [PATHWAY: NHS GP / Private test / Private consultation] | [WHEN: This week / Within 1 month / Within 3 months] | [APPROX COST if private: £X or Free]\n\n' +
    'Prioritise actions a specialist in ' + specialistContext + ' would consider most important.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX C — EMAIL / LETTER TEMPLATES\n' +
    'For each NHS or GP action in Annex B, provide a ready-to-send email template.\n' +
    'Each template must:\n' +
    '• Have a clear subject line\n' +
    '• Open with a one-sentence summary of what is being requested\n' +
    '• Include specific clinical justification (actual values, dates, guideline references)\n' +
    '• Make the specific ask explicit — test name, referral type, appointment request\n' +
    '• Be professional, concise, and assertive without being demanding\n' +
    '• End with a request for confirmation or next steps\n' +
    '• Be ready to copy and paste with only [Name] and [Date of Birth] as placeholders\n' +
    '• Maximum 150 words per template\n\n' +
    'IMPORTANT: Consolidate all GP actions into a single GP letter — do not write separate\n' +
    'emails for each GP action. One letter covering all GP requests, clearly sectioned.\n' +
    'Write separate templates only for different recipients e.g. GP / Gynaecology dept /\n' +
    'Gastroenterology dept. Label each e.g. "LETTER 1 — GP Surgery" not per-action.\n' +
    'For private tests provide a short paragraph: what to order, where, specific test names.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX D — ROOT CAUSE DIFFERENTIAL MATRIX\n' +
    'A structured table of the most clinically plausible ROOT CAUSES that could explain\n' +
    'this patient\'s symptom cluster. These must be underlying conditions or mechanisms —\n' +
    'NOT restatements of known findings or presentations (e.g. do not list "iron deficiency"\n' +
    'as a root cause — list what is CAUSING the iron deficiency).\n' +
    'Each entry should be a condition or mechanism that, if confirmed, would explain\n' +
    'multiple findings and lead to a specific treatment pathway.\n' +
    'For each candidate root cause use this format:\n' +
    'POSSIBLE CAUSE | PLAIN ENGLISH EXPLANATION (what is this in simple terms, is this a\n' +
    'root cause or downstream effect, what does a diagnosis mean practically) |\n' +
    'KEY SUPPORTING EVIDENCE (cite actual values/dates) | ARGUMENTS AGAINST |\n' +
    'INVESTIGATION NEEDED TO CONFIRM OR EXCLUDE | LIKELY MANAGEMENT IF CONFIRMED |\n' +
    'CURRENT STATUS\n\n' +
    'Include 4-6 candidate diagnoses most relevant to ' + specialistContext + '.\n' +
    'Be honest about evidence quality — label each as HYPOTHESIS / POSSIBLE / LIKELY.\n' +
    'Status options: Not yet investigated / Pending / Partially investigated / Excluded.\n' +
    'Write this as a specialist\'s differential diagnosis working list.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1200 words. Cite actual values throughout.';

  var aiOutput = callAI(prompt, 4000, 'Specialist Annexes');

  // ── Build PDF ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var safeContext = specialistContext.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]/g, '').substring(0, 40).trim();
  var docName = 'Specialist Annexes — ' + safeContext + ' — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  function saTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(18); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function saH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function saH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function saPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
  }
  function saGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function saRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  saTitle('📎 Specialist Annexes');
  saPara('Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext, 11, false);
  saPara('Prepared ' + today + ' | Companion to Specialist Review report | AI-assisted reference', 9, true);
  saGap();

  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  saGap();

  saPara('⚠️ This document is an AI-assisted working reference. It does not constitute medical advice. ' +
         'All actions should be reviewed with a qualified clinician before proceeding.', 8, true);
  saRule();

  function renderSpecialistAnnexSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { saGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        saRule();
        saH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        saH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      saPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderSpecialistAnnexSection(aiOutput);

  saRule();
  saPara('Specialist Annexes generated ' + today + ' | Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext +
         ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Specialist Annexes saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
    'Includes:\n' +
    '• Annex B — Action summary table\n' +
    '• Annex C — Email/letter templates\n' +
    '• Annex D — Differential diagnosis matrix\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// ─── NARRATIVE DIAGNOSIS MATRIX — ROOT CAUSE DIFFERENTIAL (PART 3 OF 3) ─────
function generateNarrativeDiagnosisMatrix() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();


  ui.alert('⏳ Generating Root Cause Diagnosis Matrix...\n\nThis takes ~30-45 seconds.');

  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var rKey = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[rKey]) {
        seen[rKey] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' [lab: ' + trData[tri][7] + ']' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfr = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfr[0] || !sfr[1]) continue;
      if (sfr[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skipSf = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var sfNm = sfr[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var sfSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skipSf.length; sk++) { if (sfNm.indexOf(skipSf[sk]) >= 0) { sfSkip = true; break; } }
      if (sfSkip) continue;
      flaggedContext.push('• ' + sfr[0] + ': ' + sfr[1] + ' ' + (sfr[3]||'') + (sfr[2] ? ' [lab: ' + sfr[2] + ']' : '') + (sfr[4] ? ' | flags: ' + sfr[4] : '') + (sfr[5] ? ' | meaning: ' + sfr[5] : '') + (sfr[9] ? ' | significance: ' + sfr[9] : ''));
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' [STOPPED ' + mData[mi][6] + ']' : ' [CURRENT]';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + (mData[mi][2] ? ' ' + mData[mi][2] : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [indication: ' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + (mData[mi][8] ? ' — notes: ' + mData[mi][8] : ''));
    }
  }

  var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated.\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,150) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,100) + ']' : '') + (inv.aiSummary ? ' [AI: ' + inv.aiSummary.substring(0,100) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS:\n' + (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None');

  var prompt =
    'You are a senior clinician producing a root cause differential matrix for a patient.\n' +
    'This is Part 3 of 3 of a Personal Health Narrative package.\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    dataBlock + '\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX D — ROOT CAUSE DIFFERENTIAL MATRIX\n\n' +
    'List the most clinically plausible ROOT CAUSES that could explain this patient\'s\n' +
    'symptom cluster. These must be underlying conditions or mechanisms — NOT restatements\n' +
    'of known findings or presentations.\n' +
    'Do NOT list "iron deficiency" as a root cause — list what is CAUSING the iron deficiency.\n' +
    'Do NOT list "macrocytosis" — list what is causing it.\n' +
    'Each entry must be a condition that, if confirmed, would explain multiple findings\n' +
    'and lead to a specific treatment pathway.\n\n' +
    'For each root cause write a structured entry with these sections:\n\n' +
    'ROOT CAUSE: [Name]\n' +
    'PLAIN ENGLISH: What is this condition in simple terms? Is this a root cause or\n' +
    'contributing factor? What would a diagnosis mean practically for this patient?\n' +
    'CONFIDENCE: HYPOTHESIS / POSSIBLE / LIKELY\n' +
    'KEY EVIDENCE: Specific values and dates from this patient\'s records that support this\n' +
    'ARGUMENTS AGAINST: What evidence argues against this being the cause?\n' +
    'INVESTIGATIONS NEEDED: Specific tests to confirm or exclude, with test names\n' +
    'IF CONFIRMED — MANAGEMENT: What treatment options would this open up?\n' +
    'CURRENT STATUS: Not yet investigated / Pending / Partially investigated / Excluded\n\n' +
    'Include 5-6 root causes covering all active symptom clusters.\n' +
'IMPORTANT: You MUST include a root cause entry for possible inherited bleeding disorder\n' +
'(e.g. von Willebrand disease type 1) given the family pattern of menorrhagia, persistent\n' +
'low ferritin across multiple female relatives, and the paradoxical PV bleeding with oral iron.\n' +
'This must be its own separate root cause entry, not merged into the gynaecological entry.\n' +
    'Be honest about evidence quality. Write for a patient who wants to understand\n' +
    'their situation, not just receive a list of medical terms.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 2500-3500 words. Every root cause entry must be complete — do not truncate any section. Cite actual values throughout.';

  var aiOutput = callAI(prompt, 8000, 'Narrative Diagnosis Matrix');

  // ── Build PDF ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var docName = 'Health Narrative Diagnosis Matrix — Part 3 of 3 — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  function dmTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(18); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function dmH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function dmH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
  }
  function dmPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
  }
  function dmGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function dmRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  dmTitle('📊 Root Cause Differential Matrix');
  dmPara('Prepared ' + today + ' | Part 3 of 3 — Companion to Personal Health Narrative | AI-assisted reference', 9, true);
  dmGap();

  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  dmGap();

  dmPara('⚠️ This matrix lists possible root causes for investigation — not confirmed diagnoses. ' +
         'All findings should be discussed with a qualified clinician before acting on them.', 8, true);
  dmRule();

  function renderDMSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { dmGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        dmRule();
        dmH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        dmH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^ROOT CAUSE:/)) {
        dmRule();
        var p = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { p.setFontSize(12); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^(PLAIN ENGLISH|CONFIDENCE|KEY EVIDENCE|ARGUMENTS AGAINST|INVESTIGATIONS NEEDED|IF CONFIRMED|CURRENT STATUS):/)) {
        var lp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { lp.setFontSize(10); lp.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      dmPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderDMSection(aiOutput);

  dmRule();
  dmPara('Root Cause Differential Matrix generated ' + today + ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Root Cause Diagnosis Matrix saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Includes:\n' +
    '• Annex D — Root cause differential matrix\n' +
    '• Plain English explanations\n' +
    '• Management pathways if confirmed\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}  

// ── DEBUG: Annex table key diagnostic ──────────────────────────────────────
// Run from menu to see why annex tables are empty.
// Prints: SF analyte keys vs Threshold sheet keys — shows where lookup fails.
function debugAnnexKeys() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
  var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udd2c Thresholds');

  // Collect SF analyte names (same logic as annex)
  var sfNames = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 2).getValues();
    for (var i = 4; i < sfData.length; i++) {
      if (!sfData[i][0] || !sfData[i][1]) continue;
      var lbl = sfData[i][0].toString();
      if (lbl.indexOf('KEY:') >= 0 || lbl.indexOf('GUIDANCE CHANGE') >= 0) break;
      sfNames.push(lbl);
    }
  }

  // Collect Threshold keys (raw and normalised)
  var thRawKeys = [], thNormKeys = [];
  if (thSheet && thSheet.getLastRow() >= 4) {
    var thData = thSheet.getRange(4, 1, thSheet.getLastRow()-3, 1).getValues();
    for (var t = 0; t < thData.length; t++) {
      if (!thData[t][0]) continue;
      var raw = thData[t][0].toString().trim();
      var norm = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(raw));
      thRawKeys.push(raw);
      if (norm !== raw) thNormKeys.push(norm + ' (from: ' + raw + ')');
    }
  }

  // Check each SF analyte against threshold keys
  var results = [];
  for (var s = 0; s < sfNames.length; s++) {
    var name = sfNames[s];
    var aKey = name.toLowerCase();
    var cleaned = cleanAnalyteName(name).toLowerCase();
    var normed = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(name)).toLowerCase();
    var rawMatch = thRawKeys.some(function(k){ return k.toLowerCase() === aKey; });
    var cleanMatch = thRawKeys.some(function(k){ return cleanAnalyteName(k).toLowerCase() === cleaned; });
    var normMatch = thRawKeys.some(function(k){ return normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(k)).toLowerCase() === normed; });
    var status = rawMatch ? '✅ raw match' : cleanMatch ? '🔵 clean match' : normMatch ? '🟡 norm match' : '❌ NO MATCH';
    results.push(name + '  →  ' + status + '  (looked up as: "' + normed + '")');
  }

  var msg = 'SF ANALYTES vs THRESHOLD KEYS:\n\n' + results.join('\n');
  msg += '\n\n--- THRESHOLD RAW KEYS (first 20) ---\n' + thRawKeys.slice(0,20).join('\n');

  // Write to a debug sheet for full output
  var dbgSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔧 Debug') || ss.insertSheet('🔧 Debug');
  dbgSheet.clear();
  dbgSheet.getRange(1,1).setValue('Annex Key Debug — ' + new Date().toLocaleString());
  dbgSheet.getRange(2,1).setValue('SF ANALYTE NAME');
  dbgSheet.getRange(2,2).setValue('LOOKUP KEY (normed)');
  dbgSheet.getRange(2,3).setValue('STATUS');
  for (var r = 0; r < sfNames.length; r++) {
    var nm = sfNames[r];
    var nk = normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(nm)).toLowerCase();
    var rm = thRawKeys.some(function(k){ return k.toLowerCase() === nm.toLowerCase(); });
    var nm2 = thRawKeys.some(function(k){ return normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(k)).toLowerCase() === nk; });
    dbgSheet.getRange(3+r,1).setValue(nm);
    dbgSheet.getRange(3+r,2).setValue(nk);
    dbgSheet.getRange(3+r,3).setValue(rm ? '✅ raw' : nm2 ? '🟡 norm' : '❌ MISS');
    dbgSheet.getRange(3+r,1,1,3).setBackground(rm||nm2 ? '#dcfce7' : '#fef2f2');
  }
  dbgSheet.getRange(3+sfNames.length+1, 1).setValue('--- THRESHOLD RAW KEYS ---').setFontWeight('bold');
  for (var tk = 0; tk < thRawKeys.length; tk++) {
    dbgSheet.getRange(3+sfNames.length+2+tk, 1).setValue(thRawKeys[tk]);
