    dbgSheet.getRange(3+sfNames.length+2+tk, 2).setValue(normaliseSynonyms(cleanAnalyteName(thRawKeys[tk])).toLowerCase());
  }
  dbgSheet.setColumnWidth(1,250); dbgSheet.setColumnWidth(2,250); dbgSheet.setColumnWidth(3,120);
  ss.setActiveSheet(dbgSheet);
  ui.alert('Debug complete — see 🔧 Debug tab\n\n' + results.join('\n'));
}

// ─── TEST AI CONNECTION ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Runs a minimal live API call and shows the full response/error.
// Use this to diagnose API key or connectivity issues.
function testAIConnection() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var provider = getAIProvider();
  var apiKey = getApiKey();
  var keyProp = provider === 'anthropic' ? 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' : 'OPENAI_API_KEY';

  if (!apiKey) {
    ui.alert('❌ No API key found',
      'Script Property "' + keyProp + '" is not set or is empty.\n\n' +
      'Go to: Extensions → Apps Script → Project Settings → Script Properties\n' +
      'Add: ' + keyProp + ' = your key',
      ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    return;
  }

  ui.alert('🔄 Testing ' + provider.toUpperCase() + ' connection...\n\nKey: ' + keyProp + ' (' + apiKey.substring(0,12) + '...)\n\nClick OK — result will appear in ~10 seconds.');

  var result = '';
  try {
    if (provider === 'anthropic') {
      var response = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'post',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type':      'application/json',
          'x-api-key':         apiKey,
          'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01'
        },
        payload: JSON.stringify({
          model:      'claude-haiku-4-5',
          max_tokens: 20,
          messages:   [{ role: 'user', content: 'Reply with just: OK' }]
        }),
        muteHttpExceptions: true
      });
      var code = response.getResponseCode();
      var body = response.getContentText();
      if (code === 200) {
        var parsed = JSON.parse(body);
        result = '✅ SUCCESS\n\nProvider: Anthropic\nModel: claude-haiku-4-5\nResponse: ' + parsed.content[0].text;
      } else {
        result = '❌ FAILED\n\nHTTP ' + code + '\n\nRaw response:\n' + body.substring(0, 500);
      }
    } else {
      var response2 = UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', {
        method: 'post',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + apiKey },
        payload: JSON.stringify({ model: 'gpt-4o', max_tokens: 20,
          messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Reply with just: OK' }] }),
        muteHttpExceptions: true
      });
      var code2 = response2.getResponseCode();
      var body2 = response2.getContentText();
      if (code2 === 200) {
        var parsed2 = JSON.parse(body2);
        result = '✅ SUCCESS\n\nProvider: OpenAI\nModel: gpt-4o\nResponse: ' + parsed2.choices[0].message.content;
      } else {
        result = '❌ FAILED\n\nHTTP ' + code2 + '\n\nRaw response:\n' + body2.substring(0, 500);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {
    result = '❌ EXCEPTION\n\n' + e.toString();
  }

  ui.alert('🔌 Connection Test Result', result, ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}


// ─── SPECIALIST DEEPDIVE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Consultant-layer assessment: views all patient data through a specialist lens.
// Separate from and does not affect the GP-layer analysis pipeline.
// Flow:
//   1. Auto-sweeps workbook for condition mentions → presents found conditions
//   2. User selects a condition or writes their own specialist context
//   3. Single AI call with consultant-framed prompt
//   4. PDF output to Google Drive — nothing written back to sheets
//
function runSpecialistDeepdive() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // ── Step 1: Sweep workbook for condition/diagnosis mentions ──────────────
  var foundConditions = _sweepWorkbookForConditions(ss);

  // ── Step 2: Present found conditions + free text option ─────────────────
  var promptMsg =
    '🔬 Specialist Deepdive — Consultant-Layer Assessment\n\n' +
    'This generates a specialist-perspective review of ALL your health data through\n' +
    'the lens of a specific condition or specialism.\n\n' +
    'Unlike the GP-layer narrative, this:\n' +
    '  • Weights specialist and rehabilitation literature over mainstream GP guidelines\n' +
    '  • Flags niche associations and emerging research relevant to your condition\n' +
    '  • Identifies what a specialist would test or investigate that a GP might not\n' +
    '  • Is explicit about evidence quality (established vs emerging)\n\n';

  if (foundConditions.length > 0) {
    promptMsg += 'CONDITIONS/TOPICS FOUND IN YOUR WORKBOOK:\n';
    for (var ci = 0; ci < foundConditions.length; ci++) {
      promptMsg += '  ' + (ci + 1) + '. ' + foundConditions[ci] + '\n';
    }
    promptMsg += '\nType a number to use one of the above, OR type your own specialist\n' +
                 'context (e.g. "Post-concussion syndrome and autonomic dysfunction with\n' +
                 'focus on functional deficiencies and recovery markers").\n\n' +
                 'Be as specific as you like — more context = better assessment.';
  } else {
    promptMsg += 'No specific conditions were detected in your workbook.\n\n' +
                 'Type your specialist context — e.g.:\n' +
                 '"Post-concussion syndrome and POTS — focus on functional\n' +
                 'deficiencies, autonomic markers, and recovery trajectory"\n\n' +
                 'Be as specific as you like — more context = better assessment.';
  }

  var response = ui.prompt('🔬 Specialist Deepdive', promptMsg, ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (response.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var userInput = (response.getResponseText() || '').trim();
  if (!userInput) {
    ui.alert('No specialist context entered. Deepdive cancelled.');
    return;
  }

  // Resolve numbered selection to condition name
  var specialistContext = userInput;
  var numInput = parseInt(userInput);
  if (!isNaN(numInput) && numInput >= 1 && numInput <= foundConditions.length) {
    specialistContext = foundConditions[numInput - 1];
  }

  // ── Step 3: Confirm and run ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  var confirmMsg =
    'Specialist lens: "' + specialistContext + '"\n\n' +
    'This will run a single AI call (~30-45 seconds) and save a PDF to Google Drive.\n\n' +
    'Continue?';


  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the report include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: medication interactions, treatment history, and clinical analysis are unaffected.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  ui.alert('⏳ Running Specialist / Consultant Review...\n\nAssessing all your data through the lens of:\n"' + specialistContext + '"\n\nThis takes ~30-45 seconds.');

  // ── Step 4: Gather all patient data ──────────────────────────────────────
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  var thSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');

  // All latest clinical results
  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) {
        if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; }
      }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var rKey = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[rKey]) {
        seen[rKey] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') +
          (trData[tri][7] ? ' [lab: ' + trData[tri][7] + ']' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  // Flagged / significant findings with full context
  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfr = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfr[0] || !sfr[1]) continue;
      if (sfr[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skipSf = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var sfNm = sfr[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var sfSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skipSf.length; sk++) {
        if (sfNm.indexOf(skipSf[sk]) >= 0) { sfSkip = true; break; }
      }
      if (sfSkip) continue;
      flaggedContext.push('• ' + sfr[0] + ': ' + sfr[1] + ' ' + (sfr[3]||'') +
        (sfr[2] ? ' [lab: ' + sfr[2] + ']' : '') +
        (sfr[4] ? ' | flags: ' + sfr[4] : '') +
        (sfr[5] ? ' | meaning: ' + sfr[5] : '') +
        (sfr[9] ? ' | significance: ' + sfr[9] : ''));
    }
  }

  // Medications and supplements with indication
  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' [STOPPED ' + mData[mi][6] + ']' : ' [CURRENT]';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + (mData[mi][2] ? ' ' + mData[mi][2] : '') +
        (mData[mi][1] ? ' [indication: ' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped +
        (mData[mi][8] ? ' — notes: ' + mData[mi][8] : ''));
    }
  }

  // GP records and consultations
var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built — run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated (records have changed since last build — ' + cs.builtDate + ').\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  // Saved symptom investigations
  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);
  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,150) +
            (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,100) + ']' : '') +
            (inv.aiSummary ? ' [AI: ' + inv.aiSummary.substring(0,100) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // ── Step 5: Build consultant-framed prompt ───────────────────────────────
  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);
  var prompt =
    'You are a consultant specialist with deep expertise in: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
    'You have been asked to review a patient\'s complete medical workbook and provide a ' +
    'specialist-perspective assessment. This is NOT a GP-level review — that has already ' +
    'been done separately. Your role is to add the specialist depth that a general assessment ' +
    'cannot reliably provide.\n\n' +
    'CRITICAL INSTRUCTIONS:\n' +
    '1. Weight your assessment toward specialist and rehabilitation literature, not mainstream ' +
    'GP guidelines — those are already covered elsewhere\n' +
    '2. Explicitly flag where your assessment draws on niche, emerging, or cross-specialty ' +
    'research rather than established mainstream guidance\n' +
    '3. Be honest about evidence quality — distinguish well-established in specialist literature ' +
    'from emerging association from theoretical/plausible but limited evidence\n' +
    '4. Flag specific tests or investigations a specialist would consider that a GP assessment ' +
    'might not prioritise — with the clinical reasoning for each\n' +
    '5. Where mainstream guidance says normal/benign, but specialist evidence suggests ' +
    'otherwise in the context of ' + specialistContext + ', say so explicitly and explain why\n' +
    '6. Do NOT repeat GP-level findings unless they are specifically relevant through your ' +
    'specialist lens\n' +
    '7. Address the patient directly as you — warm but expert tone\n' +
    '8. Never suggest stopping prescribed medications without specialist/GP review\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: This report is for home/family discussion. Do not include explicit\n' +
    'references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents, or crisis presentations by name.\n' +
    'You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health challenges" where clinically\n' +
    'relevant, but do not describe specific incidents or methods. Medication history,\n' +
    'interactions, and all clinical analysis are unaffected — discuss fluoxetine, its\n' +
    'pharmacological effects, and treatment duration fully as normal.\n\n' : '') +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS (most recent value per analyte):\n' +
    latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS (with threshold context):\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None currently flagged') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' +
    (medLines.join('\n') || 'None logged') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' +
    (investigations.join('\n') || 'None saved') + '\n\n' +
    'Write a specialist deepdive assessment with the following structure:\n\n' +
    '## SPECIALIST LENS: ' + specialistContext.toUpperCase() + '\n\n' +
    '## 1. SPECIALIST OVERVIEW\n' +
    'How does this patient\'s overall picture look through your specialist lens? ' +
    'What is the most clinically significant pattern you see that a GP assessment might underweight? ' +
    '2-3 paragraphs.\n\n' +
    '## 2. RESULT-BY-RESULT SPECIALIST REVIEW\n' +
    'For each result that is relevant through your specialist lens — whether flagged or not — ' +
    'provide a specialist interpretation. Be explicit when your interpretation differs from ' +
    'mainstream guidance and explain why. Include results that are lab normal but may be ' +
    'significant in the context of ' + specialistContext + '.\n\n' +
    '## 3. WHAT A SPECIALIST WOULD INVESTIGATE\n' +
    'List the specific tests, investigations or referrals you would recommend — with the ' +
    'specialist clinical reasoning for each. Distinguish between:\n' +
    '  - High priority: should be done to rule in/out a significant finding\n' +
    '  - Worth considering: useful if the above returns positive\n' +
    '  - Lower priority: relevant but can wait\n' +
    'For each, specify the test name, what you are looking for, and what a positive result would mean.\n\n' +
    '## 4. MEDICATION & SUPPLEMENT REVIEW (SPECIALIST PERSPECTIVE)\n' +
    'Are any current medications or supplements particularly relevant — positively or negatively — ' +
    'in the context of ' + specialistContext + '? Flag any specialist-level interactions or ' +
    'considerations a GP assessment might not highlight.\n\n' +
    '## 5. SPECIALIST SUMMARY & RECOMMENDED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'A clear prioritised summary: what are the 2-3 most important things to act on from a ' +
    'specialist perspective, and what would you expect to find if you investigated them?\n\n' +
    'Then add two final sections:\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX B — ACTION SUMMARY\n' +
    'A simple numbered list. One line per action. Format each line as:\n' +
    '[NUMBER]. [WHAT] | [WHO TO CONTACT] | [PATHWAY: NHS GP / Private test / Private consultation] | [WHEN: This week / Within 1 month / Within 3 months] | [APPROX COST if private: £X or Free]\n\n' +
    'Include every action recommended in the report. Nothing new — consolidate only.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX C — EMAIL / LETTER TEMPLATES\n' +
    'For each action requiring contact with a GP, hospital department, or clinic,\n' +
    'provide a ready-to-send email template with subject line, clinical justification\n' +
    'using actual values and dates, specific ask, and a request for confirmation.\n' +
    'Use only [Name] and [Date of Birth] as placeholders.\n' +
    'For private test recommendations, provide exact test names to search for and\n' +
    'which service to use (Medichecks, Monitor My Health, Thriva).\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX D — DIFFERENTIAL DIAGNOSIS MATRIX\n' +
    'Create a structured table of the most clinically plausible explanations for this\n' +
    'patient\'s symptoms. For each candidate diagnosis use this format:\n' +
    'POSSIBLE CAUSE | KEY SUPPORTING EVIDENCE (cite actual values/dates) |\n' +
    'ARGUMENTS AGAINST | INVESTIGATION NEEDED TO CONFIRM OR EXCLUDE | CURRENT STATUS\n\n' +
    'Include 4-6 candidate diagnoses covering all active symptom clusters.\n' +
    'Be honest about evidence quality — label each as HYPOTHESIS / POSSIBLE / LIKELY.\n' +
    'Status options: Not yet investigated / Pending / Partially investigated / Excluded.\n' +
    'Write this as a clinician\'s differential diagnosis working list, not a\n' +
    'patient-facing reassurance document.\n\n' +
    'End with this disclaimer on its own line:\n' +
    'This specialist-lens assessment is AI-generated for personal reference only. It does not ' +
    'constitute a clinical opinion or diagnosis. All findings should be discussed with a qualified ' +
    'specialist in ' + specialistContext + ' before acting on them.\n\n' +
NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1100 words for sections 1-2. Complete all relevant results — do not truncate.';

  var specPart1 = callAI(prompt, SPECIALIST_TOKENS_1, 'Specialist Deepdive (1-2)');

  // ── AI CALL 2: Sections 3-5 + Annexes ─────────────────────────────────
  var promptSpec2 =
    'You are continuing a specialist consultant review for the same patient. ' +
    'Sections 1 and 2 have already been written. Now write sections 3, 4, 5, and the annexes.\n\n' +
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS:\n' + (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY:\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None saved') + '\n\n' +
    '## 3. WHAT A SPECIALIST WOULD INVESTIGATE\n' +
    'List the specific tests, investigations or referrals you would recommend — with the ' +
    'specialist clinical reasoning for each. Distinguish between:\n' +
    '  - High priority: should be done to rule in/out a significant finding\n' +
    '  - Worth considering: useful if the above returns positive\n' +
    '  - Lower priority: relevant but can wait\n' +
    'For each, specify the test name, what you are looking for, and what a positive result would mean.\n\n' +
    '## 4. MEDICATION & SUPPLEMENT REVIEW (SPECIALIST PERSPECTIVE)\n' +
    'Are any current medications or supplements particularly relevant — positively or negatively — ' +
    'in the context of ' + specialistContext + '? Flag any specialist-level interactions or ' +
    'considerations a GP assessment might not highlight.\n\n' +
    '## 5. SPECIALIST SUMMARY & RECOMMENDED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'A clear prioritised summary: what are the 2-3 most important things to act on from a ' +
    'specialist perspective, and what would you expect to find if you investigated them?\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX B — ACTION SUMMARY\n' +
    'A simple numbered list. One line per action. Format each line as:\n' +
    '[NUMBER]. [WHAT] | [WHO TO CONTACT] | [PATHWAY: NHS GP / Private test / Private consultation] | [WHEN: This week / Within 1 month / Within 3 months] | [APPROX COST if private: £X or Free]\n\n' +
    'Include every action recommended in the report. Nothing new — consolidate only.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX C — EMAIL / LETTER TEMPLATES\n' +
    'For each action requiring contact with a GP, hospital department, or clinic, ' +
    'provide a ready-to-send email template with subject line, clinical justification ' +
    'using actual values and dates, specific ask, and a request for confirmation. ' +
    'Use only [Name] and [Date of Birth] as placeholders.\n\n' +
    '## ANNEX D — DIFFERENTIAL DIAGNOSIS MATRIX\n' +
    'Create a structured table of the most clinically plausible explanations for this ' +
    'patient\'s symptoms. For each candidate diagnosis use this format:\n' +
    'POSSIBLE CAUSE | KEY SUPPORTING EVIDENCE (cite actual values/dates) |\n' +
    'ARGUMENTS AGAINST | INVESTIGATION NEEDED TO CONFIRM OR EXCLUDE | CURRENT STATUS\n\n' +
    'Include 4-6 candidate diagnoses covering all active symptom clusters.\n' +
    'Be honest about evidence quality — label each as HYPOTHESIS / POSSIBLE / LIKELY.\n\n' +
    'End with this disclaimer on its own line:\n' +
    'This specialist-lens assessment is AI-generated for personal reference only. It does not ' +
    'constitute a clinical opinion or diagnosis. All findings should be discussed with a qualified ' +
    'specialist in ' + specialistContext + ' before acting on them.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1100 words for sections 3-5 and annexes.';

  var specPart2 = callAI(promptSpec2, SPECIALIST_TOKENS_2, 'Specialist Deepdive (3-5)');
  var aiOutput = specPart1 + '\n\n' + specPart2;
  // ── Step 6: Build Google Doc → PDF ───────────────────────────────────────
  var safeContext = specialistContext.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]/g, '').substring(0, 40).trim();
  var docName = 'Specialist Consultant Review — ' + safeContext + ' — ' + todayFs + ' — ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  // Helper functions
  function sdTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(20); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }

  function sdH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function sdH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function sdPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
    return p;
  }
  function sdGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function sdRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  // Cover
  sdTitle('🔬 Specialist / Consultant Review of History');
  sdPara('Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext, 11, false);
  sdPara('Prepared ' + today + '  |  Consultant-layer AI assessment', 9, true);
  sdGap();

  // Profile box
  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   •   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  sdGap();

  // Context banner
  var ctxTbl = body.appendTable();
  ctxTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var ctxRow = ctxTbl.appendTableRow();
  var ctxCell = ctxRow.appendTableCell('SPECIALIST LENS: ' + specialistContext);
  ctxCell.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
  ctxCell.editAsText().setFontSize(10).setBold(true).setForegroundColor('#ffffff');
  sdGap();

  sdPara('⚠️  This is a consultant-layer AI assessment designed to complement — not replace — ' +
         'GP-level care. It intentionally weights specialist and emerging research over mainstream ' +
         'guidelines. All findings should be discussed with a qualified specialist before acting on them.', 8, true);
  sdRule();

if (cs.stale || cs.missing) {
    para('⚠️ Clinical Summary ' + (cs.missing ? 'has not been built yet' : 'may be outdated — records changed since ' + cs.builtDate) +
         '. Run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.', 8, true);
  }

  // Render AI output
  function renderSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { sdGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        sdRule();
        sdH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        sdH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^(HIGH PRIORITY|WORTH CONSIDERING|LOWER PRIORITY)/i)) {
        var hp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { hp.setFontSize(10); hp.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '•' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*•]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      sdPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderSection(aiOutput);

  // Footer
  sdRule();
  sdPara('Specialist / Consultant Review generated ' + today + ' | Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext +
         ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only — not a clinical opinion.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  // Convert to PDF
  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Specialist / Consultant Review saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
    'Contains:\n' +
    '• Specialist overview — patterns a GP assessment may underweight\n' +
    '• Result-by-result specialist interpretation\n' +
    '• Specific investigations a specialist would order\n' +
    '• Medication review through specialist lens\n' +
    '• Prioritised next steps\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}


// ─── SPECIALIST / CONSULTANT REVIEW — RICH (PRE-REASONING) ──────────────────
// Identical to runSpecialistDeepdive except it runs a silent pre-reasoning
// AI call before the main specialist writing call. The reasoning pass focuses
// specifically on the chosen specialist lens, generating a clinical hypothesis
// list that the writing call then engages with in depth.
// Total: 2 AI calls (~60-90 seconds). Falls back gracefully if call 0 fails.
function runSpecialistDeepdiveRich() {
  var ss  = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui  = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  // ── Step 1: Sweep for conditions ────────────────────────────────────────
  var foundConditions = _sweepWorkbookForConditions(ss);

  var promptMsg =
    '\u26a1 Specialist / Consultant Review \u2014 Rich (Pre-Reasoning)\n\n' +
    'Like the Standard review, but runs a pre-reasoning pass first:\n' +
    '  \u2022 Silent AI call reasons through the clinical picture under your specialist lens\n' +
    '  \u2022 Generates a hypothesis list before writing\n' +
    '  \u2022 More likely to surface niche, evidence-based insights\n\n';

  if (foundConditions.length > 0) {
    promptMsg += 'CONDITIONS/TOPICS FOUND IN YOUR WORKBOOK:\n';
    for (var ci = 0; ci < foundConditions.length; ci++) {
      promptMsg += '  ' + (ci + 1) + '. ' + foundConditions[ci] + '\n';
    }
    promptMsg += '\nType a number to use one of the above, OR type your own specialist\n' +
                 'context. More specific = better assessment.';
  } else {
    promptMsg += 'No specific conditions detected.\n\nType your specialist context.';
  }

  var response = ui.prompt('\u26a1 Specialist / Consultant Review \u2014 Rich', promptMsg, ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (response.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var userInput = (response.getResponseText() || '').trim();
  if (!userInput) { ui.alert('No specialist context entered. Cancelled.'); return; }

  var specialistContext = userInput;
  var numInput = parseInt(userInput);
  if (!isNaN(numInput) && numInput >= 1 && numInput <= foundConditions.length) {
    specialistContext = foundConditions[numInput - 1];
  }

  var confirmMsg =
    'Specialist lens: "' + specialistContext + '"\n\n' +
    'This will run 2 AI calls (~60-90 seconds total):\n' +
    '  1. Pre-reasoning pass: hypothesis generation under your specialist lens\n' +
    '  2. Report writing: enriched with the prior reasoning\n\n' +
    'Continue?';

  var mhChoice = ui.alert(
    'Mental Health References',
    'Should the report include explicit references to self-harm, overdose episodes, and crisis history?\n\n' +
    'YES — Include all (appropriate for clinical/consultant audience)\n' +
    'NO — Omit explicit crisis details (appropriate for home/family discussion)\n\n' +
    'Note: medication interactions, treatment history, and clinical analysis are unaffected.',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  var omitMHDetail = (mhChoice === ui.Button.NO);

  ui.alert('\u23f3 Running Specialist / Consultant Review \u2014 Rich...\n\n' +
           'Step 1 of 2: Pre-reasoning pass for: "' + specialistContext + '"...\n' +
           'This takes ~60-90 seconds total.');

  // ── Gather data (identical to standard) ────────────────────────────────
  var today   = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var todayFs = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);

  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc8a Meds & Supplements Log');
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var rKey = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[rKey]) {
        seen[rKey] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' [lab: ' + trData[tri][7] + ']' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var sfr = sfData[sf];
      if (!sfr[0] || !sfr[1]) continue;
      if (sfr[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skipSf = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var sfNm = sfr[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var sfSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skipSf.length; sk++) { if (sfNm.indexOf(skipSf[sk]) >= 0) { sfSkip = true; break; } }
      if (sfSkip) continue;
      flaggedContext.push('\u2022 ' + sfr[0] + ': ' + sfr[1] + ' ' + (sfr[3]||'') + (sfr[2] ? ' [lab: ' + sfr[2] + ']' : '') + (sfr[4] ? ' | flags: ' + sfr[4] : '') + (sfr[5] ? ' | meaning: ' + sfr[5] : '') + (sfr[9] ? ' | significance: ' + sfr[9] : ''));
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' [STOPPED ' + mData[mi][6] + ']' : ' [CURRENT]';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + (mData[mi][2] ? ' ' + mData[mi][2] : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [indication: ' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + (mData[mi][8] ? ' \u2014 notes: ' + mData[mi][8] : ''));
    }
  }

var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built — run "Build Clinical Summary" from the menu for best results.'
    : cs.stale
    ? '⚠️ Clinical Summary may be outdated (records have changed since last build — ' + cs.builtDate + ').\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,150) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,100) + ']' : '') + (inv.aiSummary ? ' [AI: ' + inv.aiSummary.substring(0,100) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

 var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE:\n' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'RESEARCH-FLAGGED FINDINGS:\n' + (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' + (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY (complete screened medical history):\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' + (investigations.join('\n') || 'None');

  // ── AI CALL 0: Silent pre-reasoning pass (specialist-scoped) ──────────
  var priorReasoning = '';
  try {
    var promptReason =
      'You are a senior specialist in: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
      'Your task is NOT to write a report. Your task is to produce a structured clinical\n' +
      'hypothesis and reasoning summary to guide a colleague who will write the full assessment.\n\n' +
      'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
      dataBlock + '\n\n' +
      'Work through the following specialist-scoped questions:\n\n' +
      '1. PATIENT CONTEXT THROUGH SPECIALIST LENS — BEFORE ANY NUMBERS\n' +
      'Set the blood results aside. Based on the patient profile, family history, medication history,\n' +
      'and treatment response trajectory: through the lens of ' + specialistContext + ', what is your\n' +
      'clinical impression? Does the family history suggest a heritable pattern relevant to your\n' +
      'specialism? Does the treatment failure trajectory (what has not worked and why) tell you\n' +
      'something diagnostically that the blood numbers alone do not?\n\n' +
      '2. SPECIALIST PATTERN RECOGNITION\n' +
      'Through the lens of ' + specialistContext + ', what is the most clinically significant\n' +
      'pattern in this data? What does a specialist in this field see that a GP assessment\n' +
      'would likely underweight or miss entirely?\n\n' +
      '3. NICHE RESULT INTERPRETATION\n' +
      'Which results — whether lab-flagged or lab-normal — carry specific significance in the\n' +
      'context of ' + specialistContext + '? Include results that appear benign under mainstream\n' +
      'guidance but are meaningful through your specialist lens. Cite specific values.\n\n' +
      '4. FUNCTIONAL vs ABSOLUTE DEFICIENCIES\n' +
      'In the context of ' + specialistContext + ', are there analytes where the absolute value\n' +
      'is within standard range but functional status may be impaired? Give clinical reasoning.\n\n' +
      '5. SPECIALIST INVESTIGATION PRIORITIES\n' +
      'What specific investigations would you order? Rank: high priority / worth considering /\n' +
      'lower priority. For each: test name, what you are looking for, why it matters here.\n\n' +
      '6. MEDICATION INTERACTIONS (SPECIALIST LENS)\n' +
      'Are any current medications particularly relevant — positively or negatively — in the\n' +
      'context of ' + specialistContext + '? Any specialist-level interactions a GP might miss?\n\n' +
      '7. EVIDENCE QUALITY NOTES\n' +
      'For your key hypotheses, flag evidence quality: well-established in specialist literature\n' +
      '/ emerging association / theoretical but plausible. Be honest.\n\n' +
      '8. CLINICAL RECORDS MINING\n' +
        'Read the GP consultation records carefully for specific clinical\n' +
          'details that are diagnostically significant but may not appear in\n' +
          'blood results. Look specifically for:\n' +
          '• Exact nature and site of any reported bleeding — is it PV (vaginal),\n' +
          '  GI, or other? This distinction is diagnostically critical.\n' +
          '• Precise temporal relationships — what started or stopped when,\n' +
          '  particularly in relation to starting or stopping medications\n' +
          '• Patient-reported treatment responses — what worked, what did not,\n' +
          '  what caused unexpected symptoms\n' +
          '• Symptoms that began or resolved in direct temporal relation to\n' +
          '  a specific medication, procedure, or event\n' +
          '• Referral outcomes — what was investigated, what was found,\n' +
          '  what was referred but outcome not recorded\n' +
          '• Any clinical observation in the records that refines or contradicts\n' +
          '  the headline diagnosis or treatment plan\n' +
          'Cite specific consultation dates and exact wording where relevant.\n\n' +
         'Output as structured clinical notes (not a patient report). Cite actual data values.\n\n' +
          'CRITICAL: Only assert facts that are explicitly stated in the data provided.\n' +
          'Do not infer, extrapolate, or combine patient notes with clinical data to create\n' +
          'conclusions that go beyond what is directly evidenced. If something is plausible\n' +
          'but not confirmed, label it explicitly as HYPOTHESIS not as fact.\n' +
          'Patient notes are anecdotal self-report and may be inaccurate — never treat them as\n' +
          'equivalent to clinical records or test results.\n' +
          'When inferring patterns from family history, always consider the base rate of the\n' +
          'finding in the general population before concluding a familial pattern is significant.\n' +
          'Many common conditions affect 15-30% of the population — multiple affected family\n' +
          'members may reflect population prevalence rather than inherited pathology.\n' +
          'Label any family pattern inference as HYPOTHESIS unless supported by specialist\n' +
          'clinical confirmation.\n\n' +
          '800-1000 words.';

    priorReasoning = callAI(promptReason, SPECIALIST_TOKENS_REASON, 'Specialist Rich \u2014 Pre-reasoning');
  } catch(e) {
    priorReasoning = '[Pre-reasoning pass failed: ' + e.message + ' \u2014 report generated without enriched context]';
  }

  var reasoningContext = priorReasoning
    ? '\n\nSPECIALIST PRE-REASONING (clinical hypothesis notes from a prior analysis pass \u2014\n' +
      'engage with these hypotheses in your assessment; do not reproduce them verbatim):\n' + priorReasoning + '\n'
    : '';

  // ── AI CALL 1: Specialist report (enriched) ────────────────────────────
  var prompt =
    'You are a consultant specialist with deep expertise in: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
    'You have been asked to review a patient\'s complete medical workbook and provide a ' +
    'specialist-perspective assessment. This is NOT a GP-level review. Your role is to ' +
    'add the specialist depth that a general assessment cannot reliably provide.\n\n' +
    'CRITICAL INSTRUCTIONS:\n' +
    '1. Weight your assessment toward specialist and rehabilitation literature\n' +
    '2. Explicitly flag where your assessment draws on niche, emerging, or cross-specialty research\n' +
    '3. Be honest about evidence quality — distinguish well-established from emerging from theoretical\n' +
    '4. Flag specific tests or investigations a specialist would consider\n' +
    '5. Where mainstream guidance says normal/benign, but specialist evidence suggests otherwise\n' +
    '   in the context of ' + specialistContext + ', say so explicitly\n' +
    '6. Do NOT repeat GP-level findings unless specifically relevant through your specialist lens\n' +
    '7. Address the patient directly as you \u2014 warm but expert tone\n' +
    '8. Never suggest stopping prescribed medications without specialist/GP review\n' +
    '9. The prior specialist reasoning below has already worked through key hypotheses \u2014\n' +
    '   engage with them substantively in your assessment\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    (omitMHDetail ?
    'AUDIENCE NOTE: This report is for home/family discussion. Do not include explicit\n' +
    'references to self-harm episodes, overdose incidents, or crisis presentations by name.\n' +
    'You may reference "a difficult period" or "mental health challenges" where clinically\n' +
    'relevant, but do not describe specific incidents or methods. Medication history,\n' +
    'interactions, and all clinical analysis are unaffected — discuss fluoxetine, its\n' +
    'pharmacological effects, and treatment duration fully as normal.\n\n' : '') +
    dataBlock + reasoningContext + '\n\n' +
    'Write a specialist deepdive assessment with the following structure:\n\n' +
    '## SPECIALIST LENS: ' + specialistContext.toUpperCase() + '\n\n' +
    '## 1. SPECIALIST OVERVIEW\n' +
    'How does this patient\'s overall picture look through your specialist lens? ' +
    'What is the most clinically significant pattern you see that a GP assessment might underweight? ' +
    'Engage with the hypotheses from the prior reasoning. 2-3 paragraphs.\n\n' +
    '## 2. RESULT-BY-RESULT SPECIALIST REVIEW\n' +
    'For each result relevant through your specialist lens — flagged or not — provide a specialist ' +
    'interpretation. Be explicit when your interpretation differs from mainstream guidance. ' +
    'Include lab-normal results that are significant in the context of ' + specialistContext + '.\n\n' +
    '## 3. WHAT A SPECIALIST WOULD INVESTIGATE\n' +
    'List specific tests, investigations or referrals with specialist clinical reasoning:\n' +
    '  - High priority: should be done to rule in/out a significant finding\n' +
    '  - Worth considering: useful if the above returns positive\n' +
    '  - Lower priority: relevant but can wait\n' +
    'For each: test name, what you are looking for, what a positive result would mean.\n\n' +
    '## 4. MEDICATION & SUPPLEMENT REVIEW (SPECIALIST PERSPECTIVE)\n' +
    'Are any current medications or supplements particularly relevant in the context of ' +
    specialistContext + '? Flag specialist-level interactions a GP assessment might not highlight.\n\n' +
    '## 5. SPECIALIST SUMMARY & RECOMMENDED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'Clear prioritised summary: the 2-3 most important things to act on from a specialist\n' +
    'perspective, and what you would expect to find if investigated.\n\n' +
   'End with this disclaimer:\n' +
    'This specialist-lens assessment is AI-generated for personal reference only. It does not\n' +
    'constitute a clinical opinion or diagnosis. All findings should be discussed with a qualified\n' +
    'specialist in ' + specialistContext + ' before acting on them.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 800-1100 words for sections 1-2. Complete all relevant results — do not truncate.';

  var specRichPart1 = callAI(prompt, SPECIALIST_TOKENS_1, 'Specialist Rich \u2014 Report (1-2)');

  // ── AI CALL 2: Sections 3-5 (enriched) ────────────────────────────────
  var promptSpecRich2 =
    'You are continuing a specialist consultant review for the same patient. ' +
    'Sections 1 and 2 have already been written. Now write sections 3, 4, and 5.\n\n' +
    dataBlock + reasoningContext + '\n\n' +
    '## 3. WHAT A SPECIALIST WOULD INVESTIGATE\n' +
    'List specific tests, investigations or referrals with specialist clinical reasoning:\n' +
    '  - High priority: should be done to rule in/out a significant finding\n' +
    '  - Worth considering: useful if the above returns positive\n' +
    '  - Lower priority: relevant but can wait\n' +
    'For each: test name, what you are looking for, what a positive result would mean.\n\n' +
    '## 4. MEDICATION & SUPPLEMENT REVIEW (SPECIALIST PERSPECTIVE)\n' +
    'Are any current medications or supplements particularly relevant in the context of ' +
    specialistContext + '? Flag specialist-level interactions a GP assessment might not highlight.\n\n' +
    '## 5. SPECIALIST SUMMARY & RECOMMENDED NEXT STEPS\n' +
    'Clear prioritised summary: the 2-3 most important things to act on from a specialist ' +
    'perspective, and what you would expect to find if investigated.\n\n' +
    'End with this disclaimer:\n' +
    'This specialist-lens assessment is AI-generated for personal reference only. It does not\n' +
    'constitute a clinical opinion or diagnosis. All findings should be discussed with a qualified\n' +
    'specialist in ' + specialistContext + ' before acting on them.\n\n' +
    NARRATIVE_RULES + '\n\n' +
    'Total length: approximately 600-900 words for sections 3-5.';

  var specRichPart2 = callAI(promptSpecRich2, SPECIALIST_TOKENS_2, 'Specialist Rich \u2014 Report (3-5)');
  var aiOutput = specRichPart1 + '\n\n' + specRichPart2;

  // ── Build PDF ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var safeContext = specialistContext.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9 ]/g, '').substring(0, 40).trim();
  var docName = 'Specialist Consultant Review (Rich) \u2014 ' + safeContext + ' \u2014 ' + todayFs + ' \u2014 ' + getProviderLabel();
  var doc  = DocumentApp.create(docName);
  var body = doc.getBody();
  body.setMarginTop(54); body.setMarginBottom(54);
  body.setMarginLeft(72); body.setMarginRight(72);

  function sdTitle(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.TITLE);
    try { p.setFontSize(20); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function sdH1(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
    try { p.setFontSize(13); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function sdH2(txt) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt);
    p.setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
    try { p.setFontSize(11); p.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
    return p;
  }
  function sdPara(txt, sz, italic) {
    var p = body.appendParagraph(txt || '');
    p.setFontSize(sz || 10);
    if (italic) p.setItalic(true);
    return p;
  }
  function sdGap() { body.appendParagraph('').setFontSize(4); }
  function sdRule() { body.appendHorizontalRule(); }

  sdTitle('\ud83d\udd2c Specialist / Consultant Review of History');
  sdPara('Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext, 11, false);
  sdPara('Prepared ' + today + '  |  Consultant-layer AI assessment  |  \u26a1 Rich (pre-reasoning) mode', 9, true);
  sdGap();

  var profTbl = body.appendTable();
  profTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var profRow = profTbl.appendTableRow();
  var profCell = profRow.appendTableCell(profile.split(',').map(function(s){ return s.trim(); }).join('   \u2022   '));
  profCell.setBackgroundColor('#f1f5f9');
  profCell.editAsText().setFontSize(9);
  sdGap();

  var ctxTbl = body.appendTable();
  ctxTbl.setBorderWidth(0);
  var ctxRow = ctxTbl.appendTableRow();
  var ctxCell = ctxRow.appendTableCell('SPECIALIST LENS: ' + specialistContext);
  ctxCell.setBackgroundColor('#1e3a5f');
  ctxCell.editAsText().setFontSize(10).setBold(true).setForegroundColor('#ffffff');
  sdGap();

  sdPara('\u26a0\ufe0f  This is a consultant-layer AI assessment designed to complement \u2014 not replace \u2014 ' +
         'GP-level care. It intentionally weights specialist and emerging research over mainstream guidelines. ' +
         'All findings should be discussed with a qualified specialist before acting on them.\n' +
         '\u26a1 Generated in Rich mode: a specialist pre-reasoning pass ran before writing.', 8, true);
  sdRule();

  function renderSection(text) {
    if (!text) return;
    var lines = text.split('\n');
    for (var li = 0; li < lines.length; li++) {
      var line = lines[li].trim();
      if (!line) { sdGap(); continue; }
      if (line.match(/^## /)) {
        sdRule();
        sdH1(line.replace(/^## /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^### /)) {
        sdH2(line.replace(/^### /, '').replace(/\*\*/g, ''));
        continue;
      }
      if (line.match(/^(HIGH PRIORITY|WORTH CONSIDERING|LOWER PRIORITY)/i)) {
        var hp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { hp.setFontSize(10); hp.setBold(true); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      if (line.charAt(0) === '\u2022' || line.match(/^[-*]\s/)) {
        var bp = body.appendParagraph(line.replace(/^[-*\u2022]\s*/, '').replace(/\*\*/g,''));
        try { bp.setFontSize(10); bp.setIndentStart(18); } catch(e) {}
        continue;
      }
      sdPara(line.replace(/\*\*/g,''), 10);
    }
  }

  renderSection(aiOutput);

  sdRule();
  sdPara('Specialist / Consultant Review (Rich) generated ' + today + ' | Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext +
         ' | Medical Tracker v5 | AI-assisted personal reference only \u2014 not a clinical opinion.', 8, true);

  doc.saveAndClose();

  var docFile = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = docFile.getAs('application/pdf');
  pdfBlob.setName(docName + '.pdf');
  var pdfFile = DriveApp.createFile(pdfBlob);
  docFile.setTrashed(true);

  ui.alert(
    '\u2705 Specialist / Consultant Review (Rich) saved to Google Drive.',
    'Filename: ' + pdfFile.getName() + '\n\n' +
    'Specialist lens: ' + specialistContext + '\n\n' +
    'Generated using pre-reasoning mode:\n' +
    '\u2022 Pre-reasoning pass ran first: pattern recognition, functional deficiency screen,\n' +
    '  niche investigation priorities, evidence quality grading\n' +
    '\u2022 Report writing call used this as enriched context\n\n' +
    'Open Google Drive to download.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}

// ─── AUTO-SWEEP: Find condition/diagnosis mentions across workbook ────────────
// Searches Profile, Records, Symptom Investigations, Meds (indication field)
// Returns deduplicated list of conditions/topics found.
function _sweepWorkbookForConditions(ss) {
  var found = {};

  // Condition keywords to recognise (add to this list over time)
  var CONDITION_PATTERNS = [
    // Neurological / trauma
    { pattern: /(post.?concussion|PCS|mTBI|TBI|traumatic brain|concussion)/i,
      label: 'Post-concussion syndrome (PCS) / mTBI' },
    { pattern: /(POTS|postural orthostatic|orthostatic tachycardia|dysautonomia|autonomic dysfunction)/i,
      label: 'POTS / Dysautonomia / Autonomic dysfunction' },
    { pattern: /(fibromyalgia|fibro)/i,
      label: 'Fibromyalgia' },
    { pattern: /(chronic fatigue|CFS|ME\/CFS|myalgic encephalomyelitis)/i,
      label: 'Chronic fatigue syndrome (CFS/ME)' },
    { pattern: /(long covid|post.?covid|post.?viral)/i,
      label: 'Long COVID / Post-viral syndrome' },
    // Endocrine / metabolic
    { pattern: /(thyroid|hypothyroid|hyperthyroid|Hashimoto|Graves)/i,
      label: 'Thyroid disorder' },
    { pattern: /(PCOS|polycystic ovary)/i,
      label: 'Polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS)' },
    { pattern: /(diabetes|insulin resistance|prediabetes|type 2|type 1)/i,
      label: 'Diabetes / Insulin resistance' },
    { pattern: /(adrenal|cortisol|Addison|Cushing)/i,
      label: 'Adrenal / cortisol dysfunction' },
    // Haematological / nutritional
    { pattern: /(anaemia|anemia|iron deficiency|iron deficient)/i,
      label: 'Anaemia / Iron deficiency' },
    { pattern: /(B12 deficiency|B12 deficit|cobalamin deficiency)/i,
      label: 'B12 deficiency' },
    { pattern: /(folate deficiency|folic acid deficiency)/i,
      label: 'Folate deficiency' },
    // Cardiovascular
    { pattern: /(hypertension|high blood pressure)/i,
      label: 'Hypertension' },
    { pattern: /(heart failure|cardiomyopathy|arrhythmia|atrial fibrillation|AF)/i,
      label: 'Cardiac condition' },
    // Autoimmune / inflammatory
    { pattern: /(autoimmune|lupus|SLE|rheumatoid arthritis|RA|Sjögren|Sjogren)/i,
      label: 'Autoimmune condition' },
    { pattern: /(inflammatory bowel|Crohn|Crohns|ulcerative colitis|IBD|IBS)/i,
      label: 'Inflammatory bowel / IBS' },
    { pattern: /(EDS|Ehlers.?Danlos|hypermobility)/i,
      label: 'Ehlers-Danlos syndrome (EDS) / Hypermobility' },
    // Mental health
    { pattern: /(anxiety|panic disorder|generalised anxiety|GAD)/i,
      label: 'Anxiety disorder' },
    { pattern: /(depression|depressive|MDD)/i,
      label: 'Depression' },
    { pattern: /(ADHD|attention deficit|attention deficit hyperactivity)/i,
      label: 'ADHD' },
    // Hormonal
    { pattern: /(endometriosis|endo)/i,
      label: 'Endometriosis' },
    { pattern: /(menopause|perimenopause|HRT|hormone replacement)/i,
      label: 'Menopause / HRT' },
    // Sleep
    { pattern: /(sleep apnea|sleep apnoea|OSA|CPAP)/i,
      label: 'Sleep apnoea' },
    // Infections
    { pattern: /(Lyme|Lyme disease|borreliosis)/i,
      label: 'Lyme disease' },
    { pattern: /(EBV|Epstein.?Barr|glandular fever|mononucleosis)/i,
      label: 'Epstein-Barr / Post-viral (EBV)' },
  ];

  // Sources to sweep
  var sources = [];

  // Profile sheet — all values
  try {
    var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('👤 Profile');
    if (profSheet) {
      var profData = profSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var pi = 0; pi < profData.length; pi++) {
        var pCell = profData[pi].join(' ');
        sources.push(pCell);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Records sheet — type and detail columns
  try {
    var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
    if (recSheet) {
      var recData = recSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 7).getValues();
      for (var ri = 0; ri < recData.length; ri++) {
        if (!recData[ri][1]) continue;
        sources.push([recData[ri][2], recData[ri][3], recData[ri][4]].join(' '));
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Meds sheet — indication column
  try {
    var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Meds & Supplements Log');
    if (medSheet) {
      var medData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
      for (var mi = 0; mi < medData.length; mi++) {
        if (!medData[mi][0]) continue;
        sources.push([medData[mi][0], medData[mi][1], medData[mi][8]].join(' '));
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Symptom investigations from script properties
  try {
    var invIdx = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx.length; ii++) {
      var rawInv = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx[ii]);
      if (rawInv) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(rawInv);
        sources.push([inv.symptoms||'', inv.gpAdvice||'', inv.aiSummary||''].join(' '));
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Significant Findings — meaning and causes columns
  try {
    var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🚨 Significant Findings');
    if (sfSheet) {
      var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 8).getValues();
      for (var si = 0; si < sfData.length; si++) {
        sources.push([sfData[si][5], sfData[si][6]].join(' '));
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Match all sources against patterns
  var allText = sources.join(' ');
  for (var cp = 0; cp < CONDITION_PATTERNS.length; cp++) {
    if (CONDITION_PATTERNS[cp].pattern.test(allText)) {
      found[CONDITION_PATTERNS[cp].label] = true;
    }
  }

  return Object.keys(found).sort();
}


// ─── RESET FOR NEW USER ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Wipes all patient-specific data (test results, records, profile, supplements,
// investigations, treatment trackers) and resets the sheet for a new user.
// PRESERVES: Thresholds sheet and Name Mappings (reusable clinical reference
// data, not patient-specific — saves AI cost for the new user).
// Clears all ScriptProperties EXCEPT the OpenAI API key.
function resetForNewUser() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();

  var confirm1 = ui.alert(
    '⚠️ Reset for New User',
    'This will PERMANENTLY DELETE all patient data:\n\n' +
    '• All test results (TestResults sheet)\n' +
    '• All consultation / GP records (Records sheet)\n' +
    '• All medications & supplements\n' +
    '• All saved investigations\n' +
    '• All treatment tracker tabs (📈 sheets)\n' +
    '• Patient profile fields\n' +
    '• All AI-generated analysis & findings\n' +
    '• All stored AI results & job data\n\n' +
    'The OpenAI API key will be preserved.\n\n' +
    '⛔ This CANNOT be undone. Are you sure?',
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO
  );
  if (confirm1 !== ui.Button.YES) return;

  var confirm2 = ui.alert(
    '⚠️ Final confirmation',
    'All data will be wiped and cannot be recovered.\n\nType YES to confirm — click OK to proceed.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL
  );
  if (confirm2 !== ui.Button.OK) return;

  var errors = [];

  // ── 1. Clear data rows in fixed sheets ───────────────────────────────────
  var dataClearMap = [
    { name: 'TestResults',              startRow: 5, numCols: 10 },
    { name: 'Records',                  startRow: 5, numCols: 7  },
    { name: '💊 Meds & Supplements Log', startRow: 5, numCols: 10 },
    // Thresholds + Name Mappings are NOT cleared — they are reusable clinical
    // reference data, not patient-specific. Saves AI cost for new users.
  ];
  for (var dc = 0; dc < dataClearMap.length; dc++) {
    try {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(dataClearMap[dc].name);
      if (!sh) continue;
      var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
      var clearFrom = dataClearMap[dc].startRow;
      if (lastRow >= clearFrom) {
        sh.getRange(clearFrom, 1, lastRow - clearFrom + 1, dataClearMap[dc].numCols)
          .clearContent().clearFormat();
      }
    } catch(e) { errors.push('Clear ' + dataClearMap[dc].name + ': ' + e.message); }
  }

  // ── 2. Clear profile fields (keep labels in col A, clear col B values) ───
  try {
    var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc64 Profile');
    if (profSheet) {
      profSheet.getRange(3, 2, 20, 1).clearContent();
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('Profile: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 3. Clear Significant Findings data rows ───────────────────────────────
  try {
    var sfSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
    if (sfSheet) {
      var sfLast = sfSheet.getLastRow();
      if (sfLast >= 3) sfSheet.getRange(3, 1, sfLast - 2, 14).clearContent().clearFormat();
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('SF: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 4. Clear Analysis sheet data ─────────────────────────────────────────
  try {
    var anSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udcca Analysis');
    if (anSheet) {
      var anLast = anSheet.getLastRow();
      if (anLast >= 3) anSheet.getRange(3, 1, anLast - 2, 14).clearContent().clearFormat();
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('Analysis: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 5. Clear Investigation Records sheet ──────────────────────────────────
  try {
    var invSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udccb Investigation Records');
    if (invSheet) {
      var invLast = invSheet.getLastRow();
      if (invLast >= 4) invSheet.getRange(4, 1, invLast - 3, 10).clearContent().clearFormat();
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('Investigations sheet: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 6. Delete all Treatment Tracker tabs (📈 prefix) ─────────────────────
  try {
    var allSheets = ss.getSheets();
    for (var ts = 0; ts < allSheets.length; ts++) {
      var tName = allSheets[ts].getName();
      if (tName.indexOf('\ud83d\udcc8') === 0 && tName !== '\ud83d\udcc8 Treatment Tracker') {
        ss.deleteSheet(allSheets[ts]);
      }
    }
    // Reset the template tracker tab to blank
    var trackerTemplate = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udcc8 Treatment Tracker');
    if (trackerTemplate) {
      // Clear the data cells (B4:B17 are the input fields)
      trackerTemplate.getRange(4, 2, 14, 1).clearContent();
      // Clear chart data area
      var ttLast = trackerTemplate.getLastRow();
      if (ttLast >= 21) trackerTemplate.getRange(21, 1, ttLast - 20, 4).clearContent();
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('Treatment trackers: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 7. Delete backup sheets ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  try {
    var allSheets2 = ss.getSheets();
    for (var bs = 0; bs < allSheets2.length; bs++) {
      var bName = allSheets2[bs].getName();
      if (bName.indexOf('BACKUP_') === 0 || bName.indexOf('Backup_') === 0) {
        ss.deleteSheet(allSheets2[bs]);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) { errors.push('Backups: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 8. Clear all ScriptProperties EXCEPT OpenAI key ──────────────────────
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var oaiKey = props.getProperty('OPENAI_API_KEY');
    var antKey = props.getProperty('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY');
    var provider = props.getProperty('AI_PROVIDER');
    props.deleteAllProperties();
    if (oaiKey)   props.setProperty('OPENAI_API_KEY', oaiKey);
    if (antKey)   props.setProperty('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', antKey);
    if (provider) props.setProperty('AI_PROVIDER', provider);
  } catch(e) { errors.push('ScriptProperties: ' + e.message); }

  // ── 9. Done ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var msg = '✅ Reset complete. The tracker is blank and ready for a new user.\n\n' +
    'All data has been wiped.\nOpenAI API key preserved.\n\n' +
    'Next steps for the new user:\n' +
    '1. Fill in 👤 Profile\n' +
    '2. Paste SAR data into TestResults and Records\n' +
    '3. Run 🏥 Quick Refresh';
  if (errors.length > 0) {
    msg += '\n\n⚠️ Some errors during reset (data may be partially cleared):\n' + errors.join('\n');
  }
  ui.alert('Reset Complete', msg, ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}

function rebuildOperationsReference() {
  var ss    = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var name  = '\ud83e\udde0 Operations Reference';
  var existing = ss.getSheetByName(name);
  var sheet = existing ? existing : ss.insertSheet(name);
  sheet.clear(); sheet.clearFormats();
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getMaxRows(), sheet.getMaxColumns()).breakApart();
  buildOperationsReferenceSheet(sheet, ss);
  ss.setActiveSheet(sheet);
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('\u2705 Operations Reference tab rebuilt.');
}

function clearStuckThresholdJob() {
  var ui    = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();

  // Delete all threshold job properties
  var jobKeys = ['TH_JOB_ANALYTES','TH_JOB_PROFILE','TH_JOB_TODAY',
                 'TH_JOB_START','TH_JOB_RESULTS','TH_JOB_ERRORS','TH_JOB_SSID'];
  for (var i = 0; i < jobKeys.length; i++) props.deleteProperty(jobKeys[i]);

  // Clear refresh chain stage if stuck in threshold stage
  var stage = props.getProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  if (stage === 'thresholds' || stage === 'waiting_thresholds') {
    props.deleteProperty('REFRESH_CHAIN_STAGE');
  }

  // Kill any continuation triggers
  _clearContinuationTrigger();
  var allTriggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var t = 0; t < allTriggers.length; t++) {
    var fn = allTriggers[t].getHandlerFunction();
    if (fn === '_runRefreshChain') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(allTriggers[t]);
  }

  ui.alert(
    '✅ Stuck job cleared',
    'All threshold job state and stuck triggers have been removed.\n\n' +
    'You can now run:\n' +
    '• 🔬 Refresh ALL Thresholds with AI  — to regenerate thresholds only\n' +
    '• 🔄 Full Refresh  — to run the complete pipeline\n\n' +
    'If you see "Exceeded maximum execution time" again, the batch size\n' +
    'has been reduced to 8 analytes per call which should prevent this.',
    ui.ButtonSet.OK
  );
}
function unhideStatus() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName('⏳ Refresh Status');
  if (sh) { sh.showSheet(); } else { Logger.log('not found'); }
}
function fixRecordsDates() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  // Sort Records — all 7 columns, data from row 4
  var recSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Records');
  if (recSheet && recSheet.getLastRow() >= 4) {
    var recNumRows = recSheet.getLastRow() - 3;
    var recData = recSheet.getRange(4, 1, recNumRows, 7).getValues();
    recData.sort(function(a, b) {
      return parseDMY(a[1]) - parseDMY(b[1]);
    });
    recSheet.getRange(4, 1, recNumRows, 7).setValues(recData);
  }

  // Sort TestResults — cols B-J only (skip col A = ARRAYFORMULA)
  var trSheet = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  if (trSheet && trSheet.getLastRow() >= 4) {
    var trNumRows = trSheet.getLastRow() - 3;
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(4, 2, trNumRows, 9).getValues();
    trData.sort(function(a, b) {
      return parseDMY(a[0]) - parseDMY(b[0]);
    });
    trSheet.getRange(4, 2, trNumRows, 9).setValues(trData);
  }

SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert('✅ Sorted Records and TestResults by date.');
}

// ── PATIENT CHAT WEB APP ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

function doGet(e) {
  return HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('PatientChat')
    .evaluate()
    .setTitle('My Health Assistant')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

function logChatEntry(question, response) {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var logSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💬 Chat Log');
    if (!logSheet) {
      logSheet = ss.insertSheet('💬 Chat Log');
      logSheet.hideSheet();
      logSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([['Timestamp', 'Question', 'Response']]);
      logSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setFontWeight('bold');
      logSheet.setColumnWidth(1, 160);
      logSheet.setColumnWidth(2, 280);
      logSheet.setColumnWidth(3, 400);
    }
    var timestamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMM yyyy HH:mm');
    logSheet.appendRow([timestamp, question, response]);
  } catch(e) {}
}

function processPatientQuestion(question) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy');
  var profile = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);

  // Build flagged context
  var flaggedContext = [];
  try {
    var thSheet = ss.getSheetByName('🔬 Thresholds');
    if (thSheet) {
      var thData = thSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var i = 1; i < thData.length; i++) {
        var analyte = thData[i][0] ? thData[i][0].toString().trim() : '';
        var value   = thData[i][1] !== '' ? thData[i][1] : '';
        var unit    = thData[i][2] ? thData[i][2].toString().trim() : '';
        var flag    = thData[i][6] ? thData[i][6].toString().trim() : '';
        var meaning = thData[i][7] ? thData[i][7].toString().trim() : '';
        var thresh  = thData[i][5] ? thData[i][5].toString().trim() : '';
        var labSt   = thData[i][3] ? thData[i][3].toString().trim() : '';
        if (analyte && flag && flag.toLowerCase() !== 'normal' && flag !== '') {
          flaggedContext.push('• ' + analyte + ': ' + value + ' ' + unit +
            ' (' + labSt + ') — ' + thresh + ' — ' + meaning);
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Build latest results
  var latestResults = [];
  try {
    var rSheet = ss.getSheetByName('📋 Results');
    if (rSheet) {
      var rData = rSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      var seen = {};
      for (var ri = rData.length - 1; ri >= 1; ri--) {
        var an = rData[ri][0] ? rData[ri][0].toString().trim() : '';
        if (an && !seen[an]) {
          seen[an] = true;
          latestResults.push(an + ': ' + rData[ri][1] + ' ' +
            (rData[ri][2]||'') + ' (' + (rData[ri][3]||'') + ')');
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Build med lines
  var medLines = [];
  try {
    var mSheet = ss.getSheetByName('💊 Medications');
    if (mSheet) {
      var mData = mSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (var mi = 1; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
        var mName = mData[mi][0] ? mData[mi][0].toString().trim() : '';
        if (mName) {
          medLines.push(mName +
            (mData[mi][1] ? ' — ' + mData[mi][1] : '') +
            (mData[mi][2] ? ' (' + mData[mi][2] + ')' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  // Build clinical summary
  var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built.'
    : cs.summary;

  // Build investigations
  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx2 = JSON.parse(
      PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = invIdx2.length - 1; ii >= 0 && investigations.length < 10; ii--) {
      var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
        .getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx2[ii]);
      if (raw) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' +
            (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0, 100));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var dataBlock =
    'PATIENT PROFILE: ' + profile + '\n\n' +
    (patientNotes ? 'PATIENT CONTEXT NOTES:\n' + patientNotes + '\n\n' : '') +
    'FLAGGED RESULTS:\n' +
    (flaggedContext.length > 0 ? flaggedContext.join('\n') : 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'ALL LATEST RESULTS:\n' + latestResults.slice(0, 40).join('\n') + '\n\n' +
    'CURRENT MEDICATIONS & SUPPLEMENTS:\n' +
    (medLines.join('\n') || 'None') + '\n\n' +
    'CLINICAL SUMMARY:\n' + clinicalSummary + '\n\n' +
    'SYMPTOM INVESTIGATIONS:\n' +
    (investigations.join('\n') || 'None');

  var prompt =
    'You are a knowledgeable personal health assistant for this specific patient. ' +
    'The patient or a family member has just asked a question or shared an observation. ' +
    'Your job is to respond helpfully and specifically — using only the patient\'s actual records below.\n\n' +
    'RULES:\n' +
    '• Answer directly and specifically — do not give generic health information\n' +
    '• If the observation or question connects to something in the records, explain the connection clearly\n' +
    '• If it does not appear relevant to anything in the records, say so honestly\n' +
    '• If it raises something worth mentioning to a GP, flag it clearly\n' +
    '• Keep the response concise — 150-300 words maximum\n' +
    '• Never recommend stopping prescribed medications\n' +
    '• End every response with: "⚠️ This is AI-assisted personal reference only — ' +
    'discuss anything relevant with your GP or specialist."\n\n' +
    'TODAY: ' + today + '\n\n' +
    dataBlock + '\n\n' +
    'PATIENT QUESTION OR OBSERVATION:\n' + question;

  var response = callAI(prompt, 800, 'Patient Chat');
  logChatEntry(question, response);
  return response;
}

// ─── NARRATIVE BACKGROUND JOB SYSTEM ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Single combined PDF — all sections in one document.
// Runs as a background trigger with status updates so you can see progress.
//
// HOW TO WIRE IN (3 changes to existing functions):
//
// 1. In generatePersonalHealthNarrativeRich(), after the omitMHDetail line,
//    replace the ui.alert "⏳ Generating..." line with:
//
//      _scheduleNarrativeJob('rich', omitMHDetail);
//      ui.alert('⏳ Generating Personal Health Narrative',
//        'Running in the background — takes approximately 10-12 minutes.\n\n' +
//        'Progress is shown in cell A1 of the ⏳ Refresh Status tab.\n\n' +
//        'You can close this and keep working. Your PDF will appear in Google Drive when complete.',
//        SpreadsheetApp.getUi().ButtonSet.OK);
//      return;
//
// 2. Remove the generateNarrativeAnnexes() and generateNarrativeDiagnosisMatrix()
//    menu items (they are now merged into one document via generatePersonalHealthNarrativeRich).
//    Or keep them as-is for backwards compatibility — they won't conflict.
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function _scheduleNarrativeJob(jobType, omitMHDetail) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_TYPE',    jobType);
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_OMITMH',  omitMHDetail ? 'true' : 'false');
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_SSID',    ss.getId());
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_TODAY',   Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd MMMM yyyy'));
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_TODAYFS', Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'ddMMMyyyy'));
  props.setProperty('NR_JOB_STATUS',  '⏳ Step 1/5: Starting...');
  _clearNarrativeTrigger();
  ScriptApp.newTrigger('_runNarrativeJob').timeBased().after(5000).create();
  _writeNarrativeStatus(ss, '⏳ Step 1/5: Starting — pre-reasoning pass queued');
}

function _clearNarrativeTrigger() {
  var triggers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  for (var t = 0; t < triggers.length; t++) {
    if (triggers[t].getHandlerFunction() === '_runNarrativeJob') {
      ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(triggers[t]);
    }
  }
}

function _writeNarrativeStatus(ss, message) {
  try {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName('⏳ Refresh Status');
    if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet('⏳ Refresh Status'); }
    if (sh.isSheetHidden()) sh.showSheet();
    var now = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'HH:mm:ss');
    var isComplete = message.indexOf('✅') === 0;
    var isError    = message.indexOf('❌') === 0;
    var bg = isComplete ? '#dcfce7' : isError ? '#fee2e2' : '#fefce8';
    var fg = isComplete ? '#166534' : isError ? '#7f1d1d' : '#713f12';
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).merge()
      .setValue('📖 PERSONAL HEALTH NARRATIVE  |  ' + message + '  |  ' + now)
      .setBackground(bg).setFontColor(fg).setFontWeight('bold').setFontSize(10)
      .setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment('middle');
    sh.setRowHeight(1, 36);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  } catch(e) {}
}

function _runNarrativeJob() {
  _clearNarrativeTrigger();
  var props   = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var ssId    = props.getProperty('NR_JOB_SSID');
  var today   = props.getProperty('NR_JOB_TODAY');
  var todayFs = props.getProperty('NR_JOB_TODAYFS');
  var omitMH  = props.getProperty('NR_JOB_OMITMH') === 'true';
  if (!ssId) return;
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(ssId);

  try {
    _workerNarrativeFull(ss, today, todayFs, omitMH);
    _writeNarrativeStatus(ss, '✅ Complete — PDF saved to Google Drive');
  } catch(e) {
    _writeNarrativeStatus(ss, '❌ Error: ' + e.message.substring(0, 120));
  }
}

// ─── SHARED DATA BUILDER ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _buildNarrativeData(ss) {
  var trSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('TestResults');
  var sfSheet  = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udea8 Significant Findings');
  var medSheet = ss.getSheetByName('\ud83d\udc8a Meds & Supplements Log');

  var flaggedContext = [];
  if (sfSheet) {
    var sfData = sfSheet.getRange(1, 1, sfSheet.getLastRow(), 10).getValues();
    for (var sf = 4; sf < sfData.length; sf++) {
      var r = sfData[sf];
      if (!r[0] || !r[1]) continue;
      if (r[0].toString().indexOf('KEY:') >= 0) break;
      var skip = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness score','body fat','total body water','height','weight','resting heart rate'];
      var nm = r[0].toString().toLowerCase();
      var isSkip = false;
      for (var sk = 0; sk < skip.length; sk++) { if (nm.indexOf(skip[sk]) >= 0) { isSkip = true; break; } }
      if (isSkip) continue;
      var sig = (r[9] || '').toString().toLowerCase();
      if (sig.indexOf('clinically irrelevant') >= 0 || sig.indexOf('not clinically significant') >= 0) continue;
      flaggedContext.push({ analyte: r[0], value: r[1], unit: r[3]||'', labStatus: r[2]||'', threshold: r[4]||'', meaning: r[5]||'', significance: r[9]||'' });
    }
  }

  var latestResults = [];
  if (trSheet) {
    var trData = trSheet.getRange(5, 1, 1996, 10).getValues();
    var seen = {};
    var skipTerms = ['wellbeing','alcohol score','smoking score','fitness','body fat','water','height','weight','resting heart'];
    for (var tri = trData.length - 1; tri >= 0; tri--) {
      if (!trData[tri][1] || !trData[tri][3]) continue;
      var aName = trData[tri][3].toString().toLowerCase();
      var skip2 = false;
      for (var st = 0; st < skipTerms.length; st++) { if (aName.indexOf(skipTerms[st]) >= 0) { skip2 = true; break; } }
      if (skip2) continue;
      var key = aName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      if (!seen[key]) {
        seen[key] = true;
        latestResults.push(trData[tri][3] + ': ' + trData[tri][4] + ' ' + (trData[tri][5]||'') + (trData[tri][7] ? ' (' + trData[tri][7] + ')' : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  var medLines = [];
  if (medSheet) {
    var mData = medSheet.getRange(5, 1, 200, 9).getValues();
    for (var mi = 0; mi < mData.length; mi++) {
      if (!mData[mi][0]) continue;
      var stopped = mData[mi][6] ? ' (stopped ' + mData[mi][6] + ')' : ' (current)';
      var notes   = mData[mi][8] ? ' \u2014 Indication: ' + mData[mi][8].toString().trim() : '';
      medLines.push(mData[mi][0] + ' ' + (mData[mi][2]||'') + (mData[mi][3] ? ' x' + mData[mi][3] + '/day' : '') + (mData[mi][1] ? ' [' + mData[mi][1] + ']' : '') + stopped + notes);
    }
  }

  var cs = getClinicalSummary(ss);
  var clinicalSummary = cs.missing
    ? 'Clinical Summary not yet built.'
    : cs.stale ? '\u26a0\ufe0f Clinical Summary may be outdated.\n\n' + cs.summary
    : cs.summary;

  var investigations = [];
  try {
    var invIdx2 = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_INDEX') || '[]');
    for (var ii = 0; ii < invIdx2.length; ii++) {
      var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('INV_FULL_' + invIdx2[ii]);
      if (raw) {
        var inv = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (inv.symptoms && inv.symptoms.indexOf('Leave blank') < 0) {
          investigations.push((inv.date||'') + ': ' + (inv.symptoms||'').substring(0,100) + (inv.gpAdvice ? ' [GP: ' + inv.gpAdvice.substring(0,80) + ']' : ''));
        }
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {}

  var profile      = getProfileSummary(ss);
  var patientNotes = getPatientNotes(ss);
